Chapter 701: Talking Leads to Arguing

Faced with Bao Li Gen's declaration of war, the response from the holy warriors' spirit within the tombstones was sparse.

Even allies of Gu Dong's standing did not approve of Bao Li Gen's offensive and quest for vengeance.

It was too reckless.

Gu Dong paused, choosing not to directly refute Bao Li Gen. Instead, he brought up another matter: "Although Jia Bing, Lin, and the Axe Gang leader have all fallen in battle, judging by the state of their tombstones, their bodies remain remarkably well-preserved. We must recover their remains and lay them to rest in Tranquil Hill. Only then can they find peace, and we can minimize the losses suffered by the Dueling Church."

Unlike the violent declaration from earlier, these words immediately drew agreement from many.

“The Axe Gang leader, Lin, and most crucially, Jia Bing, their life experiences shouldn't be wasted.”

"The more powerful warriors buried in Tranquil Hill, the more combat experience Tranquil Hill gains. The stronger Tranquil Hill becomes, the better it is for all of us."

"Since their bodies remain intact, we should retrieve them whenever possible. This is the honor of a Dueling God's holy warrior, and their true final resting place."

Xue Tuan Zi piped up timidly, "But current intelligence suggests the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are exceptionally formidable. Could they possess saint level combat prowess? “On the surface, Long Fu is silver level, but in reality, he might be stronger than gold level!”

A ripple of unease swept through the group.

Gu Dong hesitated before analyzing, “I believe that possibility is unlikely.”

“If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were merely arms smugglers or just a mercenary group, they absolutely wouldn't possess saint level combat power.”

“If they're a vanguard sent by an enemy nation, it's unthinkable to deploy saint level forces as the initial reconnaissance wave, sacrificing such elite assets for reconnaissance.”

“I lean toward them possessing extraordinary, unconventional methods to achieve such an absurdly overwhelming advantage in combat.”

Bao Li Gen snorted coldly: "So what if they have saint level combat power?"

“Our kingdom has saint level fighters too, three of them, in fact.”

“If we expose the enemy's saint level fighter, he's finished. The kingdom will hunt him down.”

"Even if they're legend level, there's nothing to fear. Hasn't our kingdom had legend level fighters throughout history?" The royal family must have ways to deal with legend levels!"

Silence fell over the group once more.

Bao Li Gen was a fierce warrior in the military, unafraid of death.

But the prevailing thought among the others was: If the enemy had a saint level among them, then confronting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be nothing short of suicide.

As gold holy warriors of the Dueling God, they did not shy away from challenges or the face of death. Yet this did not mean they were willing to “throw themselves into certain death.”

Mei Lin's spirit waves remained steady and calm: “Regardless of their strength, if they are enemies, we must find ways to confront them, not flee.”

“If saint level foes storm our homeland, slaughtering and pillaging our loved ones, defending our nation is our only choice!”

"However, given their current, eerily formidable strength, our top priority now is reconnaissance. I believe securing critical intelligence will significantly boost our odds of victory."

Mei Lin held a military position, and her personal strength was widely acknowledged.

Among those discussing, she was arguably the strongest combatant. Moreover, among Tranquil Hill's 38 gold holy warriors, Mei Lin was likely second only to Long Meng in combat prowess.

Second to none, above all others.

Thus, no one challenged her perspective, and even the hothead fell silent.

Seeing no objections, Mei Lin pressed further: "Is anyone willing to take on the intelligence-gathering mission? Any volunteers? If you choose to act, I can provide additional intelligence, equipment, and compensation."

Spirit waves rippled across the gravestones, then fell silent.

No one stepped forward.

Bao Li Gen, Gu Dong, and the rest certainly had strong motives to target the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, but they were all frontline combatants, not thieves.

He Gai Tou suddenly spoke up: "According to intelligence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is currently in Snow Rabbit City. And as far as I know, the one closest to Snow Rabbit City right now should be Xue Tuan Zi."

No sooner had the words left his mouth than Xue Tuan Zi interjected, her tone slightly urgent, "But I still have to do my alchemy! Since the end of last year, I've been preparing for this year's Warm Snow Cup."

"Besides, this mission clearly isn't suited for me. It should be handled by someone specialized." Xue Tuan Zi insisted.

He Gai Tou immediately agreed: “Xue Tuan Zi's right. Among us here, Yi Jiu is the most suitable candidate.”

Named, Yi Jiu felt compelled to speak up: "I'm an assassin, not a thief. My specialty is killing, not gathering intelligence."

"Of course, if the pay is high enough, I might consider it. But I can't guarantee success."

He Gai Tou added, “Besides Yi Jiu, Zhu Gan could also handle it, you are a ranger after all.”

Zhu Gan flatly refused: "I'm not from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. I won't get involved in such matters. I've never had any interest in this sort of operation. But I am interested in fishing at Ice Pillar Lake again. Does anyone care to join me?"

Gu Dong's eye twitched.

Here we are, burdened with grievances and national hatred, and you're inviting friends to go fishing?

Yet Zhu Gan, being a holy warrior of the Dueling God, had every right to participate in this discussion.

No one was willing to take on this espionage mission.

Though all were of the gold level, most excelled in direct combat. Thieves were few and far between.

Gu Dong couldn't help but sigh inwardly: “What a pity Hua Tang isn't here!”

The composition of the present holy warriors was quite complex. Though all were believers of the Dueling God and shared a fellowship, their bonds weren't particularly strong.

The largest group of holy warriors had military backgrounds. Examples included Teng Dong Lang, the Axe Gang leader, Gu Dong, and Mei Lin. A smaller group hailed from diverse origins: Xue Tuan Zi from the Alchemy Guild, the foreign ranger Zhu Gan, and Yi Jiu from the Thieves' Guild. Enlisting their aid demanded additional compensation.

Mei Lin lamented inwardly: without Long Meng, her personal authority was insufficient to mobilize these forces.

Long Meng commanded the highest prestige.

He was the undisputed top duelist of the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom, having crossed swords with many present and sparing their lives on multiple occasions. Thus, they owed him favors. Moreover, Long Meng possessed an open, generous personality and profound personal charisma, earning the respect and admiration of many.

Mei Lin sighed inwardly: “If he were here, he could rally them all with a single call.”

“Though I hold high rank and authority, I cannot directly mobilize the army's gold level soldiers to confront the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

The military boasts many skilled scouts whose intelligence gathering is highly specialized.

But Mei Lin couldn't do that.

The reason was akin to the Axe Gang leader seeking vengeance, he'd never openly mobilized the gang's forces for a direct confrontation with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

They were the holy warriors of the Dueling God, an organization hidden in the shadows. They couldn't openly reveal that identity.

In a sense, whoever exposed that identity would be the loser.

Mei Lin waited a while, but seeing no one step forward to take the intelligence mission, she reluctantly conceded: “Intelligence gathering is crucial. We must wait for the right person to carry it out. We cannot rush this.”

“In the short term, we can weaken the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from another angle.”

"Long Fu's group is currently touring major cities across the kingdom, opening shops everywhere and mass-selling their potions. Clearly, they aim to penetrate our nation's potion market."

“Regardless of their true motives or plans, whatever the enemy seeks to do, disrupting it is never wrong.”

“This operation requires no prior intelligence gathering—we simply intercept their supply convoys en route.”

“Success isn't guaranteed every time. Dealing them several heavy blows will inflict significant losses, forcing them to deploy more forces to protect their caravans and divert precious resources.”

As soon as she finished, Bao Li Gen cheered loudly.

Gu Dong furrowed his brow slightly, pondered for a moment, and also deemed it a sound plan.

But Mo Gui Ji sneered coldly, “How amusing. A commander from the Kingdom's Navy actually taking on the role of a bandit.”

“Of course, I understand.”

"You are soldiers, after all! War has no room for ethics."

"But you shouldn't always talk to yourselves and act so self-righteous. I've never once considered the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps my enemy."

"If they were enemies, I'd rather invite them to a heroic duel, a fair fight face to face. Even if I lost or died, it would be honorable!"

Mo Gui Ji publicly undermined him, enraging Bao Li Gen into shouting, “You want a duel? Fine! Come fight me! Let's settle this once and for all!”

He remained unfazed, even taunting him, "You think I'm afraid of you? Come on then! I'm in Aurora City. I'll be waiting for you!"

Bao Li Gen roared, "Aurora City, huh? Fine, I'm coming. Just wait!"

The two bickered incessantly, giving others waves of headaches.

Zhu Gan remarked, “How amusing. Perhaps I should alter my plans and head to Aurora City to witness your duel.”

Though a ranger, combat was his second passion, second only to fishing. Otherwise, he wouldn't have followed the Dueling God and become one of the holy warriors.

“I'm interested too!”

“Then I'll join as well.”

Suddenly, several gold holy warriors emitted spirit wave fluctuations.

They hadn't participated in the earlier discussion, remaining silent until now.

Gu Dong let out a long sigh.

He was speechless.

Weren't they here to discuss how to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

How did the conversation veer off course like this?

Accustomed to the military atmosphere where orders were followed without question, Gu Dong felt utterly helpless in the face of these holy warriors each pursuing their own agendas.

Chapter 702: Attacking the Caravan

Xue Tuan Zi sighed regretfully, "I'd love to watch too, but I need to prepare for the Warm Snow Cup Alchemy Tournament. Sigh... If you guys go, please record the magic footage for me."

“Speaking of which, I wonder if the duel is public or private?”

When two heroic duelists wished to avoid spectators, it would become a private duel. Only the combatants would enter the arena, which would transform into a sealed battlefield isolated from the outside world.

In reality, private duels far outnumbered public ones.

Even when invitations were extended, most duelists only invited a select few to witness the fight.

Mo Gui Ji declared, "I couldn't care less! A real man should fight with honor! Besides, letting everyone witness me defeat this brute, Bao Li Gen, would be quite entertaining."

“Defeat me? You're dreaming!” Bao Li Gen roared, “Then let's have a public duel! I'll crush you!”

Suddenly, someone interjected: "Bao Li Gen, aren't you under confinement right now? Do you really have the chance to come out and duel?"

Mei Lin: ...

Xue Tuan Zi: ...

Bao Li Gen fell silent for a moment, then suddenly roared with fury.

Mo Gui Ji sneered, “This is hilarious! Turns out you can't even get out of there!”

Bao Li Gen: "Confinement? What's that? I'll sneak out tonight and be in Aurora City tomorrow!"

Mo Gui Ji sneered coldly: “Fine, I'll wait for you. If you don't show up, you're a coward!”

“You're the fucking coward!” Bao Li Gen bellowed.

“My apologies, Bao Li Gen.” Mei Lin had silently resolved to report this immediately through military channels after this conversation.

Compared to the excitement of a duel, she couldn't tolerate soldiers violating military discipline.

Gu Dong, meanwhile, observed the figure who’d spoken up to remind Bao Li Gen about his confinement: "It’s Yun Zhong. He never says much, but when he does, he hits the bullseye."

Yun Zhong was the most rapidly rising archer in the royal capital lately.

His formidable strength had earned him fame through numerous heroic duels.

Numerous factions sought to recruit him, including the influential Li Jian Clan of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, but Yun Zhong declined every offer.

Accustomed to solitude, he disliked being bound by obligations.

Recently, he had also ascended to become a holy warrior of the Dueling God.

The scene was quite “lively.”

Bao Li Gen and Mo Gui Ji bickered incessantly, their intense spirit wave fluctuations severely disrupting others' conversations.

Without Long Meng present, Mei Lin couldn't control the situation.

She tried to intervene, but Bao Li Gen, consumed by rage, ignored her completely.

Mo Gui Ji, equally furious, paid no heed to Mei Lin's urges either.

Pushed to her limit, Mei Lin temporarily withdrew and immediately used emergency communications to report Bao Li Gen to the army in her personal capacity.

“What? Intelligence indicates Bao Li Gen is secretly preparing to escape the barracks?” The army was startled upon receiving the report.

Though Mei Lin belonged to the navy, her reputation was widespread, extending even to the army.

The army took no chances, immediately dispatching personnel to break into the detention cell.

Disturbed, Bao Li Gen was forced to open his eyes, halting his prayers. This severed his connection to Tranquil Hill's remote communication.

“Aaaah!” He bellowed incessantly, his rage piercing the heavens.

"As expected, Bao Li Gen's condition is abnormal! " The army swiftly verified Mei Lin's intelligence and immediately mobilized elite forces to suppress Bao Li Gen.

Chaos erupted throughout the military camp.

Mei Lin resumed her prayer, her spirit returning to the divine domain of Tranquil Hill to reconnect with the others.

The group remained puzzled, wondering why Bao Li Gen's spirit had abruptly withdrawn.

Mei Lin stated calmly, “I reported him.”

Everyone: ...

It was highly unusual for the holy warriors to communicate across such distances. Each prayer drained a massive amount of divine favor. Only when their spirits were anchored within the tombstones did they cease consuming divine favor.

This meant Mei Lin's two consecutive prayers had cost twice as much divine favor as the others combined!

For a moment, everyone was stunned by Mei Lin's ruthlessness.

After an awkward silence, Mi Fang cautiously offered, “If we need to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' caravan, I can assist.”

Xue Tuan Zi's mind flashed through intelligence on Mi Fang.

He was a human alchemist who had come to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom years ago to study. At the academy, he had romantic entanglements with several noble ladies, earning him the enmity of many male students.

Consequently, Mi Fang frequently participated in heroic duels.

He himself proved highly skilled in combat, winning more often than he lost, and was eventually taken in as a son-in-law by the kingdom's Jing Xiang Clan.

The Jing Xiang Clan has long dominated the kingdom's potion market. Is this move by Mi Fang against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps driven by a dispute over interests?

“Then...” Mei Lin was about to agree.

Suddenly, another spirit fluctuation rippled through the air: “Step aside. I'll handle this.”

Instantly, many hearts sank. Gu Dong felt a faint headache returning as he muttered to himself, “Qing Hong Yan... Another troublesome fellow.”

Gu Dong voiced his concern: “Qing Hong Yan, if I recall correctly, it was Teng Dong Lang who defeated and captured you back then, right?”

Qing Hong Yan stated bluntly: “To be precise, that day, Teng Dong Lang spared my life. He could have killed me outright, but he didn't. He was an excellent soldier. Even after he retired and formed a gang, in my eyes, he remained a man worthy of respect."

“Therefore, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is my enemy!”

"To take revenge on my enemies, I'll start by eliminating their caravans. Heh heh heh, Hahaha, muah hahaha..."

As he spoke, Qing Hong Yan erupted into maniacal laughter, radiating a chilling aura of murderous intent!

“Has this guy gone mad again?”

"Bao Li Gen and Mo Gui Ji might be a bit violent, but they're still normal people. This Qing Hong Yan... Dealing with someone like him always keeps you on edge."

“Qing Hong Yan...” Mei Lin's mind swiftly recalled his information.

Qing Hong Yan was a snow rabbitman captured as a slave, then resold multiple times until ending up in the hands of the Alchemy Great Master Luan Gai.

During Luan Gai's alchemy experiments, he was transformed into a half-flesh, half-alchemy being. While his combat power skyrocketed, his sanity suffered severe damage.

During one alchemy experiment, he completely lost his mind, destroying the laboratory, severely injuring Luan Gai, and escaping. He caused extensive destruction to surrounding villages.

The kingdom mobilized its army to search the mountains. Teng Dong Lang was part of one such squad. They encountered Qing Hong Yan, clashed with him, and ultimately subdued him.

After his capture, though treated by royal physicians, Qing Hong Yan retained his profound mental instability.

He could not tolerate stimuli. Often, the slightest emotional fluctuation would trigger uncontrollable rage, plunging him into a dangerously enraged state where he indiscriminately slaughtered friend and foe alike.

Thus, for years, he had lived in seclusion on the mountain, moving alone.

“Does anyone else wish to make a move?” Mei Lin asked. No one responded.

Her mind raced: Should she choose Mi Fang or Qing Hong Yan?

The former was backed by the Jing Xiang clan, while the latter was one of the elders of the snow rabbitman tribe. Qing Hong Yan's lineage was also extraordinary, descended from the snow rabbitman tribe's high command. His capture as a slave prompted the snow rabbitman higher-ups to intervene directly, appealing to the royal court. The Ice Sculpture Royal Court held the snow rabbitman tribe in high regard, consistently nurturing their relationship.

When Qing Hong Yan rebelled, broke out of the alchemy chamber, and revealed his whereabouts, the court mobilized troops and ordered his capture alive.

Though Mei Lin was a naval commander, her position naturally required strong political acumen.

After some consideration, she offered a pretext: “Then I request your assistance, Qing Hong Yan. Your location is closest to Snow Bird Harbor.”

"I shall relay all pertinent intelligence to you. In short, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' production bases are all in Snow Bird Harbor, and their caravans all depart from there."

“Intercepting them around Snow Bird Harbor would be the most convenient and efficient approach.”

"Of course, I'm not rejecting you, Mi Fang. You may also take action simultaneously. I'll have someone deliver the corresponding rewards to both of you."

Mi Fang gave a fake smile: "Heh. Since Qing Hong Yan is handling it, I'll stay out of it."

After assigning this task, Mei Lin initiated a mental communication: "The Axe Gang leader is dead. His coffin bearer is his son, Fu Zhao. Gu Dong, can you go retrieve him?"

Gu Dong immediately replied, “Of course! I'm honored to take this assignment!”

Mei Lin felt deeply relieved. After all, these were professionals from the regular army, their dedication was incomparable.

Zhu Gan sighed and volunteered, “Lin and I were fishing buddies. Now that he's gone, I'd like to assist his coffin bearer.”

Xue Tuan Zi added, “I can handle Jia Bing's side too.”

Mei Lin considered, "Zhu Gan will take charge of Lin's coffin bearer. But Jia Bing hasn't designated one yet, it's unfortunate."

Not every gold holy warrior of the Dueling God had a coffin bearer; in a sense, the coffin bearer was the heir.

Choosing an heir was often exceedingly difficult.

Gu Dong then asked, “Bing Xiao brought many outsiders here to Tranquil Hill without authorization. How should these people be dealt with?”

"During his ritual, the divine domain unexpectedly expanded its reach. This anomaly was likely orchestrated by hostile forces to facilitate their infiltrators entering Tranquil Hill!"

“As we all know, covert factions or individuals have persistently spied on us and Tranquil Hill.”

He Gai Tou concurred, "Perhaps even the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps this time was targeting us. We've already lost four gold level holy warriors to them!"

“Why not just kill them outright?” Mo Gui Ji sneered, “What's there to hesitate about?”

"Bing Xiao is our Lord's silver level holy warrior. Though he lacks the standing to erect a tombstone, he's worthy of trust. Killing his men outright wouldn't be wise." Gu Dong stood up for his comrade-in-arms.

Mei Lin echoed the sentiment: "I spoke privately with Bing Xiao. He's already shared his proposed solution with me. I believe it's prudent."

Mei Lin emitted a wave of spirit fluctuations, relaying Bing Xiao's arrangements to the group.

“Then let's proceed with that plan for now.”

“Just because they've converted, can we truly trust them?”

“At least they won't threaten Tranquil Hill anymore.”

“I have no objections.”

“Relax, Tranquil Hill is a divine domain.”

“Hmph! If anything goes wrong, you'll all be held responsible!”

Opinions varied, but most accepted Mei Lin's solution.

After that, everyone spoke their minds. Since private conversations weren't possible, constant mental fluctuations flooded in, filling the air with chaotic messages.

Mei Lin felt utterly drained.

She'd rather command an army than make arrangements here.

But there was no choice.

Even she had to admit: these gold level holy warriors were formidable fighters individually, stronger than the elite in the military. They were essentially the backbone of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's combat strength!

“Our military infiltration here isn't deep enough.”

“The situation was steadily shifting in our favor, but the death of Teng Dong Lang, Jia Bing, and the other two has suddenly pulled it back.”

“For now, we can only treat them as high level mercenaries.”

“Though if Long Meng were here, he could likely command their obedience.”

Truthfully, Mei Lin deeply envied the stature and authority Long Meng commanded among them.

Chapter 703: Fu Zhao vs Qing Hong Yan

Gu Dong, a former soldier, sprang into action the moment he received orders.

The intelligence from Mei Lin deepened his understanding of Blood Kin City, the Axe Gang, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

As a gold level soldier, his formidable strength drew widespread attention.

But asking him to run all the way to Blood Kin City on foot, like Lin had done, was simply too far.

Mei Lin stated directly, “Use the secret teleportation formation. Destroy it after you're done.”

Gu Dong swiftly reached the vicinity of Blood Kin City via the hidden teleportation formation.

“Blood Kin City...” Gu Dong gazed at the city walls and sighed.

He disliked the city's name, just like other soldiers from other kingdoms.

“It seems even the Axe Gang leader didn't want to come here back then, but he caused quite a ruckus.”

“Still, bringing in Fu Zhao at the cost of destroying a secret teleportation formation... that price is truly steep.”

Gu Dong mused inwardly.

Such a people-centered action strengthened his sense of belonging to the kingdom.

“According to intelligence, Fu Zhao possesses exceptional bloodline potential, capable of reaching gold level strength.”

“If we could recover his father's remains and inter them in Tranquil Hill, given time, he might become a new gold level combat force!”

The corpse of the Axe Gang leader was akin to a private gold level inheritance.

From this perspective, sacrificing a teleportation formation for a gold level combatant was hardly a significant loss.

The Axe Gang was the foremost power in Blood Kin City, even surpassing the authority of the city government palace.

After probing the situation, Gu Dong sensed the Axe Gang was teetering on the brink of collapse.

Their leader hadn't been seen for days. Normally, this wouldn't be unusual, but rumors were spreading throughout the city that the Axe Gang leader was dead!

“This must be a rumor deliberately spread by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, right?”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps isn't targeting the Axe Gang directly. They're using rumors to expose the gang's inherent weakness.”

“They plan to exploit the open conflict between the Axe Gang and the city government palace, leveraging the city lord's power to eliminate the Axe Gang!”

Gu Dong deduced the truth.

Indeed, that was the case.

After slaying the Axe Gang leader, Zong Ge and Zi Di had discussed how to handle the gang.

Undoubtedly, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Axe Gang were mortal enemies, despite having forged a magic cooperation pact.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps couldn't spare more forces to deal with the Axe Gang.

Even if they had the strength, Zi Di didn't want to do it.

They had no connections or influence in Blood Kin City. Even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wiped out the Axe Gang, they couldn't absorb most of the Axe Gang's assets. They'd only be doing the work for the Blood Kin City Lord.

Ultimately, they agreed: they would only spread rumors, prompting Blood Kin City's authorities to intervene. Meanwhile, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would seize the opportunity to expand. Should the chance arise in the future, they might also get involved, devouring some of the Axe Gang's assets to bolster their own strength.

Gu Dong located the Axe Gang's headquarters with ease.

He wasn't concerned about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ scheme; his focus was entirely on Fu Zhao.

That was his mission.

However, after asking, he received bad news, Fu Zhao had left, leading the Fire Axe Cavalry to escort a large merchant caravan. The vast majority of the caravan's cargo consisted of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' dragon rider potion.

Gu Dong immediately grew anxious: “This is bad. Qing Hong Yan has already gone to ambush the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' caravan. What if he encounters Fu Zhao?”

If it were any other holy warrior, Gu Dong wouldn't be so worried. The main concern was that Qing Hong Yan possessed a significant hidden danger. He couldn't handle too much provocation; rage would easily consume his mind, causing him to completely lose his senses and desire only to slaughter everything in sight.

Fu Zhao rode his horse, leading the gang members as they slowly escorted the caravan forward.

One of the Fire Axe Cavalrymen assigned to reconnaissance returned to report: “All clear.”

Fu Zhao nodded and continued assigning replacements for the patrols.

Seeing his relentless vigilance, a senior subordinate advised, "Young gang leader, such frequent reconnaissance isn't necessary. We're already close to Blood Kin City. No one would dare provoke the gang leader."

Fu Zhao's brow furrowed slightly.

He detected a hint of testing in those words.

After all, rumors of the Axe Gang leader's death had already spread throughout Blood Kin City.

"According to the original plan, Father should have returned that very night. At the very latest, he should have been back by the next day."

“But now, so many days have passed, and not a single word has come from Father.”

“Something must have gone wrong. Damn it...”

“What should I do?”

Just as Fu Zhao pondered how to respond to this probe, he suddenly heard the whoosh of wind.

The next moment, he saw a figure flying toward him from a distance.

The aura of a gold level fighter swept over him without the slightest concealment.

“Who goes there?!”

“A gold level fighter, and one who masters flight battle skill!”

“Who could it be?”

Fu Zhao's expression darkened instantly. He immediately ordered everyone to prepare for battle.

As the figure drew nearer, the group could make out the gold level fighter's features.

He was a snow rabbitman, a rabbit head, and a human body.

His eyes were ice blue, his build robust, with extensive metal structures replacing flesh and bone.

He resembled an alchemy puppet more than a living being.

“What kind of monster is this?”

“Are there fighters like this near Blood Kin City?”

None of them recognized Qing Hong Yan.

For one thing, Qing Hong Yan was a snow rabbitman, a race not known for its activity. In recent years, he had secluded himself somewhere in the Splice Mountain.

On the other hand, the uproar Qing Hong Yan caused back then was confined to the circles of magicians and the kingdom's military. Although several rural villages were slaughtered by Qing Hong Yan, the royal family suppressed the incident to maintain relations with the snow rabbitman tribe, doing everything possible to minimize Qing Hong Yan's exposure.

Therefore, to Fu Zhao and the others, Qing Hong Yan was an unknown entity.

Qing Hong Yan halted abruptly, blocking the path of Fu Zhao and his companions.

Spotting Fu Zhao, he recognized a striking resemblance to someone from his intelligence reports. Instantly, everything clicked into place.

“You are Fu Zhao?”

“Hand over the caravan behind you!”

Qing Hong Yan's demand was blunt, his voice thick with murderous intent.

“My Lord, may I ask who you are...” Fu Zhao bowed respectfully before inquiring sternly, adding, “You recognize me, yet I do not know you. What is your relationship to my father?”

Fu Zhao reflected: Before Lin's arrival, he too had been unaware his father maintained such close ties with a gold level figure.

But Qing Hong Yan misinterpreted Fu Zhao’s meaning. He snorted coldly: “Your father is dead. The owner of the caravan you swear to protect is the one who killed him, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“You still intend to shield your enemy’s caravan?”

Qing Hong Yan’s blunt truth instantly sparked uproar among the caravan and Axe Gang members.

Fu Zhao's heart lurched violently, and he hastily denied it.

This was a world of the strong.

The Axe Gang's power, surpassing even the acting city Blood Kin City Lord, stemmed primarily from their gold level leader.

If the leader died, and the Axe Gang had no second gold level member, the situation would inevitably collapse.

Thus, Fu Zhao denied it immediately. After all, he was only silver level. With his father missing and unreturned, he couldn't yet shoulder the Axe Gang's responsibilities.

Qing Hong Yan's patience had nearly vanished. He didn't bother with lengthy explanations: “Fu Zhao, get lost! Every member of this caravan dies today!”

These words terrified the caravan members.

They immediately shouted, reminding Fu Zhao of their mutual cooperation and magic contracts.

Others attempted to placate Qing Hong Yan's murderous intent with money.

“Young gang leader, what should we do?” asked the Axe Gang elder who had previously sounded him out.

Fu Zhao's face darkened. After rapid deliberation, he slowly shook his head, “How can we trust the enemy's words?”

“We've already escorted the caravan this far. Blood Kin City isn't far now.”

"If we retreat here, how can we ever hold our heads high in Blood Kin City? What credibility would we have left to convince anyone we can handle escorting caravans?"

The elder looked troubled. "But this one is gold level. With the leader absent, the Fire Axe Cavalry is our strongest force. We can't just start a fight and lose them all here."

Fu Zhao: “We can retreat. Scatter the caravan and rush back to Blood Kin City.”

“We'll assess the situation and try to pin this guy down!”

“Pin down a flying gold level?” The gang elder's expression darkened further.

Fu Zhao's expression hardened: “This is an order. Execute it!”

The Axe Gang members blocked the path before Qing Hong Yan, while the caravan members behind them scattered wildly. Some clutched their goods, others abandoned everything, fleeing at breakneck speed.

Qing Hong Yan froze for a moment, then roared with fury: "You dare play tricks on me!"

Fu Zhao bellowed in response: “Fire Axe Cavalry, follow me! Move out!”

He spurred his horse forward, leading the charge. The Fire Axe Cavalry, mounted on fiery red horses, followed closely behind. In an instant, thunderous hoofbeats surged forth like a raging torrent.

But Qing Hong Yan did not pursue them. Instead, he turned his attention to the scattered members of the merchant caravan.

His formidable strength left the caravan members utterly outmatched, resulting in heavy casualties.

“He's not coming after us!” The Axe Gang members breathed a sigh of relief.

Witnessing this, Fu Zhao gritted his teeth, yanked the reins hard, and forced his horse to change course.

Led by Fu Zhao, the cavalry swept around like a fiery streak across the snowy landscape, closing in on Qing Hong Yan.

“What is the young gang leader doing?!” The Axe Gang members were stunned.

“This fool...” The gang's elder gritted his teeth in frustration.

The Fire Axe Cavalry represented the gang's ultimate strength. He feared they might be decimated here.

But Fu Zhao was too young, unaware of the stakes, driven only by reckless bravado. He had actually charged at a gold level fighter!

“Elder, what should we do?”

“Attack together!” Cowardice was intolerable within the gang. The elder had no choice but to follow suit and join the assault. If he didn't, he would surely be branded a traitor and purged after the battle.

Chapter 704: Gu Dong vs Qing Hong Yan

“Throw your axes!” Fu Zhao roared, drawing a throwing axe from his waist and hurling it with all his might.

The cavalry behind him followed suit.

A volley of axes ignited mid-air without flame, spinning like torches as they hurtled toward Qing Hong Yan.

He dodged swiftly, only a few axes striking his raised forearm.

With clanging metallic clashes, the axes fell limply to the ground.

“Damn it, damn it!” Qing Hong Yan seethed with rage.

He clearly hadn't intended to attack Fu Zhao, yet the latter had dared to be so insolent as to initiate an assault!

Qing Hong Yan respected Teng Dong Lang, but he had no particular regard for the Axe Gang leader.

Much less for Fu Zhao, who was merely the leader's son.

Stung by the provocation, Qing Hong Yan's eyes reddened, a vein bulging on his forehead.

“I'm not your enemy! I wanted to spare you, yet you dare challenge me? You dare insult me?!” Rage surged through Qing Hong Yan's mind like a tidal wave.

Gritting his teeth, he murmured a chant:

“When rage burns within my heart; I shall keep my mind's lake calm as a mirror; Gather inner composure as solid as ice.”

Through the chant, a cool sensation materialized, like a spring welling up in his fiery heart, dousing much of his fury.

Qing Hong Yan soared skyward, then descended upon the densest cluster of merchants, bringing death to these unfortunate souls.

“Follow me!” Fu Zhao bellowed, leading the cavalry squad to charge once more at Qing Hong Yan.

The elder behind the cavalry watched in horror and screeched in his mind, “Young gang leader! Are you mad? Provoking a gold level opponent again and again?”

But there was no choice. The elder had to follow the charge.

The Axe Gang's morale soared as Qing Hong Yan deliberately evaded their charge.

The elder desperately wanted to call out but now was not the moment.

Seeing this reckless horde charging once more, another vein bulged on Qing Hong Yan's forehead.

He swiftly murmured incantations, forcing himself to endure.

“Let not rage cloud my mind; I shall rise above like ice and snow; Unshaken by the world's petty words.”

He dodged the encircling Fire Axe Cavalry once more, targeting only the fleeing merchants and their laden carts.

Watching his brutal slaughter, even the Axe Gang members felt a chill.

“Young gang leader!” roared the elder.

Many others kept glancing at Fu Zhao, their eyes silently urging him to “know when to quit.”

Fu Zhao sensed the shifting mood. Truth be told, the brutal methods displayed by Qing Hong Yan had him trembling inside.

“But I cannot retreat!”

"I refuse to believe Father is dead! He has the power of three gold level experts on his side."

"But accidents happen. He might be gravely wounded or trapped by some ambush. That's why he hasn't returned yet."

"Father bound himself to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps through a magic contract. He may have broken free of that bond, but I can't gamble on it!"

“If he's gravely wounded now and I deliberately abandon protecting the caravan, the magic contract will activate. Father could die from his injuries because of my retreat!”

Fu Zhao's gaze was unwavering. He had reasons he couldn't back down.

He had to protect the caravan at all costs.

Leading the Fire Axe Cavalry, he charged once more at Qing Hong Yan.

A third vein pulsed across Qing Hong Yan's forehead. His eyes blazed scarlet as murderous intent surged to his brow.

Gritting his teeth until they ached, every muscle trembling, he chanted:

“Embarking on the journey of the soul; I part the dust of this world's vanity; From the depths of my soul, I chant a melody... a melody... of serenity and light... Ah!”

He exploded.

Red-Eye was pushed to the brink, shattering his limits.

He threw his head back and let out a deafening roar. Vast gold battle qi erupted from him without restraint, unleashing a storm that crushed everything in its path.

“Damn it!” The Axe Gang members trembled in terror.

After his roar, Qing Hong Yan's face twisted with savage intent as he fixed his gaze on Fu Zhao and the others.

He extended his palm; its center aimed squarely at the Fire Axe Cavalry.

His hand was unlike any ordinary palm, bearing five fleshy pads positioned at the bases of each finger.

This was the physiological structure of a snow rabbitman.

But his palms differ from those of ordinary snow rabbitmen, his five pads have been modified, each replaced by a gemstone: red, blue, green, yellow, and purple.

Gem Ray!

In the next instant, the gems erupted with blinding brilliance, each firing a laser beam of distinct color.

The lasers swept through the Axe Gang members with lightning speed.

When Fu Zhao finally registered what had happened, he frantically scanned his surroundings.

The next instant, his mouth gaped open, eyes wide with terror.

Right beside him, the Fire Axe Cavalrymen were either sliced in half, incinerated into flames, or frozen solid into ice blocks, many met gruesome deaths.

This was merely the first wave of attack. The Axe Gang's hard-earned cavalry squadron had already suffered thirty percent casualties!

"Retreat! This isn't just any ordinary gold level!" Fu Zhao's heart pounded in terror as he truly grasped the enemy's power. He urgently commanded his cavalry to turn and flee toward Blood Kin City.

He was finally retreating.

But Qing Hong Yan had already lost his sense of reasoning. Soaring skyward, he then dove down in a steep plunge.

Fu Zhao's horse was swift, and the Fire Axe Cavalry behind him were no slackers either, dodging this assault.

But the other gang members trailing behind them suffered terribly.

Qing Hong Yan slaughtered them like chickens and ducks being butchered.

The elder bellowed for help yet could only watch as Fu Zhao and the others grew ever more distant.

"Damn it, come back and save me!" The elder was crushed by Qing Hong Yan's battle qi in his despair, his body reduced to a pile of bone fragments and flesh.

Fu Zhao glanced back hurriedly. Seeing this, a flicker of satisfaction crossed his mind: “Good. Only with this old man dead can I rest easy.”

The elder's earlier spying and the ambition he'd displayed routinely had made Fu Zhao feel threatened.

His provocation of Qing Hong Yan had another purpose: to use him as a tool to eliminate internal threats within his own gang!

He succeeded.

But Qing Hong Yan didn't let him off easily. He abandoned the scattered merchants and redirected his assault onto Fu Zhao's gang members.

The Axe Gang members, utterly demoralized, were slaughtered in droves as they fled.

“Damn it, did I screw up?”

Fu Zhao's face turned ashen as Qing Hong Yan finally intercepted him.

“Kill! Kill! Kill!” Qing Hong Yan, driven into a severe frenzy, had completely forgotten Fu Zhao's identity. Any living thing within his current field of vision was nothing but an eyesore, something to be eliminated.

The laser fired again, locked onto Fu Zhao.

Fu Zhao had no time to dodge, forced to watch helplessly as the laser approached.

But in the next instant, a figure abruptly materialized, interposing itself between Fu Zhao and the laser.

Boom!

After the violent explosion, the smoke and dust cleared, revealing Gu Dong finally arriving on the battlefield.

“Thank goodness I made it,” Gu Dong gasped, panting heavily, “That was no easy feat!”

“Qing Hong Yan, calm down! Listen to me... dammit.”

Qing Hong Yan ignored the warning, charging relentlessly.

Gu Dong stepped forward to intercept Qing Hong Yan, shouting to Fu Zhao, “I'm your father's friend! Get out of here! I'll hold him off!”

“T-Thank you!” Fu Zhao hurriedly thanked him, leading his dwindling cavalry in a hasty retreat from the battlefield.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Qing Hong Yan and Gu Dong clashed fiercely through their battle skills, thunderous explosions echoing as shockwaves surged between them.

After several rounds, Gu Dong found himself at a disadvantage.

Following more exchanges of offense and defense, Gu Dong's fighting spirit faltered. Bruised and battered, he muttered, “Damn it. When Qing Hong Yan goes berserk, his combat power easily ranks among the top five holy warriors!”

“Fu Zhao is gone. I've no reason to risk my life against a madman.”

Gu Dong was no match for Qing Hong Yan. Even as a holy warrior of the Dueling God, his combat prowess ranked among the kingdom's top five gold level experts.

But being a gold level meant one thing: if you couldn't win, you could still escape.

Snow Bird Harbor.

Fei She knocked on the door and saw Tripleblade looking grim and deeply troubled.

Fei She’s heart skipped a beat. He knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch was ostensibly run by Men Shi and Chi Lai, but the real puppet master behind the scenes had always been Tripleblade.

“If nothing serious happened, Tripleblade wouldn’t look like this.”

Having survived together and escaped Mystifying Monster Island, Fei She understood Tripleblade well enough.

Sure enough, Tripleblade first motioned for Fei She to sit down before handing him a report.

After reading it, Fei She leapt from his seat in shock: “What? A caravan suffered a gold level attack? All the goods are lost?”

“And this happened while the Axe Gang's young gang leader personally escorted the convoy, protected by their Fire Axe Cavalry?”

Fei She fell silent for a moment, his mind racing, “Could it be that intelligence about the Axe Gang leader's death leaked out?”

“Unlikely,” Tripleblade shook his head, “The Axe Gang suffered heavy losses in this battle, especially their Fire Axe Cavalry, only a third remain.”

"The silver lining is that most of the magic potions we transported via caravan were counterfeit. The actual losses are far less than outsiders estimate!"

After Zong Ge slew three gold level warriors, Zi Di and the others uncovered the identities of Jia Bing and Lin. After ransacking Jia Bing's alchemy lab, though they still knew nothing of the Dueling God's holy warriors, they pieced together much information.

Such vital intelligence was, of course, relayed immediately to the mercenary guild's branch in Snow Bird Harbor.

Thus, Tripleblade and Fei She were well aware of the hardships Zong Ge and Zi Di faced, as well as the survivors' current predicament.

Even though the Axe Gang had signed a magic contract with them, they could no longer be trusted.

After deliberation, they decided to establish a decoy operation. Under normal circumstances, they would pose as the primary merchant caravan, transporting massive quantities of counterfeit potions.

The genuine potions, however, would be secretly transported through channels controlled by the Li Jian Clan and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Though the losses this time were minor, our cover has been blown,” Fei She said gravely. “If the enemy discovers our potions haven't run out and are still selling well, they'll realize we have alternative transport routes and target them more precisely.”

This was his greatest concern.

Chapter 705: Magician Level Advancement

Tripleblade nodded slightly and analyzed, “Our enemies have made their move!”

“But they didn't target Lord Zong Ge or Miss Zi Di. They might have been intimidated by Lord Zong Ge's astonishing combat record.”

“Attacking the caravan shows precise targeting, they found our weak spot.”

"Moreover, two gold level fighters appeared in this assault. At least one was likely dispatched by the enemy, though it's also possible both gold level fighters were acting in concert. After all, this enemy organization operates in the shadows, seemingly afraid to openly reveal their true identities."

"Miss Zi Di and Lord Zong Ge's analysis is correct; the enemy is formidable, having once again deployed gold level combat power!"

Tripleblade's brow furrowed deeply as he felt immense pressure.

This was a clash between two organizations!

The enemy possessed immense strength, concealed in the shadows, making them impossible to guard against.

In contrast, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was significantly weaker. Their strongest visible member was the dragonman youngster, merely a silver level with a record of slaying a gold level opponent. Their true strongest member was Zong Ge, but he had only recently ascended to gold level.

As for the other survivors, their combat prowess was weak.

Tripleblade, as the leader of the mercenary division, was only at the iron level. He couldn't personally handle many matters and had to rely on Chi Lai and Men Shi to handle them.

“Do you think the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord truly doesn't know Teng Dong Lang's mysterious identity?”

“Perhaps that's his real intention for dragging our group into this!”

Tripleblade speculated, his expression dark.

Fei She sighed, “What good does it do to dwell on that now?”

"Given the current situation, our business plan will likely fail. Though it hasn't been destroyed yet, it will inevitably suffer heavy blows in the future."

“These enemies are too powerful!”

“Our current stability stems from them being intimidated by the practical effectiveness of the Deceptive Disguise spell.”

“Our strongest trump card is the Deceptive Disguise spell, yet the Pearl Bubbles have been severely depleted, few remain.”

"For this business venture, we invested most of the funds acquired from Two Eyes Island. Should we fail this time, we stand to lose not just this investment, but our entire future prospects."

"The remaining funds will barely sustain our faction's operations. Our growth and expansion will be painfully slow. We will be severely crippled!"

Fei She, with his broad vision and rich experience, already foresaw the bleak future awaiting the survivors.

The best solution was to eliminate the enemy.

But facing an adversary who casually deployed gold level combatants, Tripleblade and Fei She felt utterly lacking in confidence.

Tripleblade sighed deeply: “Our group has only Lord Zong Ge as a gold level, one man cannot hold the fort alone.”

Fei She added: “We can also count the commander. Though he's silver level, his combat prowess is truly gold level. But with just these two Lords, passive defense would be a stretch, wouldn't it?"

After this analysis, Tripleblade and Fei She felt immense psychological pressure.

With unclear intelligence, the holy warriors of the Dueling God had misjudged the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' strength, even imagining deeper connections. Out of various apprehensions, they had only attempted tentative counterattacks and retaliations.

Similarly, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could only speculate about their enemies' strength, their estimates wildly inaccurate, their psychological pressure immense.

Fei She suddenly declared, "This intelligence must be reported immediately to Zi Di and Zong Ge. They must heighten their vigilance to guard against a heavy blow from the enemy."

In this regard, Tripleblade proved more seasoned: “I've already dispatched men to do so, but Lord Zong Ge's group has just departed Snow Rabbit City. We cannot contact them for the time being.”

“Why hasn't the commander returned yet?” Fei She lamented.

Tripleblade was merely iron level, while Fei She fared worse, just an ordinary mortal. They truly lacked any real backing.

This was the world of the strong.

Only the strong could serve as pillars of support, ensuring morale remained steadfast.

“Let us hope the commander returns swiftly,” Tripleblade's gaze hardened once more, “Until then, we must do everything in our power to stabilize the situation and minimize all losses!”

“Yes, that is precisely what we must do,” Fei She echoed, his expression hardening with resolve.

Unlike the holy warriors of the Dueling God, the survivors shared no common faith.

But they shared a common interest, they were in the same boat, destined to be purged by the Sheng Ming Empire if their atonement failed!

Helping each other meant helping themselves.

Coupled with their experience of working together on Mystifying Monster Island, they had forged a solid bond of comradeship.

“I'll speak with Bu Quan to have her accelerate alchemy production as much as possible, preventing disruptions to future supply lines,” Fei She declared, preparing to act, “We must also inform President Zi Di about this business plan to expedite their efforts.”

Tripleblade added, "If the business plan fails, we'll face an economic winter. During this period, I'll focus on maximizing revenue and minimizing expenses."

"As a mercenary group, we need to undertake mercenary missions to build market confidence in us. This way, in the future, mercenary missions can become our primary source of funding."

“I'll contact Lord Zong Ge to explain my perspective, attempting to reduce or even suspend the current training exercises and recall Chi Lai and Men Shi.”

Fei She nodded, hurriedly took leave, and raced against the clock to do what he could.

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest.

Deep Sea Monster Fish, meditation chamber.

The fishman youngster sat cross-legged, eyes tightly shut, deep in meditation.

Massive quantities of water elements poured in from all directions, flooding into him.

For a water magician, this was an ideal meditation environment.

On one hand, it was the deep sea—where water elemental energy was most abundant. On the other hand, the Deep Sea Monster Fish's meditation chamber continuously purified and replenished the water elemental energy, ensuring its concentration was higher than that of the ship and remained constant despite the fishman youngster's constant absorption.

The water element was ceaselessly drawn in by the fishman youngster, transforming into pale blue magic power within his personal magic pool.

The next moment, the fishman youngster's body trembled slightly.

The scattered spirit power that had been drifting during his meditation suddenly coalesced, forming a spiritual space uniquely his own level!

At the same time, the youngster's magician level advanced from bronze level to iron level.

“My magician level has risen,” the fishman youngster opened his eyes, his expression a mix of delight and confusion, “But why... do I have a spiritual space?”

The fishman youngster was previously a silver level fighter and an iron level magician.

Just now, his magician level had risen from bronze level to iron level.

This was within his expectations.

His innate life level far surpassed bronze level, and his fishman bloodline was exceptionally potent, rapid advancement was natural.

Zhong Yong had predicted this beforehand.

“After reaching iron level, the storage capacity of my magic pool has increased significantly.”

The fishman youngster meditated briefly before abruptly stopping his meditation. His verification confirmed: “The efficiency of refining magic power has also skyrocketed.”

“But why has my spirit coalesced into a space?”

Bronze level magicians transitioned from ordinary humans to the extraordinary, formally possessing their personal magic pool.

Iron level magicians experienced substantial enhancements in personal magic power and spirit, enabling them to cast iron level spells.

Silver level magicians underwent a qualitative transformation in their spirit power, forming a stable spiritual space. Casting spells within this space allowed magic models to form rapidly and precisely. Magicians could even refine and modify these models or build multiple models simultaneously. At this level, various spellcasting techniques came into play.

Upon reaching gold level, magicians established stable conduits to the outside world, enabling constant magic power conversion without meditation. Of course, efficiency remained higher during meditation.

Unlike fighters, a magician's magic power possessed various hues depending on their magic affinity, as different elements absorbed distinct external energies. For instance, the water affinity manifested as a pale blue.

A fighter's battle qi color corresponds to their life level. bronze level appeared bronze. In fact, the naming of life levels was derived from the distinct hues of battle qi at each level.

Though the fishman youngster was only iron level, he possessed a spiritual space typically found in silver level magicians.

This puzzled the youngster. After pondering it himself, he formed a hypothesis.

He shared this observation with Zhong Yong, who looked at him excitedly: “Lord Hou Lang, it seems your bloodline is more suited to cultivating magic!”

"Only exceptional bloodlines grant cultivators certain special talents. Some talents are particularly outstanding, enabling them to surpass their inherent level!"

"Your spirit is such a case. A silver level spirit power is required to construct a spiritual space, yet yours underwent this qualitative transformation at the iron level."

“This is truly a great blessing!”

Zhong Yong's response confirmed the fishman youngster's conjecture.

“Just as I suspected.”

“It's like the legendary bloodline of the Flame Dragon King, granting me gold level defense at the silver level.”

“Applied to the fishman bloodline, this advantage shifts from defense to the spiritual realm.”

The fishman youngster pondered for a moment, finding Zhong Yong's assessment spot-on—this fishman bloodline offered far greater benefits in spell cultivation than in battle qi cultivation!

This only deepened the fishman youngster's curiosity about the true nature of this mysterious bloodline.

For the young commander, this question held considerable importance.

After all, the concentration of this fishman bloodline was relatively low, and he intended to increase its potency.

However, the fishman youngster found no answers in the War Merchant's database. He knew why: the bloodline's low concentration likely made its characteristics too subtle for identification.

The same reason prevented Cang Xu from assisting him.

With his newly elevated magician level, the fishman youngster resumed his spell training.

The routine remained unchanged.

First, Zhong Yong instructed him in water attribute spells. Then, in solitude, he practiced necromancy magic.

“I must hurry!”

“Before the next teleportation blood sacrifice, I must master the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell!”

As the fishman youngster pushed forward toward his goal, Cang Xu was also actively advancing his own plan.

He paid a visit to Xiao Wang Zi.

Chapter 706: Cang Xu’s Blackmail and Extortion

Ever since Cang Xu teleported back, Xiao Wang Zi had been avoiding him. Even during the undead gatherings, he refused to meet Cang Xu’s gaze.

But this time, Xiao Wang Zi could not avoid him anymore. Cang Xu was outside at the door.

“Won’t you invite me in for a moment?” Cang Xu smiled.

Xiao Wang Zi froze for a moment, his voice dry as he lowered his head, “Come in.”

His dwelling, like everyone else's, was a simple shack, easily erected.

"How much longer do you intend to avoid me? Xiao Wang Zi." There were no chairs inside the shack, so Cang Xu simply sat cross-legged.

Xiao Wang Zi remained silent, head bowed.

Cang Xu spoke then: "I don't blame you, and I understand your choice. When it comes to feelings and bonds, Ku Feng truly runs deeper for you than I do."

“But what I hate is our current state. I don't want this barrier between us.”

“Xiao Wang Zi, though our time together was brief, I've already decided you are my friend!”

Xiao Wang Zi's body trembled. He finally lifted his gaze to look at Cang Xu, still appearing dazed.

Cang Xu continued, “These past days have been truly painful for me.”

“I still remember when we first met... how we fought side by side, how perilous that battle on the Underworld River was... and how you lent me resources, helping me get back on my feet... And after I began training in the Flying Head Spell, our teamwork, do you remember that ambush on the magic beast fish school?"

Cang Xu's recollections caused Xiao Wang Zi to lower his head once more.

Inside the shack, only Cang Xu spoke.

Yet he seemed to have endless words, pausing not once, his tone gentle as he recounted their history.

To Xiao Wang Zi, it felt like reliving those past experiences.

“Let's make things right again,” Cang Xu finally said.

Xiao Wang Zi's tiny skeletal frame trembled slightly.

If Cang Xu blamed him, despised him, or hated him, it would have been easier to bear. But hearing those words made Xiao Wang Zi feel even more miserable and guilty.

He couldn't help but sob softly.

Though no tears fell.

“You treated me like a good friend,” Xiao Wang Zi said, filled with remorse, “But I treated you like this.”

“I'm so sorry!”

“I'm sorry. I'm truly, truly sorry.”

“Waaah...”

Cang Xu stood up, sat beside Xiao Wang Zi, and placed a hand on his small shoulder. His voice was gentle, “I told you; I don't blame you.”

Xiao Wang Zi looked up, his expression dazed. At that moment, he seemed to see his father's figure reflected in Cang Xu.

Xiao Wang Zi: "Xu Jia, you're so kind! I'll make it up to you. What do you want? Even... even if it's Generalissimo, I'll give it to you."

Generalissimo was the three-headed zombie sea serpent that had accompanied Xiao Wang Zi for so long, his most cherished mount.

Cang Xu smiled faintly, “I don't want Generalissimo. I want your heavy cavalry.”

“Okay!” Xiao Wang Zi answered without hesitation, “I can always recapture and recreate them anyway.”

The next moment, he frowned with difficulty: "But I've lost them now. Zhou Zhang has captured all my heavy cavalry. I haven't seen them for many days, though the soul connection remains."

“Ha! I was just teasing. I don't want your compensation, don't worry about it,” Cang Xu chuckled, patting Xiao Wang Zi's shoulder.

But Xiao Wang Zi grabbed Cang Xu's sleeve and wouldn't let go. Looking up with wide eyes, he insisted firmly, "No, I must compensate you. You absolutely must accept it. Only then will I feel better."

“You yourself are my best compensation...” Cang Xu's gaze grew distant as laughter welled up inside him.

Of course, he kept these thoughts to himself. After offering Xiao Wang Zi a few words of comfort, Cang Xu left the shack, now certain: Xiao Wang Zi stood firmly by his side.

This encounter marked a breakthrough in Cang Xu's relationships with Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi.

With these connections solidified, Cang Xu gained greater leverage over Jian Gui's small faction.

Should the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps ever need to use Jian Gui's channels to contact the undead forces led by You Shen, cultivating such influence would prove invaluable.

“Next up is Qi Ling.”

Cang Xu smiled confidently before arriving at Qi Ling's residence shortly after.

Qi Ling opened the door with a smile, inquiring about Cang Xu's purpose.

Cang Xu requested to discuss matters inside.

Qi Ling was reluctant, yet she still welcomed Cang Xu into her hut.

As they sat facing each other, she asked again, her face clearly guarded and distant.

Cang Xu chuckled, “Don't be so tense, Qi Ling. I'm only here to claim compensation.”

“Compensation? What do you mean?” Qi Ling frowned, then relaxed her expression.

Cang Xu explained, "It's about how you schemed against me, secretly instigating Xiao Wang Zi to help Ku Feng accelerate the progress of his memory fabrication. How long has it been? Have you, Magician Qi Ling, forgotten it all already?"

“Perhaps I should blame myself. I should have discussed compensation with you the very night I teleported back.”

On the surface, they exchanged prayer experiences; in secret, they traded private information.

Qi Ling smiled outwardly while seething inwardly: “Xu Jia, have you misunderstood something?”

“Didn't you do it?” Cang Xu pressed.

Qi Ling remained expressionless: “The misunderstanding I speak of is that you seem to think I, Qi Ling, am easy to bully.”

“That's right, I was targeting you specifically. Who else would have such low progress in memory fabrication, leaving themselves so exposed?”

“I want to survive, so I'll do whatever it takes to increase my chances.”

"Honestly, if it were Ku Feng or Xiao Wang Zi, I might hesitate. But you? I don't know you at all. You're a complete stranger! Is it strange that I'd target you under those circumstances?"

“Of course not,” Cang Xu replied, “I understand you, but that doesn't mean I can forgive you.”

“It seems you've misunderstood my purpose as well.”

“I came here demanding compensation, not to lecture you about reason or team camaraderie.”

“As you said, we're strangers to each other.”

“And even if we were to reason, we each have our own justifications. Using schemes and tricks to survive is perfectly normal.”

Cang Xu paused, staring intently into Qi Ling's eyes before continuing, “I'm here demanding compensation on account of Jian Gui, Xiao Wang Zi, and Ku Feng.”

“You targeted me, so I must retaliate.”

“But if I retaliate against you, it will ruin the grand scheme of forging false memories. Therefore, I hope you can see the bigger picture, compensate me, and quell the flames of my vengeance.”

Qi Ling chuckled softly. On the surface, she praised, “Magician Xu Jia, your experience as a magician is quite rich. It gives me great inspiration.”

Yet inwardly, she sneered coldly: “You seek vengeance?”

“How?”

“Don't forget, you still bear a double seal.”

“Come to think of it, that's rather peculiar, isn't it?”

"You've clearly converted, yet why do Zhou Zhang and the others still distrust you? Why haven't they touched the seals on your body?"

"You see, at the end of the day, you're a necromancer. How could normal people possibly trust you?"

“Even if your conversion succeeded, it would be the same!”

“You should realize by now, we're the ones who belong together.”

Qi Ling was cunning, preemptively using words to block Cang Xu's threatening rhetoric.

But this reaction was entirely within Cang Xu's expectations.

Cang Xu continued to stare into Qi Ling's eyes, his gaze sinister and icy. He silently communicated: “I must avenge myself on you. But why should I take action myself?”

“That would be so boring.”

“I never even considered laying a hand on you. After all, we're both magicians, we're on the same side.”

“Heh heh heh.”

At this, Cang Xu laughed.

Qi Ling's gaze shifted dramatically, growing fierce and furious. She growled under her breath, "Xu Jia, what do you intend to do? If you dare lay a finger on the Da Ji Clan, I will never let you off!"

Qi Ling's weakness was painfully obvious.

Lady Sea Snake could leverage the Da Ji Clan to demand Qi Ling serve her.

Such a weakness was something Cang Xu could certainly exploit!

Qi Ling's growl had already laid bare her fear.

Cang Xu promptly ended the conversation, rising to leave!

Just as he pushed open the hut door, Qi Ling called out to stop him: “Wait!”

Cang Xu returned with a smile, returning to his seat.

Qi Ling's gaze turned icy: "What do you want? Make it clear now, if you demand too much, negotiation is off! I won't satisfy your greed so easily!"

After another half hour of back-and-forth, Cang Xu and Qi Ling finally reached an agreement.

Cang Xu acquired a wealth of knowledge from Qi Ling.

Although Cang Xu had already obtained the intermediate soul aspect inheritance from Jian Gui, knowledge encompassed far more than just magician inheritance.

For instance, Qi Ling's assistance with Jian Gui's alchemy experiments, the content, data, and critical results.

Her unique expertise in the Soul Send Spell proved immensely valuable to Cang Xu.

Knowledge formed the bulk of the exchange, but there were also numerous undead supplies.

Of course, Qi Ling couldn't provide these supplies yet, bound as she was by dual seals. They had only reached a verbal agreement.

With a satisfied smile, Cang Xu crossed his arms behind his back and departed Qi Ling's quarters.

Watching his retreating figure, Qi Ling gritted her teeth, cursing silently: “Xu Jia, this humiliation, I will repay you in full.”

“You despicable, greedy goblin, daring to blackmail me!”

"Too bad our agreement is only verbal. Without a magic contract, what can you do to me once I regain my freedom?"

Beneath Qi Ling's fury lay a thread of smug satisfaction.

She reasoned: The dual seals bound her, yet indirectly aided her cause.

However, this notion shattered spectacularly the very next day!

At the undead gathering, Qi Ling's rage boiled over as she confronted Cang Xu: “We reached an agreement! Why did you attack the Da Ji Clan?!”

Chapter 707: No Need to Thank Me for Helping Root Out Your Nuisances

“We've already reached an agreement, why did you still attack the Da Ji Clan?!” At the undead gathering, Qi Ling's rage boiled over as she confronted Cang Xu.

At these words, Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi both shot Cang Xu astonished glances, while Jian Gui remained impassive.

Cang Xu immediately grasped the situation upon seeing their expressions.

“It seems that Magician Qi Ling had already informed Jian Gui before making this accusation.”

“Which means Jian Gui has become her protector.” Cang Xu understood that any misstep now would invite relentless questioning from the others. All his previous efforts would be in vain.

However, having already taken action, Cang Xu was prepared for this outcome.

Cang Xu did not rush to refute, instead adopting a cold demeanor: “Magician Qi Ling, please calm yourself.”

“I bear a double seal, weak and powerless, unable to even defeat a bronze level fighter. Where would I have obtained the ability to target the entire Da Ji Clan?”

“Moreover, why would I target them?”

Qi Ling sneered: "Before everyone here, before Lord Jian Gui, do you still intend to weave lies and deceive us?"

“This very morning, I received a plea for aid from the Da Ji Clan Leader.”

“He informed me it was you who approached Zhou Zhang, advising the Da Ji Clan to participate in the next teleportation blood sacrifice.”

“Dare you deny this?”

Cang Xu fell silent.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng remained quiet too.

Jian Gui fixed his gaze on Cang Xu: “Magician Xu Jia, explain yourself thoroughly.”

His tone was cold, his heart seething with anger.

He was fully absorbed in the escape plan involving forged memories and deeply reluctant to deal with any complications.

In truth, Qi Ling had already sought out Jian Gui earlier to report: Cang Xu spent his days aimlessly socializing, paying no attention whatsoever to the memory fabrication task.

Jian Gui endured all this.

In his view, Cang Xu had already undergone the teleportation blood sacrifice and returned successfully, he no longer possessed the strongest motivation to fabricate memories.

Cang Xu considered himself safe now; relaxing a little was understandable, excusable.

But this time, Jian Gui could no longer hold back.

Because Cang Xu's actions had severely angered and disrupted Qi Ling, crossing her absolute boundaries.

Qi Ling was furious!

In Jian Gui's eyes, Qi Ling was his best and most capable assistant.

Xiao Wang Zi was naive, and Ku Feng was a fool obsessed with the blade path.

Qi Ling possessed cunning intellect, had been personally summoned by Jian Gui, and had served as his alchemy assistant for years.

She was Jian Gui's staunchest ally, his most outstanding subordinate.

While Jian Gui admired Cang Xu, in terms of closeness, Cang Xu didn't even rank above Xiao Wang Zi or Ku Feng.

With Qi Ling being bullied by Cang Xu, Jian Gui naturally had to avenge her.

Cang Xu instantly understood Jian Gui's intentions from his tone.

He had long seen through Jian Gui, even bearing the marks of Jian Gui's trap upon his body. His very life hung on Jian Gui's whim.

Yet Cang Xu showed no fear, nodding slightly. "That's correct. That was indeed my suggestion to Lord Zhou Zhang. However, at that time, Lord Zhou Zhang merely said she would consider it. She did not agree."

Qi Ling gritted her teeth, her voice cracking with emotion, “Zhou Zhang... she has already issued the order!”

“Oh, that's wonderful! I'm truly delighted for you,” Cang Xu laughed heartily, “The entire Da Ji Clan undergoing this trial, those who survive will undoubtedly grow immensely powerful, becoming the model tribe among the Charming Blue believers. This is a tremendous blessing for the Da Ji Clan!”

“You're full of shit!” Qi Ling's eyes blazed with fury as she leapt to her feet. Had Xiao Wang Zi not startled and grabbed her, she would have lunged at Cang Xu to tear him apart.

Her rage was justified.

Participating in the teleportation blood sacrifice, while offering the chance to receive divine blessings, also carried the immense risk of being slain by the saint beast.

Only those with unwavering faith in their beliefs, those filled with reverence for the Charming Blue Goddess, and those harboring ambitions for their future within the Charming Blue Church would risk their lives to choose participation in the teleportation blood sacrifice.

No tribe would ever send all their members to participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice.

Because for most people, their level of faith simply wasn't that high. When an entire tribe underwent the ritual, few survived the ordeal.

After they returned, a tribe of thousands might be reduced to a mere dozen survivors, effectively ending the lineage. The remnants were more likely to be absorbed by rival tribes than to rebuild their numbers through gradual growth over generations.

Population size was crucial for any tribe.

Therefore, Cang Xu's proposal was tantamount to dooming the Da Ji Clan!

Qi Ling was stopped by Xiao Wang Zi and gradually calmed down. She immediately turned to complain to Jian Gui: “My Lord, I did not lie! It was Xu Jia, he admitted it himself! He is a traitor!”

“That scoundrel came to my door yesterday, blackmailing me.”

“I clearly agreed to his demands, yet he committed such a treacherous act the very next day.”

“He is our enemy!”

Jian Gui shot Qi Ling a cold glance.

Qi Ling's rage was understandable, but what angered Jian Gui was that she dared to use him as a tool for revenge against Cang Xu.

She had trampled upon the pride of Jian Gui as the superior.

However, Jian Gui did not lash out at Qi Ling. Compared to Qi Ling, he was far more displeased with Cang Xu.

Jian Gui addressed Cang Xu: “Xu Jia, you are an accomplished magician. You should understand the implications of your actions this time. You had better explain yourself. After all, Qi Ling is one of our own.”

“If you made an agreement with Qi Ling only to renege the very next day, that only deepens our concerns.”

“You must understand that integrity is the world's most intangible yet indispensable treasure!”

Cang Xu paused, then spoke with utmost sincerity: "Lord Jian Gui, I admire and respect you. Over the past period, I've been honored to follow you, receive your guidance, and grow immensely under your command. My gratitude toward you is profound and heartfelt. I often find myself wishing I had met you sooner."

“That's why I've always envied Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, and Ku Feng, they encountered you earlier. To me, that's undoubtedly a stroke of good fortune.”

These well-chosen words instantly softened Jian Gui's gaze.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng nodded in agreement.

Cang Xu looked at the two of them and continued, “I've learned a great deal from both Ku Feng and Xiao Wang Zi.”

“But with Magician Qi Ling... perhaps because we've spent so little time together, I feel a significant gap. Take what she said to me yesterday, for instance, we're not exactly close.”

“In fact, not only are we not close, but there's some grudge between us.”

“My first participation in the teleportation blood sacrifice was entirely thanks to her.”

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng remained silent.

Cang Xu spoke the truth.

His previous efforts in building relationships had led to breakthrough progress in his ties with the two of them. This confrontation hadn't pushed them to his opposing side.

Thus, up to this point, both maintained a neutral stance.

This was the fruit of Cang Xu's prior efforts.

Jian Gui remained silent. The outcome of the first teleportation blood sacrifice was, in truth, largely tied to him as well. He had turned a blind eye to Qi Ling's underhanded maneuvers. To some extent, Cang Xu's selection as the first victim was also his responsibility.

Cang Xu continued, “Once Qi Ling agreed to my demands, I had no intention of seeking revenge against her.”

“My target isn't her, it's the Da Ji Clan!”

"Why? They're not your enemies at all! They're all innocent!” Qi Ling protested, utterly baffled.

“Perhaps they were before. But when we rescued you only to end up in this mess, the Da Ji Clan became my mortal enemy,” Cang Xu replied coldly.

"We once lived freely. Even navigating the Underworld River was safer than this. We thrived peacefully in the White Hair Trench. My Flying Head Spell flourished, my future wide open. We came here solely to save you."

"I have no objection to that. I even admire Lord Jian Gui's character. Put yourself in my shoes, if I were in your position, held hostage by enemies, I'd want my comrades to rescue me this way too."

“But reality is far more complex.”

“Think carefully. You, Magician Qi Ling, specialize in the soul aspect of necromancy. You are a ghost yourself. Why were you trapped and so easily blackmailed?”

“Wasn't it all for the Da Ji Clan?”

"Rescuing you would be simple. But why did we have to lie low, contact you cautiously, and wait so painstakingly for the right moment? Wasn't it precisely because we had to consider the Da Ji Clan while saving you?"

"Without the tribe, you could have escaped easily on your own. Had we rescued you without reservations, we would have succeeded swiftly. We wouldn't have ended up in this predicament!"

"The Da Ji Clan dragged you down and dragged us down too. They made all of us captives. Isn't this tribe my enemy? Isn't it the enemy of us all?"

Cang Xu pressed relentlessly.

Qi Ling stared in disbelief.

Jian Gui cast a look of astonishment.

For the first time, Cang Xu displayed such astonishing eloquence.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng remained silent, but both agreed.

Cang Xu continued, “As far as I know, you've been dragged down by the Da Ji Clan countless times before.”

“Even Lord Jian Gui has intervened multiple times to save the day for you, hasn't he?”

“You've burdened them in the past, and now you're burdening us. How long do you intend to let this damned fishman tribe keep dragging us down?”

Qi Ling fell silent, taking a step back. Her expression turned panicked as she glanced around.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng remained quiet, yet their looks clearly showed agreement.

Qi Ling turned to Jian Gui, as if grasping at a last lifeline.

Jian Gui remained expressionless as he spoke soothingly to Qi Ling.

“I summoned you into existence. You are my companion. My aid stems from my own free will.”

Qi Ling burst into tears, collapsing beside Jian Gui. Clutching the thigh of his cross-legged form, she clung to it with the fragile vulnerability of a woman bullied by a tyrant.

Chapter 708: Prey and Hunter

Jian Gui reached out, gently stroking Qi Ling's head.

The moment held no awkwardness, for both were essentially ghosts capable of physical contact.

Glancing around, Jian Gui spoke: “Come to think of it, this whole situation is actually my fault...”

Then, he unexpectedly admitted his error, shouldering the responsibility.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng immediately responded. One tried to exonerate Jian Gui, while the other claimed he too was at fault.

Cang Xu's influence had already surpassed that of Qi Ling, but compared to Jian Gui, it was like comparing a minor sorcerer to a grand wizard.

With a single exertion from Jian Gui, all of Cang Xu's previous efforts shattered instantly.

However, this outcome was entirely within Cang Xu's expectations. In truth, he never intended to defy Jian Gui.

“Lord Jian Gui, I am profoundly grateful and in awe of you, yet I also feel deeply that this is not what you deserve.”

“You are such a prodigy, possessing a gold level bloodline and being the direct heir to the true undead orthodoxy. Your future was so bright, yet now you find yourself in such peril, all because of the Da Ji Clan!” Cang Xu continued his plea.

Jian Gui seethed inwardly.

He now fully understood that Cang Xu was a stubborn adversary, driven by a fierce desire for retribution. Cang Xu's flattery served only his own ends. Yet, ironically, Jian Gui could not punish Cang Xu now.

Though he had planted a hidden mechanism within Cang Xu, capable of killing him with a single thought, in this situation, taking Cang Xu's life would be going too far.

Jian Gui sighed inwardly.

He understood Cang Xu. If he had been betrayed, he would seek revenge too. Qi Ling's extortion was somewhat understandable.

Honestly, Jian Gui had long been mentally prepared for this. If Cang Xu hadn't reacted this way, he would have been more concerned about Cang Xu's hidden agenda.

Thus, when Qi Ling came to him weeping and reporting the incident, Jian Gui felt a quiet relief deep within.

As for the Da Ji Clan, Jian Gui naturally felt no affection for them.

More than anything, he merely used them as a means to restrain Qi Ling.

In a way, Jian Gui and Lady Sea Snake were no different.

Cang Xu was the same, all of them exploited the Da Ji Clan to deal with Qi Ling.

The effect was remarkably effective!

After all, Qi Ling possessed such an obvious flaw, one she had even exposed herself.

Cang Xu seized this weakness. With just a little pressure, he struck Qi Ling hard enough to hurt, forcing her to act according to his plan.

Cang Xu concluded: "Once I recognized this reality, I decided to send them into the Ocean Mother Nest. I refuse to let such events repeat. If blame must be assigned, direct it solely at me!"

Xiao Wang Zi couldn't help but look up at Cang Xu.

Even Ku Feng secretly admired Cang Xu's decisiveness.

“Spat! You clearly failed to extort me, so now you threaten me with the Da Ji Clan to make me bleed more for you...” Qi Ling blurted out once more.

Cang Xu cut her off with a cold snort, "I demanded compensation because you targeted me earlier, making me the first to participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice.
