I was fortunate, the Lord took pity and spared my life, teleporting me back. Demanding compensation from you, the perpetrator, instead of taking action, is already a show of restraint on my part."

“My demand for compensation is sincere!”

"Otherwise, wouldn't it have been far simpler and quicker to threaten you with the Da Ji Clan during the compensation process? Why escalate things to this point now?"

“The night I returned from the transmission, I thought long and hard.”

“I made a firm decision: I will eradicate the Da Ji Clan!”

"Even if you hate me, it doesn't matter. I will eliminate this weakness for you."

“If you dare, if you dare do this, you declare war on me! We'll fight to the death!” Qi Ling raged, cursing furiously.

Cang Xu remained expressionless.

Xiao Wang Zi tried to intervene, but after opening his mouth, he decided against it. Truthfully, he disliked both Qi Ling and the Da Ji Clan. Many of Cang Xu's words struck a chord with him.

Truth be told, Xiao Wang Zi had opposed rescuing Qi Ling from the start.

A child's emotions were just this simple!

Ku Feng furrowed his brow slightly, his gaze fixed on Jian Gui.

Only Jian Gui could resolve the current conflict, and he remained confident Jian Gui could handle such troubles.

But just as Jian Gui was about to speak, laughter suddenly echoed, followed by a figure materializing within the shack.

The group was startled.

Yet upon recognizing the face, their surprise faded.

It was Zhou Zhang.

This saint level Charming Blue Archbishop was no stranger to eavesdropping and spying.

“Interesting. Truly fascinating,” Zhou Zhang murmured with a satisfied smile.

Her gaze swept the room, radiating the predatory confidence of a cat toying with mice.

Qi Ling snapped to attention, kneeling before Zhou Zhang and pleading for mercy for the Da Ji Clan.

Zhou Zhang smiled, gazing down at Qi Ling with gentle demeanor as she uttered words that plunged Qi Ling into despair: "I have given Xu Jia's suggestion serious consideration. Indeed, the Da Ji Clan maintains close ties with necromancers. Their composition is complex, and there exists the possibility of feigned faith, exploiting the Charming Blue Church to counter formidable foes."

"They claim that most of their tribe are Charming Blue believers; verifying this would do no harm."

Qi Ling hurriedly interjected, “Lady Zhou Zhang, they are all fishmen! Like you, they are creatures of the sea.”

“So what?” Zhou Zhang's expression grew cold. "Not all fishmen are believers of my Lord. Is that not so? If my perspective remained confined to race, then all believers here should be octopusmen."

Zhou Zhang's reply plunged Qi Ling into an icy abyss.

Zhou Zhang continued to toy with Qi Ling: "If you wish the Da Ji Clan to survive, why not attend the teleportation blood sacrifice in their place? After all, isn't it because of you that they're under suspicion?"

“Had you not been coveted by Lady Sea Snake, the Da Ji Clan wouldn't have suffered this calamity, nor fallen into their current predicament, would they?”

Qi Ling was struck by a crushing blow, collapsing onto the ground.

Zhou Zhang continued, leisurely: “You truly should participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice as soon as possible.”

"Both you and Xu Jia are necromancers, but he has already become a believer of my Lord, receiving divine blessing. You have not. Who do you think I would trust more?"

Qi Ling's face paled, and she was speechless.

When her very survival was at stake, Qi Ling dared not take any risks. The memory fabrication plan had given her new hope, but before that, she had no certainty about converting.

Protecting the Da Ji Clan was Qi Ling's obsession since becoming a ghost.

But if she couldn't even survive herself, what was the point of any obsession?

Jian Gui and the others remained silent.

As silence hung heavy in the air, Zhou Zhang turned her gaze back to Cang Xu, a knowing smile once more playing on her lips.

“Xu Jia, you too have been reported.”

“Many of the believers feel you abused your position this time, attempting to harness the divine authority of our Lord to eliminate your own enemies.”

Cang Xu scrambled to explain.

After patiently listening, Zhou Zhang's expression grew more mocking, “Your reasoning holds merit.”

"But now, a significant number doubt you. They claim that even if you received divine blessing, it might have been a temporary change triggered by the immense pressure of facing death. Once back in safety, your faith has waned."

"This isn't impossible either. After all, faith stems from the heart and can shift. The crucial point is, it cannot be accurately measured."

“Lord Zhou Zhang, please hear me out...” Cang Xu's tone and expression betrayed utter panic.

The saint level octopusman raised a tentacle, cutting him off: “Therefore, for the next ritual, you shall participate alongside the Da Ji Clan.”

Cang Xu's expression instantly “changed dramatically”.

The necromancers and magicians stared blankly, shock evident on their faces.

Qi Ling, stunned, opened her mouth to mock Cang Xu, but the thought of the Da Ji Clan instantly drained her of any desire to speak.

Jian Gui's heart grew cold, his expression grim.

Zhou Zhang laughed brightly: “Of course, besides Xu Jia, you must select one more person. Rest assured, there's plenty of time. Take your time choosing."

With that, she successfully observed the darkening expressions of Jian Gui and the others before fading away with a smile.

Toying with these captives brought her immense amusement, one of the few pastimes she enjoyed during her duties as the archbishop.

This was a world of the strong, where might ruled all. Playing with the weak was nothing out of the ordinary.

In a foul mood, Jian Gui warned Cang Xu and Qi Ling not to cause any more trouble, now was no time for infighting.

Qi Ling naturally obeyed Jian Gui's command.

Jian Gui then quietly instructed Cang Xu: "You should see it now. Even if you convert successfully, you won't gain Zhou Zhang's trust. If she can let you participate once or twice, she can keep doing it."

Cang Xu wore a heavy expression, solemnly agreeing.

As the undead gathering dispersed, Xiao Wang Zi cautiously comforted Cang Xu.

Ku Feng remarked, "It seems we must journey together this time. Let's strive together!"

Cang Xu returned to his shack and finally relaxed, inwardly rejoicing: “My goal is achieved!”

How to enter the Ocean Mother Nest a second time had always been a problem.

Few believers ever ventured back into the Ocean Mother Nest. On one hand, most believers didn't want to risk their lives again. On the other hand, even if there were fanatical believers, Zhou Zhang wouldn't grant them the opportunity.

The reason was simple: spots were limited.

From Archbishop Zhou Zhang's perspective, she needed to seize this opportunity to recruit believers for the goddess, strengthen the convictions and personal abilities of its core adherents, and help the Charming Blue Church deepen its foundations.

Having a fanatical believer go in and out frequently offered poor value for effort. Unless exceptional circumstances arose, such as a fanatical believer possessing exceptional aptitude and extraordinary talent.

Cang Xu had maintained contact with the fishman youngster. In their most recent exchange, he was delighted to learn the youngster could now cast the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell.

Given this development, Cang Xu had to return promptly.

This challenge did not faze Cang Xu.

After pondering for a moment, he devised this solution.

He succeeded.

Leveraging the conflict between him and Qi Ling, and exploiting the distrust that Zhou Zhang and others harbored toward necromancers, he deceived Jian Gui and others, capitalizing on Zhou Zhang's competitive mindset.

Everything unfolded naturally, like water flowing downhill, without the slightest hint of coercion.

The most skilled hunters often appeared as prey.

This was Cang Xu's precise grasp of human nature and individuality, the result of his adept execution of cunning schemes.

No other survivor could have achieved this level of accomplishment.

Only Cang Xu.

He understood the art of politics deeply.

Of course, he hadn't been entirely certain of success before acting. But the presence of the fishman youngster and the Deep Sea Monster Fish gave him added confidence.

If this attempt failed, there would be another.

If the next scheme fell through, there would be another.

Success on the first try would be ideal, of course!

“Now, we just await the teleportation blood sacrifice.” Unlike Ku Feng and the others, Cang Xu was filled with anticipation.

After all, this return marked the first step in formally altering his innate potential.

Chapter 709: Cang Xu’s Bloodline

White Hair Trench.

Radiance flooded the entire undersea valley, illuminating everyone’s features with stark clarity.

Jian Gui and the others wore solemn expressions, their gazes fixed upon Cang Xu and Ku Feng.

Cang Xu and Ku Feng knelt on the ground, surrounded by a sea of believers.

The fishmen of the Da Ji Clan remained silent, yet the glances they cast toward Cang Xu burned with seething hatred!

If looks could kill, Cang Xu would have been slain a hundred times over by the Da Ji Clan’s fishmen.

After all, he was the enemy responsible for the annihilation of their entire clan.

This was a clan-annihilating grudge!

“Once we reach the Mother Nest, even if the saint beast kills me, I'll kill you first!” Many fishmen of the Da Ji Clan harbored this thought.

Many had even conspired beforehand to join forces and eliminate Cang Xu!

Ku Feng, kneeling beside Cang Xu, gave a bitter smile, “Looks like we'll face a tough battle first thing when we reach the Ocean Mother Nest.”

Cang Xu reassured him: "Rest easy. Compared to the battle at the Underworld River, this won't be nearly as perilous. Most fishmen haven't experienced teleportation before. We'll react faster than they do. If we move in on the saint beast proactively, we still have a chance."

After saying this, Cang Xu glanced toward Qing Xin.

Standing at a distance, Qing Xin sensed the fishmen's hostility toward Cang Xu. She had no interest whatsoever in drawing closer to him.

Cang Xu's gaze flickered slightly before shifting away.

Approaching Qing Xin now would only expose their connection, drawing calamity upon her.

As before, the magic formation hummed with activity, divine scripts and magic runes intertwining.

Hordes of sea beasts were absorbed by the golden altar, their flesh and blood vanishing as bones fell to the ground, strengthening the altar's structure.

Spatial ripples surged, transporting all the surrounding believers into the Ocean Mother Nest.

“We're in!” The fishmen of the Da Ji Clan snapped to attention, scanning their surroundings.

“Found Xu Jia! Charge together!”

“He's right there, don't run!”

“Trying to escape to the saint beast? That's suicide.”

“You harmed our Da Ji Clan! We'll tear you apart limb by limb!”

The Da Ji Clan's fishmen tore “Cang Xu” to shreds, devouring him piece by piece, their hatred for him palpable.

Yet they didn't know this was all an illusion.

What represented Cang Xu was merely an alchemy ingredient aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the corpse of a giant sea creature, but enchanted with the Deceptive Disguise spell.

The real Cang Xu had been swallowed whole by the Rubber Swallower and carried into the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Ku Feng and Qing Xin suffered the same fate.

Both silver levels were subjected to double seals, allowing the Rubber Swallower to devour them whole with effortless ease.

Cang Xu raced against time. Emerging from the Rubber Swallower's belly, he scrambled through the corridor, rushing into the meditation chamber.

The metal door slid open. The fishman youngster had already completed his hybrid transformation.

Before Cang Xu stood the fishman youngster, his chest and back twisted with grotesque patterns forming two distinct ghostly faces.

It was through this Ghostface Fish bloodline that the youngster possessed the qualification to refine and wield undead magic power.

“Commander!” Cang Xu bowed, his expression tinged with excitement and anticipation.

The fishman youngster nodded slightly. Once Cang Xu entered the meditation chamber and the metal door closed behind him, the youngster pointed a finger at him.

Necromancy - Undead Bloodline Detection Spell!

The spell drained his magic power reserves voraciously, nearly depleting the iron level magic pool.

The Undead Bloodline Detection Spell was silver level, consuming magic power at the standard rate for such spells.

The fishman youngster could cast it independently solely due to his bloodline talent, granting him silver level magic power reserves even at iron level.

Even so, his spellcasting success rate hovered around 30%.

Numerous factors influenced spell release – magic power was only one aspect, alongside magic model construction and more.

This time, perhaps due to his prolonged preparation, the success rate had slightly improved. Thus, his first attempt succeeded.

A streak of gray light erupted from the fishman youngster's fingertips.

The light was not dazzling, traveling in a straight line with laser-like speed as it struck Cang Xu's body.

His body trembled violently as his temperature soared, as if plunged into boiling water.

Soon, wisps of gray mist began to rise from his skin.

The mist swirled and thickened, eventually coalescing into a massive sphere that completely enveloped Cang Xu. A potent undead aura radiated outward in all directions.

The fishman youngster undid the Bloodcore's hybrid transformation and began waiting patiently.

Within the thick gray mist, Cang Xu was the first to sense a change.

His toes began to itch - or more accurately, his bones itched.

The itching grew worse!

Within mere seconds, the sensation intensified to the point where Cang Xu's expression changed. Knowing Cang Xu was no ordinary being, as an undead entity, it was rare for him to experience such intense sensations of life.

Cang Xu couldn't help but fidget his toes. Just as he bent down to scratch them, excruciating pain erupted.

This agony didn't replace the previous itch; it felt as though his ten itchy toes had pierced through his flesh and skin, burrowing straight out!

Pain and itching, both at once!

Cang Xu's body shook violently, nearly toppling him over.

At that very moment, the fishman youngster outside the gray mist saw it, a magic image forming on the mist's surface.

A bone claw gradually materialized, growing larger and larger.

The claw slammed into the ground. The razor-sharp tips of its claws pierced the earth's surface, then relentlessly extended and spread deeper into the ground.

“It's the Twisted Withered Claw bloodline!” the fishman youngster recognized it instantly.

Ever since he had received the intermediate soul aspect inheritance from Cang Xu, he possessed extensive knowledge about undead lifeforms.

The fishman youngster's expression remained impassive, showing no excitement.

Because the Twisted Withered Claw bloodline was merely bronze level.

“Cang Xu must possess stronger bloodlines!”

The fishman youngster was utterly certain.

It was self-evident.

Cang Xu had already reached the iron level. If he possessed only the Twisted Withered Claw bloodline, he could never transcend bronze to reach iron level.

On the surface of the gray mist, the sprawling white bone claws suddenly shattered.

The gray mist shifted ceaselessly, beginning its transformation anew.

On Cang Xu's body, the tingling in his toes vanished, and the pain in his feet subsided. Instead, agony surged through his neck.

This wasn't the dull ache of overworked muscles, but a wrenching pain.

It felt as if someone were violently twisting his head, forcing it to spin relentlessly.

In this process, his neck, the link between head and torso, twisted like a rope being violently coiled into a spiral.

Not only twisted, but his head was also being yanked upward, causing his neck to stretch relentlessly.

Cang Xu felt like a toy fallen into the hands of a giant child. The brutal giant child was preparing to yank his head clean off his body.

He instinctively clutched his own head and pressed down hard, trying to counter the pulling force.

This was entirely his illusion!

Yet the excruciating pain was all too real, making Cang Xu gasp sharply.

At the same moment, a magic vision manifested once more upon the surface of the gray mist.

It was a wailing humanoid ghost. The most striking feature of the ghost was its head, severed from its body. The neck connecting head and torso was thin and elongated, resembling a twisted, spiraled rope.

“Hanged Ghost bloodline... This bloodline is excellent; it is gold level.” The fishman youngster's eyes glowed faintly.

But in the next instant, the magic image shifted again!

The Hanged Ghost, which had swayed like willow branches in the wind, gradually froze rigid, as if struck by a petrifying spell.

Moreover, the Hanged Ghost was steadily shrinking.

Around it appeared one, two, three... a dozen... dozens... hundreds of corpses.

The fishman youngster scrutinized them, recognizing the remains belonged to various races.

He saw fishman, beastman, elves, dwarves, gnomes, and even numerous beasts, dragons, and phoenixes.

All these corpses shared one common feature: their necks had been stretched thin and elongated.

Their heads were clustered together, joined by interlocking teeth, imperfectly fused, and assembled into a single structure. All the heads formed the trunk of a giant tree.

Their necks served as flexible branches, extending outward in all directions.

Their bodies hung like ripe wheat stalks in autumn, heavy and drooping, bending the slender necks beneath their weight.

The fishman youngster gasped at this magic spectacle.

He couldn't help but utter a low cry: “Corpse-Hanging Tree... a saint level undead bloodline!”

Cang Xu's body actually contained a saint level undead bloodline.

The fishman youngster felt genuine joy for Cang Xu from the bottom of his heart!

Ten minutes later.

Cang Xu slumped in a heap, his expression listless and weary, looking utterly bedraggled.

“I never imagined the subject would suffer so terribly after being subjected to the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell,” the old magician sighed.

The agony was real, Cang Xu's body and soul had sustained severe wounds.

Yet the sensation of his neck being stretched during this ordeal was merely an illusion caused by the injuries and the awakening of his bloodline.

When the magic image of the Corpse-Hanging Tree appeared on the surface of the gray mist, his mind momentarily fractured, even inducing hallucinations.

He felt as though he had become one of the corpses hanging from that tree!

"The Undead Bloodline Detection Spell aims to uncover the hidden secrets within the target's bloodline. That's why it's so ‘brutal’, inflicting injury on the subject. Only such force can achieve the desired effect," the fishman youngster explained.

Lately, he had even abandoned his cultivation of battle qi, focusing solely on spellcasting.

He constantly pondered the intermediate soul aspect inheritance brought back by Cang Xu, while continuously practicing the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell in real combat. These efforts had accumulated profound insights within him.

Though Cang Xu felt immense discomfort, his heart was filled with joy.

“It seems my internal condition is quite complex; three bloodlines are actually intertwined within me.”

The state of his bloodlines was indeed as he described.

Some individuals possessed no transcendent bloodlines at all.

Others carried a single transcendent bloodline, but it was extremely faint.

Still others bore multiple transcendent bloodlines, varying in number and forming a relatively stable state, much like Cang Xu's case.

Coincidentally, Cang Xu possessed three undead bloodlines, each corresponding precisely to the three major schools within undead cultivation.

The Twisted Withered Claw bloodline aligned with the bone aspect.

The Hanged Ghost bloodline aligned with the soul aspect.

The Corpse-Hanging Tree bloodline aligned with the corpse aspect.

All three bloodlines were extremely diluted, collectively amounting to less than 10%.

The Corpse-Hanging Tree bloodline was the weakest, estimated by the fishman youngster to be under 1%. Though it possessed the highest quality, its scale was too small to significantly influence Cang Xu.

The Twisted Withered Claw bloodline had the highest concentration, yet it remained insufficient to overcome the quality gap. Thus, it remains suppressed by the Hanged Ghost bloodline. After all, the Twisted Withered Claw bloodline itself was only bronze level.

The Hanged Ghost bloodline's concentration fell between the other two, and its level likewise ranked midway. Yet overall, it exerted the greatest influence on Cang Xu.

Thus, when manifested in Cang Xu's cultivation, his progress in the soul aspect proved most effective among the three.

The truth of bloodlines was this complex.

It could not be determined solely by a transcendent's cultivation performance.

“I have a feeling the Corpse-Hanging Tree bloodline was brought about by my bloodline mutation this time,” Cang Xu said, barely clinging to life.

The fishman youngster shook his head: "That theory has no basis whatsoever, nor is there any point in pursuing it. But without a doubt, the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline is the most suitable for refinement. That shall be our next objective."

"Truthfully, we've been fortunate this time. Had it been another undead bloodline, collection might prove far more difficult. The Hanging Corpse Tree stands as a singular anomaly among undead bloodlines."

"Within the bloodline spectrum, its upstream undead sources are scarce, while its downstream manifestations are as numerous as night stars. We have numerous targets to choose from, capable of extracting the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline from many undead beings."

Cang Xu added, “But this also means that collecting 100% of the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline is more difficult than with other undead bloodlines.”

“Correct,” the fishman youngster nodded slightly. "But realistically, achieving 100% purity is exceedingly difficult. This holds especially true for high level bloodlines."

"Lower level bloodlines can easily reach 100% purity, but what use is that to you? The value is negligible!"

On this matter, the fishman youngster spoke with authority.

His Flame Dragon King bloodline had long hovered above 30%. His current fishman bloodline concentration was even lower than that of the Flame Dragon King bloodline.

The higher the level of the bloodline, the harder it was to increase its concentration.

Unless the fishman youngster directly slew a Flame Dragon King, his bloodline concentration would skyrocket.

But if he possessed such combat prowess, would he even need to deliberately boost his Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration?

Under normal circumstances, they would select targets from the lower tiers of the bloodline spectrum, accumulating small gains to gradually build high-concentration, high level bloodlines.

Chapter 710: 3%

Standing within the Bloodline Purification Formation, Cang Xu felt a wave of emotion wash over him.

He remembered clearly how he had poured his heart and soul into constructing this formation, exhausting every possible method only to face insurmountable obstacles.

After leaving the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and temporarily joining Jian Gui's crew alone, the other survivors had managed to complete the Bloodline Purification Formation.

"The Commander never disappoints us. Miss Zi Di is also incredibly dependable!" Cang Xu thought to himself.

He stood in the center of the formation.

The fishman youngster stood at the periphery, channeling his Bloodcore with mental focus to generate a crimson energy stream.

This energy flowed into the formation's panels, causing the entire alchemy formation to erupt with a blood-red radiance. It coalesced into a three-dimensional magic formation centered around a seven-pointed star.

The formation was compact, occupying only a quarter of the alchemy chamber's space.

Cang Xu was bathed entirely in the crimson glow. The faint tension on his face gradually faded as time passed.

Finally, he closed his eyes slightly, quietly sensing the changes within his bloodline.

He felt nothing.

When the crimson energy depleted and Cang Xu stepped out of the formation, he offered only one assessment: “This was far more bearable than enduring the Undead Bloodline Detection Spell.”

The fishman youngster explained, "After this purification, your Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline concentration is now 3%. At this level of saint level bloodline concentration, it hasn't reached a stage where you can immediately perceive it."

“You'll only feel it distinctly during your next meditation.”

Cang Xu's Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline had been less than 1% before; reaching 3% now was a tremendous leap forward.

“You should be able to cultivate to the silver level now,” the fishman youngster stated.

Even a slight increase in a saint level bloodline could bring powerful changes to a low level transcendent.

Cang Xu struggled to contain his excitement.

His lack of talent had been a lifelong torment, was now, at last, improving!

As an iron level, he had been blending in with Jian Gui's crew, constantly relying on the Deceptive Disguise spell to pass as a silver level magician. The psychological pressure had been immense!

“The problem now is that we've exhausted the undead materials we had available for absorption,” the fishman youngster explained.

Since leaving Mystifying Monster Island, the survivors had diligently gathered undead materials in secret.

Only half of their painstakingly collected resources remained.

It wasn't that the fishman youngster was reluctant to use these materials—they simply couldn't be utilized.

The Hanging Corpse Tree's bloodline reached the saint level. While not occupying the absolute apex of the bloodline hierarchy, it undoubtedly belonged to its upper echelons.

The most ideal targets for bloodline absorption were the subordinate lifeforms one tier below the Hanging Corpse Tree; those connected to its bloodline. These were primarily gold level entities.

The next tier down, silver level, was still considered ideal.

The lowest tier was iron level transcendents. Absorption from these yielded very poor results.

Most undead materials collected by the survivors were low level; gold level materials were exceedingly rare.

“We desperately need suitable undead materials now!”

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu exchanged glances, both falling silent.

Acquiring these undead materials was no easy feat.

The current state of the undead faction was one where everyone wanted to hunt them down.

Even if undead materials were produced, they were strictly controlled by local factions. What trickled into the market were often borderline materials.

The world's largest source of undead materials was undoubtedly the Ghost World.

In this alternate plane, undead roamed everywhere.

But Cang Xu, who had ventured into the Ghost World, made it clear to the fishman youngster: only those at the saint level possessed the qualifications to explore the Ghost World and withstand its dangers. Beyond that, even the combination of Jian Gui and his ghost ship struggled through it with great difficulty and constant peril!

Though Jian Gui had the ghost ship, he dared not covet the Ghost World under normal circumstances. Only when driven to desperation did they risk teleporting into the Ghost World to seek refuge.

This alone demonstrated the advantage held by the orthodox undead faction.

The leader of the orthodox undead faction was Founder You Shen, a legendary figure. His personally trained disciples, known as the Twelve Saints, include several at the saint level!

With such powerful figures exploring the Ghost World and bringing back vast undead resources, they could sustain the orthodox necromancers like Jian Gui.

However, other necromancers weren't without their own means of acquiring undead materials.

For rogue magicians and wandering practitioners, their primary method was self-creation.

After a lifeform died, its flesh could be fashioned into zombies, its bones into skeleton warriors, and its spirit transformed into ghosts.

Previously, the surge in undead forces commanded by Jian Gui and others had been obtained through extensive hunting in the White Hair Trench.

"The Ghost World is beyond our reach for now. Beyond market purchases, most necromancers rely on their own hands to provide for themselves." Cang Xu shot a meaningful glance at the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster let out a sigh of realization. "No wonder the entire world targets the undead faction. Within their ranks, all living things can be turned into undead materials. In a sense, the whole world is brimming with undead resources!"

Cang Xu shook his head slightly. "Transforming into undead requires negative energy. Higher level undead often demand corresponding necromancy magic." Only advanced necromancers can cast such spells."

"Actually, other magic schools are no different. Take pyromancy, for instance. For a long time in history, countless fire magicians hunted fire dragons across the land, seeking their scales, eyes, and dragon roots to craft superior spellcasting materials."

The fishman youngster nodded in agreement, “Cang Xu, you're right. The fundamental reason the undead school faces persecution and suppression lies in its involvement with the mysteries of the soul!”

The fishman youngster was himself a bona fide necromancer.

The undead inheritance he had access to included some of the intermediate level content. The resources he commanded and the knowledge he possessed far surpassed those of most necromancers in the world.

At this point, the fishman youngster suddenly understood the deeper meaning behind Cang Xu's earlier words.

He stared at Cang Xu, expressionless: “Cang Xu, are you planning to target these sea beasts and the believers of the Charming Blue Goddess? To kill them all and turn them into undead materials?”

Cang Xu immediately sensed the fishman youngster's displeasure and hastily lowered his head, “Lord, you mentioned this yourself. I never suggested it.”

Cang Xu understood his commander well.

After following Jian Gui for some time, he had come to grasp Jian Gui's true nature.

And having spent even longer alongside the fishman youngster, he naturally understood the latter's disposition even more deeply.

It was precisely because of this that he chose to follow the fishman youngster, regarding the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as his true allegiance. It was also why, when the fishman youngster had rescued him earlier, he felt a deep sense of guilt and a desire to repay the debt.

The more talented a person was, the harder it was to recruit and make them truly loyal. Even if they served under someone, winning their hearts remained difficult.

The fishman youngster's ability to unite Zi Di and Cang Xu stemmed from more than just the core interest of the Bloodcore.

His many shining qualities in human nature earned the admiration of magicians and schemers like Cang Xu, who chose to trust the fishman youngster, obey his commands, and follow him.

In truth, Cang Xu was an exceptionally talented individual!

His limited personal achievements stemmed entirely from the constraints of his bloodline, which capped his life level ceiling far too low.

This was a world of the strong, without transcendent ranks of sufficiently high level, one commanded no respect. Even amassing staggering wealth could be easily seized; the Wisteria Merchant Alliance stood as the prime example.

Within the current Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Tripleblade clearly possessed the talent to lead a mercenary band and the richest experience. Yet, constrained by his own limitations, merely an iron level cultivation, he couldn't step directly into the limelight. He could only arrange personnel and manage affairs behind the scenes.

In Snow Bird Harbor.

He stared intently at the financial statements in his hands; his brow furrowed for a long moment before he finally set them aside.

Leaning back in his chair, he let out a deep sigh.

Ever since the last shipment of potions was intercepted by a gold level force, their distribution channels have suffered greatly, shrinking significantly.

Bu Quan truly lived up to her reputation as an alchemy genius. Even though potions weren't her strongest suit, she had flawlessly completed the task Tripleblade assigned her, boosting potion production by another significant margin.

But the transportation problem remained unsolved.

Using alternative delivery methods was far too costly. The most economical solution was caravan transport.

Tripleblade wrote to Zong Ge and Zi Di explaining the situation and suggesting halting training due to the tight timeline. He hadn't received a reply yet.

Nevertheless, Tripleblade had already ordered Men Shi and Chi Lai to remain stationed at the docks. Even if training continued, it would be conducted at leased facilities within Snow Bird Harbor.

Simultaneously, Tripleblade contacted the Mercenary Guild, securing numerous assignments to distribute among his mercenary group.

His seasoned experience allowed him to select suitable missions for the right individuals.

Consequently, their mission completion rate far exceeded that of typical mercenary companies.

Yet the rewards from these tasks fell far short of covering the Dragon Lion Mercenary Company's expenses.

“Sigh, I hope Chi Lai's visit to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord yields results!” Tripleblade mused to himself as he looked towards the city government palace.

Inside the Snow Bird Harbor city government palace, the city lord was personally attending to an important guest at the dinner banquet.

Hearing that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' Chi Lai was seeking an audience, he smiled lightly, "Let him in."

Chapter 711: The Commander Should be Zong Ge

Chi Lai entered the dining hall.

Inside, two transcendants sat at opposite ends of the long table.

One was none other than the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, seated at the head.

The other was a male sea elf. His ears resembled scallop-shaped fins, and his body was covered in scales, possessing the distinctive, ethereal beauty characteristic of the elven race. However, his gloomy gaze diminished the charm of his appearance.

After a quick glance around, Chi Lai fixed his eyes on the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He bowed respectfully, using carefully prepared words to seek the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's assistance.

A sharp gleam flashed in the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes.

He had already known Chi Lai's purpose when his subordinate reported the visit.

In truth, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had long maintained an interest in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Thus, he had already received intelligence about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ caravan being ambushed and intercepted by a gold level powerhouse, resulting in the loss of both men and goods.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is my ally. Without your assistance back then, I would have lacked a proper justification to wipe out the Bayonet Gang. Nor would Snow Bird Harbor exist as it does today.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was willing to assist.

On one hand, he was eager to uncover the arms dealer behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. On the other, Lan Zao, also known as Hen Fa, had infiltrated Tranquil Hill.

Thus, maintaining ties with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would greatly benefit the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

After pondering, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord presented his solution.

Not only that, he personally funded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with substantial supplies and expressed concern over their recent heavy losses.

Chi Lai thanked him and departed with a full load.

The restaurant was once again left with only the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and his guest.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled as he introduced the sea elf across from him: “The silver fighter who just entered is a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. This mercenary group is the most prominent in our nation. Their leader, Long Fu, though only silver level, publicly slew a gold level opponent. Now, his name is known throughout the kingdom!"

The sea elf shook his head. "None of that concerns me. My sole focus now is whether I can create a Sea Feast worthy of my standards for this kingdom's celebration."

“You know why I'm here, don't you?” the sea elf asked, turning to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Of course,” he replied without hesitation.

He then called someone in from outside.

This was his head chef, responsible for the city lord's meals.

The chef was a human, ordinary-looking with a large belly, a silver level magic chef.

“You are Mei Lan Du?” The sea elf scrutinized him from head to toe.

The human chef nodded and bowed, “Lord Ci Hai Shen, greetings. I am indeed Mei Lan Du.”

The sea elf nodded, his gaze lingering on Mei Lan Du's belly. "I am to attend the kingdom's celebration this time. What do you think? Would you join me to participate in such a grand event?"

“I need you to serve as one of my deputies, assisting me in completing the Sea Feast.”

"Upon its completion, I shall impart to you our sea elves' exclusive gold level recipes!"

Yet Mei Lan Du shook his head, declining the invitation.

This human magic chef looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with utmost loyalty: "I will follow the city lord my whole life. Serving him meals each day brings me the greatest joy and gives my life its deepest meaning!"

At first, the sea elf Ci Hai Shen thought Mei Lan Du was playing hard to get, so he raised his offer several times.

But each time, Mei Lan Du refused outright, his stance unwavering.

Only then did the sea elf Ci Hai Shen grasp his true intentions and reluctantly withdrew his offer.

After Mei Lan Du withdrew, Ci Hai Shen inquired of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: "What would it take to make Mei Lan Du my deputy?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head: "Mei Lan Du is not my slave. I deeply respect his personal wishes!"

Unwilling to concede, Ci Hai Shen attempted to raise the offer again, only to face another refusal from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He snorted coldly, finally falling silent, his expression deeply displeased.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, however, grew even more cordial, smiling warmly as he understood the other's foul mood.

He initiated conversation, complimenting Ci Hai Shen as the most formidable magic chef at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's grand ceremony.

Ci Hai Shen shook his head. Though he possessed the proud nature of his elven race, he hadn't lost his sense of self-awareness.

He said, "Both the Earth Feast of the giant magic chef Da Dou and the Snow Feast of your nation's Xue Ying pose a formidable threat to me. Moreover, this occasion marks the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's once-in-a-century grand celebration, likely drawing other powerful figures as well."

“When the head chefs are of comparable strength, the role of the deputy chef becomes crucial to the overall outcome.”

“With so many ports in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, I specifically chose Snow Bird Harbor to enter your kingdom because of your subordinate, Mei Lan Du.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head with a bitter smile, indicating that while Mei Lan Du was willing to follow him, he was exceptionally unique. The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord refused to force him to do anything against his will.

At this, the sea elf Ci Hai Shen's expression turned icy, and he said no more.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord seemed oblivious, maintaining his composure. Even without Ci Hai Shen's response, he could carry on the conversation himself, keeping the atmosphere lively.

After the banquet ended, Ci Hai Shen, wearing a sour expression, promptly declined the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's invitation to stay at the city government palace.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood by the floor-to-ceiling window, watching Ci Hai Shen's departing figure below.

In the shadows behind him, Mei Lan Du's silhouette gradually emerged. The latter also gazed at Ci Hai Shen's back, sighing regretfully, "What a pity. If only the Tranquil Hill mission hadn't made such significant progress this time. I could have followed Ci Hai Shen and infiltrated the grand ceremony."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head slightly, “Tranquil Hill takes precedence above all else!”

Snow Bird Harbor Dock.

Just as Chi Lai was about to knock, the door opened.

Tripleblade welcomed Chi Lai warmly, ushering him to the seat of honor.

“You're finally back,” Tripleblade remarked.

Though only an iron level fighter, Chi Lai did not underestimate him.

For one thing, Tripleblade was Shi Qi's confidant. Underestimating him would easily offend Shi Qi, the second-in-command of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Moreover, Tripleblade had recently managed the corps' affairs with remarkable efficiency. His demonstrated competence forced Chi Lai to take him seriously. This explained why the leadership of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had entrusted command of the branch to Tripleblade, an iron level fighter.

“I hope I haven't disappointed your expectations. This trip yielded significant results,” Chi Lai detailed the specific assistance offered by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Tripleblade's face lit up with delight.

“So, the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance was secretly controlled by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord all along. By leveraging their transport convoys, we can resume delivering magic potions.”

“Ah, this truly solves our most pressing crisis.”

"Ever since our transport convoy was ambushed by a gold level foe, all major guilds have been watching from the sidelines. Previous negotiations to utilize their convoys all failed."

"This time, the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance's assistance means their caravans will face annihilation if the enemy discovers them. Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took a tremendous risk helping us this time."

“Moreover, he's funded us with substantial supplies... Hmm, we'll never reveal the truth about our minimal losses this time.”

Tripleblade visibly relaxed, “Outstanding work, Chi Lai. You've truly excelled this time.”

His words were laced with praise for Chi Lai.

Chi Lai, a former Imperial Navy officer, remained pragmatic. He shook his head. "It wasn't my doing, but Commander Long Fu's. I believe the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord assisted us so generously because of the commander's standing. I merely handled some minor tasks."

Tripleblade nodded. "Your perspective is correct, but your personal contribution cannot be overlooked. Per our corps' regulations, your earned merits will be duly recorded."

After Chi Lai departed, Tripleblade pursed his lips.

He held differing views on Chi Lai's praise for Long Fu: “The commander has been missing for far too long. He's utterly incompetent.”

“Even if his absence was to recover Lan Zao, his dereliction of duty remains undeniable!”

“The current Long Fu is merely a disguise worn by Lord Zong Ge. Only Lord Zong Ge can resolve the crisis before us.”

“Even if Lord Zong Ge has no such intention, he is the rightful leader of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the leader of all us survivors!”

“He alone is gold level. If he isn’t our leader, then who could possibly be?”

“Once he demonstrates his transcendent gold level prowess, the outside world will finally recognize our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as a true gold level force!”

Chapter 712: Is this Depravation or Righteousness?

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the atmosphere between Cang Xu and the fishman youngster felt heavy.

“Cang Xu, are you planning to target these sea beasts and the believers of Charming Blue Goddess? Kill them all to make undead materials?” The fishman youngster looked at Cang Xu expressionlessly.

Cang Xu sensed the youngster's displeasure and quickly lowered his head, “Lord, you were the one who suggested it. I never brought it up.”

He understood the fishman youngster well. Not initiating the idea meant he knew the youngster's stance.

The fishman youngster didn't grasp it at first, but after a moment, he realized Cang Xu's true intent.

Cang Xu knew the consequences of his suggestion, but he had no choice!

Such was the situation.

Cang Xu had been captured, while the dragonman youngster had been teleported and imprisoned within the Ocean Mother Nest, making escape extremely difficult.

Of course, a forced attempt could breach the Mother Nest, but after careful consideration, destroying the nest and charging straight out was not only extremely dangerous but would also cause significant loss of resources. For this was the only known supplementary channel for Pearl Bubbles!

Neither the fishman youngster nor Cang Xu wished to lose this vital supply route.

They felt no particular urgency.

This mindset stemmed from their assessment of the situation.

Teng Dong Lang had been eliminated, the Bayonet Gang dismantled, and both the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Li Jian Clan had become allies of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. The former had effectively turned Snow Bird Harbor into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' de facto headquarters, while the latter, the Li Jian Clan, served as the ideal conduit for the corps' integration into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

The fishman youngster trusted Zong Ge and Zi Di, believing both the atonement and commercial plans were progressing steadily. Thus, even trapped here, he felt relatively secure.

His decision to rescue Lan Zao stemmed not only from his refusal to abandon any comrade and his steadfast commitment to promises. It also reflected his optimistic assessment of the situation.

After all, back then, no gold level enemy had emerged to ambush the survivors.

Nor could he have foreseen a massive faction emerging to challenge the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, one capable of casually deploying gold level combat power!

Given these considerations and the immense profit at stake, both the fishman youngster and Cang Xu were patient, determined to peel back the layers slowly and meticulously.

The hidden trap implanted in Cang Xu by Jian Gui, coupled with Zhou Zhang's dual seals, was another crucial factor in their calculations. Caught between a rock and a hard place, the dragonman youngster couldn't act forcefully, mindful of Cang Xu's life. Thus, outsmarting the situation was the only way forward.

According to the plan, Cang Xu would deceive the goddess to obtain divine blessings, using them to resolve his own hidden dangers. the optimal outcome.

He could also gain the trust of Zhou Zhang and others, facilitating his and the fishman youngster's future escape.

The pair sought to avoid direct conflict whenever possible. If feasible, they could even rescue Jian Gui and others, thereby naturally establishing connections with the orthodox undead faction.

Becoming allies with the legendary faction led by You Shen would offer immense aid to the survivors.

Beyond their unparalleled undead knowledge, they possessed vast quantities of undead materials for trade. Should their atonement succeed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could even betray You Shen and his group, turning their defection into a merit for aligning with the Empire.

To be fair, this plan, conceived by Cang Xu and the fishman youngster, was quite sound.

Progress so far has been promising.

Cang Xu's bloodline had not only been accurately detected but had also undergone its first round of purification.

This advancement was a step ahead of their expectations.

Cang Xu's innate potential had been enhanced. The Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline was saint level, and even at just 3% purity, it was sufficient to elevate Cang Xu to silver level.

However, to advance further, Cang Xu was severely lacking in the requisite undead materials.

There were roughly three avenues to obtain such materials.

First, purchasing them on the market.

Second, exploring the Ghost World.

Third, crafting them personally.

Only the third option held any practical possibility for the fishman youngster and Cang Xu.

Thus, Cang Xu proposed targeting the teleported fishmen!

Knowing this would displease the youngster, he skillfully guided him to grasp the necessity himself.

The fishman youngster gazed silently at Cang Xu for a moment before sighing bitterly and shaking his head. "Cang Xu, do not casually create slaughter in pursuit of personal power. For doing so easily leads to corruption, causing us to lose our true selves."

"Some things seem within easy reach, yet the simplest paths often conceal immense, unseen costs. The true path is often fraught with hardship and peril, longer than any shortcut."

“Precisely because of this, the true path holds the greatest value!”

“Commander, you are absolutely right!” Cang Xu nodded repeatedly, acknowledging the words with an expression of attentive learning.

Had Jian Gui witnessed this demeanor, he would have been utterly astonished, unable to resist asking: Where had that defiant, silver-tongued Cang Xu from the undead gathering gone?

Cang Xu treated Jian Gui as an adversary yet faced the fishman youngster as a true subordinate confronting his leader!

It stemmed from the personal debt of gratitude owed to the dragonman youngster who had repeatedly saved him. It stemmed from the immense, unique benefits of the Bloodcore!

And most crucially - it stemmed from the call of humanity.

No one was immune to the allure of truth, goodness, and beauty. Even a schemer like Cang Xu yearned for a world filled with light and love.

For the commander who steadfastly upheld his principles, Cang Xu felt a deep, ingrained respect.

During his years as a noble butler, he witnessed too many wolves in sheep's clothing. Outwardly noble and virtuous, yet inwardly driven by selfish desires, utterly ruthless and without scruples.

The youngster rejected Cang Xu's proposal, yet Cang Xu showed no impatience.

He adopted an attitude of "I'll leave everything to you, Commander! I'll follow your orders."

Thus, silence fell over the scene.

As the leader, the fishman youngster had rejected Cang Xu's suggestion without hesitation.

But he had to confront reality and solve the immediate problem.

This was what a leader had to do!

How could he possibly meet the demand for undead materials?

What could he do?

Cang Xu lowered his head, maintaining silence, adopting a submissive demeanor that said, “I have no solution, but I'm certain you, the commander, can resolve this. I'll follow your lead.”

Just as Cang Xu expected, he soon heard the fishman youngster let out a deep sigh.

The fishman youngster paced back and forth, racking his brain, yet no solution came to mind.

Deep down, he had to admit that Cang Xu's proposal was the best, and only way to solve the problem.

“No, let me think some more...”

Watching the fishman youngster pace before him, his expression torn with struggle, a pang of guilt stirred in Cang Xu's heart.

Yet he remained steadfast in his suggestion, deliberately offering counsel: “My lord, there's no need to rush.”

"We have the undead materials. Xiao Wang Zi's turtle heavy cavalry, along with Jian Gui's gold level ghost ship, have all been captured by Zhou Zhang. Absorbing these will surely purify the Hanging Corpse Tree bloodline."

“Once I teleport out this time, I'll find a way to petition Zhou Zhang, urging her to use these as sacrificial offerings!”

The fishman youngster shook his head immediately: “That's like trying to quench a burning thirst with water from a distant river.”

“We must seize every moment and opportunity.”

“Following this method is too slow. We'd have to wait until the next blood sacrifice to possibly succeed.”

“And it's unlikely you'll be able to teleport in a third consecutive time.”

“Moreover, persuading Zhou Zhang to accept these undead lives as offerings is difficult, there are far too many variables involved.”

Cang Xu fell silent, his expression calm.

Of course, he wasn't truly persuading the fishman youngster. He was using this subtle approach to convey a single message: Listen to me. There's no other way!

The fishman youngster buried his head in deep thought, silent for a long while.

Cang Xu silently calculated the time before suddenly speaking: “Commander, time is limited. Should I proceed with the prayer first?”

The fishman youngster snapped out of his reverie. After communicating with the tower spirit and learning the situation in the Mother Nest, he hurriedly replied: "Yes, proceed with the prayer first. The sea beasts slaughtered by the Snow Pear Octopus have already exceeded half!"

The fishman youngster watched as Cang Xu entered the compartment.

The metal hatch closed before his eyes.

Leaning against the wall of the metal passageway, the fishman youngster clenched his fists.

The moment the metal hatch sealed shut, he already regretted it!

“Am I being too rigid in clinging to the righteous path?”

"Cang Xu is my comrade. He still bears the enemy's seal and a fatal hidden trap. Every ritual puts his life on the line!"

“Yet here I am wasting time, letting my comrade take risks. Isn't that selfish?”

"Compared to Cang Xu, those fishmen are strangers to me. In fact, they are my enemies."

“Am I prioritizing my enemies?”

"Have I considered the greater good? Am I even a worthy leader?"

Despite the abundant air in the metal passageway, the fishman youngster felt suffocation creeping over him.

Thoughts swirled through his mind. The more he reflected, the deeper his guilt toward Cang Xu grew.

“He trusted me. Even when I rejected his proposal, he didn't argue. He kept fighting with his life, striving for a sliver of hope.”

“Yet I hesitated, leaving Cang Xu exposed to danger!”

“For Cang Xu's sake, for our comrades, what's wrong with sacrificing a few fishmen?”

The fishman youngster's face remained deeply troubled.

He agonized inwardly: “If I do this, slaughter others to benefit my own kind, is that a form of depravation?”

“Is this truly a righteous act?”

“Who can tell me?”

The fishman youngster felt utterly lost.

He closed his eyes and prayed silently: "Justice God, my Lord, I follow Your path."

“At this very moment, a lost believer, Your lamb prays to You!”

“Grant me the answer, help me choose, let me walk forever in the light You believe in, far from all sin and corruption.”

The fishman youngster's eyes remained tightly shut, his face contorted with anguish.

His clenched fists gradually relaxed as his prayer stretched on.

When he opened his eyes, an expression of utter exhaustion washed over him.

The Justice God...

Did not respond.

Chapter 713: Respective Breakthroughs

White Hair Trench.

Ocean Mother Nest.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

“My name is Duo Yu. I'm a fisherman.”

"From a young age, I've worshiped the Charming Blue Goddess, I am a monk of my Lord."

“During a shipwreck, I was captured by an evil necromancer. He turned me into his test subject, ultimately transforming me into an undead being with his wicked magic!”

"Though I am no longer truly alive, I still yearn to return home. My wife must be waiting for me. My son, daughter-in-law, and my newborn grandson..."

“My Lord, I beseech Your mercy. Please rescue me.”

Cang Xu knelt on the ground, sincerely praying.

“My Lord, the mighty Charming Blue Goddess!”

“You are the powerful Sea Goddess, possessing infinite wisdom and strength.”

“I beseech You to rescue me, to have mercy on me, to help me break the seal upon my body, to restore my freedom, and to return me to safety.”

“As Your lamb, I deeply feel Your love and grace. I trust You will guide me and grant me strength to rise and bravely face all challenges in my destiny.”

“I shall serve You for life. Even though I have become an undead monster, it does not diminish my faithful devotion to You!”

Cang Xu prayed over and over.

After several repetitions, a surge of divine energy descended from the void, entering his body. Most of it remained within him, bringing vast knowledge and precious information.

“I've ascended to become a missionary.”

“These are all divine spells...”

A vast array of divine spells!

Missionary Mark, Soul Link, Spiritual Sound Sermon, Spiritual Navigation, Synchronized Conversion, and Opportunistic Deception.

“My Lord... I thank You for Your blessings and aid!” Cang Xu was filled with profound gratitude. Had he not been an undead being, tears would surely have welled in his eyes.

Yet, the moment his hand touched the metal hatch, the Memory Switching Spell activated.

A deluge of suppressed true memories surged into his consciousness. Two conflicting streams of memory collided, their contradictions leaving Cang Xu frozen in place, writhing in agony and plunged into profound self-doubt.

At that moment, the Subconscious Reminder Spell also took effect. It allowed Cang Xu to swiftly discern which stream of memories was his true self.

The look of confusion gradually faded from Cang Xu's face.

Soon, his eyes regained their clarity and composure.

“Success. This blasphemous prayer has succeeded once more.”

The joy vanished as quickly as it had come, and Cang Xu began calmly analyzing the outcome.

He had deliberately crafted this prayer to closely mirror the first one, with the fabricated memories nearly identical.

On one hand, the carefully designed prayer invoked specific needs: “breaking bonds” and “averting mortal peril.” The purpose was clear: to harness the divine blessing of the Charming Blue Goddess to resolve the seal planted by Zhou Zhang and the undead trap set by Jian Gui.

On the other hand, Cang Xu intended to compare this outcome with the first attempt. With most conditions identical, contrasting the two results would more clearly reveal the impact of his altered aptitude.

This was the mindset of a magician conducting an experiment, in a sense, it was already bordering on blasphemy.

If the deity were in good standing, such testing would likely prove futile, or even provoke divine wrath. But the Charming Blue Goddess lay gravely wounded and slumbering.

Such a deity was the perfect target for sacrilege!

The contrast between the two outcomes was stark.

“I recall that during my first prayer, the divine blessing merely restored my mental state to wholeness.”

“Only after repeated prayers did the Charming Blue Goddess bestow upon me the clerical position of monk.”

"This time, however, after my innate aptitude shifted, a single prayer elevated my rank: from monk to missionary. This is a qualitative leap!"

According to the divine message, monkhood was a preparatory position, while missionary was the true clerical position.

In other words, Cang Xu had been formally accepted into the Charming Blue Church. It was like an apprentice magician being officially promoted to a full-fledged magician!

“The gods select their believers and bestow divine blessings based on individual merit.”

“My aptitude has improved, making me a more promising investment. Thus, the gods granted a more generous divine blessing.”

Cang Xu's emotions were complex.

On one hand, he was filled with profound emotion. The Corpse-Hanging Tree bloodline truly lived up to its saint level status. Merely 3% concentration had caused the gods to visibly alter their attitude.

Yet he also felt the world's cold cruelty anew.

This was a world that revered bloodlines.

Whether cultivating battle qi, magic, or becoming a clergyman, innate bloodline talent was paramount.

Without it, even boundless talent drifted like rootless duckweed. Grand ambitions remained unfulfilled, and noble ideals forever unattainable.

Cang Xu examined his body closely.

Now, his body was saturated with Charming Blue divine power.

Undoubtedly, this was a superior form of energy.

It lingered within Cang Xu. Whenever he cast any divine spell he had mastered, this divine power would act as fuel, enabling the spell to be executed smoothly.

Simultaneously, the divine power continuously nourished Cang Xu's body and soul. Every moment, it slowly transformed his entire being. Over time, Cang Xu would undergo a profound transformation from within, aligning more deeply with the path of the Charming Blue believers.

If left unchecked, this transformation would, after a certain period, make Cang Xu an exceptional vessel for the Charming Blue divine power. Should his faith meet the required standard, he might even become a saint believer of the Charming Blue Goddess. After death, souls were guided more smoothly and swiftly by the Charming Blue Goddess into Her divine domain.

Cang Xu now stood alongside Zhou Zhang, not merely a magician, but also a clergyman.

Though the power of the Charming Blue divine power resided within Cang Xu's body, it did not conflict with undead magic power.

On one hand, the divine power existed within Cang Xu's physical form, while undead magic was concentrated within his personal magic pool. The two remained distinctly separate.

On the other hand, divine power was a superior energy source with universal attributes, comprehensively surpassing undead magic. It could encompass, absorb, and transform it.

In truth, battle qi, magic power, and divine power were all fundamentally compatible.

Thus, it was possible for a transcendent to be not only a fighter but also a magician and a clergyman. Of course, such instances were exceedingly rare.

“This time, I've been granted so many divine spells, and I can use them all. Unlike magic models, I don't need to study or research them at all; I can cast them directly.”

Cang Xu hadn't attempted any yet.

Among the vast information bestowed by the divine blessing were comprehensive descriptions of the corresponding divine spells. These descriptions were exceptionally detailed and specific.

In truth, upon acquiring these divine spells, Cang Xu experienced a powerful instinctive sensation.

Much like those transcendents with highly concentrated bloodlines who mastered racial magic without study. Employing these racial magics felt as simple, natural, and direct as moving one's limbs.

This undoubtedly saved clergymen a tremendous amount of learning time. Compared to magicians, this was also an advantage for them.

"However, without the process of learning, there is no true self-mastery. From another perspective, this is also a means by which the deities exert control over their transcendent believers."

When Cang Xu became a monk, he had already acquired much doctrinal knowledge. But the teachings of the Charming Blue Church primarily concerned personal cultivation, prayer, and other ritualistic norms.

This time, upon becoming a missionary, Cang Xu gained a wealth of new information. Among them were many more doctrines. Yet these teachings transcended personal cultivation, focusing more on how to preach and guide others in prayer.

“The most crucial concept among them is - divine favor.”

“Upon becoming a missionary, I automatically received ten units of divine favor.”

Divine favor differed from divine power.

Divine power was a form of energy, while divine favor was more akin to information.

Cang Xu attempted to grasp it: divine favor was the fusion of a believer's authority and currency within the divine domain.

Often, when activating major divine spells, not only divine power but also divine favor must be expended. Take the Resurrection Spell, for instance, this divine spell consumed vast amounts of divine favor.

Thus, merely mastering divine spells did not grant clergy the freedom to abuse them.

This was the deities' means of restraining their believers.

Greater divine favor signified higher authority for clergy, increasing the likelihood of divine favor and recognition.

Divine favor was immensely precious, influencing all aspects of life and could not be squandered.

Moreover, avenues for acquiring divine favor were limited. For Cang Xu at present, he possesses only one path: proselytizing.

The more successful his missionary work, the greater the divine favor he gained.

“This prayer yielded immense rewards, surpassing all previous prayers combined.”

“I've become a full-fledged clergyman, mastering these divine spells. The previous challenges have been effortlessly resolved.”

“However... there's no need to inform the commander just yet.”

“I believe he is capable of transcending his own limits.”

Holding deep anticipation, Cang Xu slowly pulled open the metal hatch.

The next moment, he saw the fishman youngster, weary yet resolute.

As before, they exchanged their secret signal. After confirming Cang Xu’s status was reliable, the fishman youngster sighed deeply and shared his latest decision.

The fishman youngster agreed to Cang Xu's earlier proposal, deciding to take action against those fishmen!

A smile immediately curved Cang Xu's lips.

The fishman youngster's reaction was exactly as he had anticipated.

The old magician, well-versed in human nature, promptly offered comfort: “Commander, there's no need for such heaviness in your heart. I can assure you; this is not a fall from grace!"

“Of all the survivors, even if everyone else were to fall, I would never believe you would.”

"Because you have your limits. You have always adhered to your principles and maintained unwavering vigilance against any actions that might lead to corruption."

“You are a true knight. Honest, kind, and steadfast in your convictions.”

“A knight?” The fishman youngster shook his head with a bitter smile. "Perhaps I'm merely a beast. Remember what we said during our final charge on Mystifying Monster Island? At best, I suppose I'm a beast knight."

Cang Xu shrugged, a faint smile touching his lips. “Who among us isn't a beast?”

“If becoming a beast is the only way to survive, then I choose to be one.”

“And I can say with certainty: you are far more than just a beast knight. You are our commander—the commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“You haven't let personal attachments cloud your judgment.”

“The path you chose is the survival strategy best suited for all of us who survived.”

“In formulating this strategy, you even set aside parts of yourself to some extent.”

"I know you have your principles, your noble character, yet you were willing to genuinely consider my proposal. Truly consider what we stand to gain by slaughtering these people. And what the consequences would be if we did not act."

“You weighed the greater good and considered every angle.”

“You are the leader of us all. No one can take your place.”

“Only you are worthy to be our commander!”

Chapter 714: Death Deception

Cang Xu smiled as he looked at the fishman youngster before him, offering heartfelt praise.

He knew at this very moment, the fishman youngster had undergone a pivotal transformation!

This metamorphosis was akin to the youth publicly declaring his identity on Mystifying Monster Island - no longer a paladin, but a beast knight.

It was also akin to the young man publicly proclaiming his justice during the battle of Snake Rat Island, affirming the righteousness of the path of redemption.

And now, within the Ocean Mother Nest, the fishman youngster had fully transformed from a beast knight into a mature mercenary leader.

This transformation was neither abrupt nor without cause. Before this moment, signs had been present all along—a gradual evolution built upon the foundational accumulation of quantitative change preceding qualitative transformation.

“Commander, you always manage to surpass yourself, breaking through your own limitations,” Cang Xu inwardly marveled.

In a way, Cang Xu placed more faith in the fishman youngster than the youngster had in himself.

In the Sheng Ming Empire's Count Qiang Kang's classic treatise, <Discourse on Ruler and Subjects>, there was a timeless maxim: The ruler chooses his subjects, and the subjects choose their leader!

Cang Xu had fully chosen the fishman youngster as his leader and had begun to assist him to the utmost of his ability.

This support wasn't mere obedience; it sprang from his heart, seeking every means to aid the fishman youngster.

Just as now, Cang Xu deliberately withheld information, granting the youngster a crucial opportunity for growth, and his wish was fulfilled as he witnessed the fishman youngster's personal breakthrough, seeing a more mature side emerge.

Having reached this point, Cang Xu held nothing back.

He addressed the fishman youngster: “Commander, we must indeed take action against these fishmen. But this time, it is not to kill them, turn them into undead fodder, and harvest the bloodline of the Corpse-Hanging Tree.”

“There has been a welcome development...”

Cang Xu then revealed everything he had gained from his prayer to the fishman youngster, holding nothing back.

The fishman youngster was both astonished and delighted.

“I never imagined your prayer would yield such rich rewards!”

“The Opportunistic Deception... This divine spell is likely the very method Zhou Zhang used against you, enabling her to capture you and the human pirates like Ba Qi so easily.”

“Combined with the Missionary Mark, Soul Link, Spiritual Sound Sermon, Spiritual Navigation, and Synchronized Conversion, these divine spells complement each other in layers, offering immense aid to your missionary work.”

"The more missionary success you achieve, the greater the divine favor you'll receive. According to divine blessings, once your favor reaches a certain threshold, you may perform purposeful prayers—consuming favor to obtain corresponding divine blessing!"

Cang Xu nodded repeatedly. “Following this path, once we accumulate sufficient favor, we can use prayer to teleport the Deep Sea Monster Fish and quietly depart this Ocean Mother Nest.”

“In the future, when my clerical position advances further and I obtain the Resurrection Spell, after expending vast amounts of divine favor and power, I should be able to truly revive Miss Zi Di!”

The prospect Cang Xu described filled the fishman youngster with yearning.

The gods were omnipotent.

Every deity possessed resurrection techniques, capable of bringing the dead back to life.

Yet differences existed among them.

Certain deities possessed specialized divine duties, excelling in resurrection magic and achieving the same effect with lesser cost.

The Charming Blue Goddess's duty lay in deception and disguise, bearing no relation to resurrection. Yet with sufficient effort, she too could bring others back to life.

With communication established, Cang Xu urged the fishman youngster to act swiftly.

Their target remained the numerous fishmen of the Da Ji Clan.

Their original plan had been to kill these fishmen, craft them into undead materials, and use them to collect the bloodline of the Corpse-Hanging Tree.

The new plan was still to capture these fishmen, then have Cang Xu preach to them. The more fishmen who worshipped the Charming Blue Goddess, the greater the divine favor Cang Xu would gain.

The contrast between the two plans was stark. The latter was easier to execute, more likely to succeed, and offered a higher return.

How much Corpse-Hanging Tree bloodline could they realistically gather by killing fishmen and crafting undead materials? The outcome was predictably meager, and the process was cumbersome.

The fishman youngster immediately dispatched the Rubber Swallowers to target the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan.

Under his prior design, the Da Ji Clan's fishmen had already “mercilessly slaughtered” Cang Xu, not only tearing him into countless fragments but even devouring his remains.

This revealed the depth of their hatred for Cang Xu.

After all, Cang Xu was the instigator who proposed to Zhou Zhang that the entire Da Ji Clan participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice!

Without a doubt, had the fishman youngster not intervened, the Da Ji Clan would have suffered catastrophic losses in this teleportation blood sacrifice. Even if any survivors remained, they would number in the single digits, leaving the entire Da Ji Clan existing in name only.

After all, the faithful chosen for the teleportation blood sacrifice were almost universally unwavering in their devotion and confidence in their beliefs. Thus, countless more believers met their end within the Ocean Mother Nest.

Among the Da Ji Clan, most of the Charming Blue believers were casual adherents, making it difficult for them to successfully endure the trials.

The Rubber Swallowers, disguised as seawater, emerged in droves. They approached the fishmen with ease, swallowing them whole in a single gulp.

The fishmen of the Da Ji Clan quickly noticed their companions mysteriously vanishing one by one.

They let out startled cries and instinctively huddled together.

Some fishmen, thinking themselves clever, shouted loudly, believing this was a test from the Charming Blue Goddess, urging their kin to pray fervently and ignore everything else.

Of course, others suspected this was a scheme by Zhou Zhang or a mysterious attack by the Snow Pear Octopus.

Even huddled together, the fishmen could hardly withstand the attacks of the Rubber Swallowers.

While the fishmen suffered, the fishman youngster and Cang Xu prioritized dealing with two heavyweight captives.

These captives were Ku Feng and Qing Xin.

Ku Feng had been chosen as the second candidate by the necromancers. Qing Xin, meanwhile, had been collectively selected by the human pirates as the first sacrifice.

Shortly after the fishman youngster attacked Cang Xu, the Rubber Swallowers, controlled by the tower spirit, swallowed both Ku Feng and Qing Xin.

The process went smoothly, without the slightest hitch.

On one hand, the Rubber Swallowers were all imbued with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell, disguising themselves as a mass of seawater. Drifting within the Ocean Mother Nest saturated with seawater, their disguise was flawless, leaving no trace of their presence.

On the other hand, Ku Feng and Qing Xin possessed no means to resist. Both were bound by a double seal cast by Zhou Zhang.

Swallowed by the Rubber Swallowers, they were carried deep into the depths aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The Rubber Swallowers kept them securely within their stomachs.

To the captives' senses, the surrounding seawater tightly enveloped their bodies, rendering them utterly immobile. Meanwhile, the fishmen had all mysteriously vanished.

They had struggled, of course, attempting to free themselves.

Ku Feng fared somewhat better, after all, as a silver level fighter with a zombie body, he possessed considerable strength. Yet he still couldn't match the Rubber Swallower, who wielded extraordinary power as a silver level.

As a magician, Qing Xin possessed far less physical strength and stamina than Ku Feng, making escape utterly impossible.

Whether they attributed this mysterious predicament to the Ocean Mother Nest or Zhou Zhang no longer mattered.

“What should we do with these two?” the fishman youngster asked Cang Xu.

Cang Xu replied, “Commander, proceeding as we previously discussed seems the most prudent course.”

“And now that I've mastered the Opportunistic Deception, I can fully imbue these two with its power, making the entire capture operation absolutely foolproof!”

Moments later, Cang Xu and the fishman youngster arrived at the landing pod. Here, two Rubber Swallowers stood motionless.

Their bellies were distended, each holding Qing Xin and Ku Feng captive.

Standing before them, Cang Xu began casting the Opportunistic Deception divine spell.

What was unusual, however, was the Pearl Bubble clutched in his palm.

The Opportunistic Deception released smoothly, and the pearl bubble in Cang Xu's hand was simultaneously depleted.

Both the Opportunistic Deception and the Pearl Bubble originated from the Charming Blue Goddess, sharing an inseparable, interconnected lineage.

The divine blessing also contained information on utilizing other resources to assist in releasing divine spells.

After falling victim to the Opportunistic Deception, Ku Feng suddenly sensed a massive octopus tentacle piercing through the seawater and coiling around him.

With a slight tug, the tentacle instantly shredded Ku Feng into pulp.

His vision plunged into utter darkness.

Meanwhile, Qing Xin felt the spell sustaining her breathing and countering the oceanic pressure abruptly vanish. Massive seawater pressed in, instantly forcing her to cough up blood. Simultaneously, countless waves of seawater flooded her nose and mouth. Within seconds, Qing Xin suffocated to death.

The sensations of both captives converged within Cang Xu's perception.

As the caster of the divine spell, Cang Xu could perceive the sensory outcomes of its targets.

This was Cang Xu's first genuine use of divine magic. Through Qing Xin and Ku Feng, he unearthed two fresh, hot pieces of practical experience. Thus, he grasped the formidable effect of the Opportunistic Deception Spell.

“So-called ‘opportunistic’ means exploiting the current trend to deceive the target.”

“And the outcome of my deception is death!”

Before his demise, Ku Feng's greatest imagined adversary was the saint level Snow Pear Octopus. Thus, after being deceived by the flow, he imagined it was the octopus's tentacles that killed him.

Qing Xin herself bore a double seal, but as a human, she couldn't naturally survive long underwater, especially at the ocean depths. Thus, Zhou Zhang had also imbued her with additional spells. Yet she deeply distrusted Zhou Zhang. This inner anxiety amplified, manifesting as the cause of her death, suffocation.

Precisely because it flowed so naturally, everything appeared perfectly plausible to both targets. Thus, they harbored not a shred of suspicion.

Now both were dead.

Though they still breathe and show signs of life, they have fallen into a deep, comatose slumber.

This was precisely the outcome Cang Xu had programmed when casting Opportunistic Deception.

He only needed to set the desired result. Through the unfolding situation, the deceptive narrative would automatically manifest within the targets' minds. These deceptive narratives would inevitably lead to the outcome Cang Xu had predetermined.

Now, both Qing Xin and Ku Feng had been deceived into believing they were already dead.

Chapter 715: Dealing With the Captives

“Now, bring them all out.” Cang Xu was satisfied with the results of his first divine spell.

At the fishman youngster's command, the two Rubber Swallowers regurgitated their captives from their stomachs.

Ku Feng and Qing Xin kept their eyes tightly shut, their bodies limp and utterly devoid of resistance.

“This is divine magic...” Cang Xu could already glimpse a fragment of the vast power of the Charming Blue Goddess.

The fishman youngster did not relax. He gave the order again for the other alchemy puppets to bring the two captives into the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Simultaneously, he commanded the remaining Rubber Swallowers to continue diving out of the ship's hold to capture the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan alive.

Ku Feng and Qing Xin, carried by the Rubber Swallowers, passed through the metal corridor and entered a compartment.

Within the compartment, several longhorn cages had been arranged.

The primary material for these alchemy cages was the spiral horns of narwhals.
