Earlier, the fishman youngster had instructed Zhong Yong to hasten their production. Shortly after entering the Ocean Mother Nest, the fishman youngster had used these very cages to imprison numerous naga transported in via blood sacrifices.

Spiral horns protruded densely from every part of the longhorn cages like spikes.

These slender spikes, like countless needles, pierced into Qing Xin and Ku Feng's bodies.

Unlike their use against the naga prisoners before, the spiral spikes now felt far gentler. Though they inflicted minor wounds on the two captives, the injuries were not severe and certainly not fatal.

The longhorn cage began to function, suppressing Qing Xin and Ku Feng's transcendent powers.

They had already been subjected to a double seal earlier.

Later, they fell victim to the Opportunistic Deception Spell. (This wasn't just any divine spell as it consumed the god level material Pearl Bubble!)

Now, the longhorn cage served as the final seal.

With these layers of suppression, Qing Xin and Ku Feng couldn't even think of escaping, even waking up was already extremely difficult.

The fishman youngster was as steady as a rock.

Handling prisoners demanded utmost caution! After all, the Deep Sea Monster Fish was their sole stronghold now.

Without any gold level powerhouses, a gold level magic ship was a rare trump card capable of slightly countering saint level threats.

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu had discussed repeatedly how to deal with Qing Xin and Ku Feng.

Ultimately, they reached a consensus: treat them as prisoners.

This was the safest course.

Their first agreement was clear: they had to intervene to aid these two.

Relying solely on conversion efforts would yield little. Left unattended, both would likely meet a gruesome end at the hands of the Snow Pear Octopus.

The question remained, how to help?

Without revealing their own existence, they could only rely on the Deceptive Disguise spell, alchemy puppets, and similar methods.

The fishman youngster had considered helping Ku Feng, altering his memories, and then sending him back. The advantage of this approach was that Ku Feng could assist Cang Xu to some extent.

But Cang Xu rejected it.

Cang Xu believed that even if Ku Feng offered help, it would be extremely limited. Jian Gui's influence over him far surpassed Cang Xu's hold over Ku Feng.

Moreover, converting two undead beings in succession would draw attention and suspicion. It would place Cang Xu squarely under the spotlight. Already walking a tightrope, further exposure would severely hamper his operations.

Thus, Ku Feng's fate was capture and imprisonment.

“This approach carries a potential risk,” Cang Xu had once pointed out, “Jian Gui planted a lethal backdoor within me. Given his nature, he likely did the same to Ku Feng.”

“The proof lies in how Jian Gui's faction can secretly communicate, discussing and sharing techniques for fabricating memories to bypass Zhou Zhang's dual seals.”

The necromancers achieved this level of coordination precisely because Jian Gui implanted hidden gateways within each of them. This forged a profound, clandestine bond between their souls and his, effectively turning these undead into his summoned entities, akin to the ghost ship itself.

Since Ku Feng can communicate in secret, it's almost certain he has a hidden passage on his body.

But the one who set up the hidden passage might not only be Jian Gui, but also Doctor Sui Shi who originally turned Ku Feng into a zombie. The latter might have secretly revealed the hidden trap to Jian Gui.

Cang Xu, meticulous in thought, stated bluntly, “The Deceptive Disguise spell might not be able to rule out this kind of soul connection either.”

“For even if the Deceptive Disguise spell employs pearl bubbles, it cannot forge one's faith.”

"Earlier, you, the commander, attempted to use the Deceptive Disguise spell to empower an alchemy puppet, hoping to pass it off as a Charming Blue believer for teleportation. The result? The alchemy puppet was promptly destroyed by the saint octopus."

Only by forging memories and tampering with the soul could one possibly deceive the gods.

It was also possible that the Deceptive Disguise spell was ineffective against the Charming Blue Goddess, though it might work on other deities, this remained to be verified.

But one truth was clear, in the realm of souls, undead research stood at the world's forefront, rendering the Deceptive Disguise spell obsolete.

The fishman youngster found Cang Xu's reasoning compelling.

After much contemplation, he still resolved to capture Ku Feng.

"The secret bond between Ku Feng and Jian Gui is a soul-level resonance between summoned entity and master. The Deceptive Disguise spell still holds the potential for deception. At present, we cannot rule it out."

“More importantly: even if Jian Gui senses Ku Feng is not dead and remains in the Ocean Mother Nest without returning, he won't report it. He will only conceal his suspicions, sensing something amiss within the Ocean Mother Nest.”

“This has minimal impact on the situation.”

“Moreover, from Jian Gui's reaction, we might infer whether the Deceptive Disguise spell can truly deceive a soul-level connection.”

“Understanding the limits of this technique will guide our future use of it.”

Now was an excellent opportunity to test it.

The fishman youngster decided to seize this chance.

He successfully persuaded Cang Xu.

Next came the plan for Qing Xin.

“Qing Xin is from the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, one of our own.” The fishman youngster harbored a natural trust toward her.

He thus asked Cang Xu whether Qing Xin was trustworthy.

Cang Xu directly recounted their shared experiences and Qing Xin's conduct throughout.

“In short, capture her and treat her the same as Ku Feng,” Cang Xu stated his proposed course of action.

Hearing this, the fishman youngster felt puzzled, for Qing Xin had shown no signs of being an enemy.

Cang Xu offered a logical explanation: “Precisely because of that, we cannot trust her lightly. Because Qing Xin hasn't shown any signs of being our ally either. Before truly joining us, she must prove herself.”

“In a way, we resemble the orthodox undead faction, so we can reference their methods.”

"The orthodox undead spread their legacy to expand the ranks of necromancers from the grassroots level. On the other hand, they intensify their screening process to control new recruits."

"Assessing a recruit's aptitude and talent is key; beyond that, they assign tasks to test new magicians' loyalty to the undead orthodoxy."

"They definitely conduct background checks, another way to gauge loyalty."

"Getting into Jian Gui's ranks was mostly luck!" Under normal circumstances, even with another two or three years, I wouldn't have been able to attain the intermediate undead inheritance."

Regarding Qing Xin, Cang Xu stated plainly: “I don't doubt Qing Xin's identity. I preliminarily recognize her as a potential ally.”

“But only after proving her loyalty can we gradually reveal our secrets to her.”

"Don't let emotions cloud your judgment. Just because you feel she's trustworthy doesn't mean she is. That's wrong!"

“A competent organization must have strict, well-defined protocols for integrating new members.”

The fishman youngster listened intently, finding his words deeply resonant.

Thus, he ultimately treated Qing Xin as a prisoner.

At this stage, neither the youngster nor Cang Xu could judge Qing Xin's loyalty, making it impossible to reveal secrets of the Deep Sea Monster Fish's caliber.

Moreover, even if they could verify her loyalty, the fishman youngster would struggle to secure Qing Xin's immediate allegiance. Zi Di was not present at his side.

Without Qing Xin's swift pledge of loyalty, her power remained inaccessible to him.

The more painful truth was that even if Qing Xin pledged her loyalty and devoted herself wholeheartedly to the fishman youngster, her power is of little use in the current situation. With or without her, it made no difference!

Qing Xin and Ku Feng were both locked inside a longhorn cage.

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu left the compartment, watching as the metal hatch slid shut with a final click.

The fishman youngster then stationed two swordsman guards on both sides of the hatch.

These alchemy automatons were among the spoils seized when they captured the Deep Sea Monster Fish. They could provide some combat and defensive support to survivors within the compartments.

Next, it was the turn of the fishmen from the Da Ji Clan.

The fishman youngster and Cang Xu returned to the landing pod. The first fishman was spat out by the Rubber Swallower, crashing onto the metal floor.

The fishman looked up at Cang Xu, believing he had arrived in the underworld and was seeing his exterminated clan's enemy.

But the pain from hitting the metal floor quickly brought him back to reality, he wasn't dead. Not only that, but Xu Jia was still alive too!

The fishman roared in fury and lunged at Cang Xu.

The next instant, he was pinned to the ground by the Rubber Swallower.

To the fishman's eyes, it was as if a torrent of seawater crushed him.

Rage and hatred surged within him as he struggled desperately, his bloodshot eyes fixed on Cang Xu. If looks could kill, Cang Xu would have died seventeen or eighteen times over.

Cang Xu remained expressionless as he channeled divine magic toward the fishman.

As divine power drained from him, the spell Soul Link manifested.

A pale blue radiance extended from the depths of Cang Xu's soul, coalescing midway into a slender chain that shot into the fishman's body.

The fishman's flesh offered no defense as the chain pierced directly into his soul.

Chapter 716: Cang Xu – Watch Me Fleece Charming Blue Goddess

Two souls, bound by the links of the chain, formed a mysterious connection.

Yet the moment was fleeting. As the fishman struggled desperately, his furious roars causing the pale blue chain to sway violently, tiny cracks began to form on its surface.

Within seconds, the cracks multiplied until they reached their limit.

Snap.

With a soft crackle, the chain shattered, dissolving into a pale blue mist that swiftly dissipated in midair.

Cang Xu’s body swayed, while the fishman’s eyes rolled back as he collapsed unconscious.

The divine spell Soul Link had failed!

Cang Xu steadied his emotions and calmly assessed his condition: “The backlash from a failed divine spell is far weaker than that of a regular spell.”

“If the target resists too fiercely, the soul link will fail. This outcome was within my expectations.”

Unlike other divine spells, the information granted by the divine blessing regarding Soul Link and related divine spells was concise and not exhaustive.

Yet Cang Xu possessed extensive undead heritage, whose teachings contained profound insights into souls.

Knowledge was a magician's wellspring of power.

Though he had pursued dual paths and become a clergyman, Cang Xu's essence remained unchanged - he was a magician!

As Jian Gui had stated, Cang Xu was an exceptional magician, embodying the finest qualities of his profession.

Through this knowledge, even without explicit mention in the divine blessing, Cang Xu deduced other crucial aspects of soul linking.

Thus, he deliberately exposed his true form to provoke the fishman, facilitating a test to uncover the limits of this divine spell.

His first attempt at soul linking failed; the bond snapped. Yet, in truth, his experiment succeeded!

“The divine spell Soul Link consumes not only the divine power within my body, but also my mental strength.”

“Should the link break midway, the souls of both parties endure turmoil, suffering mental damage.”

"In practical terms, my soul foundation is vastly stronger than the fishman's, so I could withstand it. My spirit plummeted from its peak, but only slightly. The fishman, however, suffered severe soul trauma. His spirit collapsed, triggering his body's self-preservation mechanism, causing him to collapse and lose consciousness on the spot."

“As a bronze level missionary, the total divine power within me can sustain nearly 20 uses of the divine spell Soul Link.”

“But my mental strength only allows me to cast the Soul Link 10 times consecutively.”

"As an iron level necromancer, my soul has been cultivated, granting me greater mental fortitude than a typical magician of the same level. Thus, the divine spell Soul Link is unique, it consumes more mental energy than divine power."

A single experiment yielded vast insights for Cang Xu.

After a minute's rest, he proceeded with subsequent trials. The subjects' levels gradually increased, from bronze to iron, then to silver level.

After several rounds of testing.

The fishman from the Da Ji Clan landed on the metal floor, thrashing wildly and screaming frantically.

In his perception, the Snow Pear Octopus' tentacles had wrapped around his entire body, slowly tightening to strangle him.

At the critical moment, Cang Xu arrived and gently stroked the octopus tentacles.

The next instant, the tentacles miraculously released their grip.

The fishman of the Da Ji Clan stared at Cang Xu, exclaiming in delight, “Lady Qi Miao!”

Qi Miao was indeed the priestess of the Da Ji Clan, being only iron level. Cang Xu had assumed her identity through the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Cang Xu addressed the fishman: “I have successfully converted and received a divine blessing. Next, I shall preach to you. You must cooperate fully. Failure will mean I cannot save you a second time."

The tribe's fishman nodded urgently.

With their full cooperation secured, Cang Xu cast his first divine spell: the Missionary Mark.

This spell consumed the least divine energy and had an almost instantaneous casting time.

A faint glow flickered, and the mark was imprinted upon the fishman's souls.

Next, Cang Xu cast the divine spell Soul Link.

The fishman cooperated fully, creating a remarkably stable bond.

Divine spell - Spiritual Sound Sermon.

The divine power within Cang Xu diminished slightly, while his mental energy depleted even faster. The teachings of the Charming Blue faith stored in his memory, along with his profound understanding of them, flowed through the Soul Link, pouring deep into the fishman's soul.

Within seconds, the fishman grasped the essence of the Charming Blue faith, achieving an understanding equal to Cang Xu's own.

“Next, I shall guide your prayer,” Cang Xu declared.

The fishman nodded fervently.

Divine spell -Spiritual Navigation.

As Cang Xu performed the divine spell, the image of the Charming Blue Goddess automatically appeared in the fishman's mind. The fishman's spiritual energy flowed like water seeking its lowest point, pouring into the divine image, transforming into faith, and being offered up.

“Keep your prayers going!” Cang Xu reminded him.

Kneeling on the ground, the fishman prayed incessantly, his expression deeply pious and tense. Yet genuine faith could not be feigned; his devotion remained merely superficial.

Conversion was difficult. No matter how well one performed outwardly, one could not deceive one's own heart.

Divine spell - Synchronized Conversion!

Cang Xu unleashed the most crucial divine spell.

The faint blue glow emanating from the soul chain linking the two suddenly blazed brilliantly.

Divine power surged ceaselessly from Cang Xu, flowing through the chain into the depths of the fishman's soul, violently reshaping it.

The fishman felt intense agony, but he endured it, persisting in his prayers.

His level of faith began to deepen rapidly, but after mere seconds, it halted.

Cang Xu immediately halted the divine spell, panting heavily.

He also actively severed the soul link between them.

Freed from the burden, the fishman's trembling body steadied. Just as he lifted his head, he met Cang Xu's stern gaze. Startled, he immediately resumed his prayer without pause.

Cang Xu sighed inwardly, concluding: "Among all divine spells, the Synchronized Conversion Spell consumes the most energy. Its effect is also the most wondrous, it synchronizes the faith of both parties."

“The reason the effect wasn't pronounced is because my own faith in the Charming Blue Goddess is merely that of a casual believer.”

Since he and the fishman were on equal footing, Cang Xu could only offer minimal guidance to the latter.

Even so, seeing the small changes revealed the larger truth. Judging by the depth of the fishman's deepening faith, this divine spell was extraordinary!

“To test the true power of this divine spell, I must first forge memories to deepen my own faith before casting it on the fishman.”

“However, this kind of divine spell cannot be used frequently.”

“It consumes not only divine power and mental energy, but also my soul's foundation!”

Cang Xu keenly sensed his soul foundation had weakened slightly. He surmised the principle behind Synchronized Conversion lay in forging a soul link to rapidly reshape the target's spirit.

A clergyman's lifelong cultivation involved their body and mind being nourished and transformed by divine power, gradually molding them into a form fit to bear that power and fulfill the deity's will.

Synchronized Conversion essentially accelerated this divine power transformation process, condensing what would normally be a lengthy journey into the single minute it took to cast the spell. This compression made the transformation violent, causing the target to endure intense agony.

The template for this soul reshaping was the caster's own soul.

When Cang Xu's soul was heavily referenced, it also suffered damage, causing its essence to dissipate and transform into a form of nourishment that fed the fishman's soul.

A sudden thought struck Cang Xu: “Isn't this using my soul as a spell-casting material to transform and enhance the fishman's soul?”

“How does this cultivation method differ from my use of soul crystals to appease the ghost body's appetite while nourishing my own soul and enhancing my foundation?”

“Essentially, they are identical.”

“The only difference is one uses soul crystals, while the other directly consumes my soul.”

“If I were to use soul crystals instead of my soul, wouldn't the effect be even better?”

Cang Xu's thoughts diverged, leading him to a crucial question.

Soul crystals were processed materials, craftable only by necromancers who mastered the technique.

This method was recorded within the intermediate soul aspect inheritance.

Cang Xu paid a heavy price to obtain it by sheer luck from Jian Gui.

Soul crystals were pure soul essence, devoid of any memory impurities. Every ghost possessed a hunger to consume. Feeding on other souls caused memory confusion; in severe cases, it transformed them into monsters saturated with negative energy, their sanity utterly lost.

But consuming soul crystals avoided this pitfall. Without foreign memories, there was no conflict or alteration of self-awareness.

“No, perhaps that's not it!”

“Soul crystals lack memory, yet memory is the cornerstone of faith.”

“By fabricating memories, I can deepen faith to a certain extent.”

“Through Synchronized Conversion to reshape a target's soul, my own memories of worshipping the Charming Blue Goddess should be a crucial factor!”

“Though my memories remain intact, it is precisely this memory that can guide others, deepening their faith in the Charming Blue Goddess.”

A thread of contemplative light flickered in Cang Xu's eyes: “In that case, my need for soul crystals grows even greater.”

The Synchronized Conversion divine spell was the most crucial tool for proselytizing; its use would inevitably become frequent.

The more Cang Xu used, the greater the toll on his soul. Fortunately, soul crystals could replenish his soul. Naturally, the demand for soul crystals grew ever greater.

“Thankfully, I've already mastered the method of crafting soul crystals...”

After pondering, Cang Xu outlined a systematic process: craft soul crystals, cast the Deceptive Disguise spell along with a series of divine spells, successfully proselytize to gain divine favor, then expend that favor to obtain targeted divine blessings... This entire cycle would create a positive feedback loop. While the Charming Blue Goddess lay in slumber, he could continuously fleece the faith system to strengthen himself!

With joy in his heart, Cang Xu shared his conclusions with the fishman youngster.

The fishman youngster immediately understood: “So the undead spell I need to practice next is Soul Crystal Creation.”

Cang Xu remained sealed and unable to cast spells, but the divine spells of the Charming Blue Church were permissible.

The reason was obvious, the sealer, Zhou Zhang, was the archbishop of the Charming Blue Church.

Therefore, Soul Crystal Creation could only be performed by the fishman youngster.

“Time is limited. I must convert as many fishmen as possible to gain greater divine favor.”

“I have a better idea...”

"After successfully converting these captives from the Da Ji Clan, we could release them. These very people will be my strongest justification for participating in the next teleportation blood sacrifice!"

Cang Xu's voice trembled with excitement, his eyes gleaming.

Chapter 717: Qi Ling - ?!

White Hair Trench.

The teleportation blood sacrifice had been underway for some time, drawing ever more believers to the golden altar.

Qi Ling's face was as still as water, cursing inwardly: “Xu Jia, I swear I'll make sure you don't die so easily!”

In Qi Ling's world now, the most hated enemy was Cang Xu, there was no contest.

Jian Gui sighed: "Time's almost up for this batch to transmit back. "

Standing beside Qi Ling, he observed her expression.

"Qi Ling's obsession is the Da Ji Clan. Her enmity with Xu Jia is irreconcilable."

Jian Gui understood: Ghosts could be endlessly conjured through necromancy, but these manufactured spirits possessed greatly diminished sentience, their memories from life nearly erased. Only rare cases possessed such intense fixation that they retained most of their spirit and memories, enabling them to become necromancers through cultivation.

Qi Ling, Xiao Wang Zi, and Ku Feng were all such cases.

Only such undead beings could be considered sentient lifeforms.

From Jian Gui's perspective, he understood Xu Jia's act of vengeance this time. Yet, alongside this understanding, Jian Gui also felt disgust and hatred toward Cang Xu.

This was the natural emotion of a superior witnessing subordinates act independently, triggering events beyond their control.

Naturally, Jian Gui's loathing for Qi Ling was no less intense than his hatred for Cang Xu.

Earlier, Cang Xu's words had struck a nerve deep within Jian Gui. Jian Gui had been captured by Zhou Zhang and driven to the brink of death precisely because he had attempted to rescue Qi Ling. How could he not hate and despise Qi Ling?

As a superior, Qi Ling was merely a subordinate in his eyes, or even an undead pet. A subordinate or pet had led their master to the brink of death. Jian Gui's thoughts were obvious.

He simply didn't show it.

He was deceitful, skilled in the art of disguise.

Combined with his previous care for others, he left no cracks in his facade, keeping Qi Ling and the others completely deceived.

Only Cang Xu saw through Jian Gui's true nature.

Continuing to deceive Qi Ling also served Jian Gui's interests perfectly. After all, if things turned sour, he would lose the valuable asset that was Qi Ling. In the current situation, Jian Gui desperately needed others' help to fabricate memories together.

Therefore, he found the present predicament quite troublesome.

This internal conflict was extremely sharp and fundamentally irreconcilable.

“Best to stall for time. Wait until the moment is ripe before making a decision.”

“The most crucial thing now is fabricating memories. That's the key to my survival!”

Jian Gui had secretly resolved to adopt a strategy of delay and appeasement toward the conflict between Xu Jia and Qi Ling. He would placate both sides, preventing the conflict from escalating further. Instead, he would focus all efforts on easing tensions, uniting everyone's strength, and advancing the grand plan of forging memories to escape.

Zhou Zhang was also waiting.

She was deeply intrigued by the teleportation process, her heart filled with anticipation. Having presided over countless blood sacrifices, she found them increasingly tedious. This was a rare spice to liven things up.

Some questioned Zhou Zhang's selection process this time.

After all, a significant number of Charming Blue believers also wanted to participate in the teleportation blood sacrifice. But this time, Zhou Zhang had allowed the Da Ji Clan to take up many of the spots.

Yet, no matter how much questioning arose, what could be done?

At most, grumblings would surface, but no one dared take concrete action to oppose it.

Zhou Zhang's saint level power alone crushed all dissent.

Moreover, the octopusman Zhang Yu Wan and the Jiu Hua Bi were formidable gold level experts, second only to Zhou Zhang.

This was the world of the strong.

Led by Zhou Zhang, these three transcendent beings possessed the greatest combat prowess, cementing their status as unshakable pillars.

Amidst the crowd's anticipation, spatial ripples emerged.

One by one, fishmen of the Da Ji Clan appeared, their numbers steadily growing.

The onlookers' expressions shifted, some murmuring in astonishment.

The return of so many fishmen caught many off guard!

A flicker of light danced in Zhou Zhang's eyes. She instantly understood that something significant had occurred.

Ultimately, dozens of members of the Da Ji Clan had returned!

Such a situation was truly rare.

"Could it be that the entire Da Ji Clan holds such intense and unwavering faith in our Lord?!" exclaimed a Charming Blue believer.

Others began flattering Zhou Zhang, praising her discerning eye for recognizing the Da Ji Clan as such a worthy clan.

Qi Ling felt as if she'd been struck by immense joy!

The overwhelming delight even caused a slight dizziness.

When she spotted a familiar figure, she swiftly reined in her smile, her expression turning cold.

It was none other than Cang Xu.

Qi Ling let out a shrill laugh and cursed loudly, "Xu Jia, you're responsible for the deaths of hundreds from my Da Ji Clan. This debt, I will repay you a hundredfold! But you never expected this, hahaha! Our Da Ji Clan's faith in the Charming Blue Goddess stands firm through trials."

“You sought to wipe out our entire tribe, but your plan has failed!”

The Da Ji Clan had suffered heavy losses, yet many members remained—it could hardly be called annihilation. Given time, combined with the fishmen's formidable reproductive capacity, they would recover.

Cang Xu looked at Qi Ling, his expression calm, even tinged with a hint of peculiarity.

The next moment, a fishman from the Da Ji Clan suddenly knelt at Cang Xu's feet, repeatedly kowtowing and shouting: "Lord Xu Jia! My life is saved only because of you! You are my savior!"

Qi Ling: ?!

Only a handful of fishmen remained prostrate at Cang Xu's feet.

The majority of the Da Ji Clan's fishmen wore complex expressions as they looked at Cang Xu, saying in unison: "It's true we survived this time because of you, Xu Jia. But the hatred our clan holds isn't so easily erased."

“Following the ocean's code, I won't seek revenge against you now. But my descendants will never forget this grudge!”

Qi Ling: ??!!

Clan Leader Qi Ren of the Da Ji Clan stared at Cang Xu with a complicated expression, saying nothing.

Qi Shu, standing not far away, shouted, "Even if you guided me to conversion and saved my life, you indirectly caused the deaths of hundreds of our clan members. Don't think you can sweep this under the rug. I will kill you to avenge my people. But after killing you, I'll take my own life—to repay the debt of your blood!"

Qi Ling: ???!!!

Her face twisted in confusion. She couldn't comprehend it, screaming out in frustration: “Damn it! What the hell is going on here?!”

It wasn't just her curiosity; everyone else felt the same.

The enmity between Xu Jia and the Da Ji Clan was common knowledge. Yet after this teleportation blood sacrifice, an utterly bizarre scene unfolded before them.

“It was Xu Jia who guided me to convert to our Lord.”

“He saved many lives, like the clan leader, the great warriors, and even the high priest.”

“Without him, none of us would have survived.”

“He even personally stopped the saint beast from slaughtering us...”

Chapter 718: Zhou Zhang – I Understand

The fishmen exchanged words, revealing the events that unfolded within the Ocean Mother Nest - Cang Xu prayed and once again received divine favor. Using the divine spells granted by the divine blessing, he guided many fishmen to successfully convert, passing the saint beast's death trial!

Upon hearing this, Qi Ling froze in place, utterly stunned.

Jian Gui narrowed his eyes, his gaze turning inquisitive.

"How did he manage it? With the dual seals that Zhou Zhang placed upon him, how could he possibly wield divine magic?"

"Is it because this is the divine blessing of the Charming Blue Goddess, immune to the dual seals? Or has Xu Jia secretly broken the seal, and deceived me?"

Just as Jian Gui pondered, murmurs of doubt immediately rose from the surrounding believers.

These voices swelled rapidly, like an unending tidal wave crashing over Cang Xu at the center of the fishmen.

Cang Xu knew exactly what to do when faced with public suspicion at this moment.

In the next instant, he turned toward the golden altar and bowed to Zhou Zhang standing upon it.

Zhou Zhang stood on the altar, surveying the believers who had been teleported back. Her gaze lingered primarily on Cang Xu, but when she saw him bowing to her, the slight frown on her brow eased a little.

For Cang Xu was performing the ritual etiquette of the Charming Blue Church.

Cang Xu declared, “By the grace of our Lord, divine blessing has once more been bestowed upon me, granting me mastery over a series of divine spells capable of swiftly guiding others to conversion.”

"Though sealed, it is strange that within the Ocean Mother Nest, I can freely wield these divine spells. Yet the undead spells I once used remain beyond my reach."

Cang Xu spoke not only to Zhou Zhang but also to Jian Gui.

He continued, “This must be the Lord's will.”

“Under the divine favor's call, I have understood my own transgressions!”

"I was wrong! Before this, driven by personal greed, I used the banner of testing faith as a pretext, my true intent was to exact vengeance upon my enemies. Thus, I suggested sending the Da Ji Clan into the Ocean Mother Nest."

"This was wrong. Completely wrong. After receiving divine blessing, I deeply understood this truth."

"Therefore, to atone for my sins, I took direct action, devoting myself to converting the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan. I risked communicating with the saint beast, and only then did I manage to save some of my Lord's believers."

"Alas, time was too short. Had I been given sufficient time, I should have been able to save most of the fishmen."

"I am guilty! Please punish me, Lord Zhou Zhang."

“Everything is my fault.”

“Originally, all the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan should have become believers of our Lord.”

By the end of his words, Cang Xu was kneeling on both knees, nearly prostrating himself, and emitting a wail of anguish.

Were he not an undead being, his acting alone would have produced rivers of tears of remorse.

The surrounding believers fell into a profound silence.

They could all sense the genuine sorrow and remorse emanating from Cang Xu.

Some devout, even fanatical believers, empathizing deeply, chose to forgive him on the spot.

Yet the majority remained skeptical.

Qi Ling: ...

She stared intently at Cang Xu, her face showing not a shred of forgiveness.

By all logic, seeing her sworn enemy kneel and utter such agonized remorse should have filled her with joy.

Yet why did she crave nothing more than to beat him to death? To grind his bones to dust?

Zhou Zhang suddenly threw back her head and laughed, pointing at Cang Xu beneath the altar.

“Interesting,” she declared repeatedly. "This is fascinating. You are fascinating. Far more amusing than I ever imagined."

“Follow me.”

“We will talk privately.”

Though she spoke thus, Zhou Zhang extended a single hand. Her fingers curled, and a surge of water flowed, sweeping the kneeling Cang Xu directly toward her.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the fishman youngster sat in the cockpit, watching the scene intently through the magic projection.

Zhou Zhang raised Cang Xu by his clothes and jumped up softly, reaching beyond the crowd and going toward a secluded area.

The two began to talk.

Cang Xu first informed Zhou Zhang that his clerical position had been elevated, from monk to missionary.

Unlike faith itself, such clerical ranks could be verified.

After confirming Cang Xu’s promotion to missionary, Zhou Zhang’s expression softened further.

Cang Xu then detailed a series of divine spells to Zhou Zhang, all except the Opportunistic Deception Spell.

Zhou Zhang's face flashed with astonishment, and she immediately pressed for detailed information about the divine spells.

Her reaction caught Cang Xu off guard.

“Your Lordship, is there... something wrong with these divine spells?” Cang Xu inquired.

Zhou Zhang stared intently at the old necromancer for several seconds, her gaze making Cang Xu visibly uneasy. Finally, she spoke: “Do you know that even I have not mastered these divine spells?”

Cang Xu was utterly astonished. “How could that be?”

He had always assumed the divine spells of the missionary series were commonplace. But now, even an archbishop like Zhou Zhang admitted she did not possess them. This proved these divine spells were exceptionally rare.

Zhou Zhang shook her head. "It's not just me. Even other bishops likely haven't mastered them. Truthfully, before you mentioned it, I'd only heard of isolated cases, and those weren't even from the Charming Blue Church."

Cang Xu was dumbfounded.

“Hearing about it is nothing compared to witnessing it firsthand.”

Zhou Zhang led Cang Xu back to the gathering place of the believers. There, she casually selected a believer and commanded Cang Xu: “Come, perform a divine spell before me.”

Divine spell - Missionary Mark.

Cang Xu first imprinted his mark upon the Charming Blue believer before him.

Zhou Zhang's expression remained calm.

This spell was standard issue for the masses. Future believer advancements would include credit for the imprint's originator.

Divine spell - Soul Link.

Zhou Zhang watched the soul chain connecting Cang Xu and the Charming Blue believer, her eyes suddenly flashing with sharp brilliance.

Divine spell - Spiritual Sound Sermon.

After Cang Xu cast the spell, Zhou Zhang raised her hand to signal a pause, then carefully inquired about the Charming Blue believer's experience.

The Charming Blue believer replied that he had gained extensive knowledge of Charming Blue doctrine, though he had studied these teachings before. Yet receiving this information now brought insights and realizations he hadn't possessed previously.

Zhou Zhang nodded slightly, then signaled Cang Xu to continue.

Divine spell - Spiritual Navigation.

This time, Zhou Zhang's expression was no longer serene.

Her understanding of clerical cultivation ran far deeper than that of Cang Xu or the fishman youngster.

“The Spiritual Navigation divine spell directly channels the believer's spiritual energy toward our Lord.”

“Normally, achieving this step solely through the believer's own efforts is no easy feat. Often, divine statues must provide coordinates to accurately guide the direction of faith.”

“This is precisely why major denominations establish churches, altars, and statues across the world—to facilitate this process.”

"Occasionally, statues or churches may even lose connection with the deity itself. This causes accumulated faith to stagnate, unable to ascend upward."

“But with Spiritual Navigation, believers no longer require statues or altars as intermediaries. They can offer their entire devotion instantly.”

“Though I wonder what the loss rate might be?”

“No, the true significance of this divine spell lies in its precision.”

"Many evil and demonic deities disguise altars or tamper with statues to intercept faith mid-transmission. This spell effectively prevents such interference."

“This spell is incredibly practical!”

Zhou Zhang's mind churned with thoughts, his emotions running high.

Cang Xu continued casting the divine spell.

Divine spell - Synchronized Conversion!

Witnessing this, Zhou Zhang felt a tidal wave of shock surge through her. She could no longer maintain her composure, her face betraying palpable astonishment.

“There exists such a divine spell!”

“It's incredible! With this spell, one could practically mass-produce believers.”

“With such divine power, how could our Lord ever lack believers?”

“This will surely bring our Lord to prominence!”

Amateurs watched for spectacle; experts observed the essence.

The shock Zhou Zhang felt far surpassed that of Cang Xu and the fishman youngster. The latter two were laymen, utterly unaware of the profound significance of these divine spells.

Under the spell's influence, the Charming Blue believer trembled with excitement.

Though pain coursed through him, tears streamed down his face as he choked out, “Never before have I felt my Lord's true intent and grace so profoundly!”

“Lord Xu Jia, your devotion to our Lord surpasses even mine!”

“Though you are an undead, you command my deepest respect.”

Cang Xu gave him a slight nod, then frowned in puzzlement. “This is odd. Why isn't the effect of this divine spell as pronounced in the Mother Nest?”

“Is that so?” Zhou Zhang snapped back to attention.

Though faith remained unfathomable, Zhou Zhang could discern from the Charming Blue believer's expression and words, combined with her vast experience as an archbishop, that the believer's devotion had surged dramatically in a short time.

"It is the soul that undergoes transformation... thus, when the divine spell is performed upon a believer, they will feel an intense, heart-wrenching agony emanating from within their very being!"

Zhou Zhang performed divine spells, observing both the Charming Blue believer and Cang Xu from every angle.

As a saint level archbishop of the Charming Blue Church, she had long understood the profound mysteries of the soul.

After completing his proselytizing divine magic, Cang Xu let his hands fall naturally at his sides. He looked at Zhou Zhang with reverence, quietly awaiting her judgment.

Zhou Zhang scrutinized Cang Xu once more, her gaze having undergone a complete transformation.

Where before it had been filled with interest and a desire to explore, now she looked at him as if he were a peerless treasure.

Zhou Zhang dismissed the Charming Blue believers who had served as test subjects, then led Cang Xu to a secluded corner for private conversation: “Xu Jia, I now fully understand why our Lord bestowed divine blessing upon you and granted you these unique divine spells.”

Cang Xu immediately inquired.

Zhou Zhang replied, "When you wield these divine spells, have you noticed how intensely they drain your spirit and soul? This depletion far surpasses the consumption of divine power."

Cang Xu nodded urgently.

Zhou Zhang continued, “These are divine spells refined by our Lord, or perhaps by some believer within our church.”

“In essence, they align more closely with necromancy magic, spells that target the soul itself.”

“In truth, you could potentially cast them using undead magic power, though the effects would be drastically diminished.”

Chapter 719: The Seal is Lifted

A flicker of surprise crossed Cang Xu's face.

Zhou Zhang's attitude toward necromancy and necromancers caught him off guard.

She smiled, reading Cang Xu's thoughts: "What's so strange about that? Didn't the gods suppress the necromancer faction precisely because they delved into the mysteries of the soul?"

“I worship my Lord. You needn't doubt the steadfastness of my faith.”

“Do you know what true steadfast faith is?”

“It is faith that endures without the slightest wavering, even after learning the world's true nature!”

“How the gods gather faith involves secrets tied to the soul. For a bishop level clergyman, there are scarcely any secrets left undiscovered.”

“And the reason I became a bishop is that even after learning these truths, my faith remained unshaken.”

These words held great weight.

Cang Xu nodded, convinced by Zhou Zhang's reasoning.

Now reflecting, Cang Xu suddenly remarked, “In that case, these new Charming Blue divine spells might not be suitable for widespread dissemination either.”

“On one hand, the vast majority of lower-level clergy aren't necromancers and lack the ability to cast such spells.”

"On the other hand, forcing their adoption would be disastrous. Because they touch upon the mysteries of the soul, they could shake the faith of the clergy themselves."

Many low-level believers were unaware they were offering themselves as tribute. But mastering this series of divine spells would make them realize they too contributed to the deities, rather than everything being solely divine blessing as they had previously believed.

Thus, everything the deity bestowed upon the faithful ceased to be unconditional grace and instead resembled a transaction.

'You give me your spiritual energy, and I repay you with divine power and divine spells.'

This, of course, would deliver a powerful shock to one's faith.

If stabilized, the believer gained steadfast conviction, laying a solid foundation for ascending to a higher level. But in most cases, faith would waver and steadily decline. It might well condemn the believer to a lifetime of being merely a casual believer.

Zhou Zhang regarded Cang Xu with a gaze of recognition: "You grasped this so quickly, clearly a sharp mind. Tell me, what else can you think of?"

Cang Xu paused, then a flicker of surprise crossed his eyes: "It is my Lord who chose me. With boundless magnanimity, She has made this necromancer a missionary to proclaim Her path, Her majesty, and Her mercy."

He suddenly realized: beyond high-level clergymen, necromancers were the most suited for this missionary divine spell.

On one hand, necromancers possessed profound soul foundations and innate spellcasting abilities. On the other hand, they long understood the truth behind spiritual energy, faith, and exploitation. Thus, the vast majority of necromancers developed a rebellious consciousness and refused to worship deities.

"Converting necromancers to faith is far more difficult. But as seen with Archbishop Zhou Zhang, once their faith takes root, it becomes exceptionally steadfast, able to withstand repeated trials!"

Seeing Cang Xu's expression of sudden understanding, Zhou Zhang laughed heartily and slapped his shoulder. "Whether these divine spells were refined by our Lord or by other believers, the fact that our Lord can present them as divine blessings signifies a major shift in our church's policy."

“Truthfully, there's nothing inherently wrong with collaborating with the undead faction.”

"Our Lord was gravely wounded and plunged into slumber. all by the gods of the Sheng Ming Empire! The deities of other factions stood idly by, offering no aid whatsoever."

"The great gods suppress the undead faction solely because it poses a grave threat to their own interests."

"Hmph! Under these circumstances, why shouldn't we recruit necromancers as believers? We're already in this predicament anyway."

Zhou Zhang muttered under her breath, her discontent palpable.

Cang Xu listened with trepidation.

Though his innate talent had improved somewhat, he remained a low level transcendent at heart. It was only after accompanying Jian Gui that he glimpsed the vistas of the mid-to-high level world. Even then, he was merely an iron level pretender masquerading as silver level.

Zhou Zhang wasn't just a saint level cultivator; she was high-ranking within the Charming Blue Church. Her casual remarks revealed the world's true structure, the secrets of its highest echelons!

After muttering for a moment, Zhou Zhang realized she'd spoken too much. She cut herself off and offered Cang Xu a few words of encouragement.

Cang Xu sensed she wanted to end the conversation and quickly asked, “Lord Zhou Zhang, may I freely preach here?”

Zhou Zhang smiled. “Of course you may.”

"As a missionary, spreading faith earns you divine favor. With such divine spells at your command, I trust divine favor will come easily."

“I too was once a missionary. Looking back now, if only I'd possessed such divine spells back then. it would have been splendid!”

"Successful proselytizing yields 1 unit of divine favor for bronze level, 10 for iron level, and a whopping 100 for silver level. Of course, this figure represents only an average. The actual amount of divine favor granted depends heavily on the faith levels before and after conversion."

“But regardless, this offers substantial rewards for a new clergyman.”

At this point, Zhou Zhang suddenly interjected, “I nearly forgot, I can remove the seal now.”

“Without the seal I placed, you'll be able to unleash the full power of your missionary divine spells.”

"Do you have soul crystals? Frequent use of the divine spell Synchronized Conversion will drain your soul. Therefore, it's best to have ample soul crystals for replenishment."

Cang Xu first beamed with delight, then shook his head with a wry smile. “I don't have many soul crystals on hand.”

Zhou Zhang pressed, “Can you craft soul crystals?”

Though Cang Xu had already obtained the intermediate soul aspect inheritance from Jian Gui, he shook his head without hesitation. “My contact with Jian Gui was specifically for that intermediate soul aspect inheritance. I haven't secured it yet.”

Zhou Zhang smiled encouragingly. “Then strive to spread our teachings. Convert those necromancers into our believers. Perhaps then, you might obtain the soul crystal creation method from Jian Gui. Who knows?”

A sharp gleam flashed in Cang Xu's eyes. “Lord Zhou Zhang, are you suggesting...”

Zhou Zhang had already turned to leave, tossing back one final remark: “Such an exceptional divine spell of proselytizing naturally requires more necromancers to wield it, wouldn't you agree?”

Following this incident, Zhou Zhang's attitude toward Jian Gui and other necromancers underwent a shift.

Cang Xu watched Zhou Zhang's departing figure and keenly sensed this change.

He hurried back to the area surrounding the golden altar, only to find that the crowd from earlier had largely dispersed over the intervening time.

The circle of believers surrounding the altar remained kneeling solely for prayer.

"My return has severely disrupted my former relationships. Zhou Zhang's relationship with me has developed perfectly, while Jian Gui's requires more maintenance."

Cang Xu sought to find Jian Gui, but at that moment, an unconscious voice warned him: returning to his own shack to rest was the wisest strategy.

Suppressing his urge to seek out Jian Gui, Cang Xu returned to his previous humble dwelling.

Closing the shack door, he appeared to pray while secretly activating his Memory Switching Spell!

Segments of memory previously concealed now resurfaced.

“So, I had hidden my true memories.”

“I fabricated numerous false memories, implanting them into key moments of my past life.”

“All to bolster my faith in the Charming Blue Goddess.”

"This approach did indeed elevate my devotion to that of a devout believer. It was precisely this foundation that allowed my missionary divine spells to manifest such potent effects before Zhou Zhang!"

These fabricated memories had significantly heightened Cang Xu's recognition and closeness toward the Charming Blue Goddess.

Now, as the true memories surfaced, they clashed with the fabricated ones.

Cang Xu immediately cast a Memory Concealment Spell, hiding each fabricated memory one by one.

"This brings my level of faith back to mediocrity. But this is the real me. Only in this state can I better face Jian Gui!"

Cang Xu now understood the origin of the subconscious promptings in his mind.

Before teleporting back, he had not only fabricated memories but also cast a Subconscious Reminder Spell, instructing himself to return to his shack after passing the trial.

He had set the Memory Switching Spell to activate upon reaching the shack's door.

All these were deliberate preparations and arrangements made before his return, now taking effect one by one.

Cang Xu silently analyzed the situation.

"To them, Ku Feng and Qing Xin are dead. Only a few dozen fishmen from the Da Ji Clan survived the teleportation back."

“But in reality, they're all aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

"Qing Xin and Ku Feng are under strict surveillance, with multiple layers of security ensuring their safety. As for the other fishmen, far weaker in power, their movements are also restricted and they are under strict guard."

"Most crucially, I have already imprinted the Missionary Mark upon these fishmen captives!"

The spells the fishman youngster was currently studying, besides Soul Crystal Creation, included Memory Concealment and Memory Switching.

His purpose in practicing these latter two was to manipulate the memories of the fishman captives. Just as he had done with Cang Xu, he would conceal parts of their true memories and implant fabricated ones, thereby converting them all into believers of the Charming Blue Goddess!

Once they became believers, the Missionary Mark would confirm Cang Xu's role in their conversion.

Through subsequent prayers, Cang Xu would gradually receive corresponding divine blessings as rewards.

"In a sense, we’re turning every fishman captive into a blasphemous priest."

"But these fishman captives remained utterly ignorant."

Using the memory fabrication device to aid his proselytizing, this was the ingenious plan Cang Xu conceived while at the Ocean Mother Nest.

During the capture of the fishmen, he repeatedly employed the Opportunistic Deception Spell to complement his efforts.

Cang Xu had a premonition: "The divine spells of proselytizing I've acquired, the Opportunistic Deception, the magic formation for the Deceptive Disguise spell, the memory crystal fabrication device... Combining these elements must yield synergistic effects. But time is limited, leaving me no chance to delve deeper."

“Now, it's time to meet Jian Gui.”

Undoubtedly, returning in this state, Cang Xu would need to earn Jian Gui's trust anew.

It was impossible for Jian Gui not to harbor suspicion toward him.

This was yet another trial.

But Cang Xu was brimming with confidence!

Chapter 720: Jian Gui – I am in Control of the Situation

“Lord Jian Gui.” When Cang Xu paid his respects to Jian Gui, he noticed both Qi Ling and Xiao Wang Zi were present in the shack.

This was entirely within Cang Xu's expectations.

In truth, this gathering of the undead had been specifically arranged to await Cang Xu's arrival.

“You finally came!” Qi Ling sneered coldly at Cang Xu, “You still dare to show your face?”

Cang Xu could easily imagine how, before his arrival, Qi Ling had been endlessly slandering him to Jian Gui, painting him as a threat.

Xiao Wang Zi's face also bore a look of wariness.

He turned to Cang Xu, his tone accusatory. “Ku Feng didn't teleport back. Is he dead?”

“You could save the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan. Why didn't you save him?”

Cang Xu sighed deeply, grief-stricken. “After receiving the divine gift once more, I was both shocked and overjoyed. Ku Feng was the first I tried to aid, but I failed.”

“He didn't follow my guidance. His faith was insufficient. He perished at the hands of the saint beast!”

Xiao Wang Zi fell silent.

A heavy silence settled over the hut, the air thick with suspicion.

Cang Xu turned to Xiao Wang Zi, delivering his prepared words: “Therefore, I refuse to let such a tragedy happen again.”

“Henceforth, I shall propose to Lord Zhou Zhang that I participate in every teleportation blood sacrifice.”

"Next, I will accompany you, Xiao Wang Zi. If you fail to convert, I will aid you within the Mother Nest, guiding you to renounce your faith, just as I helped those fishmen of the Da Ji Clan."

Xiao Wang Zi's face lit up with joy.

Suddenly, Qi Ling spoke: "Why did you help our fishmen? Did you not once hate them deeply? You even sent them to the teleportation blood sacrifice!"

“Yet suddenly, you aid them in the Mother Nest, helping your enemies survive.”

“Has faith altered your heart? Are you still one of us?”

Through covert channels, Qi Ling used sharp words to question Cang Xu's loyalty.

Cang Xu replied calmly, “If I had betrayed us, I would have long since revealed the escape plan with the fabricated memories to Lord Zhou Zhang.”

“But I did not do that, did I?”

'If you had, you would already be dead,' Jian Gui thought darkly.

Cang Xu looked at Qi Ling with a mocking smile. "Besides, I saved the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan and pulled you back from despair. Shouldn't you thank me?"

“Bullshit!” Qi Ling erupted in fury. “If not for you, why would my entire tribe have been forced into the teleportation blood sacrifice? Why would so many have died?”

“And you expect me to thank you? Do you take me for a fool?”

Cang Xu shook his head and sighed. “After this ordeal, I've vented all my anger.”

“Qi Ling, I've never considered you an enemy, believe it or not.”

"In any case, moving forward, I'll not only aid Xiao Wang Zi but also help you convert. Of course, Lord Jian Gui, you remain my primary focus."

“Ever since our first meeting, you've shown me kindness. Being able to offer you some small assistance in this situation is the least I can do.”

Qi Ling sneered coldly: “You really have been changed by the Charming Blue Goddess.”

“Now you're a missionary. Let me guess at how much divine favor will you gain for successful proselytizing?”

“What's the rate for silver level? Gold level must be even more, right?"

Cang Xu nodded. “That's correct. Naturally, I wish to gain as much divine favor as possible.”

“Divine favor is immensely beneficial to me!”

“I'm willing to help you all, and I also want to see everyone become believers. Isn't that a wonderful thing?”

“In fact, Lord Zhou Zhang also greatly desires to see everyone convert successfully. Just moments ago, she hinted at this to me.”

“Her attitude has shifted, it's different now.”

"The reason lies in the divine magic I recently acquired. Let me explain, and you'll understand."

After Cang Xu's explanation, Jian Gui and the others fell deep in thought.

“It seems the Charming Blue Goddess is gravely wounded, and the situation is dire. That's why She's willing to make necromancers, or magicians, Her believers!” Qi Ling remarked.

“Perhaps the Charming Blue Goddess recognized this very point, which is why She allowed you to pass the trials twice,” Xiao Wang Zi speculated, looking at Cang Xu.

Cang Xu continued his pitch: “Divine favor is immensely useful to us. We can request for specific divine blessings in our prayers."

"The situation for the Charming Blue Goddess and the Charming Blue Church is indeed dire, but precisely because of this, they are natural allies for our undead faction, wouldn't you agree?"

“To be honest, I don't think it's a bad thing for a necromancer to also be a believer of the Charming Blue Goddess!”

Xiao Wang Zi fell silent.

Qi Ling remained quiet too.

Jian Gui remained impassive, his expression betraying no inner turmoil.

But Cang Xu knew his words had struck a chord deep within the others.

Judging by the series of proselytizing divine spells, the prospects for conversion now seemed far greater than before.

Conversion offered tangible benefits! At the very least, successful conversion meant they could survive.

Qi Ling glared at Cang Xu: “So you're already one of them.”

Cang Xu shrugged, spreading his hands. “I'm both a Charming Blue believer and a necromancer!”

Qi Ling gritted her teeth in frustration. She hadn't expected Cang Xu to be so shameless, openly admitting his shift in allegiance.

This rendered her carefully prepared interrogation utterly pointless.

Qi Ling instinctively glanced at Jian Gui.

Jian Gui stared expressionlessly at Cang Xu.

Cang Xu's behavior hadn't surprised Jian Gui.

Even before this, Jian Gui had assessed Cang Xu, concluding he had deepened his faith in the Charming Blue Goddess. He was both a necromancer and Charming Blue believer.

His identity as a necromancer, along with shared interests and bonds of loyalty, still made him a viable partner for cooperation.

His newly embraced faith, however, hadn't yet grown strong enough to make him willing to expose the secret of the fabricated memories.

This situation was not uncommon.

The specific factors depended on the deity being worshipped. At the very least, the Charming Blue Goddess wasn't like the Light God, she wasn't the sworn enemy of the undead and could coexist with them.

Moreover, the current situation provided a foundation for cooperation between the two sides.

In truth, even believers of the same deity could become enemies. Take the beastman race's mightiest principal god, the Berserker God, as an example. He was the god of chaos, madness, and frenzied combat, championing frenzy, disorder, and melee.

His believers honored him with blood sacrifices, typically a massive brawl where they slaughtered each other until only one survivor remained.

The surviving believer would then receive the Berserker God's bountiful divine blessing.

Jian Gui noticed that Zhou Zhang had already removed the seal from Cang Xu.

"But it doesn't matter. The hidden trap I planted within him remains. The situation is still under my control."

With this understanding, Jian Gui accepted Cang Xu once more and continued assigning him the task of fabricating memories.

Simultaneously, Jian Gui sternly warned Cang Xu and Qi Ling, making it clear he did not wish to see conflict between them again.

Qi Ling and Cang Xu hastily agreed.

Snow Bird Harbor, the docks.

The retired warship of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom now served as the flagship of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In the flagship's forecastle, the big guy encountered Tripleblade.

He clutched his stomach in distress and pleaded, “Hungry... need food...”

Tripleblade looked troubled.

After communicating with Zi Di and Zong Ge, they'd decided to temporarily ration the big guy's food to prevent his strength from advancing too rapidly.

Following this measure, the big guy's total food allowance had instantly been cut in half.

His cries of hunger were perfectly understandable.

Tripleblade explained, “The corps' finances are tight right now, big guy. You can't keep eating like this, it's costing too much gold.”

“Let me break it down for you...”

Tripleblade spread his fingers wide, standing before the big guy as he detailed the precise calculations.

The big guy, of course, couldn't understand a word. He stared wide-eyed, his expression pure innocence and confusion.

After a lengthy explanation, Tripleblade shrugged regretfully. “So, for now, this is all the food you'll get...”

The big guy was furious, but unless pushed to the brink of life and death, where immense pressure drove him, he wouldn't resort to violence.

At heart, the big guy was timid.

So, faced with this outcome, he puckered his lips as if to protest, but ultimately only asked, “Father, Xiao Guai wants Father...”

Tripleblade sighed. "The commander is out on business. He won't be back for a long time. The big guy, you have to be good. If you're not, when the commander returns, I'll report it to him."

The big guy shrank back, his eyes brimming with hunger for food.

Just then, his stomach growled again.

“Xiao Guai is hungry...”

“Xiao Guai is hungry enough to eat a whole cow.”

“Xiao Guai wants to eat too. Give me one more bucket, please.”

At the thought of rice, drool began to drip from the big guy's mouth.

Tripleblade revealed the true purpose of his visit: “I can't give you more food, but I can make you feel less hungry!”

“The reason you get hungry easily and eat so much is because you're constantly cultivating.”

"Every time you fall asleep, you activate the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique. As long as you stop cultivating, you won't feel this hungry anymore."

The big guy shook his head at the suggestion: “No, help, Father, practice, hard!”

“Xiao Guai can practice, Father, happy.”

“Xiao Guai not practice, Father, not happy.”

Tripleblade tried to persuade him: “It's just to pause your practice. Once the mercenary corps has money, you can practice again.”

“The commander isn't back. If you pause your cultivation, I won't tell him this secret.”

The big guy shook his head repeatedly: “Xiao Guai, honest child. Deceiving Father, wrong!”

Tripleblade's face darkened as he threatened: “But if you keep cultivating like this, you might starve to death.”

“Starve... to death?” The big guy froze.

Tripleblade wore a solemn expression: “I don't want to ration your food, and I certainly don't want to halt your training.”

“But training like this, starving yourself like this... you'll starve to death easily!”

“If you starve to death, and the commander returns to find you dead... he'll be heartbroken.”

The big guy froze for a moment, his face clouding with sorrow before he burst into tears. “No... can't starve, death.”

“Starve to death... can't meet Father anymore...”

“Xiao Guai can't starve to death. Xiao Guai has to see Father!”

Chapter 721: Iron Level Big Guy

“The big guy is truly loyal to the commander,” Tripleblade mused upon witnessing this scene.

He seized the moment to persuade him, successfully swaying the big guy's resolve.

But getting the big guy to voluntarily halt his cultivation was impossible.

For the big guy only circulated the battle qi technique through bodily instinct while asleep. When asleep, he was unconscious and utterly incapable of halting the refinement of his battle qi.

But Tripleblade had prepared for this.

Upon the big guy agreeing to pause his cultivation, Tripleblade retrieved a vial of potion from his robe.

"Your ability to cultivate while sleeping stems from your body's muscle memory. By repeatedly interrupting this process, we can disrupt that muscle-like memory."

“Drink this, and I'll help you halt your cultivation.”

The big guy pinched his fingers, carefully took the vial, and tipped his head back to swallow the potion.

His face had twisted in anticipation of bitterness, but the liquid was surprisingly sweet and pleasant.

After finishing, he licked his lips, wanting more: “Any... more?”

He was starving, desperate for food, anything at all.

The tiny vial emptied, and his stomach growled again.

Tripleblade’s eye twitched. He sighed inwardly: This big guy needs more training, especially in safety awareness. He’s way too reckless! Poisoning him would be too easy. Though Tripleblade and the big guy were very familiar, he still had to guard against enemies disguising themselves as survivors to gain the big guy's trust.

Tripleblade waited for quite a while, but the big guy showed no signs of sleepiness.

Realizing the truth, Tripleblade decisively pulled out a second bottle.

Only after finishing this bottle did the big guy feel a heavy wave of sleepiness wash over him, and he soon fell into a deep, snoring slumber.

Not long after he fell asleep, an iron sheen began to appear on his skin.

Iron level battle qi!

Indeed, during this period, the big guy had advanced from bronze to iron level.

He mainly ate, drank, and slept. He used to go out to work, but after the survivors discovered his potential, they decisively protected him, rarely letting him venture out to the forecastle.

The big guy spent almost all day cooped up in his room. Every time he woke up from a nap, he felt hungry. After a big meal, he'd get sleepy and nap again.

In this cycle, his strength had broken through.

Among all the survivors, his cultivation was the most leisurely.

“Such bloodline, truly...” Tripleblade sighed inwardly, his face betraying deep envy.

“I'd already factored in his physical constitution, so each vial contained double the standard dose. Yet the big guy showed no reaction whatsoever.”

“It took two vials to finally send him into a coma.”

“That's a quadruple dose!”

“This kind of physical constitution...” Tripleblade marveled inwardly.

Even at bronze level, the big guy was already nearly impossible to subdue.

Tripleblade was merely an iron level. At the big guy's current vitality level, if he went berserk, Tripleblade would be powerless to subdue him.

That's why, during this period, Tripleblade had arranged for Men Shi and Chi Lai to guard the dock, ensuring at least one remained on the ship—all to prevent the big guy from losing control.

“But honestly, even a silver level fighter suppressing a berserk big guy wouldn't be entirely reliable.”

“With his physical prowess, the big guy could already match a silver level opponent.”

Tripleblade believed: Though the big guy was currently iron level and possessed no battle skills, his terrifying physical strength alone made him capable of challenging a silver level opponent.

Tripleblade took a deep breath, approached the big guy, and placed both hands on his belly.

He channeled his battle qi, and soon, his hands were coated with an iron sheen.

Tripleblade “attacked” the big guy, attempting to rouse him.

But his first “attack” elicited no reaction whatsoever.

“Seems my force is too weak,” Tripleblade thought, gradually increasing the pressure.

The second time, the third time...

Each time, Tripleblade carefully calibrated the increase in force. He didn't want to use too much force and injure the big guy.

Yet, in the end, Tripleblade's face was filled with disbelief.

"How is this possible?"

“I've used all my strength, and I still can't shake him? I can't even wake him up?”

Tripleblade was stunned speechless.

“Even without using battle skills, the big guy was completely defenseless... This is absurd!”

“What kind of bloodline does he have?”

With no other choice, Tripleblade resorted to using his weapons.

He carried three blades, his primary weapons, but naturally, he wouldn't use them.

He selected a staff from the ship's armory and returned to the forecastle.

This time, wielding the staff, he finally disrupted the big guy's deep breathing rhythm, interrupting the natural flow of his body's innate battle qi circulation.

“Finally succeeded!” Sweat beaded on Tripleblade's forehead.

The key was controlling the force.

But the next moment, the big guy's breathing rhythm adjusted back, resuming his cultivation of the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique.

“Then again!” Tripleblade swung the staff relentlessly, striking the big guy's belly.

Thud. Thud. Thud.

The big guy's belly was sunken from eating little. Even so, each strike against his skin produced a deep, dull thud.

Tripleblade felt as if he were striking a piece of tanned, premium cowhide with his wooden staff.

As he continued swinging the staff, Tripleblade gradually increased his force.

Yet each time the big guy's practice was interrupted, he swiftly readjusted and resumed his cultivation.

“His physical instincts are too powerful. Once he memorized the breathing rhythm, external force proved so difficult to disrupt or alter!”

“This is also a manifestation of his superior bloodline.”

“Then I'll go all out!”

Tripleblade gritted his teeth, suddenly increasing his force, the stick in his hand striking the big man heavily.

This blow carried unprecedented power.

The big guy opened his eyes in pain. Still half-asleep, he glanced at Tripleblade before shifting slightly from his prone position to lie on his side.

Snore... snore... snore...

He began snoring again, sinking back into a deep slumber.

Tripleblade's small frame, clutching the massive staff, seemed feeble and powerless against the big guy's broad, towering form.

Soon, the big guy's battle qi circulation restarted, and he resumed his cultivation.

“I used a silver level weapon!” Tripleblade inspected the spot he had struck.

A dark blue-black club mark appeared on the big guy's belly.

But this mark was fading at a visible speed.

“Such recovery ability!” Tripleblade's heart trembled violently once more.

He stared at the sleeping big guy, motionless for a long time.

"Given time, his strength will surpass mine. No, his combat prowess already exceeds mine now."

“He'll leave me far behind, racing ahead unchallenged—silver level, gold level, saint level, even...”

“Who would've thought among the survivors, the weak big guy would be the one destined for greatness?”

"Why him? He's so dull-witted..."

“Bloodline...”

Tripleblade smiled bitterly.

He'd heard countless tales of prodigies with exceptional talent and potent bloodlines reaching heights in mere moments that most could never achieve in a lifetime.

Yet Tripleblade now felt a profound shock from witnessing it firsthand in the big guy.

Because this was happening right before his eyes.

He had personally witnessed the big guy's rise! He had also glimpsed the towering stature the big guy would one day command.

Suddenly, everything felt hollow. Tripleblade couldn't bring himself to lift the staff in his hand.

Interrupting the big guy's training now seemed utterly pointless.

His expression was heavy with disappointment, Tripleblade closed the door and left the forecastle.

The forecastle was guarded by alchemy puppets, providing a measure of security for the big guy.

Only when his subordinate reminded him did Tripleblade snap back to reality.

“Ah...” He sighed deeply, his thoughts drifting to Zong Ge.

“Even if the big guy becomes a great figure in the future, I can only look up to him. What good is that?”

“My bloodline is weak, my talent lacking, but I follow Lord Zong Ge!”

“At Lord Zong Ge's age, he's already a gold level. His bloodline is undoubtedly strong!”

“As long as Lord Zong Ge achieves greatness with me by his side, I'll be content.”

On Mystifying Monster Island, Tripleblade discovered many shared traits with the half-beastman Zong Ge. Yet he could find none of these parallels in the youth who was then merely “Zhen Jin”.

In the developments that followed, Tripleblade pinned his life's dreams upon Zong Ge.

Through Zong Ge, Tripleblade finally calmed his turbulent emotions.

Tripleblade began to assess the situation before him.

After the recent incident, he no longer wished to interrupt the big guy's transcendent cultivation.

Though he knew other methods remained available, he had genuinely abandoned that plan.

For the time being, deep down, Tripleblade didn't wish to see the big guy again. Tripleblade wasn't a saint. He possessed a certain capacity for tolerance, but the previous events had dealt him a crushing blow.

He instinctively pushed the big guy from his mind, unwilling to confront him.

“By now, the messenger should have reached Lord Zong Ge.”

“But it will take some time before we receive their reply.”

As Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their party grew increasingly distant, communication with the Snow Bird Harbor branch became more difficult.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps employed messengers to deliver encrypted letters, transmitting them via teleportation formations still required time.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom had teleportation formations installed in its cities and major towns.

The setup of teleportation formations also had limitations; it was impossible to teleport directly from Snow Bird Harbor to the Ice Sculpture Royal Capital.

On one hand, this posed technical and cost challenges. On the other, it was deliberately designed by the royal family for security reasons.

The kingdom required strategic depth, especially when facing threats.

If every town could teleport directly to the capital, the capital would inevitably become the most vulnerable location.

Therefore, messengers had to teleport from one city to another, repeating the process multiple times to catch up with Zong Ge, Zi Di, and their group.

This process itself presented complications.

Messengers had to queue.

The teleportation formations in every town were exceedingly busy.

Teleportation slots were strictly limited.

At Snow Bird Harbor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps enjoyed privileged access due to their close ties with the city lord, allowing them to jump the queue. In other cities, however, they received no such favor.

Chapter 722: Rime City & Igloo City

Deep within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom lay Rime City.

Inn 521.

The messenger, looking thoroughly worn from travel, paid his respects to Zi Di and Zong Ge: "I finally caught up with you. Urgent intelligence!"

The messenger's journey had taken even longer than Tripleblade had estimated.

The intelligence was encoded within a cryptographic letter, and the courier himself was unaware of its contents.

This encryption method was a standard practice among mercenary groups. It could be deciphered, but it demanded significant time and manpower.

Of course, this wasn't the only layer of encryption.

Zi Di retrieved a pair of alchemy spectacles and donned them. The text within the letter was transformed once more.

This was another layer of encryption, one far more reliable.

The reason was that the design blueprint for this compact alchemy device originated from the War Merchant's database.

Its inclusion in the War Merchant's archives meant it possessed unique merits.

“Could this be news about the commander?” Zi Di felt a surge of hope, but as the decryption was completed, she saw the true content of the intelligence.

Disappointment washed over her, followed by a sudden tightening of her expression as she grasped the gravity of the situation.

She scanned the decrypted intelligence a second time before handing it to Zong Ge beside her.

Zong Ge took it, read through it, and immediately furrowed his brow.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' caravan, despite being under the protection of the Axe Gang, had been attacked.

Two gold level fighters had appeared, and a fierce battle had ensued.

Clearly, the enemy had struck!

What kind of force was this? It was far too powerful! They had dispatched gold level fighters once again! Moreover, the one who arrived later might also be one.

This was definitely not an ordinary gold level force.

Groups like the Bayonet Gang were typical gold level forces, possessing one or two gold level fighters.

But this group? First they took out Teng Dong Lang, then three more fell in one go, and they could immediately deploy another gold level, maybe even two!

“Is this an alliance formed between gold level factions?” Zong Ge speculated inwardly.

Earlier, he'd guessed that this alliance likely had military connections. But now, Qing Hong Yan's appearance had thrown Zong Ge's suspicions into disarray.

Even rabbitmen had appeared. This organization's membership was far too broad. The combat power they had revealed alone was deeply unsettling.

How much more strength lay hidden beneath the surface?

“They're tough to deal with!”

“Not only do they vastly outmatch us in strength, but they've also responded correctly.”

"We traveled from Snow Rabbit City to Rime City, a considerable distance, yet we faced no attacks. Not even a single probe."

“Until they understand our group's methods, I doubt they'll attack again.”

Zong Ge's analysis earned a slight nod of agreement from Zi Di.

To be fair, Zong Ge's feat of defeating three opponents single-handedly was indeed formidable. The battlefield bore few traces, their clash erupting with minimal light or sound.

Zong Ge's swift victory had caused the enemy to severely overestimate their combat strength.

The foe hesitated yet did not cease their pursuit. Instead, they adopted the correct tactic - attacking the enemy's weak points!

Zi Di now felt profoundly distressed.

The commercial venture she had designed and spearheaded targeted the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom market.

The massive cash haul seized by the survivors on Two Eyes Island formed its foundational strength. The assessments of the situation by the young commander, Zi Di, and others, along with considerations for future retreat routes, had led Zi Di to decisively execute this plan.

Originally, she believed: the alliance with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had made Snow Bird Harbor the best starting point for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Collaborating with the Li Jian Clan had paved the way, giving them a powerful guide.

Unexpectedly, the actual execution would encounter such immense difficulties.

“The enemy is too powerful!”

“Even if Father were still alive, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance at its peak wouldn't dare provoke such a force.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is still weak. We haven't fully developed yet, and now we've made mortal enemies with this power.”

“Ah, where are you, commander?”

Zi Di frowned deeply, weighed down by immense pressure.

“Fortunately, we still have one genuine gold level member here.”

Zi Di shot a subtle glance at Zong Ge.

She believed that in this situation, Zong Ge advancing to gold level would undoubtedly bring more benefits than drawbacks.

“But we can't rely solely on Zong Ge...”

Zong Ge shared Zi Di's assessment. “Even if I reach gold level, I alone could never take on such a massive hostile force.”

“The most critical point is this: the enemy organization lurks in the shadows, revealing nothing. That prevents us from launching a preemptive strike.”

So far, the survivors only knew that the Bayonet Gang, the Axe Gang, and the exposed rabbitman Qing Hong Yan were all members of hostile factions. As for Gu Dong, it couldn't be ruled out that he was acting alongside Qing Hong Yan; suspicion remained.

The survivors didn't even know the names of the enemy factions!

Well as a matter of fact, someone within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did know.

That person was Lan Zao.

The young man had followed the remnants of the Bayonet Gang into Tranquil Hill, effectively entering the enemy's stronghold.

He had even seen the tombstones at the summit of Tranquil Hill, recognizing many of the names inscribed there.

Among those names were enemies who had attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

But Lan Zao remained unaware that the two sides were adversaries currently locked in conflict.

Even if he did know, he had no way to relay this invaluable intelligence back.

Zong Ge first sent the messenger to rest, then discussed the situation with Zi Di in a grave tone: “The situation is extremely unfavorable for us.”

“We're completely on the defensive.”

“Even if we wanted to attack, we couldn't find a target.”

"Conversely, since the enemy adopted a strategy of attacking our weak points and succeeded the first time. Even if we suffered few tangible losses, the fact remains exposed, our defenses in this area are inadequate."

“Next, the enemy will undoubtedly target our weaknesses and strike relentlessly.”

“Though we've secretly partnered with the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance, as long as our potions continue selling across the kingdom, the enemy will soon discover and intensify their attacks!”

Zi Di paced for a moment, her tone resolute: “We cannot halt our commercial plan.”

She understood all too well: the stakes were too high this time. Partnerships had already been forged with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Li Jian Clan.
