Terminating it now would mean abandoning all prior efforts and investments, all of it would go down the drain.

The resulting losses would be too great, leaving the survivors severely weakened.

Crucially, there was also the cost of time!

Regardless of the outcome of the redemption plan, strengthening the survivors as much as possible was undoubtedly the right course.

But halting this business venture would disrupt their development rhythm, causing delays that would drag down too many.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' primary funds were invested in this commercial plan.

This venture was Zi Di's initiative.

Should it fail, even with her unshakable bond to the dragonman youngster, she felt the loss would be too great.

This wasn't just her personal stake; it was the collective's capital.

Success in redemption would be one thing, but failure...

In short, she couldn't betray her comrades' trust nor bear such immense responsibility.

After rapid deliberation, Zi Di declared with resolve: “We cannot possibly overcome such a force alone. We absolutely must bring in external support.”

“The Li Jian Clan is our top choice.”

“We already have an existing partnership with them. Now is the perfect time to deepen that collaboration!”

Zong Ge pondered: "The Li Jian Clan ranks among the top three great families of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. But even so, their combat strength level can't match our enemy's."

“None of the kingdom's three saints belong to the Li Jian Clan.”

“They might not agree to deepen our cooperation.”

Zi Di gave a cold laugh, her voice tinged with confidence: “They absolutely will!”

“Many deals fall through simply because the price isn't high enough.”

Zong Ge fixed Zi Di with a deep gaze, sensing her conviction. Trusting her business acumen, he swiftly concluded this was highly feasible.

He immediately revised their itinerary: “Next, we head to the Li Jian Clan's Igloo City!”

“Additionally, we must reinforce the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch's defenses.”

“The enemy has no shortage of gold level fighters. Personal ambushes are the most likely tactic. They won't launch a full-scale assault on Snow Bird Harbor.”

Zong Ge made a series of military assessments.

The hostile organization had always concealed its strength, never making any major moves.

Even Qing Hong Yan, who attacked the caravans, was a rabbitman.

Rabbitmen were a minority within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Zong Ge estimated that even if they followed this lead, tracing it back to its source would yield little evidence.

Even if they attacked the rabbitman race, they wouldn't directly confront the main culprits.

Without locating the enemy's main force, only knowing of their flanking units, Zong Ge dared not launch a rash attack - this could very well be the enemy's bait!

Zi Di said, "The area around Bu Quan is also extremely dangerous right now! Our alchemy workshop where we brew potions is the lifeblood of our business plan. If it gets destroyed, the consequences would be unimaginable."

Snow Bird Harbor currently served as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' headquarters.

Yet it remains a critical weak point, as the troops stationed there are practically useless.

Unless a gold level guardian were stationed there.

The most prominent gold level figure in Snow Bird Harbor with ties to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord himself. But surely they couldn't ask him to guard the workshop?

It wasn't impossible, but the cost would be too high, too much exposure, too great a risk.

Zi Di continued, “The branch's defenses are extremely weak.”

“To safeguard our business plan, we must prepare contingency measures. Should the alchemy workshop be destroyed, we must have a backup.”

“This trip to Igloo City to negotiate with the Li Jian Clan might secure them as subcontractors.”

“Along our route, we can also scout uninhabited areas and covertly establish alchemy factories.”

Zi Di was resolute.

She understood clearly there was no turning back. The survivors had invested too heavily in this endeavor; the sunk costs were too great.

They had no choice but to press forward.

Of course, this wasn't mere stubbornness, nor was it blind denial of reality. It certainly wasn't the reckless gambler who, having lost everything, bet everything in a desperate, irrational frenzy. Rather, Zi Di saw that the situation still held potential.

“Deepening our partnership with the Li Jian Clan could alleviate the current dire situation.”

“But this approach carries hidden risks,” Zi Di sighed inwardly.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the weaker party, while the Li Jian Clan held the upper hand. Strengthening the alliance risked the weaker side being overtaken by the stronger.

Future gains would inevitably shrink significantly.

This was precisely why Zi Di had avoided such a partnership before.

After all, such a formidable hostile force hadn't yet emerged back then. Zi Di had believed the survivors' situation was highly favorable at the time.

“We must impose stricter limitations on the cooperation agreement.”

“Furthermore, since the enemy is likely to attack the alchemy workshop, we must also ensure Bu Quan's safety.”

“She's a rare talent!”

"I recall Bu Quan was highly regarded by her mentor at the Alchemy Guild. Could we leverage that connection to enlist her mentor's support?"

Zong Ge nodded. “It's worth a try.”

The enemy's strength was overwhelming; any external aid was worth considering.

Zi Di: "We outsiders know little of her relationship with her mentor. We should write to Bu Quan, explain the situation, urge her to stay vigilant, and ask her mentor to intervene if possible."

Zong Ge: "The enemy is too formidable. This time, we can enlist the Li Jian Clan to make the report through them. If we can mobilize the official forces of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom against the enemy, our situation will improve significantly."

After finalizing their plan, Zi Di hurried out of the inn and headed for the alchemist's shop in Rime City.

She intended to arrange personnel and consolidate the commercial hub in Rime City today, then depart for Igloo City tomorrow.

Chapter 723: Taste Testing

Three days later, the messenger returned to Snow Bird Harbor.

Walking along the somewhat familiar streets, the messenger clearly sensed something unusual about the crowd.

Midway, he encountered other members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. After exchanging information, he learned that Tripleblade and a group of mercenaries were not at the docks, but at Snow Bird Square.

The messenger had been dispatched by Tripleblade, and Tripleblade was precisely the person he needed to report to.

Without hesitation, the messenger hurried straight to Snow Bird Square.

The closer he got to the square, the denser the crowd became. Upon reaching the square, he found the area teeming with people, shoulder-to-shoulder in some spots.

The messenger initially assumed Snow Bird Harbor was hosting some kind of festival celebration.

But upon arrival, he saw a massive banner announcing it was actually a tasting banquet hosted by Ci Hai Shen.

Ci Hai Shen was a gold level magic chef, renowned throughout the culinary world. He had been invited to showcase his extraordinary skills at the capital's national banquet.

This time, he had chosen Snow Bird Harbor as his landing point in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Hosting the tasting banquet at Snow Bird Square served as both a warm-up for the national banquet and a test of the kingdom's citizens' palates.

The messenger encountered Tripleblade.

Tripleblade had been awaiting replies from Zi Di and Zong Ge: “You're finally back.”

After inquiring about the situation and learning no issues had arisen with Zi Di and Zong Ge, Tripleblade breathed a sigh of relief.

Taking the secret letter, he told the messenger, "You've traveled far. Go enjoy some magic cuisine. Try a variety, such tasting opportunities are rare. Earn some money for our mercenary corps while you're at it."

Tripleblade took the letter to a secluded corner to examine its contents.

The messenger headed toward the square, blending into the crowd until he reached its center.

He saw that the once-empty square now held three rows of long tables.

Each row was surrounded by a canopy.

The wooden tables were laden with magic dishes. It was a riot of colors, with each dish releasing an enticing aroma.

The three rows displayed bronze level, iron level, and silver level transcendent dishes respectively.

Several announcers stood atop makeshift wooden towers, maintaining order while loudly promoting the event.

Through their announcements, the messenger grasped the tasting event's fundamental rules.

Rule 1: Mortals were strictly forbidden from participating in the tasting.

The reason was simple.

These transcendent dishes were not meant for mortal consumption. To ordinary people, they were more terrifying than poison.

Rule 2: Transcendents had to queue for tasting according to their respective levels. Disturbances were forbidden; violators would be detained by the city guard.

A large contingent of city guards flanked the event, clearly indicating that such a significant gathering had been prearranged with Snow Bird Harbor authorities.

Moreover, even the mage tower of Snow Bird Harbor was operating at full capacity, focusing most of its attention here and conducting real-time surveillance. Even if troublemakers went undetected during the event, subsequent investigations into the magic footage recorded by the mage tower would inevitably reveal the perpetrators, enabling their apprehension.

Rule 3: If a transcendent met the standards set by Lord Ci Hai Shen during their tasting, they would earn additional rewards Naturally, basic tastings would also yield basic rewards.

Rule 4: Transcendent individuals were advised to act within their limits. Should physical discomfort arise, the Snow Bird Sect had organized a team of clergy to administer divine healing on the spot. However, the fee for such assistance must be paid by the recipient afterward.

The messenger listened and observed, finding it quite intriguing. He decisively followed the flow of people, slowly making his way toward the long tables.

There, organizers had divided the crowd into several lines.

Each line stretched quite long.

The messenger joined one and didn't wait long before entering the long pavilion.

The tables before him were laden with dishes of iron level.

He picked up a small plate holding three slices of sashimi.

Made from what seemed like tuna, the orange-red slices were cut thick and covered with a layer of white frost.

Seeing the label on the long table, the messenger realized the dish was called Spring Buds.

“Why would a simple sashimi dish be named that?”

“Is this the style of the Magic MasterChef?”

“I'll have to give it a proper taste.”

The messenger pinched a fish slice between his fingers, craned his neck, opened his mouth wide, stuck out his tongue, and slid the fish slice inside with his hand.

He immediately closed his mouth and began chewing steadily.

Just then, the orderly shouted at him, “Eat while you walk!”

The messenger started pacing forward again.

After just two steps, he shuddered violently, his entire body shaking with cold.

An intense chill surged from the depths of his being, racing straight to the crown of his head.

This icy sensation was refreshing, not the kind of bone-chilling cold that inflicts slight suffering before delivering an unprecedented culinary experience.

“But I can't taste anything...”

The thought barely formed in the messenger's mind when the food's true flavor exploded on his tongue.

Fresh!

So incredibly fresh!

A powerful burst of savory goodness assaulted his taste buds, making his eyes widen in an instant, his mind momentarily blank.

If the initial chill resembled winter snow, then this freshness felt like grass sprouting beneath the ice, pushing through the snow.

It was precisely the piercing cold that made the subsequent flavor all the more vivid.

In that instant, the messenger was utterly captivated by Ci Hai Shen's culinary artistry.

“Truly worthy of the title Magic MasterChef!”

“This is the taste of spring.”

“I feel spring has arrived, everything is sprouting...”

Those tasting the dish around him murmured in awe and praise.

One man devoured three small plates in succession. When he opened his mouth, he gasped in horror, actual sprouts had grown on his tongue!

Tiny, elongated flesh buds danced across his tongue, reaching out into the surrounding space, craving more delicacies.

The messenger's heart skipped a beat at this sight.

The transcendent individual with tongue sprouts was immediately pulled from the queue and sent to the clergy's line for treatment.

Though transcendent cuisine was tempting, it must never be overindulged.

Ci Hai Shen stood in a building on the plaza's periphery, quietly observing the scene at its center.

His vantage point was high, with expansive floor-to-ceiling windows offering a sweeping view.

“That's the third person with a growth on their tongue.”

“Did I add too much of the activating agent to the ingredients?”

"No, it must be the result of me using a gold level cooking tool. It seems that after applying the activating agent, I can use the Preservation Knife."

The misfortune of the man with tongue growths made the messenger cautious.

He ate only one Spring Buds dish, then carefully selected two other dishes.

The three dishes offered him a unique culinary experience, leaving a deep impression.

He wanted to try other dishes, there were at least ten varieties lined the long table. But the messenger couldn't indulge.

The magic dishes had infused him with abundant energy, surging and spreading through his body. He needed to cultivate immediately to digest this foreign energy, rather than indulge his appetite.

Thus, when Tripleblade returned to the square, he just caught sight of the messenger withdrawing.

“How much did you earn?” Tripleblade asked.

“30”

The messenger replied, “I ate three dishes and got paid 30 silver coins.”

Tripleblade waved his hand. "Go digest it quickly. You can come back for more. Remember, the more you eat, the more you earn."

“If you can meet some of the tasting requirements...”

At this point, Tripleblade sighed and changed his mind. “Never mind, I won't put you through that.”

The messenger departed, and Tripleblade sighed again.

Watching the bustling crowd before him, the weight on his shoulders lifted by at least half.

Having read the secret letter, Tripleblade knew Zong Ge, Zi Di, and the others had devised a countermeasure.

With solutions and hope emerging for the current crisis, the pressure on Tripleblade's side naturally eased.

He had already dispatched messengers to relay key details of the secret letter to Bu Quan.

“Hopefully, Bu Quan can leverage the Alchemy Guild's strength to aid the mercenary group.”

“Additionally, all alchemy workshops must bolster their defenses... No, more accurately, they must prepare contingency measures to resume production swiftly after any destruction.”

Tripleblade understood that the branch's defenses were far too weak. If the enemy sent a gold level powerhouse to launch a surprise attack and sabotage, there would be no way to stop them.

He had considered seeking aid directly from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, but the cost would be too great.

For now, they would have to wait for support from the Li Jian Clan and the Alchemy Guild.

Tripleblade knew his own capabilities were limited, but he was doing everything in his power to contribute as much as possible.

“Oh, and the big guy.”

Over these past three days, Tripleblade had adjusted his mindset.

Mainly because the big guy was starving daily, his eyes dark and glazed over, constantly moaning “hungry, hungry, hungry” in a pitiful state.

After examining his condition, Tripleblade had increased his food rations.

He had considered that perhaps he could provide the big guy with as much food as possible.

Despite the big guy's occasional bouts of madness, if he reached gold level, he would undoubtedly bring tremendous assistance to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

What the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed most right now was high-level combat power!

Typically, a transcendent's strength builds up gradually through accumulation, not through sudden mutation. But the big guy's bloodline was simply too exceptional. His growth was rapid, progressing visibly day by day.

“Maybe we really could push for a breakthrough?”

“In fact, even if he only reached silver level, his monstrous physical prowess could still hold his own against gold level opponents. Though replicating the commander's feat, defeating gold level opponents as a silver level, might be unrealistic for the big guy, he should be capable of holding his own against them head-on.”

Tripleblade regained his composure. Faced with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' current predicament, he would explore every possible solution.

His attitude toward the big guy had shifted.

Between them, there had always been a bond of comradeship.

Earlier, Tripleblade's outburst stemmed entirely from the big guy's devastating blow, a shock he couldn't process immediately.

“Should we really go through with this?”

“Can we truly rely on the big guy?”

Compared to the young commander and Zong Ge, the big guy was undeniably unreliable.

Yet his bloodline demanded respect!

Chapter 724: Thunder Black Pepper Sauce

Tripleblade still couldn't make up his mind.

"The big guy's potential risks are too great. If we cultivate him and he ends up going mad... before the enemy destroys us, we’ll be destroyed by our own people. That would be a complete joke."

"Besides, can our finances sustain it? Currently, though most training has halted, the business plans are still ongoing."

“The resources I can mobilize, would they be enough to push the big guy's transcendent level to gold level, or silver level?”

"And how much time would it take? Damn it, we need professionals to calculate the answer."

From the letter, Tripleblade learned of Zi Di's intentions: she remained determined to push forward with her business plans, her stance unwavering.

As the branch head, Tripleblade prioritized fallback plans - should the business scheme fail, he'd have to cover the losses. His concerns differed from Zi Di's.

“Sigh. Regardless, even if we don't force his transcendent level advancement, the big guy's food intake must still be gradually increased.”

Tripleblade had no intention of halting the big guy's cultivation.

But as the big guy grew stronger, his efficiency in refining battle qi increased, naturally consuming more food.

If taking a conservative approach, Tripleblade would provide just enough food to keep the big guy alive and prevent starvation, and this would weaken his combat strength to avoid him going berserk and causing destruction.

Tripleblade remained undecided on how to handle the big guy.

"Honestly, it all boils down to my lack of resources! I need to keep raking in the cash."

“But right now, the money from mercenary quests alone is nowhere near enough.”

"Every day, just buying the raw materials for potion-making costs our corps a fortune. The rewards from mercenary contracts are like a drop in the bucket compared to that."

“If the commander and deputy commander could take on tasks, our income in this area would increase significantly.”

"But the problem is, they both have more important matters to attend to. The only capable individuals at this branch are Chi Lai and Men Shi, both silver levels."

"Crucially, one of them must remain stationed on the flagship."

"We still lack high-tier core members. If Qing Xin could have joined us back then, that would have been ideal."

"Even though they're both silver level, magicians are far more sought-after than fighters. Magicians are just too practical; they have far more versatile methods!"

Thinking alone wouldn't solve anything.

Tripleblade pushed aside his worries and focused on the immediate situation.

“What's the current status?” he asked his subordinates.

For this tasting event, Tripleblade had deliberately selected a group of mercenaries to bring along. His primary goal, of course, wasn't entertainment, it was to raise funds.

To host this tasting event, Ci Hai Shen not only offered free food and drink but also paid participants!

Those that met specific criteria received even greater rewards.

Such opportunities were exceedingly rare.

Thus, the moment he heard about it, Tripleblade’s eyes lit up.

“Only made 62 gold coins and over 300 silver coins...”

After actually participating, he found the earnings were far less than he'd imagined. Some mercenaries had even gotten injured from overdoing it or overeating and were now receiving divine healing from the Snow Bird Church. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would have to cover those medical costs!

Tripleblade's gaze fixed on Men Shi.

“How much we earn depends on you now, Men Shi!”

The dwarf Men Shi persisted.

He walked along the long table, carefully selecting magic dishes of silver level.

After much deliberation and circling the table twice, he finally made his choice.

He didn't pick a main course, but a serving of Thunder Black Pepper Sauce.

This sauce was meant to accompany the steak main course, but Men Shi had already eaten his fill. His remaining stomach space was only enough for the sauce.

There was another crucial reason behind his final choice.

Regarding this sauce, Ci Hai Shen had set a challenge - swallowing a full spoonful in one go would yield a major reward.

“Thunder Black Pepper Sauce...” Men Shi picked up the ladle, scooped a heaping spoonful, and placed it in his mouth.

The moment it touched his tongue, the intense aroma of black pepper flooded his senses, filling his entire mouth.

The next instant, Men Shi's eyes widened in shock.

Following the pepper's fragrance, a genuine electric current exploded in his mouth, instantly surging through his entire body.

Men Shi's hair stood on end, his muscles twitched uncontrollably, and he was completely immobilized.

As a silver level individual, even the order maintainers present dared not reprimand him at his level, allowing him to walk while eating.

It took Men Shi a long while to recover, his face still etched with astonishment. “This Thunder Black Pepper Sauce packs a punch!”

His reaction had already drawn the attention of many in the crowd.

Seeing him steady himself, staff immediately approached to ask detailed questions about his experience tasting the sauce.

Men Shi struggled to respond, but the aftereffects of the electric current lingered, causing his tongue to stumble and his speech to be slurred.

Tripleblade watched, his heart sinking. “Dwarves typically possess higher elemental resistance than other races. It seems Men Shi's condition is no longer sufficient to carry him through!”

Men Shi's answers were swiftly relayed to Ci Hai Shen.

Ci Hai Shen nodded and ordered, “Give this dwarf his due reward. He met the standard.”

"Revise the upcoming criteria to exclude dwarves from tasting the Thunder Black Pepper Sauce. If an elf or human taste tester succeeds, their reward will be increased by 10%."

After all, the majority of participants in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's national banquet hailed from these two intelligent races.

Ci Hai Shen's orders were swiftly executed, and the sign beside the Thunder Black Pepper Sauce was promptly revised.

Such high stake tasting bounties fluctuated over time until the organizers withdrew them.

In other words, as time passed, the opportunities for extraordinary individuals to earn rewards steadily diminished.

“But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has reached its limit this time,” Tripleblade observed regretfully as Men Shi withdrew from the queue.

The dwarf was already stuffed and needed time to digest.

Tripleblade felt reluctant. For someone like him, whose business acumen was scarce, such tasting opportunities were too rare.

“What if we had the big guy come for the tasting?”

The thought barely formed in Tripleblade's mind before he dismissed it.

The big guy possessed at least saint level potential. He wasn't just the young commander's prized asset but also the survivors' most valuable treasure.

Such a treasure naturally needed to be kept hidden, guarded closely, never revealed casually.

Tasting these transcendent dishes was a clear indicator of a transcendent's strength.

If the big guy came to sample them, revealing his whereabouts, he would undoubtedly perform exceptionally well, drawing curiosity, speculation, and even spying.

Once his potential was confirmed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would face endless trouble.

Consider Bing Xiao of the Bayonet Gang, he had a gold level giant slave.

By then, even the Ice Sculpture Royal Family would take interest in a giant who hadn't fully matured, lacked intelligence, and hadn't reached the saint level!

Especially now, when the Ice Sculpture Kingdom lacked any legend level combat power, its strongest being merely three saint level individuals.

For the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, gaining one more saint level figure held immense significance.

This was the world of the strong.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's declining international standing over the years stemmed precisely from its lack of a legend level power and only three saint level figures.

Adding one more could significantly elevate the entire kingdom's global standing. Naturally, the potential profits were staggering!

Tripleblade had vast experience and understood one truth deeply: never underestimate others' greed!

Should the Ice Sculpture Royal Family uncover the big guy's secret, they would likely intervene directly, resorting to threats, bribes, or even other underhanded tactics.

At that point, the survivors' predicament would become far more dire than it was now!

“Hiding the big guy until he matures is the safest course of action,” Tripleblade sighed.

This was precisely why Tripleblade no longer sent other mercenaries to deliver meals to the big guy, opting instead for alchemy puppets.

Tripleblade also harbored deep concerns about the big guy losing control.

On one hand, he feared the destruction he might cause; on the other, he worried the big guy might reveal his presence, alerting the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Snow Bird Harbor wasn't like Snake Rat Island or Two Eyes Island; its docks were guarded by a mage tower.

That tower could capture magic images, and its power had been demonstrated during the Bayonet Gang's escape battle.

After that conflict, the Snow Bird Harbor Lord had immediately uncovered Lan Zao's shady dealings and invited the young commander to collaborate.

Tripleblade also knew the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was backed by an extremely powerful intelligence network.

Under these circumstances, how could he possibly feel secure exposing the big guy so carelessly?

“Everyone else is at their limit.”

“Looks like I'll have to step in myself...” Tripleblade sighed.

He was only iron level himself, unable to handle silver level magic dishes, so his earnings were limited.

But something was better than nothing.

Just as Tripleblade rolled up his sleeves to enter the fray himself, a booming voice suddenly echoed out, drowning the square's clamor.

“Disgusting. Disgusting.”

“Just by the smell, you can tell it's disgusting!”

“Ci Hai Shen, you haven't improved one bit.”

The roaring voice rolled like thunder, making many ears ring.

Countless eyes followed the sound, and in the next instant, the crowd beheld a towering figure over three meters tall.

It was a giant.

His skin glowed fiery red, while his flowing hair was golden.

On the giant's back rested an enormous wok, so massive it resembled a turtle shell strapped to his shoulders.

Flaming patterns danced across both arms, pulsing with brilliant light with each breath.

The giant openly taunted the chef, his intent dripping with malice. Yet no one in the square moved to challenge him.

The reason was simple: the giant radiated a gold level aura. He was a gold level fighter!

“Da Dou!” Ci Hai Shen was forced to reveal himself, his expression dark.

"Your mouth is still as foul as ever. Seems the lesson I taught you before wasn't enough. Now that the wounds on your arms have healed, you've started acting out of line again." Ci Hai Shen spoke, immediately exposing the giant's identity.

Chapter 725: Food Eating Competition

This giant was none other than the famous magic chef whose fame rivaled Ci Hai Shen's - Da Dou.

Spotting Ci Hai Shen, Da Dou roared with laughter. “Ci Hai Shen! Last time you schemed and plotted, nearly destroying my arms!”

“But misfortune turned to fortune, and my strength grew even greater.”

Da Dou touched the flame patterns on his arms. “This time, I'm here to settle the score!”

Ci Hai Shen's expression darkened further.

It was indeed his deliberate scheme that crippled Da Dou's arms. Unexpectedly, upon returning home, Da Dou's master had sacrificed his own arms to heal the injuries, granting him the totem tattoo of the beastman race's Fire God.

After mastering the totem tattoo, Da Dou regrouped and honed his control over fire to an extraordinary level.

He made a name for himself in the magic chef world, defeating several gold level magic chefs in culinary competitions.

He had long vowed to exact revenge on Ci Hai Shen.

This time, he must have heard Ci Hai Shen had arrived at Snow Bird Harbor and followed him here.

“I didn't expect to run into him so soon,” Ci Hai Shen remarked, caught off guard. Truthfully, he'd anticipated their confrontation at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's capital during the national ceremony.

Da Dou stood with hands on hips, glaring at his distant adversary. “Ci Hai Shen, if you want to pick your assistants, just say so! Why all this roundabout nonsense?”

“Pretending it's a tasting session? Hmph! Playing these scheming games, I despise your tactics.”

Da Dou pushed through the crowd, striding purposefully toward the long table.

His towering frame easily lifted the canopy draped around the table.

He casually picked up a dish, the “Spring Buds” made from raw fish slices. He merely sniffed it at nose level before crushing it between his fingers.

Da Dou sneered, “Same old method again!”

“Ci Hai Shen, you're still relying on the power of your Preservation Knife. Your own strength hasn't improved one bit!”

Ci Hai Shen was struck at his weakest point.

But could he be blamed?

His bloodline concentration was what it was, his personal ceiling had long been reached. Only by relying on external equipment could he achieve significant strength gains.

Da Dou was different.

This young giant possessed high bloodline concentration and was still in his growth phase.

His talent on the culinary path was also exceptional.

Back then, Ci Hai Shen had plotted against Da Dou precisely because he saw Da Dou's talent and bright future, and jealousy had driven him to act.

“Hmph, my gear is certainly part of my strength,” Ci Hai Shen retorted. "When you cook, don't you use utensils? What's strapped to your back?"

“My pot is merely silver level.”

“Ridiculous! Are you so poor you can't afford gold level gear that you envy others who use it? Poverty has warped your soul."

“Bullshit!”

…

The two gold level figures erupted into a fierce argument.

Across the square, countless transcendents watched with wide eyes, then grew thoroughly engrossed.

Attending this tasting event, they'd not only sampled transcendent cuisine but unexpectedly feasted on “gossip.”

And what a juicy scandal it was, unraveling the grudges and past conflicts between two gold level heavyweights.

Two gold level figures publicly trading insults? That was a rare sight indeed.

The verbal sparring continued for a while, but Da Dou gradually found himself on the defensive. After all, a giant's tongue couldn't match a sea elf's sharp wit. He finally snapped, "Ci Hai Shen, cut the crap. Let's settle this with a cooking contest. If you won't accept, you're nothing but a coward!"

Ci Hai Shen snorted coldly, initially refusing to respond while hurling insults.

Da Dou seized on Ci Hai Shen's apparent evasion, berating him as a coward and a chicken who dared not accept the challenge, insisting he was inferior.

Buoyed by this, Da Dou's momentum surged, overwhelming Ci Hai Shen.

Ci Hai Shen's face remained impassive, though inwardly, he took this contest with Da Dou extremely seriously. Otherwise, he wouldn't have made the special trip to Snow Bird Harbor to recruit the magic chef Mei Lan Du from the city lord's entourage.

The proud sea elf gradually grew enraged by Da Dou's taunts.

“That damned giant must be cast back into the abyss to learn respect for his elders!”

The sea elf now found himself cornered. Da Dou had exposed his true intentions and intimidated him, severely hindering his plans to recruit assistants.

Moreover, the sea elf placed immense importance on his own mindset. If he avoided battle now, he feared he would carry psychological weakness when facing Da Dou again at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's competition.

Having weighed these factors, Ci Hai Shen resolved to accept the challenge.

However, he was still a master strategist. He immediately sneered coldly, "Da Dou, you rely on being a giant with a booming voice to put on this grand show. Heh, do you really think I'm unaware of the schemes you're plotting right now?"

"Not only do you have your own hands, but your teacher's hands are also at your disposal. That's precisely why you don't need a sous chef or an assistant."

“You chose to challenge me during my vulnerable period of recruiting assistants, clearly taking advantage of my weakness. How cunning.”

“That said, your luck has been truly extraordinary!”

“Inheriting your master's arm not only restored you to your peak but propelled you even further.”

"What? Is scheming now part of your master's teachings too? You've grown a lot, youngster."

At the mention of his mentor, Da Dou's face darkened instantly, fury boiling over, “Don't you dare insult my teacher!”

“Ci Hai Shen, even as a senior, you're consumed by jealousy and envy. You're nothing like a true elder should be. So, drop that pretentious act.”

“If you fear what my mentor left me, then I won't use the Flame Totem.”

“Do you dare?”

“You and I will face off alone. Are you still afraid?”

Ci Hai Shen roared with laughter. His taunts had succeeded in provoking Da Dou, forcing him to abandon his greatest strength.

Ci Hai Shen feared Da Dou's Flame Totem above all else.

After all, Da Dou's mentor was a respected Magic MasterChef in the beastman race. The Flame Totem was his mentor's ultimate technique.

For Ci Hai Shen to face Da Dou wielding the Flame Totem would be like fighting two opponents at once.

“Since you, Da Dou, are so eager for a lesson, I shall accept your challenge this time.” Ci Hai Shen finally relented, accepting the duel on the spot.

The announcement sent a roar of excitement rippling through the square.

Many cheered loudly.

The crowd, always eager for spectacle, was thoroughly intrigued by the prospect of a duel between two gold level magic chefs.

The Snow Bird Arena's owner, present at the time, immediately offered to open his venue as a platform for the two magic chefs to face off.

Other arena owners only then realized the opportunity, but lacking the resources to compete with Snow Bird Arena, they could only sigh regretfully.

Upon hearing Ci Hai Shen finally accept the challenge, Da Dou breathed a sigh of relief and added, “A duel? That's perfect!”

“Let us engage in a heroic duel as chefs.”

“But though this duel isn't a matter of life and death, there must be some stakes involved."

"Ci Hai Shen, if you lose, you must compensate me with your Preservation Knife!"

Ci Hai Shen was furious.

The Preservation Knife was a gold level artifact, his most formidable culinary tool. It was virtually impossible to find on the market, and even with blueprints, crafting a second one was nearly impossible.

It wasn't that it couldn't be made, but it required an Alchemy Great Master's personal intervention and the collection of thirteen rare ingredients to form the “Thirteen Delicacies.” Only with the Thirteen Delicacies as the primary material could the knife be forged.

All thirteen ingredients were gold level, and most could be purchased on the high-end market. But a few, like the Purple Cloud Fresh Mushroom, existed only in the sky, and were extremely rare. That wasn't the most troublesome part. The most difficult ingredient was the Fresh Carrot Cockscomb Flower. Nowadays, it is nearly impossible to find anywhere in the world, though it was quite common over three hundred years ago.

“Da Dou has gotten clever!”

“If I lose this duel and lose the Preservation Knife, I'll stand no chance against him at the kingdom's national banquet.”

Ci Hai Shen naturally didn't want to agree to such a wager, but having already accepted the challenge, he couldn't back down. He could only say, “So you covet my Preservation Knife.”

“I can agree, but you must offer an equal wager in return.”

Ci Hai Shen had assumed Da Dou possessed no formidable culinary tools, yet once again, the latter shattered his preconceptions.

Da Dou pulled a steak from an inner pocket of his clothing.

This was no magic dish, but an alchemy artifact.

A gold level alchemy artifact!

Ci Hai Shen gasped in surprise, exclaiming, “The Meat Eye?”

The Meat Eye was a rare culinary tool of equal caliber and value to his own Preservation Knife.

Ci Hai Shen was instantly captivated.

“Then let's do it! I accept this wager!”

Ci Hai Shen's tasting session was interrupted as the two gold level magic chefs decided to hold a culinary duel at the Snow Bird Arena.

Such an event required reporting to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and advance communication was essential.

Ci Hai Shen further proposed selecting referees, suggesting the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord serve as chief judge.

After all, culinary contests were unique battles where victory was hard to judge by appearance alone.

But Da Dou refused.

“How can I be sure there aren't people close to you among the referees?”

"You arrived at Snow Bird Harbor before me, and this is the realm of the elves."

"I am a giant, an outsider."

"Therefore, this duel must be fair, let everyone serve as the judge. If you can bribe them all, I'll concede defeat without a word."

A sharp gleam flashed in Ci Hai Shen's eyes: “You want an eating contest?”

"Of course! Isn't that the most fundamental culinary contest?" Da Dou declared.

Ci Hai Shen pondered for a moment before nodding slightly. “I accept.”

Seeing the two great magic chefs reach an agreement, the crowd at Snow Bird Square erupted in commotion.

“What is an eating contest?” someone asked.

An expert replied, “There are many forms of culinary duels, and the eating contest is one of them. the most fundamental of them all.”

“The competing chefs prepare transcendent dishes of various levels on the spot, inviting others to sample them.”

“The more people eat, the more transcendent dishes are consumed. This proves the chef's popularity, making them the ultimate victor.”

Another voice raised doubts: “Isn't that similar to a tasting event?”

“Of course not.”

"Tasting events feature chefs creating novel dishes with various challenges, often deliberately difficult for diners. Therefore, chefs pay out of pocket, using prize money to entice transcendents to sample their creations."

“But in an eating contest, chefs will spare no effort to create flavors that are easier to consume and more pleasing to diners.”

“That's entirely different!”

“I can't wait! Who would've thought that two gold level magic chefs would face off, in an eating contest, no less?”

“Yeah, we're in for a real treat this time.”

The crowd buzzed with chatter, everyone buzzing with excitement.

Chapter 726: Tan Mo and Tan Qiu

The deep night shrouded Snake Rat Island.

The light from the lighthouse guided a warship of the Sheng Ming Empire to dock smoothly at the pier.

This warship featured a broad bow, with its superstructure positioned squarely at the center of the deck, a cube-like structure resembling a treasure chest of equal length, width, and height.

The treasure chest-like superstructure was adorned with intricate, layered magic formations. Embedded within these arrays were countless gems: sapphires the size of fists, diamonds as large as human heads, and topazes as big as carriages.

This was the ship, Treasure Chest.

The lord of Snake Rat Island, Tan Qiu, had long been waiting at the dock with a retinue of subordinates.

As Captain Tan Mo of the Treasure Chest descended the gangplank with his party, Tan Qiu hurried forward to greet him.

“Cousin,” Tan Qiu said, "you've arrived! You must be weary from your journey. The banquet has been prepared for some time."

Tan Qiu, the lord of Snake Rat Island, was an ordinary-looking, stocky middle-aged human man. Tan Mo, captain of the Treasure Chest, was obese with a thick, short neck. His small eyes sparkled with a shrewdness that even the night couldn't conceal.

The two were members of the same clan.

Tan Mo remarked, “Cousin, it's been three years since we last met, hasn't it?”

Tan Qiu laughed heartily.

Tan Mo signaled to his subordinates with a glance: “Go, unload the cargo. These goods were specifically transported for my cousin Tan Qiu.”

A look of delight spread across Tan Qiu's face as he watched the sailors begin unloading. He once again invited Tan Mo to join the banquet.

Tan Mo replied, "I'm pressed for time. Keep it simple. No need to meet those who aren't essential."

Tan Qiu understood immediately, nodding repeatedly as he made new arrangements.

A carriage stood ready. Tan Mo and his men boarded and were swiftly escorted to the city lord's residence.

The dinner proved lavish indeed, clearly prepared with great care.

Originally, several key figures from Snake Rat Island were to attend, but since Tan Mo declined to meet them, Tan Qiu simply dismissed them.

The vast banquet hall now felt rather desolate, occupied only by the two members of their own clan and a handful of servants.

Tan Mo held a stemmed glass, sipped a small amount of red wine, then gazed through the floor-to-ceiling windows toward the harbor.

His gaze settled on the lighthouse at the harbor.

This lighthouse was no ordinary structure. Its slender tower was crowned by a 3-meter-square cube. Both the tower and the cube gleamed silver white.

Each of the cube's four sides bore an engraved, closed eye. Atop the cube sat a prismatic crystal, standing as tall as an adult.

This was the light cannon tower.

Tan Mo's expression remained cool, yet his words were laced with flattery. "Cousin, your financial resources are unexpectedly substantial. You've ruled Snake Rat Island for only so long, but you've already set up a light cannon tower."

“I'm certain the clan will be pleased to see your successful operations here.”

Tan Qiu hastily offered a wry smile. "Cousin, spare me the sarcasm. As a naval officer, you surely understand that light cannon towers cost far less than mage towers. They're deliberately developed by the Empire as disposable defensive assets for invading the Wilderness Continent."

“As for mage towers? I dare not even dream of them.”

“Even this light cannon tower, I tightened my belt, scrimped and saved, hesitated for ages, before finally gritting my teeth and buying it.”

“Sigh, this tower has practically drained the Snake Rat Island Lord's treasury dry.”

"Life's tough, but what can I do? Pirate activity is growing increasingly rampant. The seas are turbulent, and all manner of monsters are emerging. Just months ago, Two Eyes Island, not far from ours, suffered a massive assault by the fishmen."

“I saw intelligence reports indicating the spotlight turret performed quite well in that battle.”

"For the safety of my domain, I had no choice but to procure one. And just this one!"

Facing Tan Qiu's complaints and pleas of poverty, Tan Mo merely snorted. He gave Tan Qiu a meaningful glance before focusing his attention back on the red wine glass in his hand.

Leaning back in his chair, he swirled the stemmed glass leisurely. “Tan Qiu, do you think I'm unaware?”

“You captured Rou Zang's pirate crew and amassed a fortune in war spoils!”

"You took Gu Qiao alive and had him redeemed by the Great Pain Temple. You pocketed a hefty ransom for that too.”

"And then there's Rou Zang, a gold level pirate, no less. You handed him over to the Empire and earned massive military merit. Once this achievement is properly accounted for, it could very well earn you a knighthood.”

"But if I were you, I'd sell Rou Zang to the Great Pain Temple. They're willing to pay, and with their vast wealth and influence, they can afford the hefty ransom."

Tan Qiu's expression shifted for a moment before he sighed bitterly. "Ah, to be honest, I'd love to do just that. "

“But there's no way around it. The Sheng Ming Church originally controlled Snake Rat Island.”

“After the battle with Rou Zang, the priest was killed in action, causing shifts within the diocese.”

“Now, the Justice Church has taken over, aggressively pushing out the Sheng Ming Church and gaining complete dominance.”

“I had to mend fences with the Sheng Ming Church, so I followed their approach and sent Rou Zang up the chain.”

“But with all this skimming, my share of the battle merits is drastically reduced. Forget about getting promoted to a noble rank!”

Tan Mo raised his eyebrows slightly, intrigued. “That sounds like a good thing.”

“The Sheng Ming Church is too powerful. Compared to them, the Justice Church is just a minor church.”

“This greatly benefits your authority as a lord.”

“It seems your skills are truly formidable, cousin. You managed to secure the Sheng Ming Church without getting entangled in church rivalries.”

Tan Qiu gave another bitter smile: “Cousin, bringing in the Justice Church was part of the pre-war agreement.”

"Lord Guang Ke hails from the Justice Church, and we desperately needed his gold level combat strength. Therefore, not only did I agree, but the priest also represented the Sheng Ming Church in giving his consent."

“Having a clear magic contract is the best reason to prevent the Sheng Ming Church from causing me trouble!”

Tan Mo gazed at Tan Qiu, whose face bore a bitter expression, his eyes growing distant.

He couldn't help but recall the past.

In his memory, this younger cousin had always been unremarkable. His peers within the clan had nicknamed him “Loach.”

“Who would've thought this little loach would actually revitalize Snake Rat Island?”

“On the Sheng Ming Continent, given his status, how could he possibly have acquired such vast territories?”

“Though somewhat barren, it has its pillars of industry!”

Tide snakes, smelt rats... even the Hunter Guild was drawn here, establishing a branch."

"Then there's the conflict between the Sheng Ming Church and the Justice Church... This cousin possesses considerable political skill and favorable fortune. The Sheng Ming Priest truly did perish in that naval battle."

“Henceforth, these two churches will check each other, only serving to highlight his authority as a secular lord!”

At this thought, Tan Mo's mood grew complex and subtle.

“My Lord, the cargo has been fully accounted for,” Tan Qiu's subordinate reported at that moment. “However, a batch of slime gelatin is missing.”

“Hm?” Tan Qiu's brow instantly furrowed.

The cargo unloaded from the Treasure Chest was procured through his family's channels.

This voyage happened to take Tan Mo's route past Snake Rat Island, so the family had arranged for the Treasure Chest to serve as a transport vessel.

The ship's interior offered ample space, making it ideal for shipping.

“Slime gelatin may not be expensive, but it's the most fundamental material for constructing my defenses. We absolutely cannot be without it!” Tan Mo frowned.

He pressed his subordinate, “Did you check thoroughly?”

The subordinate hurriedly replied, “We've counted it three times. The only possibility is that it's still...”

His voice trailed off involuntarily, growing faint. He quickly glanced up at Tan Mo across the table.

Tan Qiu sighed inwardly, thinking, “This was bound to happen.”

He knew his cousin's nature all too well. He had already paid the transport fees this time, yet this unexpected turn of events had still occurred.

Tan Qiu dismissed his subordinate and turned to face Tan Mo, only to see the latter wearing a smug, smiling expression.

He suppressed his anger and feigned confusion. “Cousin, our men just finished inventory and found a batch of slime gelatin is missing.”

Tan Mo let out a surprised “Oh? Is that so?”

Tan Qiu frowned, explaining that while slime gelatin was a basic alchemy ingredient, it was crucial for Snake Rat Island. He hoped Tan Mo would dispatch subordinates to search and recover the missing gelatin.

Tan Mo deliberately frowned. “That's difficult, cousin. It's not that I don't want to find it, but it's quite challenging.”

"You don't know, but there's a VIP on my ship. I dare not offend him!"

“It seems the slime gelatin was stored near the VIP's cabin, where noise is strictly forbidden.”

“Who might this important person be?” Tan Qiu pressed.

Tan Mo chuckled. "Someone from the Blood Light Punishment Institution. He's heading to Two Eyes Island to investigate matters concerning necromancers. I was ordered to escort him here. Truth be told, you owe him a debt of gratitude. Otherwise, how could my ship ever have come to Snake Rat Island?"

"The Blood Light Punishment Institution!" Tan Qiu's heart jolted, his expression shifting instantly.

Tan Mo invoking the Blood Light Punishment Institution was truly ruthless, Tan Qiu dared not entertain such thoughts.

He clapped his hands, summoning a servant. He whispered instructions into her ear.

The servant departed and soon returned, bearing a wooden gift box.

The exquisitely crafted wooden box was presented upon Tan Mo's desk.

Tan Mo feigned surprise: “Cousin, what might this signify?”

Tan Qiu plastered on a smile: "Elder cousin, just a small token. You've been escorting the Blood Light Punishment Institution's people, a demanding task, yet you still found time amidst your busy schedule to come here and help me transport supplies. Your kindness is overwhelming, how could I not show my gratitude?"

“This is a small token of my humble appreciation. Please accept it.”

Tan Mo gently opened the box, and a radiant glow instantly illuminated his face.

He saw three rare gold level treasures. The most striking was the mica pearl in the center, as large as a small bowl.

Tan Mo's heart skipped three beats.

Yet his expression remained impassive. He calmly closed the box and smiled warmly at Tan Qiu. "Cousin, this is too much! You've really gone out of your way!"

Though he protested, his hand moved smoothly and naturally, tucking the box into his storage bracelet.

Tan Qiu hurriedly insisted this was merely a token of his heartfelt gratitude, which couldn't possibly match his profound thanks.

He further emphasized his dire straits, stressing how crucial the slime gelatin was to him!

Tan Mo nodded, immediately summoning his subordinates. His demeanor shifted as he instructed them: “Find the slime gelatin and transport it down without disturbing the important figures. Do whatever it takes.”

Subordinates soon reported the supplies had been located and were being transported down at great risk.

Tan Mo's extortion succeeded. His men didn't even need Snake Rat Island's help, they transported the slime gelatin down themselves. His attitude now stood in stark contrast to before.

Tan Qiu's heart burned with rage, but he forced himself to suppress it. He raised his cup to Tan Mo in gratitude: “Cousin, you've truly done me an immense favor this time.”

Having secured what he wanted, Tan Mo relaxed and genuinely shared a few cups with Tan Qiu.

As the atmosphere grew more convivial, their conversation naturally turned to the development of Snake Rat Island.

Tan Qiu sighed, "The greatest resources in our domain are the tide snake and smelt rats in the underground caverns. To attract the Hunter Guild to establish a branch here, I offered them substantial concessions."

"Now, the populations of tide snakes and smelt rats have plummeted. According to our estimates, even if all hunting were halted immediately, it would take five years for them to naturally repopulate to their former peak levels."

Tan Mo expressed curiosity: "From what I understand, the Hunter Guild isn't known for such practices. They emphasize sustainable development. How could this local branch act so unwisely?"

Tan Qiu shook his head: “This isn't their fault.”

“The primary culprit is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

At the mention of them, Tan Mo gritted his teeth in fury. “Those bastards infiltrated my Snake Rat Island, secretly poaching countless tide snakes and smelt rats, devastating the ecosystem.”

“We were kept in the dark until recently, when we discovered massive numbers of tide snakes and smelt rats had been hunted down.”

“Oh?” Tan Mo raised an eyebrow.

He had specifically investigated Snake Rat Island's intelligence to blackmail Tan Qiu. The naval battle with Rou Zang was the most critical part, making the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps unavoidable in their collection of intelligence.

Thus, Tan Mo knew quite a lot about them.

"These people seem to be arms smugglers? Their strength is quite formidable!"

“They were a huge help to you in that naval battle.”

“Without them, you likely wouldn't have defeated Rou Zang.”

“Uh...” Tan Qiu's indignant expression faltered, replaced by awkwardness.

He continued, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps made far too much profit off me.”

“When they left, they took numerous sea vessels. These were all spoils from the naval battle."

"What's most infuriating was how they had aggressively recruited here, snatching up all the island's talent. That included giants and magicians."

“Even low level magicians, I desperately need them.”

"So now, despite replenished supplies, Snake Rat Island is starved for manpower. I'm sorely lacking capable subordinates."

Tan Mo remained noncommittal, finally asking, "Have you investigated this group? Do you know who the arms dealer behind them truly is?"

Tan Qiu shook his head and shrugged, indicating he'd tried but to no avail. They were hiding too deeply!

Tan Mo felt the same frustration.

After learning of this identity speculation from intelligence sources, he mobilized his own channels and resources to investigate the background of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

He came up empty-handed.

"It's only natural for arms smugglers to keep a low profile. This is a business where you risk your life!"

“If I uncovered them immediately, I'd actually doubt the investigation's credibility.”

“But if we could truly root out the arms dealers, that would be a monumental achievement!”

Having gained no useful information from Tan Qiu, Tan Mo saw no reason to linger.

After a lavish dinner, he parted ways with Tan Qiu, boarded the Treasure Chest, and set sail through the night.

Chapter 727: Assault Plan

While Tan Mo and Tan Qiu discussed intelligence on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, another group was also strategizing.

Snow Bird Harbor, slums.

“This is all the intel we have on our target for this operation.” The leader placed a stack of reports before the transcendents.

The transcendents radiated a lean, formidable aura, with most at the iron level. Several leaders were silver level.

The intelligence circulated rapidly among the group.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps...”

"Their strongest is the leader, Long Fu. Though young, his combat prowess is exceptional. His most outstanding achievement was the duel at the Snow Bird Arena. In that battle, he defeated a gold level opponent as a silver level, a feat unseen in a century!

“Footage of that fight spread nationwide and remains wildly popular to this day.”

Naturally, the intelligence included footage of that battle.

The transcendents watched in silence. Most had seen it before; only a few had not.

Those who hadn't watched it were nearly moved to tears afterward.

“Long Fu's victory over Lord Teng Dong Lang was actually quite narrow.”

“But his fighting spirit was incredibly resilient. He was at a disadvantage from the start, yet never gave up.”

"Ultimately, killing Lord Teng Dong Lang involved a significant element of luck. But how to put it? The victor is king!"

“Such fighting spirit... If he weren't an enemy, I'd want to be friends with him.”

The transcendents exchanged a few words.

The leader cleared his throat, his expression grave. "We must admit, Long Fu possesses undeniable personal charisma. He engaged Lord Teng Dong Lang in a heroic duel, one-on-one, overcoming strength with finesse. He earned his victory honorably, leaving nothing to criticize."

“That is precisely why he has become the hottest duelist sensation across the nation recently.”

"Numerous arenas have invited him to duel, but he has declined every offer. Currently, he and his operatives are touring the kingdom's major cities, establishing sales points for potions."

"You may not know this, but the dragon rider potion is the potion the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is aggressively promoting. This potion has already flooded the kingdom's potion market, gaining tremendous momentum and widespread popularity!"

“This alone demonstrates how strategically savvy Long Fu is.”

“He's pursuing far more significant and meaningful endeavors.”

“According to others' analysis, if their business plan continues unchecked, they'll firmly establish themselves in the kingdom's potion market and carve out a substantial niche.”

“Forget their future alchemy products, just sustained sales of this dragon rider potion alone would make them a fortune overnight!”

“This is the greatest significance of our current operation.”

“Destroy the four alchemy workshops in Snow Bird Harbor—they are the production bases for the dragon rider potion!”

The leader pointed to the next section of the intelligence: “Long Fu is currently far from Snow Bird Harbor. We don't need to engage him directly.”

“In this operation, our greatest enemy is him!”

Everyone's gaze fell upon two large characters - Shi Qi.

Zong Ge, posing as Shi Qi, served as the deputy commander of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This time, disguised as Long Fu, he set out alongside Zi Di. Simultaneously, he deployed alchemy puppets. Using the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, he mimicked Shi Qi's presence, creating the illusion that the commander had departed while the deputy remained in charge of the branch.

The Deceptive Disguise spell possessed a high rank, effortlessly fooling the enemy. Consequently, these forces remained convinced Shi Qi was stationed at the Snow Bird Harbor branch, making him their primary target.

“Long Fu's combat prowess is extraordinary, and Shi Qi's strength should not be underestimated either.”

“He demonstrated outstanding performance in both the Snake Rat Island naval battle and the Two Eyes Island battle.”

“His battle qi now carries a golden glow, proof he stands but a step away from gold level!”

They pored over intelligence, desperately seeking Zong Ge's weaknesses to exploit.

As the group analyzed the situation, the atmosphere grew increasingly tense.

“Shi Qi has almost no weaknesses!”

“As a fighter, he can withstand heavy damage and has strong recovery abilities. It'll be hard to take him down in one strike.”

"His unarmed combat skills are already formidable. Armed with weapons, he becomes even more powerful. During the battle, we must find a way to deprive him of his weapons!"

The transcendents successively devised three battle plans.

Whichever plan they chose, heavy casualties were inevitable.

There was no way around it. Though they held the advantage of surprise and initiative from their concealed position, since Long Fu departed, Shi Qi had remained anchored at the docks, rarely venturing out, not even taking mercenary contracts.

The docks were routinely guarded by the city guard.

Most crucially, there was a magician tower!

The transcendents preparing to launch a surprise attack in such an environment had all braced themselves for certain death.

Yet the next moment, the leader suddenly received the latest orders relayed from above.

He stepped out briefly, returning with a look of delight on his face: “We needn't worry about how to deal with the Shi Qi, the higher-ups have already dispatched a gold level assassin.”

Upon hearing this news, the transcendents all cheered.

The toughest nut to crack had been taken care of by others. Now, their targets were the scattered alchemy workshops.

One large alchemy workshop lay by the seaside in the southeast corner of Snow Bird Harbor.

Two medium-sized workshops were located at: Mercury Cave and Rose Beach.

One small workshop, situated within the city walls, operated year-round by Bu Quan.

Intelligence on the four workshops included extensive video footage and aerial-view sandbox models.

"The large coastal workshop can be stormed directly, it lacks significant defensive capabilities. In fact, excessive defenses would pose a threat the city government palace wouldn't tolerate."

“We can deploy heavy firepower, save this one for last, and arrange our retreat route along the nearby coastline.”

"The Mercury Cave is easy to defend but hard to attack, and this workshop is guarded by numerous alchemy puppets. Since we've already identified how these puppets are commanded, we can use alchemy devices to target them and temporarily disable them."

"The Rose Beach area is similarly open terrain, suitable for a direct assault. We can also leave a retreat route there and destroy it during withdrawal. However, the large workshop requires setting up artillery for bombardment. In short, both locations can be taken by assault or bombardment."

"Finally, the small workshop within the city poses the greatest challenge for a surprise attack. Its position is already quite close to the city government palace. Those executing the assault may not have time to retreat, be prepared to die in battle!"

“This small workshop houses extremely advanced facilities. It's the absolute priority, our primary target for destruction.”

"Bu Quan is likely inside. Intelligence indicates she's been working long-term in that small workshop! Therefore, we must also confront a silver level magician."

"The only silver lining is that Bu Quan is a dedicated alchemist with weak combat skills. Even so, she remains a magician wielding formidable alchemy power, we cannot afford complacency!"

Someone inquired: “If Bu Quan demonstrates overwhelming combat prowess and shows no mercy, may we execute her?”

The leader fell silent, his expression betraying hesitation.

Others began debating.

“Bu Quan is a member of the Alchemy Guild, one of our own. We should spare her life if possible.”

"No! In my view, since she joined the enemy, she's a traitor, our enemy! Against enemies, we strike to kill."

"Are you brain-dead? Didn't you read the intel? Bu Quan is highly capable. She was merely blinded by emotion when she agreed to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"Her joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps actually proves she's a person of honor and loyalty. She's just too young. Having focused solely on alchemy for years, she doesn't understand the treacherous ways of the world and fell victim to Long Fu's beauty trap."

"Bu Quan is valuable, but if we spare her life instead of killing her, even if we destroy all four alchemy workshops, it won't achieve much, right? Clearly, Bu Quan is the core alchemist of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

The leader pondered and concluded, then spoke: "Capture her alive if possible. If capture proves impossible, retreat, but spare her life."

“She's a rare talent and a native citizen of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Time will mature her, helping her discern friends from foes.”

The leader added, "If this operation succeeds, we'll act opportunistically. If an opportunity arises, do your utmost to destroy the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ new flagship."

"As you've long observed, the magic ship they acquired as their flagship is actually one of our nation's regular warships. It was merely retired early during this round of military optimization."

At this point, expressions of fury surged across the faces of the other transcendents.

“Those fat pigs in the Military Supply Department, so corrupt and greedy, they'd even sell warships!”

“These traitors sold out national interests for personal gain. They deserve to be hung!”

“If I survive this battle, I'll hunt down every last one of them and kill them all!”

“Count me in!”

"Damn right. I couldn't stand these bastards when I was serving. Count me in too."

As the transcendents discussed, their identities were revealed.

They had all served in the kingdom's regular army. Even after leaving active duty, they retained their loyalty to the kingdom and formidable combat prowess.

This operation was led by Mei Lin.

She was a commander in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's First Navy, but she could not deploy regular military forces. Previously, she had led a contingent of holy warriors to ambush and destroy a Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps caravan. Yet days passed, and the supply of dragon rider potions in the market showed no sign of diminishing.

Mei Lin immediately realized the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps must be secretly transporting the potions through other channels.

She couldn't trace it back to the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance in the short term.

But unwilling to delay the opportunity, she swiftly assembled a group of elite veterans and infiltrated Snow Bird Harbor. Their mission: to raid the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' branch office and their alchemy workshops!

The leader gave final instructions: “The operation is set for the day of the culinary duel.”

“By then, most transcendents will be gathered at the Snow Bird Arena, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group branch will also send representatives to watch.”

"The defenses at the branch and alchemy workshop will be at their weakest. This is our prime opportunity to strike."

“If any of us are defeated and captured, remember what to do?”

The transcendents responded in unison: "Understood, Commander! We will immediately take our own lives. We will never give the enemy any leverage to attack our nation, nor allow the kingdom to be disgraced!"

Chapter 728: Teacher: Where's My Little Quan Quan?

Bu Quan had been in a state of hesitation for quite some time.

This was within the city walls of Snow Bird Harbor.

A central area near the city government palace.

This small alchemy workshop was also the place Bu Quan had loved most before joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

On the table, a spherical beaker contained a potion being heated. Pink bubbles continuously rose from the yellow-green liquid.

Bu Quan's pupils dilated slightly as her mind drifted.

Yet her movements remained utterly precise, each step in the potion's preparation executed with flawless accuracy.

Even while multitasking, Bu Quan's profound alchemy expertise allowed this experiment to refine the formula to proceed smoothly and methodically.

“Should I write that letter after all, asking my mentor for help?” Bu Quan felt deeply embarrassed.

She knew full well that her entire journey thus far owed much to her mentor's guidance and support.

Her mentor's care and attention far exceeded that given to others, to the point of arousing jealousy.

Despite her mentor's profound devotion, Bu Quan had insisted on journeying to Snow Bird Harbor to avenge her nanny. Yet her mentor hadn't stopped her. Instead, she had mobilized her connections to protect Bu Quan.

All along, Bu Quan had harbored a deep sense of guilt toward her mentor.

After joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, this guilt only deepened. Bu Quan understood clearly that her mentor had always been cultivating her, grooming her to be the heir to the magician tower.

Yet Bu Quan had cast aside those expectations for her own sake, driven by curiosity about a different way of life.

Truthfully, Bu Quan felt the pressure too.

The moment she chose to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, she knew she could hardly face her mentor. She desperately wanted to explain yet found no way to do so. She refused to deceive her mentor.

Thus, she never reported her current choices or living situation to her mentor.

Fearing confrontation, she adopted an evasive mindset, hoping to send Magician Zhong Bei back to the Alchemy Guild as a way to inform her teacher.

After rejecting Magician Zhong Bei, she waited for her mentor's letter of reproach.

Instead, she received Magician Da Bei and his son, Magician Zhong Bei.

Bu Quan rejected Magician Da Bei once more, resuming her wait for her mentor's rebuke.

But the letter never came. Instead, she received a request from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

It was a request for her to write a letter herself, asking her teacher for assistance!

This matter presented a massive psychological barrier for Bu Quan.

“I already feel terribly guilty toward my teacher.”

“Now, to ask her to intervene... How shameless of me!”

“But... the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' situation is truly dire.”

"Especially Long Fu, the commander, he was ambushed by three gold level mercenaries. Thankfully, his strength is formidable; he killed all three."

"He truly lives up to the reputation of the man I chose."

Thinking of the dragonman youngster, Bu Quan's eyes lit up, and the corners of her mouth curved upward involuntarily, revealing the sweet smile of a woman in love.

Little did she know that the one who achieved this astonishing feat wasn't her beloved, but Zong Ge.

“The commander cares deeply for me, so he specially wrote to warn me.”

“If I hadn't gone to invite the teacher, I might have fallen into grave danger.”

“Perhaps...”

As she pondered, Bu Quan's thoughts began to wander.

"Perhaps the enemy attacked. They assaulted fiercely, ruthlessly and mercilessly, overrunning this place. Even the magician tower was destroyed by them

“I fought desperately, but four opponents were too much.”

"At the most critical moment, the commander descended from the heavens, a hero rescuing his damsel in distress! With just three moves, he knocked the enemy to the ground."

"He held me in his arms, his face filled with tenderness as he whispered, 'I'm sorry, Bu Quan, I came too late. Where are you hurt?'"

At this thought, two patches of red spread across Bu Quan's cheeks.

She shook her head, her thoughts continuing to wander.

“No, it was because I knew these potions held immense value for the mercenary corps.”

“I could have retreated, but to protect them, I had to stay and fight the enemy.”

"I battled them to the bitter end and successfully safeguarded the potions. But in doing so, I paid a heavy price."

"I was dying, beyond saving. As I lay dying, the commander arrived and swiftly cut down the remaining foes."

“He held me in his arms, heedless of my blood-soaked body, wailing with grief and regret, blaming himself for not returning sooner.”

“He cried out my name, refusing to let me slip into unconsciousness and death.”

"My face was deathly pale as I mustered every ounce of strength to reach out and touch the commander's cheek, feeling the tears streaming down his face."

"I told him, 'Lord Long Fu, I protected these potions. I did what Sister Yao Ma instructed me to do.'"

"'No, Bu Quan. You're so foolish, you're so truly foolish. In my heart, you matter more than these potions. A hundred times more, a thousand times more, ten thousand times more!' Lord Long Fu cried out."

“He glared fiercely at Magician Yao Ma beside him, then clasped my hand tightly: ‘Promise me, Bu Quan, don't leave me. Hold on, don't fall asleep, okay?’”

“But I couldn't hold on. I died.”

"From that day on, Lord Long Fu had eyes only for me. No matter how beautiful or talented those around him were, no matter how desperately Magician Yao Ma pursued him, he remained unmoved."

"In the end, as Magician Yao Ma lay dying, she sighed with profound regret: 'In my entire life, I lost to only one woman. That woman was Bu Quan. That woman was Bu Quan. Though she lost her life, she won Lord Long Fu’s heart. How I wish I could have taken her place, but now it’s too late...’"

At this thought, Bu Quan’s eyes reddened. She sank into her own reverie, enveloped by the sorrow and emotion spreading through her heart.

After a long while, her cheeks flushed crimson with heat before the strange crackling sound of the potion mixing snapped her back to reality.

Her thoughts halted abruptly as she stared blankly at the finished potion in her hands.

Her alchemy skill was profound, her talent exceptional. Even while deeply distracted, she had successfully crafted a silver level potion.

Bu Quan's emotions remained unsettled. She felt no joy at the potion's success; her mind was filled only with the dragonman youngster's image.

“I care little for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, but if Commander Long Fu grieves for me, I too will grieve!”

"Teacher... I have failed you. But this time, I must ask for your help."

“Because I refuse to see Lord Long Fu suffer for me, to remain unmarried for life, to carry regret forever.”

“True lovers should be united forever. I cannot let the world's greatest tragedy unfold!”

With this resolve, Bu Quan steeled herself. Gritting her teeth, she summoned her courage and, filled with love, began writing the letter.

After finishing the plea for help, Bu Quan immediately utilized the Alchemy Guild's channels to send the letter back by express delivery.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps relied on messengers using teleportation formations to deliver communications.

Bu Quan didn't need that.

She was a member of the Alchemy Guild, a major organization within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom that commanded considerable resources and connections.

The Alchemy Guild operated numerous teleportation formations specifically for transmitting confidential messages. These formations, incapable of transporting living beings, had obtained royal permission and passed a series of stringent inspections, enabling direct transmission from Snow Bird Harbor to the Ice Sculpture capital.

Alchemy Guild Headquarters.

Snow Honey Tower.

The tower master was none other than Bu Quan's teacher, Shuang Lian.

She pushed open the heavy door of the alchemy chamber, emerging with heavy dark circles under her eyes and a vacant expression.

The large-scale alchemy experiment that had lasted several months had ended in failure.

Shuang Lian was in a terrible mood.

Her apprentices gathered around her. Some tried to support her, but she shook off their hands. Others offered damp towels and prepared meals, but she refused those too.

“Teacher, you haven't eaten, drunk, or slept for months while researching alchemy. You need to rest.”

“Yes, we've prepared a feast for you, the result of a week's careful preparation.”

“I've compiled the mage tower's revenue reports and documented all the events unfolding across the kingdom during your alchemy seclusion. I stand ready to brief you at any moment.”

Yet none of this held Shuang Lian's interest.

She demanded, “Where is Little Quan Quan?”

Not spotting the face she sought among the crowd, her disappointment deepened. “Ah... I remember now. Little Quan Quan has already left me.”

“How is she doing?”

“Where are her letters?”

“Let me see them now! I need to read her letters to soothe my wounded heart.”

The other disciples and students looked downcast. One stepped forward: "Junior Bu Quan's letters have all been collected and stored in the mailroom on the third floor. Teacher, I'll fetch them for you immediately."

Shuang Lian nodded. “I'll go to the study. Hurry and bring the letters here.”

After Shuang Lian left the floor, the disciples and apprentices murmured discontentedly.

One pouted, “How long has Bu Quan been gone? Teacher still can't forget her.”

Another sighed resentfully, “I underestimated her. I never thought even after she left, Teacher would still care most about her!”

"Teacher clearly misses her so much, yet she's been staying alone in Snow Bird Harbor all this time, only writing back every once in a while. Why is Teacher so kind to someone like that?"

Others gloated: "Heh, Bu Quan's definitely going to get scolded this time. Teacher will surely lose her temper later; there's trouble at the Alchemy Guild. Bu Quan has joined that Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps."

"When the Teacher gets angry, someone's bound to suffer. Ah! Are there any field assignments at the mage tower? I want to go out."

“Me too, me too!”

"I'd rather stay and see how this plays out. If Bu Quan gets ordered back, that scene will be priceless!"

Shuang Lian returned to her study.

From a desk drawer, she retrieved a compact cosmetic mirror.

An alchemy artifact - the Facial Cleansing Nourishing Mirror.

She gazed into the mirror. Instantly, her face underwent thorough cleansing, followed by the application of nourishing cream and meticulous care.

Though still utterly exhausted, her complexion brightened considerably, restoring her appearance to that of the middle-aged beauty who resembled a snow spirit.

“Little Quan Quan...” Shuang Lian traced her fingers along the mirror's frame, her expression filled with longing.

This very mirror had been a small birthday gift from Bu Quan.

“Teacher, these are all the letters from Junior Bu Quan.” The student delivered the mail to the study.

Shuang Lian nodded slightly. “Place them on the desk. You may leave now.”

The apprentice had barely reached the doorway when Shuang Lian called her back. "Wait. Are these truly all the letters? Surely not? After such a long time, Bu Quan has only written these few letters to me?"

The apprentice felt uneasy, hesitating to tell the truth outright. She evaded, "Teacher, these truly are all of them. The one at the very bottom was delivered just today."

Shuang Lian's brow furrowed slightly, her heart skipping a beat as a sense of foreboding rose within her.

“This isn't normal. Could something have happened to my beloved apprentice?”

She dismissed the apprentice, swiftly tore open the letter, and examined it closely.

The earlier letters contained routine matters.

Bu Quan reported on her daily life, asking questions and discussing alchemy issues.

But the last few letters revealed a drastic change.

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?"

“Their leader, Long Fu, actually indirectly avenged Little Quan Quan.”

“To slay a gold level opponent as a silver level... this dragonman is no ordinary being.”

At first, Shuang Lian had held quite a bit of admiration for the dragonman youngster.

But then...

"What?!" Shuang Lian's eyes widened, nearly screaming.

"Little Quan Quan actually joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? At the invitation of Long Fu?"

“Foolish! Utterly foolish!”

“How could Little Quan Quan join a mercenary group? And just a small, silver level one at that?” And an outsider at that?"

"What invitation from the commander? It's clearly deception and trickery! Yes, that damned dragonman scoundrel must have used smooth talk and flattery to deceive my Little Quan Quan."

“My poor Little Quan Quan, so inexperienced in the ways of the world, has fallen victim to such deception and strayed onto the wrong path in life!”
