"Damn it! If that dragonman shows his face before me, I'll tear him limb from limb right now!

"This is outrageous! What was the Alchemy Guild thinking? Didn't I arrange for someone to guard Little Quan Quan around the clock?"

"Who was responsible for this? How could they stand by and watch Little Quan Quan get deceived and board a scammer's ship?"

The middle-aged beauty grew angrier the more she thought about it.

"Let me recall... I remember the person assigned was named something like ‘Bei’. He's also the son of some elder in the guild, supposedly very reliable."

“Is that all it takes to be reliable?”

Shuang Lian crumpled the letter in her hand so fiercely it lost its shape, yet the supervisor's name eluded her memory.

Just then, the tower spirit of the mage tower spoke: “Tower Master, I hear you've concluded your alchemy experiments. Three important visitors seek your audience. They are Master Cai Jing, Elder Da Bei, and Magician Zhong Bei.”

“Zhong Bei?” Shuang Lian froze, then flashed with fury. "Him! Yes, that one, Zhong Bei."

“Fine. Very fine. I haven’t even sought trouble with him, and he comes looking for me!”

“What? He brought his father and another henchman too?”

“Is this a threat?”

Chapter 729: Shuang Lian vs Cai Jing, Da Bei, and Zhong Bei

The three magicians, Zhong Bei, Da Bei, and Cai Jing, stood before Snow Honey Tower.

Gazing up at the towering mage tower, Cai Jing and the others wore expressions of envy.

Owning a mage tower was no easy feat.

The construction costs were exorbitant, placing them beyond the reach of most magicians. Typically, only regional powers possessed the financial resources to construct one.

Of course, such regional powers also needed to be wealthy. Places like Snake Rat Island simply couldn't afford the cost of building a mage tower.

For magicians, controlling even the lowest level mage tower brought immense benefits. The Snow Honey Tower before them boasted a full five levels.

After escaping from Qian Xing via the teleportation formation on Flower Skirt Island, Zhong Bei, Da Bei, and Cai Jing immediately headed for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

After all, the safest place near Flower Skirt Island was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Upon arriving at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, Magician Da Bei leveraged his status and authority to swiftly transport the other two through various town teleportation formations, reaching the royal capital.

Upon entering the Alchemy Guild headquarters, they stated their intentions and recounted their journey.

The Alchemy Guild took this matter extremely seriously.

For it concerned the inheritance of Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

This was a legend level treasure!

Master Cai Jing had previously clashed with the Alchemy Guild, but in the face of such immense profit, those conflicts naturally melted away.

However, before formal cooperation could begin, one obstacle remained.

As the Alchemy Guildmaster stated: “Before jointly utilizing Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's legacy, we must fully consider the stance of another elder.”

The figure commanding the respect of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Guildmaster was none other than Bu Quan's mentor - the Snow Honey Tower Master - Shuang Lian.

The Guildmaster continued to advise the trio: "You may not fully grasp Shuang Lian's feelings toward Bu Quan. She genuinely nurtured her as the chosen successor."

“Even allowing Bu Quan to depart for Snow Bird Harbor was a deliberate opportunity she granted her for growth.”

"Therefore, it would be best if you could secure Shuang Lian's personal understanding before proceeding with this collaboration. Otherwise, given her nature, she could very well create significant obstacles for our partnership."

Thus, Cai Jing and the others arrived at the Snow Honey Tower to formally visit Shuang Lian.

“I hope we can explain things clearly,” said Magician Zhong Bei, visibly uneasy.

Magician Da Bei reassured his son: “I'm here. Like Shuang Lian, I'm also an elder of the Alchemy Guild. During our conversation, stay composed and avoid any friction!"

Master Cai Jing shook his head, his tone bitter. “Bu Quan received cultivation from the mage tower master, yet she joined a mercenary group outright? What was she thinking?”

Though Cai Jing was a gold level powerhouse, he genuinely envied Bu Quan, a silver level prodigy.

In his quest for cultivation resources, Master Cai Jing had once resorted to manufacturing counterfeit potions, leading the Alchemy Guild to exile this rule-breaker.

Master Cai Jing had previously been unaware of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Bu Quan's story. But since they were visiting Shuang Lian, magicians Zhong Bei and Da Bei naturally laid everything out.

This included why they sought out Cai Jing to forge counterfeit dragon rider potions.

Master Cai Jing thus learned the full story, gaining insight into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Bu Quan, and other related information.

"We possess one of the inheritances of Fan Sheng Za Zhong. If all else fails, we'll simply hand it over to Shuang Lian as a bargaining chip!"

As Master Cai Jing spoke, the space suddenly erupted with violent fluctuations.

The three magicians attempted to vanish and retreat, but simultaneously, the mage towers before them erupted with power, pinning them firmly to the spot.

Caught off guard, the magicians were swept up by the spatial turbulence.

Their vision abruptly shifted.

In the next instant, they had been transported from the Alchemy Guild headquarters into a vast, frozen wasteland.

A fierce wind howled, whipping up mountains of snow.

Amidst the swirling snow, a colossal humanoid apparition coalesced.

This towering figure, reaching skyward and straddling the earth, bore an uncanny resemblance to Shuang Lian.

Da Bei was the first to recognize it, immediately shouting, “Elder Shuang Lian! It's me, Da Bei! Hold your fire! We can talk this out!”

Magician Zhong Bei also cried out anxiously, “Lord Shuang Lian, I can explain! I can explain everything!”

The snow-formed figure gazed down upon the three ants beneath its feet, its voice booming across the land: “Explain what? What is there to explain?”

“You lost my student, yet you dare come knocking on my door.”

“Even if you brought others with you, they're no match for me!”

With that, the shadowy figure abruptly shattered, transforming into a torrential blizzard.

Snowflakes pelted their faces, mingled with howling winds.

The temperature plummeted rapidly, soon freezing them to the bone.

In the three magicians' vision, everything was a white haze. Countless masses of ice and snow surged in from all directions, overwhelming them.

The three felt like ordinary mortals, trapped in a once-in-a-century catastrophic blizzard.

So small!

So weak!

A bitter sorrow welled up in their hearts, a thread of despair taking root deep within.

“No! I refuse to die here!” Zhong Bei shouted, clutching his head.

Cai Jing shot a glare at Da Bei. He had overestimated the latter's reputation. Never did he expect this visit to Shuang Lian would land them in such peril.

Master Cai Jing swiftly cast spells. First, he erected a magician shield to stabilize their position. Then, he swung his staff, flinging away the surrounding snow to clear their vision.

Amidst the chaos, he still found time to plant seeds, waiting for them to take root and sprout.

Magician Da Bei followed suit, casting spells to counter the raging blizzard.

Compared to them, Magician Zhong Bei was merely silver level, utterly inadequate against this natural disaster.

In truth, even the two gold level magicians found it difficult.

“This is bad. My gold level spells aren't packing their usual punch.” Master Cai Jing quickly sensed something amiss.

"This is Magician Shuang Lian's demi-plane. She's fortified it too well. Fighting here, we enemies suffer severe suppression!" Da Bei hurriedly explained.

Cai Jing's heart sank. She urgently asked, “Shuang Lian is only gold level. How could she possess a demi-plane?”

Da Bei replied, "Legend has it that she stumbled upon a young demi-plane during a stellar exploration. After capturing it, she bound the demi-plane to the Snow Honey Tower. She invested immense resources and nurtured it tirelessly. Now, even if it hasn't reached maturity, it's no infant!"

Cai Jing was speechless with envy.

“You still have the nerve to chat!” Shuang Lian's icy voice cut through the blizzard.

The next instant, the storm erupted into fury.

Countless ice blasts, ice blades, and ice arrows hurled toward the three magicians.

Spikes of ice erupted from the ground, densely packed like a phalanx of spears.

Cai Jing and his companions scrambled upward, nearly frozen solid by the gale. They had no choice but to land again, enduring the ice spike assault head-on.

Frost Magic - Ice Blade, Ice Arrow, Ice Ground Spike.

Frost Magic - Cold Wind, Tornado - Snow, Blizzard.

Frost Magic - Freezing, Frostbite, Snowflake Dance.

As Cai Jing fought back, he recognized the diverse formation of frost magic spells unleashed by Shuang Lian.

“She wields these spells with such natural ease, devoid of any trace of an amateur's clumsiness.”

“Damn it! Are you sure she's a magician specializing in alchemy, not a frost-elemental combat magician?”

Before arriving, Cai Jing had gleaned much information about Shuang Lian from Da Bei. Comparing this to the current situation, he felt the gap between intelligence and reality was absurdly huge!

Da Bei sensed Cai Jing's glare and hurriedly explained, “Magician Shuang Lian has at least three super magic formations deployed across the demi-plane!”

“What good does that do now?” Cai Jing sneered. “Hand over your son.”

“In my opinion, Shuang Lian just wants to teach your son a profound lesson!”

"You're a veteran of the Alchemy Guild, and I've only just joined. We're both gold level members, Shuang Lian wouldn't kill us outright."

Without a moment's hesitation, Da Bei rejected Cai Jing's suggestion outright.

No matter how disappointed he was in Zhong Bei, he was his son, his only son. How could he possibly hand Zhong Bei over?

Da Bei wanted to fight to the bitter end: "Hold out a little longer! Shuang Lian sucked us into this demi-plane precisely to prevent the guild from intervening in our battle."

“Those intense spatial fluctuations just now must have drawn plenty of attention.”

“If we hold out a little longer, the guild will send reinforcements!”

Faced with Shuang Lian's relentless assault, Da Bei dared not even dream of victory. His sole aim now was to stall for time until help arrived!

But how could Shuang Lian be unaware of his scheme? In the next instant, a piercing, icy wind swept into the hearts of Cai Jing and the others.

A wave of sorrow and grief surged from the depths of their souls.

In an instant, this emotion grew overwhelming, like a dam bursting, swiftly engulfing their hearts.

Their eyes reddened, and tears of profound sadness streamed down their faces.

They were overcome with grief, their minds in turmoil and no longer stable, causing their spellcasting failure rate to skyrocket.

Zhong Bei fared the worst, already half-kneeling in the snow, utterly unable to resist.

Tears and snot streaming down his face, he clung to the hem of Magician Da Bei's robes, wailing in agony until his voice cracked.

“Stop wailing! It sounds like I'm already dead!” Da Bei cursed, his own grief welling up. Fearing spell failure, he was forced to slow the construction of his magic model.

This drastically reduced his casting frequency, causing the already precarious defensive circle to shrink immediately.

The terrifying blizzard, like an avalanche of snow, bore down upon the three.

“Did Shuang Lian also study mental magic?” Cai Jing cried out to Da Bei, “At this rate, we're doomed!”

“Not a single seed I planted has sprouted. If you have any trump cards left, play them now.”

Gritting his teeth, Da Bei pulled a scroll from his robe.

He tore it open, unleashing a defensive spell.

A saint level aura!

In the next instant, towering ice walls shot up from all directions, encircling the trio. Soon, ice walls coalesced beneath their feet and above their heads, sealing them completely within.

Chapter 730: Shuang Lian – I Don’t Approve of Your Marriage!

The ice wall towered so high it rivaled the city walls in scale.

Blizzards raged, yet they could not breach the ice barrier.

The space enclosed by the ice wall was vast and open, making Cai Jing and his companions feel as if they stood at the foot of a city rampart.

Cai Jing gasped heavily, staring at the ice wall in awe. “Could this be a saint level spell?”

“It once was,” Da Bei replied. “This scroll was excavated from an ancient ruin. Originally saint level, its power has greatly diminished due to lack of maintenance.”

“In their quest to study the ancient frost magic documented within, the Alchemy Guild conducted extensive experiments on it, further weakening its potency.”

“By the time it reached me, only a fraction of its saint level power remained.”

No sooner had he finished speaking than a deafening crack echoed through the air.

The trio witnessed a massive crack appear in the ice wall.

Cai Jing and the others froze in horror.

“It couldn't even withstand that?” Zhong Bei clutched his head, utterly discomposed, his emotions already shattered.

“So Elder Shuang Lian hadn't been using her full strength before,” Da Bei's expression went blank.

Cai Jing's face darkened. "We can't count on reinforcements. We must save ourselves!"

At this point, Cai Jing suddenly shouted, “Magician Shuang Lian, you are so powerful, we humbly admit defeat!”

“Magician Zhong Bei has been terrified into paralysis by your might, cowering before your battle aura.”

“If you wish to discipline him, I fully agree. My coming here was never to support him.” You may have misunderstood my purpose!"

Upon hearing this, Da Bei's expression instantly changed.

But Cai Jing had no time to care for him.

After all, he had already used Magician Da Bei as a conduit to connect with the Alchemy Guild, even becoming one of its members himself.

For Master Cai Jing, the urgent priority was to appease the enraged Elder Shuang Lian.

Clearly aware of what Shuang Lian valued, he continued pleading, “Your student, Bu Quan, is in grave danger. You may not know this yet, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is facing immense trouble.”

“They've been attacked by a mysterious organization. Their leader was ambushed in Cold Wind Valley, and their caravan was destroyed by a gold level assassin.”

"The highest level of personnel the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps left behind in Snow Bird Harbor are merely silver level. If I were the enemy, I would certainly strike at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ weakest point."

“Therefore, Bu Quan is now in grave danger as well.”

These words immediately struck a chord with Shuang Lian.

In truth, before Shuang Lian could finish reading all the letters, Cai Jing and the others arrived.

Cai Jing's words could be easily verified.

For Shuang Lian, obtaining the latest intelligence on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was straightforward.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom's Alchemy Guild was a colossal, nationwide organization, with branches covering Ice Sculpture Island and extending to numerous surrounding islands. It maintained outposts or shops in nearly every town. Even in remote, uninhabited areas, covert alchemy laboratories were often established.

The Alchemy Guild possessed considerable financial resources. Thus, beyond its extensive network and natural access to vast information, it also had ample means to purchase intelligence. The Thieves' Guild would never neglect such a major client!

After Shuang Lian used her privileges to investigate the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ recent activities through the Alchemy Guild, the raging blizzard disaster finally began to subside somewhat.

Cai Jing had struck a nerve with Shuang Lian.

Suppressing her anger, Shuang Lian refocused her attention and swiftly scanned the remaining letters from Bu Quan.

Finally, in the last letter, Shuang Lian discovered Bu Quan's plea for help.

“That foolish child not only sold herself but gifted her entire alchemy workshop to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“It is precisely because of these workshops that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps possesses the capability to mass-produce dragon rider potions.”

“The potions themselves are merely decent, but they capitalized on a gap in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's potion market, creating enormous influence.”

“The Alchemy Guild reacted too slowly. Their structure is bloated, filled with many incompetent members, and they've only just begun operations in this area.”

“Strange. Logically, the Jing Xiang Clan should be the leading force in the potion market. Have they not moved either?”

“Is it because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is collaborating with the Li Jian Clan that the Jing Xiang Clan hesitates? No. Perhaps the Jing Xiang Clan has already taken action. Could they be behind the recent ambush that destroyed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' transport convoy?"

Though devoted to her alchemy experiments, Shuang Lian had risen to become a tower master through sharp intellect and strategic acumen.

She quickly analyzed the intelligence and assessed the current situation. Her conclusion mirrored that of Master Cai Jing, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in dire straits, and Bu Quan's position was equally perilous.

“Given the circumstances, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has realized the weakness of their branch.”

"Hmph, they actually used Little Quan Quan to leverage my forces! "

“This Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is too cunning. Long Fu exploiting Little Quan Quan's power and connections like this, his motives are impure. He's definitely a very shrewd scumbag!”

“Little Quan Quan, I'm sorry. A scumbag like that doesn't deserve you.”

“Ah, it's all my fault for focusing only on teaching you alchemy knowledge and neglecting to train you in love.”

“This time, your teacher will learn from past mistakes. Don't blame me, I won't tolerate a relationship like yours! I refuse to approve of your marriage!”

With this resolve, Shuang Lian's focus on disciplining Zhong Bei and the others faded.

Dealing with Zhong Bei could wait.

But Bu Quan's situation was urgent and perilous, prioritizing her safety was the wise choice.

The blizzard's fury gradually subsided.

Sensing the shift outside, Magician Zhong Bei instantly realized that Cai Jing's words had struck a chord with Shuang Lian.

He hastily shouted, offering to pay any price to appease Elder Shuang Lian's wrath.

Da Bei had come prepared, swiftly retrieving vast quantities of magician resources from his storage gear.

Among them, specifically tailored to appeal to her tastes, were many rare, high-grade alchemy materials.

Though the Da Bei himself lacked combat prowess, his status and seasoned diplomatic skills allowed him to accumulate quite a few valuable items over time.

These offerings as compensation tapped into his own reserves.

He endured the pain of parting with them, knowing he had no choice.

After all, Zhong Bei was his own flesh and blood!

By now, the massive ice wall was riddled with cracks, through which gusts of snow and wind were seeping.

Shuang Lian noticed the resources Magician Da Bei had produced. Only then did she truly confirm: Magician Da Bei and his party had indeed come to make amends.

The blizzard weakened again, its power reduced to less than a third of its previous intensity, yet it still raged on relentlessly.

From the blizzard's transformation, Cai Jing grasped the subtle message Shuang Lian intended to convey. Gritting his teeth, he retrieved the inheritance information gained from Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong.

Da Bei was startled but showed no intention of stopping him.

The offering of the Master Fan Sheng Za Zhong's inheritance information finally satisfied Shuang Lian, temporarily quelling her fury.

The next moment, the three were transported back to the Alchemy Guild Headquarters, standing once more before the Snow Honey Tower.

“We've been kicked out of the demi-plane!”

“Magician Shuang Lian accepted our compensation...”

Cai Jing and Da Bei quickly exchanged a glance, each seeing in the other's eyes the relief of having narrowly escaped death and overcome the crisis.

“Waaah...” As for Zhong Bei, he lay sprawled on the ground, still sobbing uncontrollably.

The teleportation ripples vanished in an instant, and Shuang Lian materialized atop the mage tower from the demi-plane.

“Little Quan Quan, your situation is dire. I'm bringing you back!” Shuang Lian resolved, beginning to manipulate the mage tower.

The entire Snow Honey Tower radiated a pale blue glow.

Within mere seconds, the light intensified, blindingly bright.

The tower trembled slightly, the ground quaked faintly, and the Alchemy Guild Headquarters blared warning sirens.

Surrounding mage towers immediately emitted magic fluctuations. Numerous tower spirits sent messages, inquiring what the Snow Honey Tower intended to do.

The response from the Snow Honey tower spirit was that everything is being personally controlled by the tower master.

“What else could it be?” A faint smile touched the corners of Shuang Lian's lips, her eyes flashing with a cold light.

In the next instant, the Snow Honey Tower lifted off the ground and began to rise slowly.

The students and disciples living within the tower were caught off guard, jolted violently, and some fell to the ground. Numerous alchemy reagent bottles shattered with a series of loud crashes against the floor tiles.

Amidst the apprentices' screams and the surrounding tower spirit's inquiries, and the stunned gazes of Cai Jing, Da Bei, and the others, the Snow Honey Tower ascended into the sky.

Then, it suddenly erupted with a powerful radiance, propelling the entire tower like a giant ignited rocket, soaring skyward!

In the blink of an eye, the Snow Honey Tower vanished, shooting into the clouds and disappearing from everyone's sight.

All that remained was the Alchemy Guild still blaring its sirens, along with a massive crater where the Snow Honey Tower had once stood.

“It, it just flew away like that?!” Cai Jing gasped, her mouth slightly agape.

Da Bei swallowed hard, struggling to process it. "Though Shuang Lian relied on the Alchemy Guild to build this mage tower, her debts were long since paid off."

"She established the nation's largest Snow Honey production base within the demi-plane."

"Every year, the Snow Honey sold externally is in such high demand that it can't meet the demand."

"What's most terrifying is that her primary customer is the kingdom's military!"

Master Cai Jing swallowed hard as well.

Intense envy and jealousy surged within his heart.

Snow Honey.

He knew it was a peculiar resource, edible by humans, not only satisfying hunger but also replenishing battle qi. It could also be used in ships, converted into magic power when placed in magic pools, though its conversion rate was lower than ice elements.

Snow Honey was so practical it had long become standard issue for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's army and navy.

Shuang Lian had amassed a fortune through it, becoming rich beyond measure.

Her mage tower was named the Snow Honey Tower for a reason!

The current head of the Alchemy Guild grew anxious, fearing Cai Jing and the others might fail in their task. He urgently contacted Shuang Lian: "Elder Shuang Lian, where are you headed? Elder Da Bei doesn't have my backing. I hope no unnecessary misunderstandings arise between us."

Shuang Lian replied coldly: "I'm going to Snow Bird Harbor to bring back my student. Keep Pit No.3 open for me."

"Understood, this spot is yours. I hope you have a fruitful journey and return quickly!" The Alchemy Guildmaster sighed a breath of relief, feeling assured.

Chapter 731: Food Eating Competition – Pickled Fish Sushi

The Snow Bird Arena was the largest dueling ground in Snow Bird Harbor. Previously, the dragonman youngster had slain Teng Dong Lang here, making this arena famous throughout the kingdom.

The arena owner had profited handsomely as a result, and to this day, sales of the magic footage from that duel remained exceptionally strong.

The spectator stands of Snow Bird Arena were now packed to the rafters.

The showdown between two gold level magic chefs ignited the fervor of Snow Bird Harbor's citizens, who flocked to witness the spectacle.

The arena floor had been specially modified, divided into two halves, with boilers and other culinary infrastructure set up.

Both chefs had taken their positions, standing in their respective halves and gazing at each other across the divide.

Unlike their earlier verbal sparring in Snow Bird Square, they now focused on inspecting their equipment and utensils.

The giant chef tossed aside the large pot provided by the arena, replacing it with an iron wok strapped to his back.

Ci Hai Shen retrieved a set of knives of varying sizes from his storage device, arranging them on the long kitchen counter.

As this was a contest of culinary prowess, neither of these esteemed gold level magic chefs employed sous chefs or assistants, only themselves.

Following tradition, the most influential figure among the spectators would declare the duel's commencement.

But the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord City wasted no words, announcing the start directly.

Da Dou roared with laughter: “Ci Hai Shen, this time you're doomed to lose, ha ha ha!”

Golden flames erupted from his palms, igniting instantly.

Fueled by gold battle qi, the flames roared fiercely, nearly engulfing the iron wok.

The iron wok, radiating a silver level aura, slowly turned crimson under the scorching flames.

Da Dou retrieved one coconut after another from his storage device.

A common ingredient - butter coconut.

He cracked open each coconut, pouring their full contents of coconut oil into the wok.

The iron level wok possessed spatial efficiency; the vast quantity of coconut oil poured in merely formed a gradually accumulating layer at the bottom.

The coconut oil sizzled in the scorching iron wok, quickly releasing a rich, golden aroma that billowed outward.

However, the magic formation conjured by the Snow Bird had already taken effect, isolating the scents and preventing them from drifting toward Ci Hai Shen.

Before Ci Hai Shen lay a kitchen counter laden with a vast formation of sea fish.

Each fish was plump and fleshy, their scales predominantly green, ranging from deep forest green to pale emerald. The deeper greens often clustered together, forming distinct patterns.

The staple ingredient - pickled fish.

Ci Hai Shen channeled his spirit, precisely controlling each kitchen knife.

In the next instant, over twenty knives of varying sizes floated into the air.

Swish, swish, swish...

The knives, anchored by the countertop, circled the arranged pickled fish, slicing relentlessly. Their speed created a blur of blades and light in the air.

Under this storm of knives, the pickled fish was swiftly cut into uniform slices.

Each fish slice was remarkably uniform in size and thickness. The remaining fish head and bones were mentally guided off the work surface.

Fresh batches of pickled fish continuously emerged from storage units onto the counter, where they were swiftly transformed into mountains of sashimi amidst the flashing blades.

Discarded bones, heads, tails, and scraps were promptly deposited into the trash bin beside the counter.

The audience erupted in cheers.

Even as the two gold level magic chefs engaged in the most fundamental culinary showdown, the spectacle was already breathtaking.

Both demonstrated rock-solid foundational skills.

This only heightened the crowd's anticipation for the upcoming rounds.

Tripleblade and his group sat in the front row of the spectator seats.

The goblin watched the match while inquiring about the odds.

A flurry of betting pools had opened around this magic chef duel.

Tripleblade hoped to get a piece of the action. Though he understood gambling carried high risks, he desperately needed funds. After much deliberation, he decided to take the chance!

Meanwhile, in the viewing chamber, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sat alongside the Sea Bird Bishop.

The magic projection before them offered far greater detail than what most spectators could see with the naked eye. Magnified, it even revealed the subtle shifts in the chefs' gazes.

This undoubtedly provided a superior viewing experience.

Besides these two gold level figures, a third silver level presence was present.

It was Mei Lan Du, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's personal chef.

As a magic chef, Mei Lan Du was deeply intrigued by this duel. Yet his primary reason for being in the viewing room stemmed from the Snow Bird Harbor Lord's arrangement.

He required an insider like Mei Lan Du to serve as commentator when entertaining the Sea Bird Bishop.

After all, a culinary showdown differs significantly from a typical heroic duel. Often, the true brilliance only becomes apparent after the commentary.

At that moment, Mei Lan Du spoke up: “Clearly, Lord Ci Hai Shen's first dish selection is the pickled fish sushi.”

“This is an extremely popular, commonplace dish, consumed by households across coastal regions.”

"The primary ingredient is pickled fish."

"Lord Ci Hai Shen selected this dish precisely because of its familiarity. Common popularity signifies that it aligns perfectly with the masses' tastes and has consistently garnered acclaim."

"This is a culinary showdown, a battle of appetites, distinct from Lord Ci Hai Shen's previous tasting events. Frankly, tasting events often involve dishes with flavors that are hard to describe. But in this kind of contest, both sides will prepare a popular dish like pickled fish sushi."

“This approach ensures the vast audience participating in the tasting later will have no difficulty accepting it.”

“Another reason is that the preparation process for this dish is quite simple, saving Lord Ci Hai Shen a significant amount of time and effort.”

Swish, swish, swish...

The blade flashed incessantly before Ci Hai Shen.

“I didn't expect him to possess decent knife skills,” the Sea Bird Bishop remarked, watching the magic projection.

"But that's about it. At best, it's culinary artistry, hardly a lethal technique," added the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. He cultivated not only magic but also battle qi, mastering numerous battle skills. His words carried weight in this matter.

Mei Lan Du remarked, “Lord Ci Hai Shen specializes in magic, but regardless of their path, any transcendent can become a magic chef.”

“Truthfully, many magic chefs possess exceptional swordsmanship.”

"One such figure is Hei Xia, a bearman from the Wilderness Continent. He specialized in battle qi with remarkable talent. In his prime, he participated in the campaign against an evil god, only to lose both eyes permanently. Forced into retirement, he reappeared in public later in life as an exceptionally skilled magic chef."

"His greatest strength remained his legendary blade techniques. Even blind, he could sense the vulnerabilities of any living creature. When cutting meat, he could follow the muscle fibers precisely, ensuring every slice retained its original flavor."

Upon hearing this, the Sea Bird Bishop instantly grasped the truth and blurted out, “So this Hei Xia has mastered spiritual power, harnessing spiritual energy?”

“Precisely.” Mei Lan Du paid the Sea Bird Bishop a compliment. “Lord Bishop's wisdom has reached the truth.”

“The knife skills Hei Xia wields make his attacks even more terrifying than in his prime.”

“It's akin to the eastern battle qi realm of the Sheng Ming Continent, which has long studied stillness cultivation. The purpose is for fighters to tap into the power of their own minds and spirits.”

“Of course, if a chef wields a tool like the Meat Eye, they too can instantly discern the muscle grain of any cut, glimpsing another realm of culinary artistry.”

Mei Lan Du suddenly brought up the Meat Eye, the very item Da Dou had staked as his wager this time.

Clearly, his explanation served a purpose, not idle chatter. Mentioning Hei Xia was to illustrate the Meat Eye's function.

Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen engaged in an eating contest, each staking their wager.

The former offered the Meat Eye; the latter presented the Preservation Knife.

Mei Lan Du's commentary rekindled the Sea Bird Bishop's curiosity. The Sea Bird Bishop inquired, “Explain the functions of these two kitchen tools in detail for us.”

“Certainly,” Mei Lan Du replied. "First, the Meat Eye, as I mentioned earlier, allows the magic chef to instantly master spiritual power. It enables them to discern the secrets of meat ingredients and cut them in the optimal manner. In combat, as long as the enemy possesses flesh and blood, it allows the magic chef to instantly pinpoint their weaknesses and vulnerabilities."

"As for the Preservation Knife, as its name suggests, using this blade to cut ingredients—whether meat, vegetables, or fruit—preserves their freshness at the moment of cutting. This is incredibly practical when preparing dishes! Remember, the fresher the ingredients, the more they reveal their true flavor and retain their full nutritional value.

The gold level Preservation Knife is essentially a part of Lord Ci Hai Shen's power. Should he lose this treasured blade, his strength would inevitably plummet."

"Likewise, if Da Dou were to lose the Meat Eye, the same would hold true."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly inquired, "The Meat Eye serves as an excellent combat aid. But what about the Preservation Knife in actual combat? How effective is it for cutting through flesh?"

Mei Lan Du was taken aback by the question: "I honestly don't know about that. I've never heard of Lord Ci Hai Shen using the Preservation Knife to attack someone either."

He paused to think before continuing: "Truthfully, as a magic chef, it's extremely rare for us to use kitchen tools in combat.

For one thing, kitchen tools were originally designed for cooking, not fighting. Even the slightest trace of blood on a tool can irreparably ruin a dish. In truth, many kitchen implements prove unsatisfactory in actual combat."

"On the other hand, the market for kitchenware isn't as robust as that for conventional weapons or gear. The higher the grade, the rarer the item, and those who possess them treasure them dearly. Even non-magic chefs typically maintain them meticulously, aiming to trade or collect them later."

“Gold level kitchenware is already a rarity on the market.”

“That's why, after Lord Da Dou proposed the Meat Eye as a wager, Lord Ci Hai Shen was tempted and agreed to stake his own Preservation Knife.”

As the conversation reached this point, the duel arena shifted once more.

Streams of people moved in orderly lines, entering the center of the arena.

They approached rows of long tables, picked up the pickled fish sushi, and placed it in their mouths with eager anticipation.

The tasting had begun.

Chapter 732: Food Eating Competition – Fluttering Boat Fried Rice

“Wow, this is delicious!”

"MasterChef cooking skills truly make a difference. Even without transcendent ingredients, achieving such incredible flavor is unbelievable."

“The pickled fish was freshly sliced, so its tangy flavor is intense, yet it doesn't overpower the fish's natural sweetness!”

“The fish is incredibly tender and melts in your mouth. My entire palate is filled with pure bliss.”

"After the tangy and spicy notes, the fresh, sweet aroma of the rice floods your senses. Even though it's just ordinary rice, it's cooked to perfection, so smooth and silky it adds an extra layer of exquisite texture!"

The crowd savored each bite, their praise flowing endlessly.

“Huh? How did it all disappear?”

“Mine! Save some for me!”

The last few fish sushi pieces sparked a scramble.

Those who had tasted it were left wanting more, glancing toward Ci Hai Shen only to find him already preparing other dishes.

“It's so popular—why not make more?” In the observation room, the Sea Bird Bishop took a bite of the pickled fish sushi before setting it aside.

As a VIP in the viewing chamber, he needn't queue; freshly prepared delicacies were personally delivered to him.

“Though merely an ordinary dish, it is indeed delicious.” Remarked the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord beside him, also leaving his portion unfinished.

Mei Lan Du explained, “This relates to the specific rules of the eating contest.”

“Participating magic chefs must prepare dishes meeting minimum standards for diners across all life levels.”

“Starting from mortal level, only after satisfying the minimum requirements can they advance to the higher bronze level.”

“Similarly, after producing a certain quantity of bronze level dishes that meet the requirements, they can then prepare iron level dishes.”

“Lord Ci Hai Shen's strategy is clear: abandoning the pursuit of mortal level points to swiftly create higher level dishes and secure greater personal points.”

“Personal points?” inquired the Sea Bird Bishop.

Mei Lan Du explained, “The eating contest is a method of competition among magic chefs, distinct from actual combat. Therefore, alternative methods are used to calculate the outcome.”

“Each type of magic chef contest employs different scoring systems.”

"Take the current eating contest as an example: each mortal consuming one portion equals one point. Each bronze level transcendent consuming one portion equals ten points. Iron level earns one hundred points, silver level one thousand points, and gold level ten thousand points!"

The bishop understood: “Points increase tenfold with each level. Naturally, crafting higher level dishes is more profitable.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord added, “However, from what I know, the higher the level of the transcendent dish, the harder it is to create. If a creation fails, the magic chef loses more than just morale. Their transcendent powers are deeply involved in the cooking process. Much like casting spells or alchemy, failure brings backlash."

The bishop glanced at his leftover pickled fish sushi and subconsciously rubbed his stomach. “I'm already looking forward to gold level delicacies, hahaha.”

Mei Lan Du poured cold water on his enthusiasm: “Tasting a gold level dish in an eating contest won't be so easy.”

“What do you mean?” the bishop asked.

Mei Lan Du explained: “Gold level dishes are extremely difficult to prepare. Failure deals a severe blow to both the physical and mental state of the magic chef.”

“In a culinary contest like this eating challenge, the magic chef only needs to defeat their opponent.”

“Therefore, once one side gains a significant lead in points, they won't risk preparing a gold level dish to play it safe.”

"If the contest is evenly matched, neither side will take such risks. They'll typically compete at the silver and iron levels, producing large quantities of dishes at those levels."

“Only when the remaining transcendent tasters among the audience can no longer eat, and the points left to be earned are scarce, do the points for gold level dishes become most valuable. It is at this moment that magic chefs will prepare gold level dishes on the spot.”

“Another scenario is when the magic chef's own strength is overwhelmingly superior, making gold level dishes mere trifles for them.”

With this explanation, the bishop finally understood.

Because the competition was points-based, the primary battleground for eating contests remained within the iron and silver levels.

For gold level magic chefs like Ci Hai Shen and Da Dou, preparing dishes of this level offered greater certainty, they faced no risk of failure or backlash. Moreover, this level contained the largest number of transcendents, making it the most crucial group to compete for.

The city lord suddenly asked, “Can points be counted for both sides?”

Mei Lan Du nodded. “If someone eats both Lord Ci Hai Shen's dish and Da Dou's meal, both magic chefs will receive corresponding points.”

"But don't forget, transcendent dishes aren't to be consumed lightly. Consuming any transcendent dish places a burden on the body. The potent energy and abundant nutrients within transcendent delicacies require timely digestion."

"Therefore, in eating contests, magic chefs select dishes with three characteristics. First, they cater to popular tastes. Second, they are easy to prepare with high success rates. Third, they are substantial and filling—once you eat mine, you'll struggle to eat my opponent's."

Hearing this, the city lord furrowed his brow slightly, sensing an anomaly: “That holds true for transcendent dishes, but for mortals, the burden of ordinary meals is solely on the stomach.”

“Precisely,” nodded Mei Lan Du. “This is a unique advantage of mortals. Mortals can consume vast quantities of food, unlike transcendents who typically feel full after just one or two servings of dishes of the same level.”

"Thus, food duels usually involve two tactics. The first, chosen by Lord Ci Hai Shen, is rapidly advancing in level. The second involves delaying advancement, remaining at the mortal level to produce vast quantities of meals. Though mortals earn only one point each, they can consume multiple portions. Moreover, mortals are the most numerous!"

“Hmm...” Mei Lan Du paused abruptly, staring at the magic projection. “It seems Lord Da Dou has adopted the second tactic.”

In the projection, Da Dou also retrieved rice and poured it into a sizzling oil-filled wok.

Bucket after bucket of rice poured into the iron wok, yet it remained unremarkable.

The iron wok possessed spatial magic, though it was only one vessel, it could store vast quantities of ingredients.

After Da Dou poured over a dozen buckets of rice, the audience finally saw the white grains cover nearly half the wok's base.

Holding the iron handle in one hand and a long ladle in the other, Da Dou continuously stirred and tossed the rice within.

“So, he's making fried rice,” the crowd realized.

Da Dou then produced eggs with colorful shells, vegetable eggs.

He placed them on the pot's rim, cracked them open, and with deft finger movements, skillfully separated the yolks, whites, and green yolks into the pot.

Unlike regular eggs, vegetable eggs contained not only yolk and white, but also green yolk. This green egg yolk possessed the same nutritional elements as fresh vegetables.

Hundreds of vegetable eggs were cracked one after another, their liquid pouring entirely into the large iron wok.

The liquid solidified rapidly, then was broken up by an iron spoon into small yellow, white, and green chunks or grains.

The rice was stir-fried until golden brown.

With each toss of the large iron wok by the chef, the tri-colored rice flew up like grains of sand, then cascaded down like a golden waterfall, a truly spectacular sight.

"Oh! Its fluttering boat fried rice!" Many recognized it at this sight.

Like the pickled fish sushi from Ci Hai Shen, fluttering boat fried rice was a common dish, a staple on countless family tables.

Both the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Bishop knew this dish.

Mei Lan Du chose to share lesser-known details: “This dish was created by Ou You Wang.”

“He was a magic chef and also a great adventurer of the human race.”

"Once, after weathering a storm, his sea vessel entered a windless zone. A vicious pack of magic beast fish targeted them."

"Without sea winds, Ou You Wang's vessel lost two-thirds of its propulsion. The shipwright had already perished in the earlier tempest."

“Though the crew exhausted every possible means, they could only watch helplessly as the magic beast fish closed in.”

“Despair gripped nearly all aboard, yet Ou You Wang alone clung to his fighting spirit, refusing to surrender prematurely.”

“He gathered the ship's last remaining provisions, some oil and leftover rice from the night before.”

“Using these meager ingredients, Ou You Wang miraculously created the fluttering boat fried rice.”

“After eating the fried rice, the sailors and crew not only regained their morale and fighting spirit, but each stroke of their oars now stirred up waves.”

“Wave after wave propelled the ship forward. With high spirits, it navigated the windless seas, ultimately shaking off the magic beast fish swarm and escaping death!”

“After this battle, Ou You Wang's fame reached its peak.”

"In the end, they returned safely to their point of departure. Their adventurous tale spread far and wide. The method for preparing fluttering boat fried rice was thus passed down."

"To this day, in many places, people eat a bowl of fluttering boat fried rice when a ship sets sail. This tradition carries blessings for the vessel and its crew—wishing them courage and hope, urging them never to give up prematurely, no matter how difficult the journey."

The bishop listened to this tale, already relishing it before even tasting the fried rice.

He murmured softly, "Fluttering boat fried rice, not bad. I rather like this story. I look forward to its flavor."

Soon after, two portions of mortal level fluttering boat fried rice arrived in the observation room.

The Sea Bird Bishop picked up his spoon, took a bite, and his eyebrows twitched.

He took another bite before setting the spoon down. “Not bad. To me, it tastes better than the pickled fish sushi.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord added, "I agree. Perhaps... the story behind the fried rice simply appeals to my palate more."

The next moment, the two gold level powerhouses shared a knowing smile.

Mei Lan Du, the commentator, witnessed this exchange and let a faint smile touch his lips.

This was precisely why he had shared the story.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had chosen him as commentator for reasons far beyond his reputation as a magic chef!

Chapter 733: Prosperous Fire

“So fragrant, so fragrant!”

People poured into the area where Da Dou was stationed. The moment they entered, they were hit by the rich aroma of fried rice.

Their footsteps quickened instinctively as they rushed eagerly toward the long table.

Da Dou's fried rice was served in large bowls, the grains piled high into peaks. This stood in stark contrast to Ci Hai Shen's delicately plated pickled fish sushi, arranged on small, elegant dishes.

Mere mortals scooped up a bowl, took their first bite, and their eyes lit up. With the second bite, saliva began to flow. By the third bite, their spooning motions grew frantic.

“Wow wow wow, this is seriously amazing!”

“I've had fluttering boat fried rice plenty of times but never imagined it could taste this good!”

“With every chew, you fully appreciate the unique texture and delicate flavor of fluttering boat fried rice. Even after swallowing, the lingering taste in your mouth is utterly unforgettable.”

Da Dou's stir-frying never paused, pot after pot filling the wok.

Endless streams of fluttering boat fried rice flowed onto his long table. Mortals lined up orderly, finishing a bowl before patting their full bellies. Reluctantly satisfied, they returned to the end of the queue for seconds.

Lord Da Dou is still making mortal level fried rice?”

“Lord Ci Hai Shen has already prepared three bronze level dishes.”

“It seems that Lord Da Dou's strategy is to dominate the common dishes? He intends to earn massive points from the mortals!”

“This is fantastic! As a mortal, I can finally eat until I'm stuffed this time.”

Cheers erupted throughout the arena.

Compared to the transcendents, mortals were naturally the majority.

Right now, in the hearts of these mortals, Lord Da Dou was far more endearing than Lord Ci Hai Shen!

Conversely, many transcendents held no interest in mortal dishes. Faced with Lord Da Dou's tactical choice, they felt as if they'd been abandoned.

They whispered among themselves.

“Lord Da Dou's approach isn't very wise.”

“Yeah, in past eating contests, few have won relying solely on mortal dishes.”

“Or is he trying to secure his base before pushing for a higher level?”

"Tough. One step behind, and you fall further behind. In terms of level, Ci Hai Shen has already started building an advantage."

"Most bronze level eaters are being severely stuffed by Ci Hai Shen's dishes. Even if Da Dou changes tactics later, he won't gain many points."

Just then, Da Dou pulled out the bronze level ingredients.

Copper-green lobster, flame radish, jet corn...

One hand flipping the wok, the other handling these bronze level ingredients. He multitasked with fluid, natural movements, not a hint of hesitation.

He dumped all the ingredients into the iron wok.

This single action drew gasps from many spectators.

It was an unusual sight.

The wok was meant for stir-frying mortal fluttering boat fried rice, yet fresh ingredients were being added.

What’s going on?

In the observation room, the Sea Bird Bishop asked Mei Lan Du about it.

Mei Lan Du shook his head, admitting he rarely witnessed such a situation. But it likely wasn't a mistake, Da Dou had done this deliberately. What exactly he intended to achieve would become clear soon enough.

Sure enough, before long, a bronze level aura began emanating from the iron wok.

With each toss of the wok, scattered flashes of green light flickered among the golden grains of rice.

Battle skill - Prosperous Fire.

Da Duo publicly unleashed a rare battle skill. The flames it generated merged into the original blaze, paradoxically dampening its intensity.

Yet soon, the green sparks within the fried rice multiplied exponentially!

From mere scattered grains, they grew to dozens, then hundreds, nearly a thousand!

The dense cluster of green specks embedded within the golden cascade of rice shimmered in perfect harmony, neither element overshadowing the other.

"How is that possible?"

"What kind of battle skill is this!"

"He only added those few bronze level ingredients. How did the bronze level fried rice he cooked become so much?"

"Could it be that this massive iron wok holds a vast quantity of bronze level ingredients within its interior?

If that's the case, then Da Dou's actions seem rather pointless..."

The audience gasped in astonishment. Many transcendents could no longer remain seated, rising to their feet to get a better look.

Such a scene was utterly astonishing, defying all logic.

Even Ci Hai Shen paused his movements, staring at Da Dou with astonishment and doubt.

Regardless of the surrounding curiosity, Da Dou showed no desire to explain. Upon noticing Ci Hai Shen's gaze, he burst into laughter and shouted once more: “Ci Hai Shen, I told you, this time, you're bound to lose!”

In the observation room, the Sea Bird Bishop also exclaimed: "Even as an outsider, I know this is highly suspicious. If he could transform ordinary materials into bronze level materials, that alone would make it unnecessary for Da Dou to continue as a magic chef. Directly crafting transcendent materials would instantly make him the world's richest man!"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord turned to look at Mei Lan Du standing behind him: “Can you discern any clues?”

Mei Lan Du's eyes were wide, his gaze sharp and intense. He shook his head slightly. “All I can tell so far is that Lord Da Dou is still making fluttering boat fried rice.”

"But generally speaking, the recipes for mortal level and bronze level fluttering boat fried rice are absolutely different. To prepare two fried rice dishes of different levels, one would typically use two separate pans for distinct cooking processes. Cooking them together in one pot is a common mistake made by novices. Yet Lord Da Dou chose precisely this method. He must have done it intentionally, which also explains the earlier inexplicable phenomenon."

"I'm sorry, City Lord. I can't decipher or explain the situation clearly."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord waved it off: “No matter.”

He found this unconventional eating contest even more intriguing.

Tripleblade's focus, however, lay elsewhere. He anxiously asked through the clamoring crowd: “What are the current odds?”

As long as the duel wasn't over, many betting windows remained open.

Tripleblade quickly got his answer.

As he'd anticipated, Da Dou's earlier baffling display had caused many to place bets on him.

“So, who should I bet on?” Tripleblade raced through his options.

Ci Hai Shen took a deep breath, immediately steadying his emotions.

“Though I don't know what tricks you're playing, I intend to win this duel!”

The Preservation Knife was crucial to him; losing it would be like losing an arm.

Ci Hai Shen couldn't afford to lose!

His bronze level dish already met the standard, so he immediately began preparing his first iron level dish.

“Even if you churn out tons of bronze level fried rice, you're still far behind me in level!” Ci Hai Shen kept a firm grip on the situation.

What reassured him was that the earlier scene never repeated itself in Da Dou's iron wok.

Da Dou retrieved large quantities of bronze level ingredients like the copper-green lobsters from his storage gear and tossed them into the iron wok.

His iron wok held an astonishing capacity; each batch of fluttering boat fried rice he poured out filled an entire bucket.

Da Dou never emptied the entire wok at once. Instead, he poured out only a portion each time, immediately adding fresh ingredients.

This cycle repeated many times until Da Dou's bronze level fried rice finally met the standard.

He could now advance to the iron level competition.

By this point, Ci Hai Shen had already completed over half of the iron requirements, still holding a clear level advantage.

Da Dou methodically retrieved the iron level ingredients.

Though it was a meager portion, he tossed it into the wok and stirred relentlessly.

Then, the astonishing scene repeated itself!

Despite the meager quantity of iron level ingredients, as the fried rice cooked and bubbled, the volume of iron level fried rice within grew exponentially—far exceeding the original amount added.

“It's happening again!”

“What on earth is going on?”

"What recipe is Da Dou using? Is it a hybrid recipe he specifically modified from fluttering boat fried rice?"

The crowd gasped in astonishment.

In the observation room, Mei Lan Du narrowed his eyes. “I've discerned something.”

"Lord Da Dou deliberately mass-cooked those low level dishes. He used them to establish a relatively intense base flavor, boosting the success rate of higher level transcendent dishes and shortening their preparation time."

“Did you two notice? He's already narrowed the gap between himself and Lord Ci Hai Shen.”

Though Mei Lan Du still couldn't fathom how Da Dou's iron level fried rice production far exceeded the ingredients invested, he understood that Da Dou was gradually catching up to Ci Hai Shen.

The iron level transcendents were in for a treat.

They all took their seats, sampling the delicacies prepared by the two magic chefs.

The gold level magic chef's craftsmanship easily satisfied these iron level participants. For a moment, praise poured in like a flood.

Ci Hai Shen's composure faltered.

“What's going on?”

“This can't be Da Dou bluffing. His personality wouldn't let him pull something like this.”

Ci Hai Shen felt an ominous foreboding.

“Its all fluttering boat fried rice... His recipe must have been modified.”

“He also used that battle skill earlier. Could the secret lie in that?”

While pondering, Ci Hai Shen retrieved silver level ingredients.

Having met the iron standard, he immediately advanced to silver level!

Though Da Dou had closed the gap significantly, a difference still remained.

“This is bad!” Tripleblade shared Ci Hai Shen's unease.

Having staked heavily on Ci Hai Shen, he now felt his heart race with anxiety.

He longed for Da Dou to falter, but Da Dou wouldn't grant him that wish.

Da Dou roared, “Ci Hai Shen, your Preservation Knife is mine now, ha ha ha!”

As he spoke, he tossed in a massive amount of silver level ingredients.

Within mere seconds of hitting the wok, they transformed into silver level fluttering boat fried rice.

Piles of fried rice were served onto the long table. This spectacle left countless onlookers wide-eyed in astonishment.

In terms of silver level dishes, Da Dou had just overtaken Ci Hai Shen!

Silver level transcendents descended one after another. After eating a bowl of fluttering boat fried rice, they were stuffed.

At this moment, the fried rice demonstrated its true power!

“Ahhh, too much energy! I must immediately refine my battle qi to digest this!”

“I tried to hold back, but I just couldn't resist eating a huge bowl.”

“It's so delicious, I want more... burp.”

Ci Hai Shen visibly quickened his pace, yet still couldn't match Da Dou's appetite. The latter brought out pot after pot, quickly leaving Ci Hai Shen behind.

“Am... am I losing?” Cold sweat covered Ci Hai Shen's forehead.

“It's over!” Tripleblade's face also turned pale.

He had bet on Ci Hai Shen, but now the situation looked dire.

Da Dou's points had surged far ahead.

Rumble...

Suddenly, a series of explosions echoed from beyond the arena.

Then, a towering column of smoke billowed skyward.

“That direction...”

“What happened?”

“The docks are under attack!”

“Could it be...” Tripleblade's face paled. Just then, his communicator buzzed.

Tripleblade activated it to hear a mercenary wailing: “We're under attack! The Deputy Commander... the Deputy Commander has been assassinated!”

Tripleblade's expression twisted in horror. He leapt to his feet and, accompanied by several mercenaries, charged toward the arena gates.

"Someone attacked my dock?!" In the observation room, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord received the same report.

His face turned ashen.

But then, intelligence from the mage tower clarified: the attackers targeted only the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, and no gold level fighters were deployed—the highest level combatants were silver level.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord exhaled a breath of stale air, relaxing.

This group of enemies seemed to know their limits - they hadn’t touched his property, and the highest level fighter was only silver level.

Without any gold level fighters involved, this was merely a minor skirmish. Not a military incident, but a public security matter.

Chapter 734: The Big Guy Runs Away

Tripleblade and his men swiftly arrived at the docks and boarded the flagship billowing thick black smoke.

Even after the attack had passed, the mercenaries aboard the flagship remained visibly panicked.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, personally trained by Zong Ge and subjected to rigorous drills, possessed military discipline that slightly surpassed that of ordinary silver level mercenary corps. But this was a world of the strong. When the mighty fell, the blow to the morale of the lower ranks was devastating.

In one corner of the deck, a crowd had gathered. Some attempted rescue, while others desperately called out the name “Shi Qi.”

Tripleblade stepped forward, using his influence as a leader to part the crowd. There he saw Zong Ge lying motionless.

To outsiders, Zong Ge appeared to have his eyes tightly shut, a wound on his chest of a shallow depth but one that continued to bleed.

But in Tripleblade's vision, a silver level alchemy puppet lay there. The wound itself was shallow, yet it bore the telltale marks of being washed by foreign battle qi.

Judging by the aura within the markings, the battle qi was only silver level.

“But could silver level battle qi truly destroy an alchemy puppet's energy pathways from the inside out, almost instantly?”

A sharp gleam flashed in Tripleblade's eyes.

He crouched down and touched Zong Ge's wound. His extensive combat experience told him the enemy who attacked “Zong Ge” was far from a simple “silver level” opponent.

“Most likely a gold level in disguise. Silver level battle skills couldn't achieve this level of destruction!” Tripleblade understood the materials used in this silver level puppet.

As for why it was disguised this way, Tripleblade understood: a silver level attacking the docks was entirely different from a gold level assault.

The gap in combat power between the two was vast. A gold level was a decisive regional force capable of stabilizing or destabilizing an entire area's order!

Tripleblade stood up and glanced around, not seeing Chi Lai.

Chi Lai had been assigned to the flagship as part of the defensive force.

Tripleblade urgently inquired where Chi Lai had gone.

The mercenaries replied, “Lord Chi Lai led men in pursuit.”

The attackers targeting the flagship were few in number. Their assault was utterly unexpected, catching the mercenaries completely off guard. After leaving several corpses behind, they retreated swiftly. Their primary objective was to draw attention, providing cover for the assassin.

The assassin struck swiftly, killing “Zong Ge” before vanishing into the distance.

Chi Lai was furious. He believed “Deputy Commander Zong Ge” had been ambushed due to carelessness. The assassin had only displayed silver level skill, so Chi Lai chose to pursue.

Even if he couldn't catch them, he wanted to gather as much intelligence and clues as possible.

“Too reckless!” Tripleblade cursed inwardly, though he didn't blame Chi Lai.

Chi Lai wasn't among the survivors and didn't know the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' true predicament. At most, he knew that the magic potion convoy had once been attacked by a gold level fighter.

But even then, another gold level fighter appeared. The fierce clash between the two gold level fighters had severely disrupted Chi Lai, Men Shi, and their group's assessment of the situation.

Tripleblade immediately ordered men to catch up with Chi Lai and stop him from pursuing further.

“Boss, we've completed the preliminary damage assessment. The losses are far less than we anticipated,” a subordinate reported.

By this time, the lingering flames on the flagship had been extinguished, and the thick smoke had dissipated.

“Please, please, don't let the losses be too heavy.”

“If the damage is too great, the funds I can mobilize might not cover the repairs.”

Tripleblade muttered a prayer as he took the report, his eyes instinctively sweeping the flagship's deck.

He noticed the damage to the flagship was minimal, with the forecastle bearing the brunt of the destruction.

“Wait... the forecastle!”

Tripleblade jolted upright, his attention instantly shifting from the battle damage report. Grabbing a nearby mercenary, he demanded urgently: Where had the giant residing in the forecastle gone?

The mercenaries, still shaken, recounted what followed the assassination attempt: After “Zong Ge” collapsed, explosions and billowing smoke erupted. Terrifying screams then echoed from within the forecastle.

Then the giant smashed through the deck, bellowing and savagely attacking everyone around him.

Numerous mercenaries fell victim to the giant's assault.

After Chi Lai pursued the assassin, the giant turned his attention to another group of retreating enemies and gave chase.

Tripleblade: !!!

The former mercenary captain swiftly grasped the truth: "During the sudden attack, the big guy likely witnessed ‘Zong Ge’ falling. His field of vision is comparable to an outsider's. We chose this approach initially because we feared his intelligence wouldn't allow him to perform and disguise himself convincingly, so we simply concealed him entirely."

The big guy saw Zong Ge fall, his fate unknown. This sight, combined with the chaos of the moment, triggered him. He went berserk, unleashing every ounce of strength to violently fight off every threat around him. Just like how he acted on Mystifying Monster Island.

“With Commander Long Fu absent, the other mercenaries saw him attacking his own men and watched the big guy chase the enemy out. They all breathed a sigh of relief. Choosing to stand by and do nothing was perfectly normal.”

“Damn it! The big guy can't be hurt!”

Tripleblade was frantic.

The big guy's potential was too great. Once Tripleblade accepted this reality, in his heart, the big guy became the survivors' treasure.

“Contact Chi Lai immediately. Order him to abandon pursuit and search for our little giant.” Tripleblade swiftly altered his command.

After a moment's thought, he added, "The enemy attacked our flagship and knocked out Shi Qi. But I'm certain their assault won't stop there."

"You all know how wildly popular the dragon rider potion is! Many covet our profits and don't want us to truly grow into a gold level mercenary group!"

“Therefore, they will definitely attack our alchemy workshop.”

“But we've made defensive preparations. We'll make the enemy suffer.”

“Stay calm. I know Lord Shi Qi possesses a remarkable battle skill that allows him to feign death for healing.”

“Carry Lord Shi Qi back to his cabin.”

Tripleblade worked to steady the troops when suddenly—BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Immediately, a report came in: “Bad news! Our alchemy workshop is under attack!”

“What's the panic for? Lord Di Bi (Tripleblade) anticipated this long ago.” A mercenary shouted back.

Earlier, the survivors had withheld most intelligence from Chi Lai, Men Shi, and ordinary members to maintain stability. Now, Tripleblade deliberately leaked some information at the opportune moment to achieve the same effect.

With Cang Xu, the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and Zi Di all missing, Tripleblade, drawing on his experience managing mercenary groups, barely managed to hold the situation together, slightly stabilizing morale.

Of course, this was only possible because the core foundation of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps remained intact.

Had Long Fu, Shi Qi, or others suffered defeat or death, or if silver level pillars like Chi Lai and Men Shi had fallen, the blow to morale would have been devastating.

At that point, no amount of Tripleblade's silver-tongued eloquence or spin could have salvaged the situation.

This was the world of the strong.

Individual strength was the absolute foundation.

Soon, follow-up intelligence arrived.

Three alchemy workshops had come under attack.

The large workshop had fallen, but the magic traps Tripleblade and his men had secretly laid around it inflicted heavy casualties on the attacking foes.

One of the medium-sized workshops also fell, yet again inflicting considerable losses on the enemy.

At the remaining medium-sized workshop within the Mercury Cave, the enemy attempted to use alchemy devices to disrupt the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' command over the alchemy puppets, an effort that failed. Combined with the protection of numerous magic traps, this workshop managed to hold on by the skin of its teeth.

"Our command protocols for the alchemy puppets have been enhanced by Bu Quan. Not only that, but we switch to a different command method every few days to prevent enemies from exploiting this vulnerability."

"Bu Quan's modifications all stem from the War Merchant's database. Several factors have ensured the alchemy puppets remain unchecked by the enemy, stubbornly fulfilling their defensive role."

“Combined with magic traps and the Mercury Cave's naturally fortified terrain, the enemy hasn't succeeded.”

“Still, that defense remains rigid. Its fall is only a matter of time.”

“The most crucial and valuable small alchemy workshop hasn't been attacked yet...”

Tripleblade's expression hardened as he swiftly assessed the situation.

He had assumed any enemy assault would come later, not now.

After all, it would take time for the enemy to uncover that the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance was secretly transporting goods for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But Mei Lin wasn't just a holy warrior of the Dueling God, she was also an exceptional commander. She understood the importance of seizing the initiative. Within days of Qing Hong Yan's successful ambush on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' convoy, she launched this surprise attack after confirming the dragon rider potion sales in the market remained unaffected.

She didn't target the Dragon Lion's new supply routes, she went straight for the heart of the operation, even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' branch was located within Snow Bird Harbor.

Tripleblade didn't know Mei Lin was behind this, but as he analyzed the situation now, a chill crept into his heart, making him realize just how formidable she was.

Mei Lin hadn't let the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lead her by the nose. Instead, she'd played her cards right and launched a surprise attack directly at the docks.

After all, Snow Bird Harbor's mage tower stood right on the docks.

“The enemy chose their timing perfectly.”

"Why didn't I see this coming? The clash between two gold level magic chefs had left Snow Bird Harbor's defenses at their lowest ebb. Even the mage tower at the docks had focused its attention on the Snow Bird Arena before the attack."

“What should I do next?”

Tripleblade faced two choices.

One was the big guy; he had to be escorted back immediately. The other was the small alchemy workshop where Bu Quan was located. Deeply aware of the enemy's tenacity, Tripleblade harbored no illusions, given their modus operandi, how could they possibly overlook the most crucial and valuable small workshop?

“That place is bound to come under attack!”

The big guy or Bu Quan?

Chapter 735: Yi Jiu vs Men Shi and Bu Quan

Tripleblade racked his brains, thinking rapidly.

Before long, he led a large contingent of mercenaries into the city, heading straight for the small alchemy workshop.

"The big guy's gone mad. He's already iron level now, matching my level. I can't handle him alone."

"Even with other mercenaries, I wouldn't dare kill the big guy outright. Hesitating to strike for fear of collateral damage only wastes our forces!"

"Instead, the small workshop inside the city will surely draw a massive enemy assault. When that happens, our reinforcements will be crucial, we'll finally have a chance to prove our worth."

After weighing these factors, Tripleblade acted decisively.

Within Snow Bird Harbor City.

Yi Jiu concealed himself in the shadows, peering through a window to covertly observe his final target.

It was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' small alchemy workshop.

“I've already killed Shi Qi,” Yi Jiu muttered, “but there was an unexpected turn at the Mercury Cave.”

“But that's none of my concern.”

“Destroy this small alchemy workshop, and I'm outta here!”

As a holy warrior of the Dueling God, Yi Jiu had no ties to the kingdom's military. He was a member of the Thieves' Guild, primarily a hired assassin. As he put it: “As long as the price is right, taking on other side gigs is no problem.”

Thus, this time, Mei Lin had hired him to take action with a handsome sum.

Though Mei Lin acted boldly and directly, she harbored reservations, she didn't want to expose the Dueling God or her own military identity.

Thus, she carefully selected a group of retired military sacrificial warriors. Then she hired Yi Jiu, rather than deploying the likes of Gu Dong or Bao Li Gen.

At the gold level, every individual's intelligence was highly prized by major factions.

If Gu Dong or Bao Li Gen had acted, their identities would likely have been compromised.

Under these circumstances, hiring Yi Jiu as an assassin was exceptionally prudent.

As a master assassin, concealing his life force and disguising himself as a low level transcendent was one of his signature skills. Such camouflage greatly facilitated his assassination operations.

Even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps or the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord investigated afterward, they would only trace it back to Yi Jiu himself. And his primary occupation was assassin, being hired for such a job was perfectly reasonable!

Yi Jiu's mission this time involved not only eliminating core members like Zong Ge from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps but also destroying the small alchemy workshop.

Originally, Yi Jiu intended to take out Chi Lai, who was pursuing him, but time was too tight.

With Chi Lai on guard and clad in silver level gear (thanks to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' relentless acquisition of transcendent equipment, plus their inventory and loaning system), Yi Jiu abandoned that target.

"Forcing a kill on him isn't impossible. But doing so would make my ‘silver level’ combat power exceed my designated role."

“Even if I take out Chi Lai, a key member, the reward afterward wouldn't match the payoff from destroying the alchemy workshop.”

“If I destroy it and still have time, then I can go after targets like Chi Lai and Men Shi.”

Yi Jiu calculated his gains, prioritizing maximum profit in his actions.

Though he had approached the small alchemy workshop, he did not strike immediately, instead waiting cautiously.

This was because his primary objective remained safeguarding his own life!

After all, he was operating solo this time, conducting an open assassination in a major city like Snow Bird Harbor.

Earlier at the docks, the mage tower's attention had been diverted by the Snow Bird Arena. Moreover, the mage tower never operated at full capacity during routine periods, that's why he'd acted.

After eliminating “Shi Qi,” he feared that the mage tower would target him, so he spared Chi Lai and withdrew immediately.

After lying low for a while, Yi Jiu confirmed Snow Bird Harbor's reaction was not overly intense, easing half his concerns.

“You may proceed,” Yi Jiu relayed to his sacrificial warriors via communication.

“Charge!”

“Destroy the alchemy workshop!”

The sacrificial warriors sprang into action.

They emerged from every corner, some wielding blades and wearing helmets, charging head-on; others using ranged weapons to attack from afar.

Their coordination was seamless.

Those leading the frontal assault quickly triggered the magic traps laid around the workshop.

Flames scorched, thunder struck, and ice froze life after life.

Yet the line of magic defenses was soon breached, a path carved through.

“Too few traps were set.”

“But what choice did we have? Within the city walls, every magic defense requires approval!” Bu Quan's heart began to race.

The city government palace permitted only limited magic defenses, for such power could too easily shift from defense to offense.

In fact, many of the magic traps had been secretly laid by Bu Quan.

The previous number alone wouldn't last even ten seconds.

The sacrificial warriors possessed solid military training and had specific countermeasures against magic traps.

"The enemy is closing in too fast! Miss Bu Quan, retreat immediately!" The dwarf Men Shi was tasked with holding this position. The sacrificial warriors' charge revealed the enemy's elite nature, and their death-defying indifference sent a chill through Men Shi's heart.

Intelligence about the attacks on the docks and other workshops had already been relayed.

Men Shi had prepared his defenses, but the moment combat began, he knew holding this position with his men alone would be extremely difficult!

Fortunately, the alchemy workshop contained a teleportation formation for retreat.

Yet Bu Quan refused to heed the warning. She shook her head, clinging to optimism: “The workshop won't fall so easily.”

“We must hold our ground. The other workshops have fallen. This is the most crucial one, it absolutely cannot be lost!”

Men Shi's heart sank. He instantly realized Bu Quan lacked combat experience and had made a grave misjudgment of the situation. The dwarf hurriedly pleaded again, "Miss Bu Quan, compared to the alchemy workshop, you are the most important! You absolutely cannot be lost. If anything happens to you, the commander will blame me."

Mentioning the dragonman youngster worked like magic. Bu Quan immediately stood tall and firm, her resolve to stay and fight strengthened.

For the sake of her beloved, she would defend this last remaining potion production base.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

After clearing a path with their lives, the sacrificial warriors charged fiercely at the workshop's entrance.

But when swords and axes struck the door, they only triggered a defensive barrier.

A metallic sheen enveloped the entire workshop, leaving no blind spots. This thin layer of light simultaneously withstood the slashes and impacts of numerous weapons, producing a series of clanging metallic sounds.

Inside the workshop, Men Shi frowned deeply, directing his dwarven subordinates to deploy their limited forces to the most vulnerable defensive points.

Bu Quan smiled: “See? I made plenty of preparations in advance.”

Men Shi sighed and said nothing more.

Battle skill - Stealth.

Battle skill - Silencing.

Battle skill - Wall Walking.

Battle skill - Eavesdropping.

Empowered by four battle skills, Yi Jiu had already crawled onto the workshop's roof.

From the sounds emanating inside, he swiftly discerned the dwarves' troop distribution and main force positions.

He lightly stroked the ring on his finger, and a magic glow flashed across its surface.

In the next instant, the enchantments on the alchemy ring activated, releasing their effects upon Yi Jiu.

Enchanted Spell - Cat's Agility.

Enchanted Spell - Ox's Endurance.

Enchanted Spell - Frog's Leap.

This was the Three Beasts Power Ring. When Yi Jiu had devised its alchemy, he had deliberately chosen the cat, ox, and frog.

Cat's Agility granted him enhanced agility; Ox's Endurance allowed him to adapt to more environments and slightly increased his magic resistance. Frog's Leap dramatically boosted his jumping ability.

After applying the spells, Yi Jiu touched the ring ornament on his forehead. This was his primary defensive item, containing the arcane spell Counter Spell.

Should a powerful spell strike him, he just had to channel his spirit to nullify it.

“The strongest fighter in the workshop is Men Shi. He's only silver level and specializes solely in defense.”

“The real threats to me are Bu Quan's magic abilities and her alchemy tools!”

Yi Jiu, although holding a significant rank advantage as a gold level fighter, remained extremely cautious and unwaveringly vigilant.

Battle skill - Wall Penetration!

Battle skill technique - Piercing Force!

Wall Penetration allowed Yi Jiu to effortlessly traverse walls and other obstacles. Piercing Force was one of the techniques applied to battle skills, granting piercing attack effects.

To ensure absolute success, Yi Jiu not only employed the gold level Wall Penetration but also layered on Piercing Force.

This allowed him to instantly pierce through the magic protective aura of the alchemy workshop, then penetrate the roof, landing directly inside the workshop.

The moment he succeeded, a shrill alarm blared throughout the workshop.

Bu Quan cried out nervously, “Watch out! An enemy has infiltrated!”

“Where?” Men Shi asked urgently.

The dwarves also looked around frantically, tension mounting.

But no matter what methods Bu Quan tried, she couldn't locate the invisible Yi Jiu.

A heavy weight settled in Men Shi's heart as he urged again, "Miss Bu Quan, you really should leave this place immediately. A powerful stealth user must have breached the defenses and could be anywhere in the workshop now. It's far too dangerous here!"

Bu Quan still couldn't quite believe it: "Nothing's been damaged, and I haven't detected any enemies either. Maybe it was just a false alarm?"

Men Shi grew increasingly anxious inside. In this kind of battle situation, Bu Quan was actually pinning her hopes on a false alarm, how naive!

But no matter how he tried to persuade her, Bu Quan refused to leave.
