Instead, she reassured Men Shi: "Maybe it really is a false alarm. Don't worry, our defenses are solid. Don't underestimate me, I can be a huge help to you all."

“If it comes down to it, I can always use the teleportation formation to leave. It's not too late.”

Men Shi froze, about to protest, but Bu Quan had already spoken.

The dwarf's voice grew increasingly muffled as he cautioned, “The enemy is likely lurking nearby, Miss Bu Quan. Next time, don't recklessly reveal our trump cards.”

“I... I'll be more careful next time.” Bu Quan covered her mouth, suddenly realizing that when Men Shi had urged her to leave earlier, he hadn't mentioned the teleportation formation at all. Yet she had exposed it voluntarily.

“Sigh...” Men Shi let out a deep sigh, thinking to himself, “I hope reinforcements arrive soon!”

Chapter 736: Speak! Do You or Do You Not Like Him?

Yi Jiu remained in a nearby corner, observing and listening to every exchange between Men Shi and Bu Quan.

He had deliberately chosen a spot close to Men Shi and Bu Quan when passing through the wall.

“So, there's a hidden teleportation formation here after all.”

“Just as the intelligence reports stated, Bu Quan is weak in combat and specializes solely as an alchemist.”

“Dwarf Men Shi, his attack methods are unimpressive, posing minimal threat.”

"Sigh..."

“If I'd mastered the Penetration Force instead of the Piercing Force, this wall-crossing wouldn't have been detected.”

Yi Jiu had never intended to assassinate Bu Quan.

When Mei Lin devised this assault plan, she had spared Bu Quan as well. There was never any intent to harm her life; if possible, she was to be captured alive.

Bu Quan was never on Yi Jiu's assassination list. The dwarf Men Shi, however, was.

But Yi Jiu had little interest in striking a fully armed dwarf.

In truth, even if the employer had ordered Yi Jiu to kill Bu Quan, he wouldn't have done it.

The Thieves' Guild possessed formidable intelligence capabilities. Yi Jiu had gathered extensive information on Bu Quan and Shuang Lian, fully aware of Bu Quan's significance to Shuang Lian.

Assassinating Bu Quan and provoking Shuang Lian was a foolish move Yi Jiu would never make.

Unless... the employer offered an astronomical sum!

But clearly, Mei Lin would never do such a thing.

“In that case...” Yi Jiu murmured, abandoning his original plan. Based on the latest intelligence, he made an impromptu tactical shift.

He actively shed his stealth and charged directly at Bu Quan.

Battle skill - Wall Walking.

Battle skill - Rapid Movement.

The moment he materialized, he was detected by numerous alchemy orbs floating in midair.

The orbs swarmed like bees, closing in.

Some fired beams, others detonated on contact.

Yi Jiu offered no counterattack, relying solely on agility and evasion to navigate the barrage. He even skillfully used beams and walls as obstacles, causing the orbs to collide and detonate mid-air.

Within mere breaths, Yi Jiu closed the distance, charging toward Bu Quan.

“Bu Quan, retreat!” Dwarf Men Shi roared, charging toward Yi Jiu.

Yi Jiu smiled faintly.

Battle skill - Instant Flash.

The next moment, he vanished from his position, reappearing behind Bu Quan.

Battle skill - Throat Slash!

Yi Jiu's dagger pressed against Bu Quan's throat, drawing back with a light flick.

“No!” Men Shi roared, utterly unable to intervene in time, only managing to turn and stare in shock.

But Yi Jiu's dagger sliced through empty air.

Bu Quan's figure dissolved - it had been a lifelike illusion all along.

Yi Jiu remained expressionless. He had recognized the illusion from the start, deliberately allowing it to happen so Bu Quan would teleport away quickly, leaving him free to destroy the alchemy workshop.

But the next moment, Bu Quan's laughter echoed: “Hahahaha, you fell for it!”

Bu Quan's true form revealed itself, immediately activating the magic trap.

Yi Jiu was now standing directly on the magic trap.

In an instant, dense metal spikes formed a forest of spears, poised to pierce Yi Jiu.

Yi Jiu activated his headpiece with his mind, unleashing the arcane spell Counter Spell to nullify the spear forest.

“She actually revealed her true form?” He looked at Bu Quan in surprise and charged forward again.

Men Shi froze for a moment, then roared at Bu Quan, “Miss Bu Quan, hide yourself!”

Bu Quan flashed a proud, confident smile. “Relax. This is my turf.”

Yi Jiu's form abruptly froze. The surrounding space solidified, trapping him like an insect in amber.

“Tactics achieved!” Bu Quan snapped her fingers triumphantly. “Not too shabby in actual combat, huh?”

The dwarf was momentarily speechless.

But the next instant, Yi Jiu's deep voice echoed behind Bu Quan: “Is that so?”

Bu Quan shuddered!

The “enemy” she had trapped in her illusion dissolved into scattered light and vanished.

Bu Quan had used an illusion to “toy” with Yi Jiu, and Yi Jiu immediately returned the favor in kind.

“No, I still have resistance...” Bu Quan attempted to summon her magic power, but suddenly, Yi Jiu touched her waist with a finger, instantly sealing her spirit power.

Battle skill - Spirit Seal!

When a magician cast spells, they relied on spirit power to construct the spell's model. A magician's personal magic power was normally stored in their personal magic pool, and spirit power was also required to access it.

With Bu Quan's spirit power sealed, she instantly lost her combat capability.

“Now, you're my prisoner,” Yi Jiu revealed a triumphant smile. “My luck really is good this time.”

His earlier tactical shift to a direct assault had been intended to intimidate Bu Quan into fleeing the workshop via the teleportation formation.

After all, he couldn't locate the formation's position.

Moreover, operating within the city this time carried greater risks and tighter time constraints.

Unexpectedly, Bu Quan didn't teleport away but instead attempted to challenge him with laughably naive tactics!

Yi Jiu decisively played along with the scheme, actually capturing Bu Quan alive.

“Release her!” Men Shi rushed over, bellowing.

The dwarves also fell into a state of confusion.

Outside the workshop, enemies still battered the walls, unable to breach the defenses.

But within, their general had been captured!

Yi Jiu smiled at Men Shi. “Want me to release her? Fine. Destroy every alchemy facility here!”

Men Shi froze.

Bu Quan snapped awake, shouting, "No! You absolutely cannot destroy these alchemy facilities! Otherwise, we won't be able to mass-produce potions in the short term!"

“Hey, if you want to kill us, just do it! Why toy with us like this?!”

“Even if I die, I won't let you destroy these alchemy facilities!”

Yi Jiu froze.

The intelligence reports didn't mention this. Bu Quan actually had this kind of temperament?

"You're a prisoner. Show some self-awareness! Otherwise, I'll make you regret it." Yi Jiu stepped forward to the right, circling around to Bu Quan's side, and swung his dagger fiercely.

The dagger sliced through the air with a cold gleam, cleaving one of the alchemy installations in two. The silver level apparatus was severed cleanly.

Witnessing this, Men Shi growled, “You're no ordinary silver level! Who are you?”

“Asking such nonsense, do you expect an answer? Get to work destroying everything, or I'll slice Bu Quan!" Yi Jiu waved the dagger beside Bu Quan, its cold gleam flashing once more.

This terrified Men Shi.

With no confidence he could save Bu Quan, he was forced to comply, using his two-handed war hammer to smash the surrounding alchemy devices.

“Now we're talking.” Yi Jiu chuckled, then turned with a vicious glare fixed on Bu Quan. “Speak up! Which alchemy device controls the defenses?”

Bu Quan's gaze, however, was even more fierce than his.

“You criminal! If you've got the guts, come at me! Not a single one of these devices will you destroy!”

"Men Shi, what are you afraid of? Don't give in to his threats! Even if we die, we shouldn't surrender!"

“My life is insignificant! Protect these alchemy devices!”

Men Shi's face twisted with agitation as he shouted his true feelings: “Miss Bu Quan, please stop provoking the criminal!”

Yi Jiu's face darkened, his eyes flashing coldly. Bu Quan's defiance deeply offended him. He swung his dagger, determined to teach her a bloody lesson, to make her understand her predicament and the proper attitude a captive should adopt toward their captor!

The dagger flashed coldly once more, slashing toward Bu Quan's face.

If it connected, Bu Quan's face would be slashed open, disfiguring her instantly.

Yet in the next instant, a spatial fluctuation erupted, sucking Yi Jiu into its vortex.

Yi Jiu's expression twisted in horror as he realized he'd been transported to a frozen wasteland!

Men Shi: ?

The dwarf was stunned. In an instant, he regarded Bu Quan with newfound respect: “Miss Bu Quan, so this was all part of your plan.”

Bu Quan shook her head blankly: “No, it wasn't me.”

The next moment, another spatial fluctuation appeared, sucking Bu Quan away as well.

At that moment, tens of thousands of meters above Snow Bird Harbor, the Honey Snow Tower stood abruptly in the sky.

Bu Quan's mentor, Shuang Lian, had arrived here long ago. She had not revealed herself immediately, instead arranging everything meticulously before treating this as a trial for Bu Quan.

The outcome of the trial left Shuang Lian both satisfied and dissatisfied.

“Teacher?!” Bu Quan materialized inside the mage tower. Upon seeing Shuang Lian, she instantly grasped the truth.

She threw herself into Shuang Lian's embrace: “Teacher, you came to help me personally?”

Shuang Lian had intended to maintain her dignity and deliver a stern lecture to Bu Quan, but the girl's actions completely disrupted her plans.

In an instant, Shuang Lian felt the entire world grow warm.

She slowly opened her arms, gently embracing Bu Quan. Her stern expression softened into tenderness: "Silly child, why did you act so foolishly? Your life is infinitely more precious than any alchemy artifact, do you understand?"

Bu Quan replied, “I thought I could defeat the enemy. I never imagined he'd be so cunning!”

“I was so close to victory.”

Shuang Lian sighed deeply, shaking her head repeatedly. "You lack real combat experience. You completely misjudged the gap between you. Now, watch carefully."

The embrace ended.

Shuang Lian summoned a magic projection, revealing Yi Jiu struggling in a blinding blizzard.

He was ambushed by Shuang Lian, who waited patiently for prey to stumble into her trap, capturing him within the demi-plane.

Faced with such a formidable assault, Yi Jiu had no choice but to unleash his true power. The gold level battle qi was unmistakable. Upon seeing it, Bu Quan's face instantly drained of color. “He... he's actually a gold level fighter?”

“It's worse than that,” Shuang Lian added. “His name is Yi Jiu, a highly successful assassin within the Thieves' Guild!”

“Do you finally grasp how perilous your situation was?”

“To taunt a formidable foe while imprisoned? Utterly reckless!”

Shuang Lian forced a stern expression.

Bu Quan lowered her head, her voice weak. “I was so worried the alchemy facilities would be destroyed. If that happened, I wouldn't be able to mass-produce potions.”

Hearing this, Shuang Lian grew even angrier, her brows furrowing deeply. “You've been used, you foolish child.”

"That Long Fu is a treacherous villain! He deceived you into joining his mercenary group, treating you as nothing more than a tool for alchemy! And all he gave you in return was a single gold coin?"

“Tell me honestly, do you like him or not?”

Chapter 737: Igloo City

The closer one approached the center of Ice Sculpture Island, the colder the weather became.

The capital of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was situated in the island's most frigid region.

Yet cold did not equate to barrenness. On the contrary, the concentration of frost elements resulted in vast deposits of unique resources. This was precisely why, when settlers first migrated from the Frost Continent to Ice Sculpture Island, they chose the site of the royal capital as their initial settlement.

The capital was not only the geographic center of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom but also its power hub.

Viewed from above, with the capital at its core, various factions, the royal family, the Alchemy Guild, and major religious sects intertwined, weaving a web of influence that blankets the entire island and extends its reach to surrounding seas and islands.

Within this vast web of power, the Li Jian Clan undoubtedly occupied a colossal mesh.

As one of the kingdom's most influential transcendent families, their authority was immense. The Second Naval Fleet of the kingdom had practically become the Li Jian Clan's private military force. Even the royal family found it difficult to intervene.

The Li Jian Clan's main city, Igloo City, was situated deep within the kingdom's heartland, only a few thousand miles from the royal capital.

This was a vast ice plain.

Snow fell year-round, with blizzards raging five days out of every week.

The ice plain had no mountains. From afar, the most striking sight was the architecture of Igloo City.

These soaring structures stood densely packed. Due to the constant snowfall, flat roofs risked collapse under the weight of accumulated snow, so nearly all buildings in Igloo City featured domed designs.

Snow elves belong to the elven race, a sentient species whose blood naturally flows with artistry and magic.

Thus, within Igloo City, circular roofs, arched portals, and a city layout shaped like petals form the cornerstone of architectural beauty.

Amidst the vast expanse of white ice and snow, pale blue and deep blue ice bricks served as the city's primary hues. These harmonize with the unique green and yellow conifers, mosses, and other vegetation of the ice plain, creating a marvelous tapestry of color.

Pedestrians traversing the city streets and life activities in every corner flow like a current, seamlessly integrating the beauty of architecture and color.

Through the floor-to-ceiling windows, Zi Di found her gaze irresistibly drawn to the exotic beauty unfolding on the streets below.

The originally cold and harsh natural environment was greatly softened by the graceful lines of the architecture. The vitality emanating from the vegetation and pedestrians dotted the cityscape of white and blue.

Zi Di understood well: the Li Jian Clan possessed ample financial resources to create even more greenery. Yet they chose not to, seemingly by deliberate design. However, precisely because it was sparse, the vitality scattered throughout appeared all the more precious.

Seated on the wide-backed chair nearby was Zong Ge.

His gaze swept over the study's furnishings, sizing up which items might be alchemy tools and assessing the room's defensive capabilities. In his mind, he ran through scenarios: Should conflict erupt, would they choose to leap through the window or break down the door?

Zong Ge was an exceptionally skilled commander, his very bones pulsing with a primal combat instinct.

He relished observing battlefields, imagining every scenario to devise tactics.

Yet this study was remarkably sparse, with few furnishings. Judging by its aura, only two or three transcendent items were present.

This was the study of the clan leader of the Li Jian Clan, yet it seemed far too ordinary.

A faint doubt lingered in Zong Ge's mind, accompanied by a subtle sense of pressure.

He yearned to unleash a reconnaissance-type battle skill to see clearly. But remembering that he and Zi Di had come knocking with a request, such a rash move was out of the question.

Ever since receiving the letter from Tripleblade, Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group had immediately altered their itinerary, traveling from Rime City to Igloo City.

This was the Li Jian Clan's stronghold.

Whether due to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' renowned reputation or their established alliance with the Li Jian Clan, Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their companions received an exceptionally high-level reception.

Moreover, they were granted an audience directly with the current clan leader, Mian Li Zang.

This undoubtedly set a promising tone.

At the appointed time, Zi Di and Zong Ge were ushered into Mian Li Zang's study to await his arrival.

“Do you know?”

"There was a time when Igloo City was filled with birdsong and flowers, resplendent and magnificent. Not only did it cultivate icefield vegetation, but it also transplanted many greenhouse blooms. Back then, man-made hot springs were everywhere, their waters tinkling as they flowed. The city's temperature was always pleasant, and fruits hung on the trees ready to be plucked. When you popped them into your mouth, they were always at their most delicious."

“Over the century following that era, our clan declined. Our descendants grew weak and feeble, our family spirit deteriorated, and we even faced the threat of extinction.”

"After deep reflection, the clan leader of that time voluntarily took responsibility and stepped down. The new clan leader led the entire clan in diligent governance, bringing in renowned masters to educate the younger generation. Resources were concentrated where they were most needed, not on the city's ornate decorations. Finally, the family creed was revised."

“Now, our creed is - Hide wealth in emptiness, stand firm amidst snow and wind!”

Zi Di heard the voice and her heart jolted. She turned swiftly to look.

The next moment, she saw a handsome snow elf. A middle-aged man, his face radiating a kind smile, yet his deep, piercing eyes revealed a profound, calculating mind.

“Mian Li Zang...”

Information about this man flashed rapidly through Zi Di's mind.

This man possessed an extraordinary blend of softness and strength. Outwardly gentle and seemingly weak, but anyone who thought him frail and easy to bully would be gravely mistaken. Once he truly took action, he often struck with pinpoint accuracy, sometimes even delivering a fatal blow. In truth, he was a deeply calculating individual with ruthless methods.

Possessing a gold level transcendent rank, he specializes in magic, excelling particularly in spatial manipulation and summoning.

Such was the nature of the Inner World bloodline - it held significant advantages in spatial powers.

Under his leadership, the Li Jian Clan has flourished even more than under its previous head.

He took great pleasure in recruiting external talent. For instance, the time magician Sand Between Fingers from the Second Naval Fleet was personally recruited by Mian Li Zang.

He also attempted to recruit Yun Zhong, but the latter showed little interest in joining any faction and politely declined.

Though rejected, Mian Li Zang remained undeterred, persistently attempting recruitment. His offers grew increasingly generous, their superiority making others envious.

Yun Zhong truly had no interest and found his persistence unbearable. But one wouldn't strike a smiling face. Left with no choice, Yun Zhong could only evade him. Mian Li Zang visited Yun Zhong repeatedly, only to find him absent each time, yet his enthusiasm never waned.

Some mocked him, but those with true insight recognized this as an exquisite political performance orchestrated by Mian Li Zang through Yun Zhong.

In this display, Mian Li Zang profoundly demonstrated his unique approach, a thirst for and willingness to embrace external talent.

If Yun Zhong agreed, the Li Jian Clan would gain another gold level member, bolstering their strength.

If he refused, he’d become Mian Li Zang’s tool, cementing the latter’s reputation as a patron of talent in the public consciousness.

“An exceptional clan leader, an exceptional politician!” Zi Di thought, steeled her resolve, and bowed in greeting.

Zong Ge, though promoted to gold level, remained outwardly a silver level member. He rose to greet Mian Li Zang proactively.

The study door remained closed; Mian Li Zang had utilized spatial abilities to appear silently.

Instead of taking his seat behind the desk, he proactively retrieved vessels from a side cabinet and inquired gently of Zi Di and Zong Ge, “Tea or wine?”

As a magician, Zi Di immediately replied, “Tea would be best.”

Mian Li Zang proceeded to fetch cups himself, placing them on the tea table before the guest chairs.

He pulled up a low stool beside the table and began brewing the tea personally.

Mian Li Zang wore a warm smile, showing none of the pride expected of a clan leader.

Yet Zi Di, seated to his left, felt immense pressure.

From Mian Li Zang's entrance to his every word, everything was calculated.

This business negotiation commenced the moment Mian Li Zang appeared.

As per prior arrangements, Zi Di remained the primary negotiator for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

As for Zong Ge, he knew this was not his forte. Although he had delivered a standout performance during the previous negotiations at the Axe Gang's headquarters. That success stemmed from his insight into the battlefield, his combat instincts, and the experience of an exceptional commander—qualities that allowed him to apply his expertise to other areas.

To replicate that performance, Zong Ge needed extensive observation.

During the Axe Gang negotiations, Zong Ge remained silent, thoroughly assessing the situation before seizing the moment to speak.

Zi Di was the main force; Zong Ge was the surprise tactic.

This was the strategic deployment of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The three sat around the tea table, beginning to sip their brew.

Mian Li Zang introduced the silver level tea, detailing its origin and properties.

Zi Di took a sip of the hot tea, first remarking how pleasant it felt to drink something warm in this cold weather. She then followed Mian Li Zang's lead, sharing her knowledge of other tea varieties and comparing them to the one they were drinking.

Having grown up immersed in her father's world, Zi Di later became a magician, studying at the academy to gain deeper knowledge. After taking charge of the dilapidated Wisteria Merchant Alliance, she gained extensive expertise in tea, a major commodity category.

Thus, Zi Di's tea-related insights piqued Mian Li Zang's curiosity, prompting him to ask questions.

Zong Ge observed for a long while, initially growing impatient.

After all, the two had chatted idly for ages, two more cups of tea had been poured, without touching on the real topic.

But then Zong Ge realized this casual banter was itself a contest.

Both sides sought to demonstrate patience; whoever broached the crucial issue first would concede an early defeat.

Zong Ge felt uneasy, for he knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the more anxious party. They had suffered an attack from a mysterious force, this crucial intelligence was surely known to the Li Jian Clan.

His group's arrival at Igloo City and their proactive visit to the Li Jian Clan made their motives and objectives all too obvious.

The Li Jian Clan Leader clearly understood this, so he sat calmly and patiently, showing no sign of urgency.

Zi Di wasn't without anxiety, but she couldn't afford to show it. Doing so would put her at a disadvantage in the real negotiations to come.

Despite her youth, however, she possessed considerable experience in negotiations.

Facing an opponent like Mian Li Zang and navigating the current unfavorable situation, she had long since devised the right countermeasures.

Chapter 738: Mian Li Zang - The New Generation Overtakes the Old

“Lord Mian Li Zang... Actually, our current collaboration on the dragon rider potion business is quite similar to the tea business...” Zi Di smoothly shifted the conversation from tea to the dragon rider potion, transitioning with remarkable ease.

She then discussed the potion's current status, its future prospects, and the substantial profits it promised.

Upon hearing this, Zong Ge couldn't help but inwardly praise her ingenuity.

Zi Di's approach was undoubtedly a clever entry point. Though she hadn't yet mentioned the mysterious forces, Zong Ge could already foresee where this was headed.

Mian Li Zang sipped his tea, equally aware of the undercurrent.

A smile played on his lips, yet his eyes remained as still as a deep well, quietly awaiting Zi Di to broach the true subject.

But Zi Di showed no haste. Instead, she proposed revisions to certain details of their prior agreement regarding the dragon rider potion.

Zi Di's aims were twofold: first, to gauge Mian Li Zang's negotiating prowess through this discussion; second, to leverage her familiarity with the details and her thorough preparation to significantly drain his negotiating energy and patience.

The latter point was particularly crucial, as patience in business negotiations is a key factor influencing outcomes.

What Zi Di hadn't anticipated was that Mian Li Zang was equally well-versed in these details! No matter which clause Zi Di brought up, Mian Li Zang could accurately assess it and voice well-founded objections.

"He knows these details so thoroughly. Back then, I finalized the terms with Magician Kong Pan. Mian Li Zang's rebuttals are so well-reasoned, this isn't just memorization. He must have communicated with Kong Pan." Zi Di's heart sank slightly.

“Perhaps his delay stemmed from contacting Master Kong Pan the moment we initiated this visit.”

The fact that the Li Jian Clan Leader had prepared so thoroughly for these negotiations only intensified Zi Di's psychological pressure.

Zi Di focused her mind and engaged Mian Li Zang in a debate over these clauses.

Observing from the sidelines, Zong Ge now noticed the stark contrast in negotiation styles between the Axe Gang leader and Mian Li Zang.

The Axe Gang leader adopted an aggressive stance. Once he latched onto certain clauses, he refused to budge, showing not an inch of retreat. Zi Di, however, skillfully employed clever maneuvers and deliberately introduced various concepts to successfully confuse the Axe Gang leader, thereby safeguarding her side's interests.

Mian Li Zang was different.

He always wore a smile, speaking with great politeness, even appearing somewhat gentle. By comparison, the silver level Zi Di seemed almost aggressive.

Mian Li Zang wouldn't stubbornly cling to a single bottom line, but whenever he lost some advantage, he'd use it as leverage to gain elsewhere.

In short, dealing with him was like kneading dough.

Though seemingly malleable, whenever Zi Di pressed down hard, the dough would bulge elsewhere, reclaiming the space it'd lost.

“Mian Li Zang is tougher to handle than the Axe Gang leader!” Zong Ge gained a deeper understanding.

Yet as time passed, Zi Di gradually gained the upper hand through her exceptional eloquence and increasingly adept business negotiation skills.

"Old age dulls the mind. Ah...” Mian Li Zang sighed wryly, massaging his temples. “Magician Yao Ma, what were you saying just now?"

“I forgot already. My apologies.”

“No, I mean, what was your last question?”

Mian Li Zang employed a strategy of retreating to advance, repeatedly thwarting Zi Di's aggressive maneuvers.

As a gold level powerhouse facing a silver level opponent, such tactics might seem somewhat underhanded. Yet he executed them without the slightest qualm.

This was his modus operandi—and precisely why he proved so formidable!

Zi Di grew increasingly frustrated. Confronting Mian Li Zang felt like punching water, she couldn’t find a solid target, making her attacks stiflingly ineffective.

Yet her expression betrayed nothing.

She understood this was Mian Li Zang wearing down her patience.

Negotiations continued.

Mian Li Zang’s tricks grew more varied.

“Oh dear, it's snowing.” Facing Zi Di's relentless pressure, Mian Li Zang abruptly changed the subject.

Both turned to the window, where scattered flakes had begun drifting down.

Mian Li Zang looked at the two across from him and said amiably, “You might not be aware, but the weather in Igloo City is notoriously unpredictable.”

“Based on my decades of experience, don't be fooled by the light snow now, it will only intensify.”

"This study of mine, you see, lacks any heating facilities. As the clan leader, every action I take must be mindful of its implications. So, do you need blankets?"

“The snow's coming down hard. Sitting in the study will get pretty chilly.”

Faced with such concern, Zi Di and Zong Ge found it hard to say anything.

Zong Ge shook his head and declined, while Zi Di smiled, saying that a blanket over her legs would surely keep her warm.

Mian Li Zang rose, retrieved a blanket from the cabinet, and handed it personally to Zi Di.

Who knows how a ghost could feel cold.

“Shall I bring you some more tea? I was right about this study having that flaw.”

“Ah, I'll fetch a blanket too. One must take care of their body in old age.”

“Look, the snow is falling harder.”

True to Mian Li Zang's prediction, the snow outside the window grew heavier. Simultaneously, the cold wind howled, causing the windowpanes to tremble slightly.

Zi Di’s negotiation rhythm was disrupted time and again.

“This opponent is tough to deal with,” Zong Ge thought. Mian Li Zang seemed like one of those enemy commanders skilled in feints and strategic deception.

Whenever their main force launched a full-scale attack, they always came up empty-handed. That feeling was unbearable.

Zong Ge glanced at Zi Di again, worried she might lose patience.

Yet her composure surpassed even Zong Ge's expectations.

From start to finish, she maintained a steady smile, her speech not once hastening. Her remarkable patience and poise were evident.

Such poise even earned her Mian Li Zang's quiet admiration. He promptly extended an invitation: “I wonder, Magician Yao Ma, have you ever considered changing your living and working environment?”

Zi Di froze.

Mian Li Zang promptly slapped his forehead, chiding himself: “Old age is catching up to me. My bad, I spoke out of turn.”

He then added meaningfully: “Still, being able to calmly drink seven or eight cups of tea, that's not something just anyone can manage.”

“Ah, my old habit resurfaces. Whenever I see talent, I can't help but want to recruit them into the clan.”

“I hope Commander Long Fu will forgive me.”

Zong Ge hastened to express his understanding.

Little did he know this would unlock Mian Li Zang's floodgates of conversation.

Shaking his head with a wry smile, he sighed, “Ah, sometimes I truly envy young people like you, always so full of drive and vitality.”

"I've held the position of clan leader for the Li Jian Clan for many years now. To keep it going this long has left me utterly exhausted, barely holding on."

“The Li Jian Clan may appear prosperous but sustaining that prosperity is incredibly difficult. The effort and sacrifices required far exceed what outsiders imagine.”

"I've pushed myself to my absolute limit for this cause. Even so, I have failed."

“I neglected cultivating the next generation, leaving only Yue Qian, a gold level fighter, as my heir!”

“You both understand the nature of the Inner World bloodline. While it grants extraordinary cultivation advantages, its notorious flaw is the scarcity of capable descendants.”

“That's why I've gone to great lengths to attract external talent.”

“But executing this well is no simple task. Talent is rare, and even after recruiting them into the clan, exceptional management is required to fully unleash their potential.”

"Thus, I also envy the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Your corps was established only recently, yet you've managed to recruit so many talented outsiders."

"Commander Long Fu and Auxiliary Commander Shi Qi need no introduction. Silver level pillars like Chi Lai and Men Shi came to you effortlessly."

“Through this negotiation, I've realized: Your value, Magician Yao Ma, extends far beyond that of a silver level magician!”

"And then there's Bu Quan... She's an alchemy prodigy with an absolutely promising future!

After lavishing veiled praise on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Zi Di and Zong Ge grew distinctly wary.

Mian Li Cang steered the conversation back: "Our clan's creed is ‘Hide wealth in emptiness, stand firm amidst snow and wind.’ But standing firm in the blizzard is no simple feat."

“The larger the snow house, the greater the blizzards it must endure and confront.”

“The higher the peak, the colder the summit.”

“Take your corps, for instance. After Long Fu, as leader, publicly slew Teng Dong Lang, your fame soared, echoing across the nation.”

“The dragon rider potion proved a commercial stroke of genius, immense profits poured in daily, with market response reaching fever pitch.”

“Such vigorous growth propelled your corps into the eye of the storm, drawing attacks from mysterious forces.”

"First, an ambush in Cold Wind Valley. Then, the caravan transporting potions was destroyed. Frankly, though the subsequent clash between those two gold level fighters followed, their motives remain highly suspicious."

“Being targeted by forces of gold level, your group's situation is truly concerning.”

The study instantly grew tense.

The sudden, pointed attack laid bare the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' predicament. Like an enemy who always plays feints, suddenly launching a preemptive strike, though hardly a fatal blow, it did strike at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' weak spot.

Even Zong Ge sensed the sharpness in these words!

If Zi Di handled this poorly, she would inevitably be at a disadvantage, and the subsequent negotiation would be controlled by the other side.

Zi Di and Zong Ge exchanged a swift glance, both registering the gravity in each other's eyes.

Little did they know that Mian Li Zang, too, was acting out of necessity.

For the first time, he faced an opponent whose patience surpassed his own, whose negotiation skills outmatched his.

The young appearance of the Magician Yao Ma stirred an uncontrollable admiration in Mian Li Zang for her talent. Had he known Zi Di's true age, he would have been stunned.

Moreover, constantly evading the younger generation's advances with excuses was hardly commendable.

Mian Li Zang valued his dignity, however modest his demands might be.

His frequent lamentations about growing old weren't entirely feigned. Facing the dragonman and Magician Yao Ma, he genuinely felt the melancholy of being overtaken by the rising tide of the next generation.

Thus, after being drained of considerable energy and patience by the previous detailed negotiations, Mian Li Zang could no longer hold back. He chose to strike first.

Chapter 739: We Don't Want Protection, We Want Cooperation!

In the business negotiations, Mian Li Zang launched his first proactive offensive.

The moment of truth had arrived for Zi Di!

Well-versed in the art of negotiation, Zi Di knew she could not panic or lose her composure at this critical juncture.

Instead of offering an immediate explanation, she calmly took a sip of tea. Then, under Mian Li Zang's intrigued gaze, she slowly set down her teacup.

Then she said, “As Lord Mian Li Zang mentioned, our corps' situation is indeed as described.”

“To be perfectly honest, prior to this, three more gold level individuals planned to ambush us.”

“They were the Axe Gang leader, Lin from Ice Spear City, and Jia Bing from Grafting Town.”

“That battle was the most vicious. Even now, recalling it sends a chill down my spine.”

Zi Di deliberately avoided detailing the battle, knowing this would heighten Mian Li Zang's concern.

This was a card held by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

A very powerful card!

Zong Ge watched with eager anticipation.

Yet Mian Li Zang showed neither surprise nor shock. He smiled gently and said, “Three gold level mercenaries joining forces, just imagining it sends a chill down one's spine.”

“Your group's combat prowess is astonishing.”

“But I suspect such unconventional tactics are rare—a one-time use, perhaps even forbidden?”

“Otherwise, why wouldn't Commander Long Fu employ it against Teng Dong Lang?”

“Or maybe the conditions for using it are so strict they're nearly impossible to meet in battle?”

Zong Ge's heart sank. Mian Li Zang's demeanor showed none of the expected signs, he had known this information all along.

Yet he had never mentioned it before. He had been waiting, waiting for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to play this card.

“That old fox...” Zong Ge cursed inwardly.

He instantly understood: Mian Li Zang had deliberately exposed a weakness to lure Zi Di into revealing this card.

Yet he had been prepared, rendering the card nearly useless, and even using it to unsettle Zi Di and Zong Ge's composure!

A fleeting, imperceptible gleam flashed in Mian Li Zang's eyes as he continued, "Oh, right. Perhaps you two aren't aware. Not long ago, your corps' branch in Snow Bird Harbor suffered an attack by a mysterious force. Your flagship and alchemy workshop were both targeted, it seems the losses were significant!"

"What?!" Zi Di gasped, instinctively rising to her feet.

Zong Ge's expression darkened instantly.

Whoosh, whoosh~

The howling wind, lashing with blinding snow, pounded against the windows.

After delivering this statement, Mian Li Zang turned his gaze toward the window, his demeanor calm and composed.

Outside, the blinding snow obscured normal vision, blanketing the roofs of numerous snow huts in pristine white.

Zi Di hadn't anticipated that Mian Li Zang's preemptive strike wasn't a one-off, but part of a coordinated offensive-defensive combo.

Hearing of the Snow Bird Harbor branch's assault, both their composure inevitably wavered.

At this moment, Zi Di and Zong Ge found themselves thoroughly outmaneuvered.

They keenly felt the overwhelming might of the Li Jian Clan. This power was now being demonstrated to the fullest extent in communications.

After a moment of silence, Zi Di drew a shallow breath, her expression utterly candid as she fixed her gaze on Mian Li Zang. "We feel deeply wronged. We have no idea why such formidable enemies would target us so relentlessly. That's precisely why we came to Igloo City, to seek deep cooperation with your esteemed family."

Mian Li Zang offered a faint smile. “Miss Yao Ma, are you certain you've come seeking cooperation, and not seeking our family's protection?”

His tone was light, yet his words were razor-sharp.

If Zi Di handled this poorly, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be relegated to a subordinate position, while the Li Jian Clan would rise to dominance.

Should negotiations continue, this power dynamic could even evolve into a master-servant relationship, stripping the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps of all initiative.

Zi Di's expression remained serene, her voice steady: “If we were merely a mercenary group, this visit would indeed be to seek noble patronage.”

“But we are not.”

“Since the clan leader is so well-informed about us, surely you understand our true identity.”

The amusement in Mian Li Zang's eyes faded, replaced by a broader smile.

This time, he remained silent. Mirroring Zi Di's earlier gesture, he took a sip of tea, leaned back in his chair, and gazed leisurely out the window.

Outside, the sky was gloomy, with wind and rain lashing fiercely. The igloos across the street were indistinct.

Just as Mian Li Zang couldn't see through the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' background!

Indeed, what Mian Li Zang truly feared was precisely the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' background.

Rumors had always persisted that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was actually a group of arms smugglers. Their sudden emergence and extraordinary actions far exceeded the ordinary. This rumor perfectly explained all the peculiarities.

Including the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' activities in Snow Bird Harbor, especially their business operations and how they effortlessly used dragon rider potions to break into the kingdom's market... Mian Li Zang leaned toward this explanation.

Yet, despite the Li Jian Clan's capabilities, they could not uncover which arms manufacturer stood behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

It had to be understood that an entity capable of mass-producing arms and smuggling them on such a scale could never be merely a gold level individual.

At the very least, it would be a saint level!

Saint level individuals, within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, already belonged to the strongest group.

More crucially, the Li Jian Clan couldn't uncover any background on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, not even a single trace.

“Perhaps there's no arms dealer behind them at all!” speculated an elder of the Li Jian Clan.

“But how could that be?!” Mian Li Zang scoffed at this conjecture.

Where did Long Fu's crew even come from?

Both Long Fu's and Shi Qi's identities were fake. They weren't even certified by the Mercenary Guild!

The Li Jian Clan had exhausted every resource and still couldn't uncover their true identities.

If they were just ordinary silver level mercenaries, so be it.

But they weren't!

Long Fu alone had slain a gold level opponent at silver level. Such a feat was unheard of in a century.

Shi Qi, though silver level, radiated a golden aura from his battle qi. In other words, he had one foot firmly planted in gold level territory.

Was this what an ordinary silver level looked like?

What kind of power could produce such a silver level?

Even the Li Jian Clan, which he himself controlled, couldn't possibly field someone like this!

Tracking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' path, from Snake Rat Island, Two Eyes Island, to Weeping Pine Island, through every naval skirmish, they faced no insurmountable challenges. Not a single battle inflicted crippling losses or severe setbacks.

Instead, they grew by leaps and bounds, expanding like a balloon being inflated.

Such rapid expansion within mere months, could this truly be just a mercenary group?

Clearly, a powerful force was backing them. They concealed numerous trump cards, wielding combat methods far beyond their stated capabilities. Thus, they arrived at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Shortly after landing at Snow Bird Harbor, they made their move, taking down the harbor's underground kingpin and publicly executing the Bayonet Gang's leader. The entire Bayonet Gang was wiped out.

This single battle made the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps famous throughout the kingdom.

What kind of tactic was this?

This tactic felt all too familiar.

In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, where dueling culture ran deep, anyone with combat prowess and a thirst for fame and fortune would typically choose to make a name for themselves through heroic duels.

Wasn't that how Yun Zhong gained his fame?

Didn't Mei Lin of the First Fleet prove her worth as a general through a string of dueling victories?

And that fearsome Long Meng, widely regarded as the top gold level fighter, didn't he establish his dominance in the dueling arena?

Mian Li Zang believed: "When Long Fu publicly challenged Teng Dong Lang, everyone assumed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the underdog. But in reality, the Bayonet Gang was the carefully selected prey!"

Holding this conviction, Mian Li Zang found the duel footage between Long Fu and Teng Dong Lang increasingly implausible. That final scene where Long Fu “struggled” to defeat Teng Dong Lang? Too fake!

Later, upon learning that Long Fu had retaliated and killed three gold level fighters, he became even more certain that Long Fu's victory over Teng Dong Lang was likely a deeply calculated performance.

It was precisely because of this combat prowess that Mian Li Zang immediately arranged this meeting when the two visited.

This was the world of the strong.

Mian Li Zang felt he had to show Zong Ge, Zi Di, and their group the utmost respect!

Zi Di's mention of the arms dealer's background plunged Mian Li Zang into silence for a moment.

After gazing at the snowy landscape outside the window for a while, the current Li Jian Clan Leader finally turned to the two and said, "Since you have such deep connections and formidable skills, why did you choose to visit me and seek my clan's assistance? Surely you could handle formidable foes without our clan's involvement?"

Zi Di spread her hands in a wry smile. "Lord Mian Li Zang, you know our purpose here is business. We're using the dragon rider potions to establish our market presence and distribution channels first, then we'll move into arms dealing later."

"Both our commander and deputy were meticulously selected. Our commander excels at defeating stronger foes with weaker forces, while our deputy is near gold level. Dispatching combatants of gold level or higher would severely hinder our operations."

Perfect explanation!

At this point, Zi Di couldn't help but feel relieved, the arms dealer excuse was truly flawless and practical.

Whoever came up with it had to be a genius.

Zi Di continued, “So, as I mentioned earlier, this visit is to seek cooperation, not shelter.”

"Collaborating with your clan wasn't part of our original plan. I'd rather believe this is a serendipitous encounter. If we can leverage your connections to weather this storm, it would be ideal. I'm confident our future collaboration will yield even greater prospects and rewards."

“Oh? Care to elaborate?” Mian Li Zang inquired, his tone laced with hidden meaning.

Zi Di promptly retrieved a document, placed it on the coffee table, and slid it toward Mian Li Zang.

Mian Li Zang, holding his teacup in one hand, reached over with the other. Using his spell, mage hand, to turn the pages, his expression gradually darkened after a few moments of reading.

Chapter 740: Shuang Lian – Cough Cough Cough

Mian Li Zang set down his teacup, abandoning the minor spell of the mage hand. Instead, he gripped the documents with both hands and examined them closely.

After flipping through several more pages, he sat up straight, instinctively straightening his back and no longer leaning against the chair's backrest.

His eyes gleamed with intensity as he scrutinized the documents, seemingly discovering hidden meanings within them.

After reviewing most of the materials, Mian Li Zang could no longer remain seated. He lifted his gaze toward Zong Ge and Zi Di, his expression shifting between surprise and uncertainty.

Zi Di smiled serenely, sitting upright. “This is our sincerity.”

Mian Li Zang nodded slightly, his expression grave. “Our side requires immediate experimental verification.”

“Go ahead!” Zi Di raised her hand.

Mian Li Zang rose instantly, spatial ripples erupting as he vanished from his spot.

Zong Ge witnessed this moment, the most confident he'd felt throughout the entire business negotiation.

He knew the data Zi Di had provided came from War Merchant's alchemy database.

This had been the bargaining chip they'd agreed upon before the operation began.

War Merchant's database contained an overwhelming formation of alchemy techniques, intricate as the stars. Zi Di had deliberately selected three of them.

“It seems our chances of partnering with the Li Jian Clan are quite high this time,” Zong Ge murmured. “This information has completely swayed the Li Jian Clan Leader, so much so that he insists on personally witnessing the experiment's success.”

Zi Di smiled faintly. “Commander, you mustn't underestimate that certain individual.”

The “certain individual” she referred to was, of course, War Merchant!

Zi Di turned her gaze toward the window.

Outside, the blizzard raged fiercely, mirroring the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' current bleak predicament. Yet Zi Di brimmed with confidence, certain they could bluff their way through this storm, using profit to leverage the Li Jian Clan's power and emerge from the tempest!

“This blizzard just won't end!” The gold level Yi Jiu rolled through the snow.

He charged left and right, racing at top speed, yet remained perpetually shrouded in the blizzard, unable to break free.

“I must be trapped within Magician Shuang Lian's ice-and-snow demi-plane.”

“She couldn't possibly blanket the entire demi-plane with this blizzard disaster; this is where she produces her honey snow.”

“Therefore, this blizzard disaster is actually following me, constantly shifting!”

“Damn it, I must break through this blizzard first, then find a weak spot in the space barrier and attempt a breakthrough back to the Main Plane.”

While Yi Jiu fought for his life, within the mage tower, Shuang Lian was concerned about her student Bu Quan's romantic troubles.

Ten thousand meters above the ground, the Honey Snow Tower soared through the sky at breakneck speed.

Inside the tower, everything remained perfectly still, with not a hint of turbulence.

Yet Bu Quan's mind was in turmoil, all because she'd heard her mentor's inquiry - “Have you fallen for Long Fu?”

Bu Quan shook her head frantically at once: “No, no, I—I haven't...”

After a stammering denial, she lowered her head, her hands fidgeting nervously like a young girl caught in a parental discovery of early romance.

Though she could be quite bold around the dragonman youngster at times, standing before Shuang Lian made her blush fiercely.

Her face turned as crimson as a ripe apple, and her eyes, fixed on the soles of her feet, grew large and watery.

Seeing this, what could Shuang Lian possibly fail to understand? What could remain uncertain?

“Ah, you silly child,” Shuang Lian sighed inwardly, a flicker of nostalgia crossing her eyes. “You really remind me of myself when I was young.”

“Back then, I too was deeply infatuated with someone...”

At that thought, an imposing, towering figure inevitably surfaced in Shuang Lian's mind.

That figure standing before her, defying every attack, then tearing through the blizzard, that image remained etched deep within Shuang Lian's heart.

"Wait, wait a moment. Teacher, now is not the time for this." Bu Quan suddenly realized her predicament.

Shuang Lian's recollections interrupted, she looked helplessly at her beloved disciple, hearing her say: “Teacher, thank you for rescuing me. You even brought the mage tower with you.”

“But... but I can't leave.”

“I joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. They need me!”

“You won't leave?” Shuang Lian's expression darkened. “Stay there and die?”

"But Master, you've already captured the strongest enemy. If I return, I should be safe." Bu Quan argued.

“Hmph. Return only to continue being nothing but a potion-making tool?” Shuang Lian reached out, pressing a finger to Bu Quan's forehead. “That little thief Long Fu clearly harbors ill intent. His motives are impure...”

“No.” Hearing the teacher disparage the dragonman youngster once more, Bu Quan couldn't hold back any longer and retorted, "Commander Long Fu is an upright gentleman. He worships the Justice God and is a good person, truly trustworthy. He invited me to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. He never forced me! I did it willingly."

A bolt of lightning seemed to strike Shuang Lian's heart.

Well, well.

For the sake of an outsider, Little Quan Quan had even learned to interrupt her teacher!

Shuang Lian suppressed the bitterness welling up inside and said gently, “Silly child... Listen to your teacher. I've eaten more salt than you've eaten rice in your life.”

“Though Long Fu doesn't say it outright, they're using his looks to lure you in!”

“You're pure-hearted and inexperienced, you fell for it instantly.”

"As your teacher, I can't stand by and watch you ruin your future. I won't let you go down the wrong path. You clearly have better options!"

“Teacher, I deliberated long and hard before choosing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps,” Bu Quan replied. "But I trust myself, I weighed every consideration thoroughly! The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is my choice. It's a decision I made with every ounce of courage I possess. I know I've let you down..."

Her voice trailed off once more.

Shuang Lian sighed deeply. "You've wronged yourself! I read your letter, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps paid you a mere gold coin!"

“That's outrageous! They're taking advantage of you!”

Bu Quan hurriedly explained, "No, I actually prefer their payment method. The point isn't the gold coin, but the alchemy knowledge they entrusted to me. "

Shuang Lian laughed in exasperation, reaching out to ruffle Bu Quan's hair. “You silly girl, you're still a bit young, even though you've already shown your talent in alchemy.”

“The Alchemy Guild holds oceans of knowledge, enough to keep you striving for most of your life.”

“You haven't seen truly advanced knowledge yet. They give you a few novel little trinkets, and you...”

Before Shuang Lian could finish, Bu Quan interrupted again.

“Teacher, it's not like that.”

“While I rarely exchange knowledge from the Alchemy Guild, please trust me, I have discernment.”

"Oh, right. This request for your assistance has Commander Long Fu's approval."

“He's authorized me to offer the alchemy knowledge I previously acquired as payment to express my gratitude to you.”

“Here it is. Please take a look.”

Shuang Lian snorted dismissively as she took it.

She glanced through it and remarked, "Bu Quan, you must understand the scale and global standing of our nation's Alchemy Guild. You must know that some of our nation's alchemy techniques are truly unique, surpassing the major powers of the Main Plane. In fact, there are certain methods even the Sheng Ming Empire covets."

“I admit you possess discerning taste, but how much of the Alchemy Guild's advanced knowledge have you truly encountered?”

“I can assure you, the alchemy materials they gave you... our guild would already... Hmm?”

As Shuang Lian spoke, her voice grew faint, punctuated by a gasp of astonishment.

Then, her eyes widened as she flipped through the pages with increasing speed.

"Huh?" She gasped again, her face betraying palpable shock.

“Hmm...” As she delved deeper into the text, Shuang Lian became subconsciously absorbed, pondering its intricacies.

Bu Quan inwardly groaned. She knew all too well that once Shuang Lian entered this research trance, it would last for ages. If interrupted early on, by the middle or later stages, even a battle erupting outside wouldn't snap her out of her contemplative state.

“Teacher! Teacher! Teacher!” Bu Quan practically yelled right into Shuang Lian's ear, finally jolting her awake.

Shuang Lian gasped, staring in shock at the documents in her hands before turning to Bu Quan with equal astonishment.

Bu Quan flashed a triumphant smile. “Teacher, now you must believe in me, right?”

Shuang Lian cleared her throat, attempting to recover from her momentary lapse. “I must admit...”

Just then, she heard Bu Quan add, "Oh, right. Commander Long Fu also mentioned in his letter that if you're interested, you might become an honorary elder of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. The reward would be alchemy knowledge of the same level as this document. Would you be interested, Teacher?"

Shuang Lian coughed twice more, this time far more violently than before.

She set down the documents: “This alchemy knowledge... never mind.”

“The thing is, I thought about it. Since you've joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, just taking you away like this without a word, that wouldn't be very gentlemanly.”

“Even though that thief Long Fu... that kid played tricks on you, he did help you avenge your grievances after all.”

“As your teacher, it's only proper etiquette to thank him in person.”

Bu Quan interrupted for the third time: "Oh, Master, please stop talking. Hurry back, enemies are attacking the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!"

“Hmph, mere petty thieves... Come! I'll bring you back at once!”

The mage tower abruptly changed course, accelerating to three times its previous speed as it charged straight toward Snow Bird Harbor.

In Snow Bird Harbor.

The big guy was frantically chasing a group of enemies.

“Why is he still after us?”

“Ugh, what a pain!”

The sacrificial warriors attempted to shoot the big guy, but all their attacks were rendered ineffective by his thick defenses.

"What do we do now? I was supposed to reinforce the small workshop!"

"This giant's not right, he's lost his mind. Then... let's lure him to Snow Bird Arena. That's where the biggest crowd gathers."

Chapter 741: The Big Guy Runs into the Dueling Arena

Snow Bird Dueling Arena.

The eating contest between Ci Hai Shen and Da Dou continued unabated, undeterred by the sacrificial warriors' assault on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Upon learning of the city's unrest, the Sea Bird Bishop sought Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's counsel on the matter.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord responded with a smile, assuring the Sea Bird Bishop: everything was under his control.

Since the sacrificial warriors' assault began, real-time intelligence had been continuously relayed to the City Lord's hands.

This attack was clearly targeted at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After holding out for some time, the alchemy workshop in the Mercury Cave was also breached. The sacrificial warriors paid a heavy price to destroy this location: one silver level and nearly ten iron level casualties.

Smaller alchemy workshops within the city also came under attack. However, thus far, the highest level transcendents encountered was merely silver level; no gold level had appeared.

Therefore, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord remained unhurried and unflustered.

What he did not know was that Shuang Lian had already made her move.

She had merely utilized the Honey Snow Tower, causing the spatial fluctuations to be extremely restrained and undetectable.

From the moment Yi Jiu appeared in battle until his capture into the demi-plane, he had consistently suppressed his power, maintaining only a silver level aura. Thus, he too remained undetected by Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“As long as this duel isn't disrupted, it's a rare spectacle,” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord thought to himself.

This heroic duel was unprecedented. If publicly disrupted, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's prestige would suffer significant damage.

The next instant, fresh intelligence reached the city lord's hands.

A small squad of sacrificial warriors deliberately approached the Snow Bird Arena, luring their pursuers - a savage giant.

The giant's identity had been confirmed: a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Moreover, the giant's condition seemed off.

“Damn these men!” A cold gleam flashed in Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's eyes.

He understood the sacrificial warriors' scheme: diverting the problem by luring the enraged giant into the arena.

"You fight your own battles, that's one thing. But now you dare provoke me?" Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sneered inwardly.

From the start of this clash, he had chosen to remain a spectator.

On one hand, he was observing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, waiting to see what trump cards they and the mysterious faction would play.

On the other hand, he was waiting.

Waiting for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to seek his aid!

Acting prematurely offered little advantage. It risked disrupting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' tactical plans and inviting their resentment.

But if they made the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps beg for help, the debt owed to the city lord would grow, ensuring greater future rewards.

A cup of drinkable water held before someone dying of thirst held far greater value.

As long as a gold level didn't appear, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would remain unmoved.

Earlier, when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had sought his aid, he had already ordered the Mountain Peak Merchant Alliance to cooperate, secretly transporting the dragon rider potion.

Ultimately, the relationship between Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was one of allies, not that of a guardian to the latter.

Of course, should the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fail to seek aid in time and suffer heavy losses, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would certainly intervene.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord issued his orders in a low voice, effectively handling the matter with the big guy. He then turned his gaze back to the magic projection, continuing to enjoy the duel.

Within the arena, a large group of transcendents lay sprawled on the ground.

Each of them rubbed their distended bellies, smacking their lips with lingering satisfaction.

“Ah, that was absolutely delicious!”

"I polished off three huge bowls of fluttering boat fried rice. Never imagined fried rice could be this incredible."

“I'm stuffed, can't take another grain.”

"The tantalizing aroma still beckons me, yet I can't swallow another bite..."

Their expressions were complex, bliss from culinary satisfaction, agony from overeating, and the helpless longing to keep eating despite being unable to.

More and more people were being carried away.

These individuals had lost the ability to move freely and were directly transported to the Sea Bird Church's ranks to receive divine healing.

The bishop was delighted to witness this scene. After all, patients had to pay for divine healing.

This single duel's large-scale treatment alone had earned the Sea Bird Church a substantial fee.

Standing behind the city lord and bishop, Mei Lan Du continued his commentary on the unfolding battle: "Everyone's stomach capacity is nearing its limit, yet the scores of Lords Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen remain neck and neck. This culinary duel has entered its final stages. I believe the outcome will be decided very soon."

"Hurry, hurry, a little faster." In many corners of the arena, fighters intensified their training, refining their battle qi on the spot to help digest the energy within their bodies.

They practiced the battle qi technique without fear of exposure.

For the vast majority of these techniques, their foundational levels were already circulating openly. The highest-level cultivator present was merely silver level.

Moreover, battle qi techniques had to align with one's bloodline.

Thus, even if an enemy learned the techniques, without the corresponding bloodline, they'd be useless, a futile endeavor.

“Opportunities like this don't come often.”

“Normally, a dish this exquisite would cost at least several gold coins, far beyond my means!”

“Right now, I'm freeloading to the max. You two magic chefs should be thanking me.”

With this mindset, fighters who had digested their meals kept rushing back into the arena to devour more transcendent delicacies.

Many were already on their second or third round.

“I... I can't do take anymore.”

“Just finished one serving and I'm stuffed. Damn it, gotta get back to training fast.”

The transcendents were overwhelmed primarily by the energy within the transcendent dishes, not the physical burden on their stomachs.

Each dish's energy content adhered to a specific standard.

Only meeting this standard qualified it as a delicacy. Only when diners finished their entire portion could the corresponding magic chef earn points.

A large team of staff calculated the points for Ci Hai Shen and Da Dou, with even the mage tower at the dock assisting in the calculations, checking for errors and filling in gaps.

“Useless!” Ci Hai Shen cursed inwardly as he observed more and more people collapsing, unable to eat any more.

In terms of points, he was still slightly behind Da Dou.

But the gap was minuscule, just a tiny bit.

Ci Hai Shen kept a constant watch on the diners within the arena.

In an eating contest, the scale and level of the transcendents served as crucial reference points. To a large extent, they determined the magic chef's tactical approach. Take an extreme example: once all bronze level transcendents could eat no more, the magic chef saw no need to continue preparing bronze level dishes.

Beyond that, magic chefs also observed each other's strategies. For instance, if an opponent establishes absolute dominance in silver level dishes, one might shift focus to iron level, aiming for the points from iron level diners.

But in this duel, Da Dou consistently prepared fluttering boat fried rice, catering to levels from mortal to silver.

This was highly unusual.

Typically, in eating contests, Ci Hai Shen's approach was the absolute norm. He attracts diners by crafting diverse delicacies.

Varied dishes and flavors stimulate diners more effectively, igniting their appetites.

A single dish holds a pitifully small advantage in this regard.

Yet by now, Ci Hai Shen recognized the strength in Da Dou's chosen tactic: "He uses only one recipe, progressing from low to high tiers with a unique approach. His efficiency in producing silver level dishes far surpasses mine."

“Despite using fewer silver level ingredients, he manages to create more silver level fried rice.”

“His silver level iron wok does possess spatial efficiency, but if he were merely storing silver level ingredients inside for covert use, it wouldn't align with Da Dou's character or operational habits.”

He wouldn't waste his time on such futile actions."

Despite Ci Hai Shen harboring considerable resentment toward Da Dou, these emotions never clouded his judgment.

Meanwhile, outside the arena.

“He's catching up fast.”

“Snow Bird Arena is just ahead. Let Snow Bird Harbor City Lord deal with the headache!”

"Move!"

Thud. Thud. Thud.

The sacrificial warriors hurled alchemy devices, unleashing thick clouds of smoke.

The big guy bellowed like a raging bull, charging straight into the thick haze.

He soon burst out of the smoke, spotting the “sacrificial warriors” fleeing ahead.

The big guy pursued them. After several dozen strides, the “sacrificial warriors” crashed headlong into the arena gates while running, disintegrating into wisps of smoke and dispersing instantly.

Their true forms lurked hidden in the corners, watching with bitter resentment as the giant charged toward Snow Bird Arena.

“If we'd known it would be this easy to mislead him, we could've used a better tactic!”

"Time was too tight. What good would wearing him down do? We couldn't deliver a devastating blow to the enemy. Don't forget what our objective is."

“Let's go. We need to reinforce the battle inside the city.”

But in the next instant, the city guard appeared, surrounding this group of sacrificial warriors completely.

Earlier, the city guards had received orders from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, to eliminate every last one of these reckless sacrificial warriors! Leave not a single survivor.

The sacrificial warriors, of course, wouldn't surrender without a fight.

A fierce battle erupted instantly.

The deafening explosions and shouts couldn't even make the big guy glance back.

He had already descended into madness, staring straight ahead as if convinced his enemies had retreated behind the gate's inner walls.

Bam!

The big guy slammed into the gate with brutal force, ignoring the guards' shouts from either side.

The thunderous impact echoed like a clap of thunder, silencing the entire arena.

Most heads turned. Countless eyes fixed on the gate.

“Did you hear that?”

“What's going on?”

Bam!

Another deafening crash.

“Stop, or we'll be forced to take action!” The guards were visibly tense.

The big guy pounded his chest, bellowing in a frenzied rage.

His shouts were loud enough for everyone inside to hear.

“What the hell is happening?”

“Sounds like an attack?”

“Could it be remnants of the Bayonet Gang?”

"No way. Who'd be stupid enough to attack here at a time like this? There are at least four gold level fighters inside the arena."

The arena erupted into commotion.

“Sigh...” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord let out a deep, hollow sigh.

The magic projection before him had shifted. No longer did it show the duel in the magic kitchen; now it displayed the big guy pounding his chest with his fists.

Both the city lord and the bishop saw it instantly: the big guy was not in his right mind. He was clearly mad, his reason gone. Perhaps he'd resorted to some method to temporarily boost his combat power—like Berserk?

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could strike hard against the sacrificial warriors but clearly couldn't do the same to the big guy. After all, so many eyes were watching.

Most crucially, why would he target the big guy? Stepping in to help would clearly yield far greater rewards!

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shifted his thoughts and turned to the bishop: “He is a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, but to restrain him, it would be most appropriate for someone from your order to intervene.”

The bishop chuckled softly and immediately relayed the order.

A silver level priest stepped forward, receiving the command, and surged through the crowd, channeling divine magic, and flew directly toward the giant.

The Sea Bird God excelled at flight. This gift extended to its clergy as well. Among the Sea Bird Church's elementary divine spells lay the very spell of flight. This granted the church's clergy a significant advantage in combat.

The priest swiftly reached the big guy, warding off the guard poised to attack.

He cast his spell upon the big guy: “Wake up!”

The big guy: “Waaah waaah waaah...”

“No effect?” He cast the spell again.

The big guy: “Aaaah aaaah...”

Realizing the bishop might be watching, the priest's expression turned grave as he channeled his full power into the spell.

The big guy: “Sob sob sob...”

He kept ramming the gate. Under such a frenzied assault, the metal gate finally reached its limit. Unable to hold any longer, it crashed down with a thunderous boom.

Now, the big guy's view was completely unobstructed.

The sea of people inside the arena saw the big guy.

Countless eyes focused on the big guy.

Under normal circumstances, the big guy's timid nature would have made him unable to bear such scrutiny; he would have hidden behind others long ago.

But now...

The big guy pounded his chest and roared at his numerous enemies: “Aaaah! Cough cough cough...”

He beat himself until he coughed incessantly, spitting blood.

Such frenzied behavior astonished the crowd.

“An iron level giant?”

“Where did he come from?”

“He actually smashed the arena gates? How reckless!”

“Careful, something's off about him!”

Curiosity and wariness dominated the reactions; fear was nowhere to be found.

Come on.

Without exaggeration, the current arena was undoubtedly the strongest gathering of power in all of Snow Bird Harbor. To participate in this rare culinary showdown, most transcendents had converged here.

Counting the two magic chefs, there were at least four gold level experts!

So even upon seeing the big guy, none of them moved.

Even though many could easily suppress the iron level big guy, a mere trifle for them, they remained unmoved.

Because many were grunting and groaning, bellies bulging—they were simply stuffed to the limit.

Why bother tangling with a big fool?

If they had the leisure, they'd rather focus on refining their battle qi.

Others noticed the priest beside the big guy. Having witnessed the priest being flung away earlier, they immediately guessed that the bishop had already dispatched someone to handle this emergency.

Since these people showed no aggressive behavior, the big guy wasn't severely provoked and gradually calmed down.

The priest's earlier divine magic had still been effective!

Chapter 742: Eating Then Sleeping

Snow Bird Arena.

Inside the viewing chamber, the bishop's expression darkened slightly.

The silver level priest hadn't handled things cleanly. Allowing the big guy to crash through the main gate and expose himself to countless spectators had brought shame upon the bishop.

“Damn it!” the priest cursed inwardly. The big guy's resistance had surpassed his expectations.

He drew a deep breath and unleashed his most potent divine spell with all his might.

The big guy stood frozen in place, showing no sign of dodging, and was struck directly.

The savage expression on his face had completely vanished, his eyes no longer bloodshot. Instead, they held a look of profound confusion.

He turned his head, scanning his surroundings as if asking himself, “Huh? How did I end up here?”

“Where is this place?”

He spun around, his expression growing even more bewildered.

He did not see a single familiar face. Originally, mercenaries like Tripleblade had been stationed here, but not long ago, Tripleblade had led his men away, rushing off to provide emergency reinforcements.

The big guy was stunned.

Behind him, the priest's eyes widened, cold sweat beading on his forehead.

“I just cast the divine spell with full power, right?”

“But why isn't he reacting at all after being hit by Sleep?”

“A normal person, no, a normal silver level would collapse instantly and fall asleep.”

“Who the hell is this giant?”

"I know giants generally have high magic resistance. But no one said their divine spell resistance was this high too!"

Just as the priest doubted himself, the big guy suddenly froze, his gaze sharpening.

What was he seeing?

He saw - rice!

Bowl after bowl of fluttering boat fried rice, arranged densely on the long table.

Though a formation sealed in the aroma, the big guy’s gaze had already gone completely blank.

“Food, food?”

"Food?!"

"Food!!!"

The big guy roared with excitement, his face aglow with ecstasy. He swung his massive legs and strode forward, charging toward the dueling arena.

His arrival immediately caused a stir.

His enormous frame, combined with the fact that he'd crashed through the gate, had kept him under close watch ever since he entered the crowd's view.

“Get in line!” The security staff swarmed toward the big guy.

“Stop right there!” The silver level priest snapped to attention, scrambling to follow behind.

The security staff, spotting the priest, assumed he would intervene.

The priest, seeing the staff closing in on the big guy, instinctively slowed his pace, expecting them to intercept him.

Then, both sides stared each other down, each waiting for the other to make the first move...

The result? The big guy miraculously burst into the main venue.

“Food! Food! Food!” He charged into the crowd, shoving aside those unable to dodge.

His target: a long table laden with iron level fluttering boat fried rice, surrounded by iron level transcendents. Most people had already scattered as the big guy approached. The few who were pushed aside had neither the inclination nor the strength to clash with him.

Had he acted this way earlier, it would have drawn widespread resentment, perhaps even sparked public outrage. But now, everyone was stuffed, preoccupied with digesting their food, with no energy left to dwell on the big guy's antics.

A series of coincidences allowed the big guy to plow through recklessly, yet smoothly reach the long table.

He grabbed bowl after bowl, tilted his head back, opened his mouth wide, and dumped the fried rice straight in.

His massive frame made what were large bowls for normal snow elves seem like teacups to him.

Bowl after bowl poured into his mouth, chewed noisily, and swallowed greedily.

Mmm—!

The big guy grunted with pleasure and astonishment as he ate.

The magic chef's heroic duel had worked in his favor. The fried rice teased his taste buds, its powerful allure striking deep into his heart. With every mouthful he swallowed, the deliciousness shot through him like golden lightning, piercing the gloom of his existence.

The big guy had been suffering!

Lately, he'd been starving, his stomach growling, his strength sapped, his vision blurring, passing out from hunger only to wake hungrier still.

He wanted to eat more, but no one listened. After he told Tripleblade, the latter would beat him with a stick.

The big guy missed the dragonman youngster terribly, but Xiao Guai's loving father wasn't in Snow Bird Harbor.

Every time the big guy dreamed of the dragonman youngster, bitter tears of sorrow and injustice would fall.

But now...

Right now, at this very moment, he's eating something.

Delicious! It was so delicious!

It's so much better than his usual meals!

The dragonman youngster took care of the big guy, specifically hiring several magic chefs for him. But most of these magic chefs were just part-timers, like magician apprentices. Their ranks were low, most were bronze level, a few iron level, and not a single silver level among them. How could they compare to the two gold level chefs here?

Crucially, Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen, these two gold level chefs, were cooking with all their might.

Eat! Eat! Eat!

The big guy's movements got more fluid as he started dumping fried rice into his mouth one bowl after another.

He ate quicker and quicker, soon moving from one end of the table to the other. All that remained on the table was a mountain of empty bowls.

Staff behind him busily tallied the points to be awarded to Da Dou.

“This giant can eat that much?”

The big guy swept through the table like a whirlwind. His wildly exaggerated eating style startled many.

The point tally showed Ci Hai Shen had already fallen slightly behind Da Dou. But with the big guy's help, Da Dou suddenly gained a significant number of points, truly pulling ahead of Ci Hai Shen.

Ci Hai Shen's face turned as dark as charcoal.

Da Dou, however, laughed heartily. He was a giant himself and found this big guy quite likable.

Especially since the big guy's fervent eating spree showed no signs of stopping.

After finishing one table, he moved on to the second.

This time, it was the table with bronze level fried rice.

The big guy was only iron level. Eating a bowl of bronze level fluttering boat fried rice earned him just 10 points. But he couldn't care less about the rules, or more accurately, he had no clue about any rules.

All he knew was that if it looked delicious, he'd eat it!

Devour every morsel within his sight!

The big guy's heart burned with fervor.

The bliss from the food kept his eyes wide open throughout, his jaws chewing with increasing force, his energy surging with every bite!

The transcendent energy carried by the food radiated throughout his entire body.

Like a parched land receiving long-awaited rain, every cell in the big guy's body was dancing with joy!

He transformed into a whirlwind, swiftly clearing every last grain of fried rice from the bronze table.

“Just bronze level fried rice, but his appetite alone is astonishing.”

“This giant really can eat.”

“Watching him devour it like that has sparked my own appetite...”

Many gasped in awe, while countless others shot the big guy envious glances. If only they could eat like that!

Ci Hai Shen's face had turned pale.

“Is Da Dou's luck really this good?”

“How did that damn fool of a giant even get in here?”

“Is he some trump card specifically meant to take me down?”

Ci Hai Shen's suspicions grew with every thought. Just as he was about to speak up, he saw the big guy collapse with a thud.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord chuckled softly. He had long noticed the big guy stuffing himself like a pig, a potential hazard, but hadn't rushed to stop him.

Now, by ordering rescue, he both taught this rule-breaker a lesson and secured future leverage over the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After all, they were allies. Selling favors like this cost him nothing.

Yet the next moment, the big guy began snoring loudly.

He had fallen asleep.

“Don't panic! It's me! I did it!” the silver level priest shouted urgently, feeling he'd finally salvaged a shred of dignity.

The big guy's sleep proved his divine spell worked, though perhaps... a bit slow to take effect?

It wasn't just the divine magic that put the big guy to sleep; his own condition played a role.

Even before his rampage, the big guy had been in poor shape, starving. Later, provoked, he descended into a frenzied state of destruction, wreaking havoc along his path.
