The attacks from the sacrificial warriors, including his final collision with the arena gates, had left his body badly injured and his spirit utterly exhausted.

After eating his fill and his mind finally relaxing, the big guy simply fell asleep right there in front of countless people!

Meanwhile.

“We finally made it!” Tripleblade breathed a slight sigh of relief as he gazed upon the small alchemy workshop within sight.

After making his decision, he had led a group of skilled fighters from Snow Bird Harbor's docks, racing full speed toward the city to provide urgent reinforcements.

En route, he received the latest intelligence: the medium-sized alchemy workshop at the Mercury Cave had ultimately fallen.

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps now relied solely on this workshop within the city.

Then came more grim news from Men Shi: Bu Quan was missing!

Tripleblade arrived at the small alchemy workshop just in time to see a group of sacrificial warriors furiously attacking, attempting to breach the workshop's gate and storm inside to wreak havoc.

There were two silver level enemies.

Six iron level enemies.

Over twenty bronze level enemies.

Among the reinforcements led by Tripleblade, he himself was only iron level, with not a single silver level fighter among them. But he brought two squads. The Quick Knife and Backwater battle squads.

“The workshop hasn't been breached yet. Men Shi is still here.”

“With this combat strength disparity, we can fight!”

Of course, this was assuming no gold level enemies showed up.

Tripleblade had already sensed something amiss about that “Shi Qi.” He secretly wondered: Could that assassin who appeared and vanished before Bu Quan be the suspected “gold level” individual?

The situation left Tripleblade no time for further thought. Without a moment's hesitation, he charged forward.

He charged ahead.

His display startled both sides; no one expected this short man to be so fierce!

With their commander leading the charge, the mercenaries' morale soared.

“Has Lord Yi Jiu not succeeded yet?” The sacrificial warriors hesitated.

They were unaware Yi Jiu had already encountered an accident. In terms of intelligence, they were slightly behind Tripleblade.

The small alchemy workshop's defenses were solid. No matter how fiercely the sacrificial warriors attacked, it showed no signs of collapsing.

Thus, the sacrificial warriors naturally pinned their hopes on Yi Jiu. Little did they know that he was now in dire straits, desperately fleeing through a blizzard!

Chapter 743: Failure and Resurgence

The sacrificial warriors had been relentlessly attacking the small workshop, and now, with reinforcements arriving from behind, they showed no panic.

Their original battle formation had already accounted for enemy reinforcements approaching from all directions.

Immediately, a portion of their forces concentrated directly behind, calmly and methodically facing the charge led by Tripleblade and his men.

“We're here to support you!” a dwarf shouted from within the workshop.

Men Shi remained perplexed.

He couldn't fathom why Yi Jiu had appeared only to vanish, followed by Bu Quan's disappearance.

Shuang Lian had yet to show herself.

To Men Shi, if this was the enemy's doing, why weren't they attacking? If it was an ally, why hadn't they continued their support?

Jolted awake by the dwarf's shout, Men Shi steadied his mind.

He rushed to the window, surveying the latest battlefront.

Tripleblade charged at the forefront. Three iron level sacrificial warriors stood ready, yet Tripleblade's momentum showed no sign of slowing.

“This human shorty is unexpectedly fierce!” Men Shi silently admired, though unease crept in.

The three iron level sacrificial warriors swung their weapons in unison as Tripleblade charged.

Tripleblade stomped hard with his front foot, shattering the stone tiles beneath him.

Shattered fragments flew everywhere, obscuring the sacrificial warriors' vision. They felt disorientation before their eyes cleared, only to find Tripleblade vanished.

“Behind you!” someone shouted from behind.

In that instant, Tripleblade had suddenly executed a battle skill, rolling swiftly across the ground and darting directly beneath the crotch of a tall sacrificial warrior.

Tripleblade directly breached the first layer of defense, while the sacrificial warriors were still adjusting their formation in the second layer.

“I'll handle it!” one of the three defenders called out, turning to pursue. The other two remained focused on repelling the advancing mercenaries.

The iron level warrior who turned to chase Tripleblade swung his heavy club.

The club came crashing down, slicing through the air with a whistling roar.

Tripleblade didn't need to look back; the sound alone told him of the attack behind him. But he had to deal with more than just the pursuit from behind, he also faced two iron level mercenaries launching attacks from the second line.

In an instant, Tripleblade found himself caught in a pincer attack.

Battle skill - Ground Pounce.

Tripleblade unleashed the battle skill, his entire body flattening against the ground as he executed a swift pounce.

Ground Pounce was a common move, but in his hands, it was executed with remarkable finesse. Crucially, his exceptionally short stature allowed this ordinary battle skill to unleash extraordinary power!

The iron level sacrificial warriors flanking him lunged into empty air.

The water pigman leader charged into the enemy ranks second, followed by his Backwater Battle Squad.

The two iron level defenders on the first line fought desperately but couldn't prevent themselves from being overwhelmed by the Backwater Squad.

“Damn it, this shorty's tough to handle!”

“He's slippery as hell. Pin him down fast.”

“I'm on it!”

The sacrificial warriors shouted commands as they launched all-out attacks against Tripleblade.

There was no solution. Tripleblade wielded his Ground Pounce battle skill with masterful skill. His small frame darted and twisted through the thicket of enemy legs, severely disrupting the sacrificial warriors' formation.

Crucially, Tripleblade's daggers posed a genuine threat. Leaving him unchecked was absolutely unthinkable.

“On my command, contract the defense!” Seeing Tripleblade couldn't be swiftly eliminated, a silver level sacrificial warrior immediately shifted tactics.

Nearly all sacrificial warriors bunched together, solidly blocking the entrance to the alchemy workshop.

Tripleblade couldn't break through!

The enemy was packed too tightly, displaying exceptional military discipline. Not only were their tactical choices and adjustments wise and decisive, but each individual's execution was flawless!

They showed no fear of sacrifice.

To maintain formation stability, front-line fighters would even willingly throw themselves into danger, shielding more crucial allies from blades!

Worst of all, while huddled together to withstand Tripleblade's assault, they simultaneously continued their relentless battering of the alchemy workshop's gate.

“This won't do. We must apply more pressure!” Tripleblade gritted his teeth, his face contorted in fury as he charged forward once more.

But this time was different. The enemy formation was tightly packed, and Tripleblade had already drawn the full attention of all opponents.

Soon, he stumbled backward, repelled by the sacrificial warriors.

Wounds gushed blood from his body.

His expression remained resolute and unflinching. He pulled out a vial of potion, downed it in one gulp, and charged forward again.

He was now the target. As he reached the enemy front, three weapons swung directly at him.

Tripleblade fought desperately to block and dodge, trying to slip through the gaps in the enemy line and roll back in.

But at that moment, an iron level warrior who had been lying in wait seized his chance, thrusting his short spear viciously.

Tripleblade could no longer evade, struck by the tip of a short spear beneath his ribs.

He could only retreat, preparing to regroup.

A meteor hammer pursued him.

Tripleblade mustered every ounce of strength, simultaneously unleashing three battle skills to barely evade the hammer's head.

But then, a long staff followed close behind, striking Tripleblade squarely in the chest.

It was a silver level enemy who struck!

Tripleblade flew backward, spitting a mouthful of blood mid-air with a gurgle.

He crashed to the ground, rolling several times before slamming into a wall corner and coming to a halt.

Tripleblade scrambled to his feet, forcing himself into a crouching stance.

His vision blurred, his chest throbbing with excruciating pain, leaving him unable to fight for the moment.

The attacker showed no mercy, breaking formation to close in.

“Die!” The silver level sacrificial warrior raised his staff high, bringing it down with brutal force aimed squarely at Tripleblade's forehead.

In that instant, the stench of death assaulted his senses!

Tripleblade tilted his head back, powerless and horrified as the staff descended toward him, unable to dodge.

“Could it be... I'm going to die here?”

Snore... snore... snore.

The big guy's snores shook the heavens.

He'd finally had a proper meal, and now he was surely dreaming sweet dreams. This was evident from the silly grin playing at the corners of his mouth.

He dreamed of the dragonman youngster returning, bringing a mountain of delicious food, allowing the big guy to eat his fill.

“Father, you're finally back.”

“You've come to see Xiao Guai at last!”

Iron level battle qi surged outward from the big guy's body.

“He's cultivating in his sleep!”

“Impressive technique.”

“What kind of battle qi technique is this? How convenient?”

The spectators witnessed this scene and began speculating.

Some guessed it was the Spring Dream battle qi technique. However, while this technique operated during sleep, it caused the cultivator to constantly experience erotic dreams, resulting in pronounced genital characteristics that also served as the refining point for the battle qi. The big guy's crotch area was perfectly ordinary, not matching this trait.

Others speculated it might be the Cluster Stone Slumber Technique. However, while this battle qi technique is also cultivated during sleep, it temporarily transforms the skin into a hard, rough, gray stone-like texture. The big guy didn't match this characteristic either.

Ultimately, the spectators concluded that the big guy was practicing the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique.

“Carry him out. Don't let him continue disrupting the order,” Snow Bird Harbor City Lord ordered calmly.

Staff members gathered around the big guy, attempting to lift him away collectively.

“He's heavy!”

“We can't budge him...”

“Should we wake him?”

But the big guy slept so soundly, he couldn't be roused.

The staff members' level was generally low. Seeing the big guy had barged in yet received some form of indulgence and preferential treatment from the bishop and city lord, they dared not enforce the rules harshly.

Under the watchful eyes of the crowd, they briefly conferred and decided to carefully carry the big guy away.

During the process, people noticed that the big guy's bulging belly was deflating at a rapid pace.

The big guy had clearly overeaten, causing his stomach to swell to an absurdly exaggerated state. But soon, his belly flattened, eventually even sinking inward.

The staff had only moved him a dozen meters when the big guy opened his eyes.

He had woken up hungry.

He sat up, rubbing his sleepy eyes. After glancing around, his movements froze.

His gaze locked on the countless bowls of fried rice lining the long table, his eyes blazing with renewed intensity. His mouth opened wide, drool streaming thickly from the corners.

The staff had already cautiously backed away the moment he sat up.

Now unrestrained, the big guy lunged toward the table once more, grabbing bowl after bowl of fried rice and devouring it voraciously.

What a glorious day!

The big guy was overjoyed, never expecting to get a second helping after his first meal.

He let out a silly, hearty laugh, his mouth smeared with grease.

The audience hadn't anticipated the big guy “reviving” so quickly. Watching his delighted eating, many swallowed hard, their own appetites stirred.

Meanwhile, the gaze of certain observant individuals toward the big guy shifted subtly.

Chapter 744: Serious Injury and Specially Made Delicacies

Snow Bird Dueling Arena.

Due to a series of unexpected events, the big guy had become the center of attention.

People watched as he shoveled fried rice into his mouth, his belly swelling visibly with each bite.

“Did you notice how much faster this giant eats now?”

"His digestion is unbelievable! He only slept for a short while, yet he's already burned through all that transcendent food."

“He is a giant, after all. He seemed incredibly hungry when he barged in.”

Before long, the big guy was full again and collapsed directly onto the floor, sleeping right next to the long table.

Staff members gathered around once more, intending to move him away.

“Leave him be. He'll wake soon enough,” Da Dou interjected, his gaze fixed on the big guy with keen interest.

"I didn't expect this giant to be so interesting." In the observation room, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said.

"His bloodline is quite special to have such an appetite. Is it the Big Eater bloodline? It could be the Abyss bloodline too." The bishop guessed.

These words would be very familiar to Tripleblade.

Because before this, Tripleblade had speculated the same thing.

But right now, Tripleblade was teetering on the edge of life and death!

The next moment, Tripleblade, who thought he was about to die in battle, heard a roar behind him.

He ducked down immediately. The water pigman leader instantly leaped over him, blocking the incoming club attack for him.

The water pigman leader staggered back several large steps before steadying himself.

He was only iron level, at a disadvantage in level, but behind him stood a group of water pigmen, forming a last-stand formation.

Relying on the strength of his formation, the water pigman leader furiously pumped up his battle qi and engaged the silver level opponent in fierce combat.

Tripleblade seized this crucial moment to breathe, immediately seizing the opportunity to retreat slightly.

In that instant, the Quick Knife Squad charged in, joining the Backwater Battle Squad in battle.

Though small in number, both squads had trained relentlessly since their founding. Zong Ge had invested immense time, resources, and manpower into their training, and the fruits of this labor were concentrated in these two squads.

This time, neither squad were a disappointment.

They played a pivotal role!

One squad engaged the silver level enemy, while the other forcibly shattered the enemy formation.

Another silver sacrificial warrior, unable to sit idly by, shouted to his comrades before launching a pincer attack against the Backwater Squad.

Their tactical intent was clear: concentrate all forces to eliminate one squad first, then deal with the other.

But at that moment, the alchemy workshop door burst open from within, and a group of dwarves charged out.

It turned out that Men Shi, after observing for a while and confirming that the fierce battle outside was neither an illusion nor a decoy, had decisively chosen to strike.

Men Shi charged into the enemy ranks, engaging a silver level sacrificial warrior and directly disrupting their tactical plan.

Thus, the silver level forces found themselves mutually counterbalanced.

Yet the Quick Knife Squad remained beyond the reach of the sacrificial warriors, despite their best efforts to target them.

Led by the dogman Kuai Tui, the Quick Knife Squad swiftly gained the upper hand, cutting down numerous sacrificial warriors.

Blood spattered, battle qi erupted.

The mercenaries fought fiercely; the sacrificial warriors fought fearlessly.

The clash between the two forces quickly reached a fever pitch, with casualties mounting relentlessly.

For every three sacrificial warriors who fell, only one mercenary collapsed. The casualty ratio stood at three to one.

The sacrificial warriors possessed high combat proficiency and decent equipment, though their average age was notably advanced.

The mercenaries fought with exceptional ferocity, a result of rigorous training and the morale surge ignited by Tripleblade's relentless combat. Most crucially, the mercenaries' gear was generally superior to their opponents'.

This was especially true for the two battle squads, where nearly every member was fully armed.

Given their small numbers, Zong Ge had invested heavily to equip them. Substandard gear would lead to heavy casualties. Too many casualties would shatter the squad's formation, rendering all their grueling training futile.

Before this battle, Tripleblade considered the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' constant expenditure on acquiring equipment to be unwise. But after this clash, he deeply understood how superficial his previous understanding had been.

Compared to their enemies, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps held another significant advantage.

They had numbers!

Mei Lin had already accomplished a great deal by organizing a group of sacrificial warriors to reach Snow Bird Harbor in such a short time.

Infiltrating this city required considerable effort.

After all, Snow Bird Harbor was under constant surveillance by the mage tower.

If the sacrificial warriors had gathered in one place, their numbers would have been formidable. But they attacked in scattered groups, deploying forces across multiple fronts. While assaulting the alchemy workshop, they suffered heavy casualties from magic traps and other defensive measures.

This resulted in their numerical disadvantage on the battlefield near the small alchemy workshop.

Battle skill - Venomous Fang Slash!

Tripleblade drew the curved blade from his waist, seized the moment, and suddenly plunged into the fray, striking a silver level sacrificial warrior.

The sacrificial warrior was slashed across the lower back, a thin, elongated wound gouged into his flesh.

Initially, crimson blood flowed from the wound, but within seconds, it turned green. Simultaneously, a greenish hue spread from the wound, gradually enveloping the surrounding flesh.

The silver level sacrificial warrior even felt a wave of dizziness.

“Damn it! That blade is poisoned!”

“How despicable!”

The silver level sacrificial warrior attempted to pursue Tripleblade, but the latter's first move after landing the strike was to retreat.

Constrained by the battle's momentum, the warrior couldn't move freely and could only watch helplessly as Tripleblade successfully withdrew.

He hastily retrieved a vial of potion and downed its contents in one gulp.

The dizziness immediately subsided, though the wound still tingled and numbed.

This antidote wasn't strong enough!

The silver level sacrificial warrior growled, warning his comrades nearby, “Watch out for that shorty's poisoned blade!”

Bam!

The next instant, an alchemy bullet whizzed toward him. Had the silver sacrificial warrior not ducked his head, it would have blown his skull apart.

“Guys, I'm here!” Chi Lai materialized atop a rooftop, bellowed once, then vanished into cover.

Men Shi and the rest rejoiced.

Chi Lai was silver level, a formidable fighter. His long-barreled musket had been upgraded to silver level, capable of hitting minute targets at extreme ranges.

Chi Lai disappeared, then fired bullets from shadowy corners. Each bullet posed a severe threat to silver level foes or claimed the lives of other transcendent sacrificial warriors.

This was the world of the strong.

Chi Lai's arrival drastically shifted the combat balance between allies and enemies at the small workshop.

The sacrificial warriors couldn't help but wear expressions of grim resolve.

They all understood this battle held little hope for survival.

Yet they showed no sign of retreat.

The two silver level sacrificial warriors exchanged a swift glance and nodded simultaneously.

They abruptly changed course, charging together toward the workshop gates.

Even as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' attacks struck them, their direction remained utterly unaltered!

“Quick! We must hold the line!” Men Shi cried urgently.

Though he had left a few dwarves inside the workshop, their numbers were meager.

Crucially, these dwarves were no match for two silver level sacrificial warriors!

Men Shi rushed to pursue them.

Joining him in this unified action were the Backwater Squad, Tripleblade, and others.

Bu Quan's disappearance meant the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps couldn't control the facilities within the small alchemy workshop.

Men Shi opened the door and went outside to reinforce the others. But he couldn't reseal the alchemy door completely.

This left an opening for the enemy.

“We can't let this effort go to waste!” Tripleblade arrived first.

Gripping the dagger Sweet Talk in one hand and the poisonous curved blade in the other, he charged at the two silver level opponents.

Boom!

A thunderous explosion sent Tripleblade flying violently, slamming him into the workshop wall.

His entire body sank into the wall, temporarily unable to dislodge himself.

Critically wounded!

Agonizing pain made him grimace. His extensive combat experience told him at least two ribs were broken.

The big guy woke up feeling great.

Just as the magic chef Da Dou had anticipated, he recovered quickly this time.

While he slept, Da Dou had once again piled the long table high with fluttering boat fried rice.

Without hesitation, the big guy grinned like an idiot and dove in with everything he had!

He wasn't the sharpest tool in the shed, but even he realized this was an exceptional, rare opportunity.

Eat! Eat! Eat!

He ate voraciously.

He stuffed himself senselessly!

His appetite had grown.

This time, he devoured not only the iron and bronze level fried rice but also several bowls of silver level food.

Consequently, the big guy's sleep cycle stretched considerably longer.

The audience was universally stunned by this spectacle. In an instant, many gathered around the big guy to discuss.

"Hey hey hey, did you guys see that? He actually ate a silver level transcendent dish!"

"Eating beyond his level isn't really surprising. Previously, iron level transcendents have tried silver level fried rice, but this giant's situation is different."

“Exactly! He devoured all the iron and bronze level fried rice first, then polished off three huge bowls of silver level fried rice on top of that!”

“How can he have such an enormous appetite?”

“What's his background, anyway?”

The big guy had been under scrutiny for quite some time, and his identity could no longer be kept secret at this point.

So, more and more people came to know him as a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

"Their leader, Long Fu, slew Teng Dong Lang in this very place. Now, his comrade is making a stunning showing here too!"

The citizens of Snow Bird Harbor held strong affection for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Upon learning the big guy's identity, they developed considerable fondness for him as well.

Many had previously perceived the big guy as crude and wild, but now, observing him eat and sleep, they found him somewhat honest and down-to-earth.

“No, this can't go on any longer.”

“This giant is absolutely the key factor in this eating contest!”

Seeing his points clearly falling behind his opponent, Ci Hai Shen was forced to adjust his strategy.

He resolved to prepare a dish tailored to the giant's palate, hoping to win the big guy over to his side.

Crafting meals specifically for particular diners, creating dishes that perfectly match their tastes, is also one of the hallmarks of a master chef.

“I've decided, I'll make a stir-fried dragon liver dish!”

Chapter 745: Falling Asleep While Eating

The dragon liver extracted by Ci Hai Shen was entirely jade-green, roughly the size of a millstone.

Initially, the liver emitted no discernible aura, but as the seal was lifted, it instantly captured the audience's attention.

A gold level ingredient!

“Could it be that Ci Hai Shen intends to prepare a gold level dish?” The bishop instinctively rubbed his stomach, anticipating a feast.

“That shouldn't be the case.” Mei Lan Du frowned slightly, puzzled. “Given the current situation, though Ci Hai Shen is behind, his disadvantage isn't yet a losing position. He still has a chance to turn things around. Preparing a gold level dish now carries significant risk.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord enlarged the magic projection, focusing on the dragon liver. Seconds later, his frown smoothed: “This liver is remarkably intact, but it's not from an ancient dragon. Likely some wind-type dragon beast.”

Consuming pure-blooded ancient dragons was taboo.

Slaughtering one almost invariably left a mark. Dragons within a certain radius would sense the bounty mark and pursue the killer relentlessly.

Though individually formidable, dragons, as an intelligent species, required lengthy maturation periods and reproduced far less efficiently than humans or elves.

It was precisely this bounty mark that safeguarded the dragon race to some extent.

Even though the dragon race had been in decline for ages, this bounty mark still struck fear into countless hearts.

Even if Ci Hai Shen wasn't the actual dragon slayer, his public cooking of dragon meat would still brand him an enemy of the dragon race.

Put yourself in their shoes, and you'll understand.

Imagine if another race had a chef publicly roasting and eating human hearts, how would humans react? They would naturally view the chef with hostility and, if possible, bring him to justice.

Moreover, within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom resided the white dragon clan. They dwelled at the highest peak of Ice Sculpture Island, and the Ice Sculpture Royal Family has always strived to maintain an alliance with the white dragons.

Therefore, if Ci Hai Shen publicly cooked a dragon's liver, the trouble wouldn't just fall on him—it would also drag Snow Bird Harbor City Lord into the mess.

What Ci Hai Shen presented, of course, wasn't a dragon's liver, nor even a wyvern's. It was a dragon beast.

Dragon beasts were typically hybrids between beasts and dragons, possessing the characteristics of beasts alongside dragon abilities like wings or fire breath. They were usually smaller in size, nowhere near as towering and formidable as true dragons.

Ci Hai Shen looked extremely solemn as he began preparing the dragon liver.

This ingredient had originally been prepared by Ci Hai Shen for the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's grand banquet, but unexpectedly, it was being used here ahead of schedule.

Though merely a dragon beast, its gold level status was undeniable, guaranteeing the liver's value.

Ci Hai Shen had no choice but to bring it out, only ingredients of this caliber could attract the big guy.

Under Ci Hai Shen's mental control, sharp knives methodically began peeling the dragon liver.

Next came the tendering.

Then, using the Spring Well, Ci Hai Shen continuously rinsed the dragon liver.

Once cleaned, Ci Hai Shen employed the largest cleaver at his disposal to slice it.

The liver was swiftly cut into thin, uniform portions.

He retrieved various seasonings and began marinating the liver.

Using his pocket watch, he adjusted the time to accelerate the liver's aging process.

Then he retrieved the crying face green pepper, angry face red pepper, and smiling face yellow pepper. All were silver level ingredients.

Seeing this, Mei Lan Du in the observation room felt a slight tremor in his heart: “These are emotion peppers. Using so many... I understand Lord Ci Hai Shen's plan now.”

“He doesn't intend to create a gold level dish. Instead, he aims to downgrade the dragon liver.”

His target must be that giant!"

"Ah? Why downgrade it?" the bishop exclaimed, his face clouding with disappointment.

Mei Lan Du explained, “High-grade ingredients are unfriendly to lower-tier transcendents. Thus, most high-level magic chefs possess the ability to downgrade ingredients.”

“Downgrading high-level ingredients yields low-level dishes with nutrition surpassing ordinary low-level dishes.”

“Typically, this extravagant practice is employed by great nobles to nurture their offspring.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed, “That giant truly has a palate for fine things. I've never enjoyed such treatment myself.”

That was the honest truth.

Though Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had Mei Lan Du by his side, the latter was merely silver level.

Mei Lan Du couldn't craft gold level transcendent dishes, while Snow Bird Harbor City Lord himself was gold level. For him to eat downgraded gold level cuisine meant that the ingredients required would have to be at least saint level.

This far exceeded the capabilities of both Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Mei Lan Du.

To create dishes downgraded to silver level, one typically needed a gold level magic chef. And to secure the long-term service of a gold level magic chef, the patron must possess at least saint level strength, or be a major noble with deep-rooted heritage and vast influence.

Mei Lan Du proved worthy of being Ci Hai Shen's chosen assistant; even in the vegetable preparation, one could discern Ci Hai Shen's purpose.

"This giant truly has a huge appetite and can eat a lot. But will Ci Hai Shen succeed?" the bishop inquired curiously.

Mei Lan Du pondered, "In my view, the chances of success are not small. According to the rules, once both magic chefs begin preparing their gold level dishes, the scent-separating formation will automatically activate. The aroma of the food alone will be enough to draw everyone in."

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pressed, “But Ci Hai Shen intends to downgrade and prepare a silver level dish.”

Mei Lan Du nodded. “He'll execute the downgrade during the final stage, but the formation will open when he uses the gold level ingredients. Thus, the intense aroma will surge forth during the cooking process.”

“Only after he completes the downgrade will the formation close again.”

The bishop understood: “So, Ci Hai Shen exploited a loophole in the rules.”

Mei Lan Du shook his head slightly: "Actually, this isn't a loophole, it's a clever application of the rules. When he utilized the gold level ingredient, he had already paid the full price."

“After all, every ingredient in the eating contest comes out of the magic chef's own pocket.”

As Mei Lan Du explained, Ci Hai Shen poured cold oil into the heated wok and added shredded ginger to stir-fry.

Next, he tossed in dragon liver, stir-frying until it changed color.

Then he added three colors of emotion peppers and continued stir-frying.

He retrieved a small glass bottle and carefully poured in some sand salt.

A ripple of excitement rippled through the crowd once more.

This seasoning was also of the gold level, known as time sand salt.

“This salt is crafted primarily from time sand,” Mei Lan Du stated. “The value of this single small bottle could purchase fifty portions of the dragon liver he's currently stir-frying.”

That's not all.

Ci Hai Shen then retrieved a portion of purple-black flavored oil, pouring some into the bottom of the wok.

This too was a gold level seasoning.

Neither Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nor the Sea Bird Bishop were magic chefs and couldn't identify it.

Fortunately, Mei Lan Du was beside them and revealed the truth: "This is hunger devil oil. It helps diners speed up digestion of the food they consume. Lord Ci Hai Shen isn't just attracting the giant—he's also aiding digestion. It seems he has quite high hopes for this giant."

The magic chef, Da Dou, widened his eyes.

After Ci Hai Shen retrieved the gold level dragon liver, his attention had been drawn.

When Ci Hai Shen then produced the emotion peppers, Da Dou instantly grasped the latter's plan.

“He's competing for the giant. Should I... change tactics?” Da Dou hesitated.

His approach was quite unique; he only prepared fluttering boat fried rice.

His recipe was a composite one, capable of producing ordinary, bronze, iron, and silver level fried rice simultaneously.

Of course, if he invested gold level ingredients, he could also cook gold level fried rice!

When making gold level fried rice, both his success rate and output far exceeded the norm.

This was precisely the power of Da Dou's tactic!

But now, it wasn't yet time to risk making a gold level dish.

Da Dou still held the upper hand.

Although Ci Hai Shen used gold level ingredients, he intended to downgrade his dish, so the risk he took was nowhere near that of creating a gold level dish.

Besides the big guy, others still had appetites. Moreover, people were constantly refining their battle qi, digesting the food.

Da Dou hesitated for a moment before choosing to continue with his plan.

The big guy's nostrils flared.

His stomach caved in again, roused by the intense aroma.

Unlike his earlier dazed gaze, this time he stared straight ahead, following the scent of food until he spotted Ci Hai Shen's large wok.

The big guy rose and ran toward Ci Hai Shen.

“Well then, I shall take the key to this victory.” Ci Hai Shen watched the big guy approach his half of the area, a smug smile spreading across his face.

In the final stage, the gold level aura rapidly dissipated, downgrading to silver level.

Food was served.

The stir-fried dragon liver was placed on a massive platter, then, under Ci Hai Shen's psychic control, flew directly to the big guy's front.

The big guy reached out to grab it, but the platter suddenly shot away.

The tray didn't fly fast, guiding the big guy step by step toward the long table by Ci Hai Shen's side before landing on the tabletop.

The big guy had been drooling all the way over. Excitedly, he grabbed the tray and devoured the dragon liver in two bites.

Then, his attention was drawn to the countless dishes spread across the long table.

His eyes lit up. So many delicious things here!

Nothing more to say, let’s eat!

Watching the big guy shovel dish after dish into his mouth, Ci Hai Shen wore a satisfied grin.

He began planning his next move: “Now that the giant's been lured here, we must prevent him from being drawn back.”

“Therefore, the next dish I prepare must be even more exquisite than before.”

“Giants require deep sleep to digest their food; that's valuable time we must minimize. That means creating easily digestible dishes.”

“Hmm... Decided! I'll make pine flower shark fin soup. Its exquisite flavor will leave him craving more!”

Magic spell - Clone.

Ci Hai Shen conjured over a dozen clones, each simultaneously preparing the soup.

By the time the big guy finished the table's offerings, platters of pine flower shark fin soup were already flying toward him.

At first, he tried to grab the plates with his hands, but soon, with Ci Hai Shen's assistance, all he needed to do was open his mouth, tilt his head back, and wait for the shark fin to be placed inside.

“Delicious! Delicious! So delicious!”

“Snore... snore...”

As he ate, the big guy drifted off to sleep.

Chapter 746: Gold Level Dish

The big guy's body once again radiated a dark iron-colored battle qi. As he channeled the battle qi technique, his bulging belly rapidly flattened.

The audience fell completely silent.

The looks they cast at the big guy grew increasingly thick with envy and admiration.

In a culinary showdown between magic chefs, vying for diners was expected.

But for a gold level magic chef to go to such lengths, using gold level ingredients and even voluntarily downgrading the dish's level, was exceptionally rare! Not even Snow Bird Harbor City Lord received such treatment under normal circumstances.

Yet upon reflection, the audience quickly understood: Ci Hai Shen had compelling reasons for this.

“That giant's appetite is truly enormous.”

“His digestion speed is too fast.”

"Bloodline... I'm curious, what exactly is his bloodline? He cultivates the Mindless Sleeping Posture battle qi technique, which doesn't aid a fighter's digestion. His ability to eat so much and digest so quickly must stem from his bloodline. The higher the bloodline level, the more efficient the refinement of battle qi."

Thus, more and more people began to wonder what exactly the big guy's bloodline was.

The big guy awoke once more.

“Have you noticed how much faster this giant wakes up now?” Many spectators observed this change.

Of course, it was so.

Though still stuck at the iron level, the big guy had consumed so many dishes and absorbed such vast amounts of external energy that his strength had surged dramatically. This change increased the reserve of battle qi within his body and also accelerated the speed at which he could refine it.

The big guy continued eating.

He didn't need to walk around the long table or even pick up a tray.

Ci Hai Shen directly used his spirit power to send all kinds of dishes into the big guy's mouth.

This service was far superior to what was offered at Da Dou's place.

The big guy tilted his head back, chewing and swallowing ceaselessly, accepting everything without refusal.

A subtle, icy glint flickered in Ci Hai Shen's eyes.

“This brute... his intelligence is indeed lacking. His bloodline is at least gold level.”

“What if... I prepared a gold level dish for him to eat? What would happen then?”

Ci Hai Shen knew nothing of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps; he had no connection to them whatsoever. His idea of creating a gold level dish for the big guy wasn't out of concern for him, but solely for victory.

As long as the big guy consumed the gold level dish, it counted as points, even if he ate himself to death afterward.

This wasn't exploiting a loophole in the eating contest rules; it was precisely where a magic chef's true power shone. Couldn't compelling a diner to lose all reason and devour a dish be seen as a demonstration of strength?

Ci Hai Shen harbored malicious intent. He'd heard of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps but couldn't care less.

In fact, he'd seen the magic footage of that heroic duel.

“So what if a silver level defeated a gold level?”

“I'm gold level too. If I don't want to fight Long Fu, I'll just leave.”

Just as Tripleblade had feared, even with such an impressive victory, the dragonman youngster remained merely gold level in battle strength. His deterrent effect simply couldn't match that of a true gold level.

Ci Hai Shen wanted to win desperately. The stakes were the Preservation Knife, which significantly impacted his strength.

But soon, Ci Hai Shen dismissed the idea of crafting a gold level meal altogether.

“Creating a gold level dish carries risks.”

No transcendent cuisine was merely a simple stacking of ingredients. Instead, it required the magic chef to employ various techniques during preparation, unifying the ingredients into a cohesive whole where they harmonized, thereby balancing the magic.

"Now is not the time to risk creating a gold level dish. I should prepare a large quantity of food. The more the giant eats, the more it benefits my points to overtake the lead."

Ci Hai Shen began designing a menu for the big guy.

Menu design and recipe development were among the core skills of a magic chef.

Designing menus wasn't straightforward. A magic chef had to consider each dish's function, how they interacted and complemented one another, a task that truly tested their skill.

“Of course, we can't neglect the others. We still need to prepare some of the previous dishes to maintain appeal for the general crowd.”

Ci Hai Shen understood the situation clearly: the other diners were the foundation, while the big guy was his weapon for catching up on points.

He kept churning out dishes.

At this stage, the pressure from the crowd's continued consumption was minimal, freeing up more of his energy to focus on preparing meals for the big guy.

The big guy didn't disappoint Ci Hai Shen. Ci Hai Shen slowly caught up, the gap between them steadily narrowing.

Mei Lan Du discerned the pattern and analyzed, "Lord Ci Hai Shen has been thoughtful. The transcendent dishes he fed the giant could already form a menu. Each dish contains copious amounts of digestive aids, and he repeatedly uses time sand salt, which accelerates the flow of time within the giant."

"Keep a close watch and take good care of this young giant. After all, he belongs to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps." Snow Bird Harbor City Lord ordered.

Da Dou's expression darkened.

The sole reason he'd been caught by his opponent was because of the big guy.

Yet his gaze toward the big guy remained gentle. As fellow giants, Da Dou felt an innate kinship and goodwill toward him.

“If all else fails, I'll just have to make some gold level fried rice!” Da Dou had already made up his mind.

Within Snow Bird Harbor City, a small alchemy workshop.

“Finally... it's over.” The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' members stood on the blood-soaked battlefield littered with corpses, panting heavily, their expressions generally dazed.

Every single sacrificial warrior had perished.

Most had fallen at the entrance to the alchemy workshop.

The final charge had been truly terrifying. The fierce determination and battle will unleashed by the sacrificial warriors had shaken every mercenary to their core.

The dwarves had fulfilled their duty to the utmost, ultimately forging a line of defense with their own flesh and blood, buying crucial time for the reinforcements led by Men Shi and Chi Lai.

At the very end, Tripleblade attacked with almost no defense, channeling every ounce of his power into the assault.

The two silver level sacrificial warriors fell first.

Expecting the sacrificial warrior's morale to plummet, they were instead met with an even fiercer onslaught.

Dwarves kept falling to their charges, though the narrow gate proved easier to defend than to breach.

Finally, seeing defeat inevitable, the last few bronze level sacrificial warriors bit through the poison sacs embedded in their teeth and took their own lives.

“These men were sacrificial warriors. They feared death not at all.”

“We were hoping to take a few prisoners alive, but they chose death instead!”

“Damn it! Where did these men even come from?”

Many mercenaries still trembled with lingering fear.

“We've won!”

“That's right, we've secured the last workshop.”

"But what about Bu Quan? And where is our giant?" Tripleblade asked Men Shi and Chi Lai respectively.

The dwarf shook his head. "Miss Bu Quan vanished suddenly from her spot. She disappeared in exactly the same way as the assassin who ambushed us earlier. She still hasn't reappeared."

Chi Lai was more direct: "Though your order was to retrieve the giant, I chose not to. Rushing to support you was the wisest decision! As things stand, my choice was correct."

"What?!" Tripleblade's eyes blazed with fury, his hands itching to slap Chi Lai across the face.

So after all that, Bu Quan's gone, and the big guy's lost too?

“Cough cough cough!” Tripleblade forced himself to stand despite the pain, coughing up blood as he staggered toward the exit.

“Wait! You need rest and healing.” Men Shi blocked his path.

This battle had made him look at Tripleblade with new respect.

In truth, all the surviving mercenaries had elevated their opinion of Tripleblade significantly.

Tripleblade had shone brightly in this fight. His fearless, fierce fighting spirit had given morale a massive boost.

Tripleblade sighed inwardly: "The big guy is one of us. Even if he went mad, he was still hunting down the enemy. We can't abandon any member lightly!"

These words lit a spark in the mercenaries' eyes, and they eagerly volunteered to follow Tripleblade in searching for the big guy.

“Hurry up! Sometimes, just a little faster support can rewrite the outcome of a battle!” Tripleblade was impatient.

He needed to keep the big guy's potential a secret, so he couldn't reveal the exact reason to Chi Lai, Men Shi, and the others.

Tripleblade understood Chi Lai's choice.

After all, the latter didn't grasp the big guy's true value!

“Hope we're not too late... big guy, hold on...” Tripleblade bolted out, a group of mercenaries trailing behind.

The big guy couldn't hold on.

He collapsed to the ground once more.

Snore... snore...

He began snoring contentedly.

As the Mindless Sleeping Posture battle qi technique cycled once more, the delicacies within his belly transformed into nourishment for his rising strength.

Soon, he awoke again and resumed eating.

In the observation room.

The bishop's eyes flashed. Finally unable to contain himself, he ordered, “Find out this giant's name, where he comes from, and his specific faith!”

The bloodline the big guy displayed was at least gold level.

This fact had been confirmed in the bishop's mind.

A future gold level!

This stirred the interest of the powerful bishop, who sought to convert the big guy to the Sea Bird Church.

Yet Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, seated nearby, harbored no such thoughts.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is backed by arms dealers. Did they truly recruit this giant aboard on Snake Rat Island?”

"Perhaps it's merely a disguise. The giant could well be the arms dealers' arrangement."

“Much like the golden-hued Shi Qi, or the silver level Long Fu who slays foes beyond his level.”

The situation in the arena had now become clear.

Only a dozen or so contestants remained capable of consuming more dishes.

Ci Hai Shen and Da Dou's scores were once again neck and neck.

“He holds the key to victory!” The gazes of both gold level magic chefs simultaneously locked onto the big guy.

"No, and the two gold level observers too. Watch my gold level fried rice!" Da Dou resolved, using gold level ingredients to risk the refining process!

This move instantly sent shockwaves through the entire venue.

“The decisive battle has begun!” Almost simultaneously, Ci Hai Shen also began preparing his gold level dish.

Chapter 747: Determining Victory and Defeat

Snow Bird Duel Arena.

In the spectator room, Mei Lan Du mused, “Who would've thought this culinary duel would actually come down to preparing a gold level dish in the end?”

Such an outcome was uncommon.

Typically, contests are decided at the iron or silver level.

But Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen possessed comparable skills. The only pivotal factor, the big guy, was seized by Ci Hai Shen, causing Da Dou to gradually lose his points advantage.

It wasn't that Da Dou's choice back then was wrong.

Even if he had kept the big guy, Ci Hai Shen would still have risked creating a gold level dish.

For a magic chef, crafting dishes of the same level is inherently risky.

Creating transcendent cuisine differs from crafting potions or equipment. The latter two may require lengthy refinement periods, whereas cooking often demands swift execution.

Within a short time frame, the chef must rapidly achieve magic equilibrium among all ingredients.

Consumption must also be prompt. Should this magic balance be disrupted, the transcendent dish risks self-destruction, or even explosion.

For Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen, the success rate for crafting gold level dishes is far lower than for silver level. As for the even higher saint level, it completely exceeds their capabilities.

Gold level fluttering boat fried rice!

Da Dou added gold level ingredients, tossing the silver level wok vigorously. Soon, golden grains of rice appeared.

“So swift!” Mei Lan Du exclaimed excitedly in the observation room. "I finally saw the recipe Lord Da Dou used. Though this recipe only produces one type of fried rice dish from start to finish, it allows for fine-tuning. More importantly, it prioritizes quantity over quality, significantly boosting the output speed and success rate of high-level fried rice dishes! "

“No wonder Lord Da Dou possesses such unwavering confidence in defeating Lord Ci Hai Shen!”

Da Dou's pace in crafting gold level dishes far surpassed Ci Hai Shen's.

Moments later.

Two bowls of gold level fried rice were presented before Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Bishop.

Both had been eagerly anticipating this moment. After finishing their portions, their eyes shone brightly as they praised the dish lavishly.

“Damn it!” Ci Hai Shen couldn't help but feel a surge of tension.

Only two gold level diners were present.

With both having consumed their bowls of gold level fried rice, Da Dou had secured a massive advantage.

For whatever dishes Ci Hai Shen prepared next, the city lord and bishop might not finish them.

Gold level Butterfly Petal Magic Salad!

The salad's main ingredient was butterfly petals, blended with night-glow lotus root, then combined with frosted giant lizard leg meat, and a sauce of magic cardamom and pearl pear vinegar.

Finally, it was drizzled with refreshing olive oil.

Ci Hai Shen's dish was also submitted.

He nervously gazed toward the observation room.

Moments later, word came down.

Both the city lord and the bishop had eaten a portion of the butterfly petal magic salad.

Hearing this, Ci Hai Shen finally exhaled a slight sigh of relief.

He glanced at the scoreboard and saw his points were once again neck-and-neck with Da Dou's.

Ci Hai Shen dared not slack off, burying himself in preparing the second gold level dish.

Unsurprisingly, Da Dou took the lead again, crafting the second wave of gold level fried rice.

However, when his fried rice reached the observation room, news arrived: the city lord had eaten half a bowl, and the bishop had eaten a third before both could eat no more.

Simultaneously, both declared they would consume no further gold level dishes.

“Hmph, those two!” Da Dou grumbled discontentedly.

Ci Hai Shen breathed a sigh of relief.

Da Dou's advantage was overwhelming, and Ci Hai Shen found it hard to believe the bishop and city lord had truly reached their limit.

Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen's suspicions were correct.

The bishop and city lord had voluntarily abandoned the feast, primarily out of political considerations for the kingdom.

For the Ice Sculpture National Ceremony, the royal family had personally invited Da Dou, Ci Hai Shen, and other magic chefs. As hosts, they couldn't intervene in the personal grudges between Da Dou and Ci Hai Shen. If things went wrong and affected the Ice Sculpture Festival, they'd be the ones to suffer.

“What a waste. Could this end in a draw?!” Da Dou gritted his teeth, glaring at his rival Ci Hai Shen. He was unwilling to accept this.

But just then, his gaze shifted back to the big guy.

The big guy had awakened.

He stretched lazily, then followed the irresistible aroma to the iron wok in Da Dou's hands.

Within the pot lay gold level fried rice grains.

“Oh no!” Amidst Ci Hai Shen's darkened gaze, the big guy leapt to his feet and dashed toward Da Dou, showing not a shred of reluctance to leave Ci Hai Shen's side.

Da Dou's heart shook: "Inside the gold level fried rice, I added the supplementary ingredient, dragon might black pepper. Logically, a low level transcendent shouldn't... He's clearly only iron level. Could it be..."

The thought struck him, and Da Dou hastily shook all the gold level rice grains onto the plate.

Just as before, the big guy picked up the plate and began shoveling it into his mouth.

This scene immediately drew gasps from the crowd.

An iron level individual being force-fed gold level fried rice? This could be deadly.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord urgently ordered care for the big guy.

The bishop also dispatched clerical healers to surround him.

The big guy could only manage half the fried rice before collapsing, falling asleep instantly.

It was the same silver level priest who had intercepted him earlier. Approaching the big guy, he cycled through several divine spells for monitoring and examination.

The results left the priest's face twisted in a peculiar expression.

“He's fine!”

“He's actively digesting the fried rice now. He'll wake up soon.”

This announcement sparked even greater commotion.

The crowd buzzed with discussion, utterly fascinated by the big guy's true lineage!

“He only ate half the fried rice,” Ci Hai Shen felt a chill run down his spine. “Once he wakes up, finishing the other half won't be difficult!”

He had already greatly overestimated the big guy, yet the strength the big guy displayed made him realize that his previous “overestimation” was actually an underestimation!

“Big guy!” Tripleblade was the first to burst into the duel arena.

Soon after, Chi Lai and the others who had been running alongside him also arrived at the scene.

Tripleblade was intercepted and hastily declared his identity: “I'm a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

Chi Lai's arrival saved Tripleblade from the predicament.

Not everyone recognized Tripleblade, an iron level mercenary, but Chi Lai, as a key figure in the Dragon Lion Corps' branch, was well-known to many.

On the way here, Tripleblade had received the latest intel on the big guy, learning of the uproar he'd caused in the arena.

Though the silver level priest assured him the big guy was in good condition, Tripleblade remained furious.

He glared at Da Dou, showing no regard for the latter's status as a gold level powerhouse: "You know full well he's only iron level, yet you still fed him gold level fried rice. Are you so desperate for victory that you'd risk a patron's life?"

Da Dou shook his head. “It's not like that...”

He began to explain but quickly stopped speaking of his own accord.

Tripleblade's heart skipped a beat.

In full view of everyone, Tripleblade's earlier rebuke had been merely a test.

Now, the test result was already out.

Da Dou could explain but he chose to keep quiet. Why would he do that?

"Bring the big guy and go back first!" Tripleblade gave orders to the rest of the mercenaries.

“Chi Lai, after fighting this long, you must be starving, right?” Tripleblade gave Chi Lai a knowing look, then led him and the others over to stand beside Ci Hai Shen.

They had been in constant combat, their bodies bearing the marks of battle.

In contrast, the transcendents inside the arena were all stuffed to the gills, unable to eat another bite. Even if they refined their battle qi to aid digestion, such behavior had its limits.

The transcendents of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps became the decisive force in this culinary duel.

Earlier, the big guy had eaten the gold level fried rice, but it was only half a portion and hadn't counted toward points.

Should they choose Ci Hai Shen or Da Dou?

For Tripleblade, the choice was clearly the former.

On one hand, he held deep apprehension and distaste toward Da Dou. On the other hand, he had placed substantial wagers on Ci Hai Shen.

The points continuously earned by Tripleblade, Chi Lai, and others eating Ci Hai Shen's dishes allowed him to pull ahead of Da Dou.

When Men Shi arrived and joined the feast, this lead persisted right up until the very last moment.

The city lord announced, “The ultimate victor of this duel is Ci Hai Shen!”

The entire crowd erupted in cheers.

The city lord then publicly presented the wager, Preservation Knife and the Meat Eye, to Ci Hai Shen.

Ci Hai Shen roared with laughter, his heart brimming with triumphant joy. He taunted Da Dou.

The latter stood arms crossed, expressionless.

After a few sarcastic remarks, Ci Hai Shen’s expression shifted abruptly as he launched an attack.

Da Dou made no attempt to dodge. After being struck, he dissolved into a shimmering mist and vanished.

Ci Hai Shen sneered coldly, "You lack even the courage to face defeat! Da Dou, how dare you ever show your face before me again?"

The audience murmured in discussion.

Tripleblade frowned, a growing sense of unease spreading through him. “Let's head back first.”

Before he could finish speaking, a wounded mercenary stumbled back, panting. “Lord Chi Lai, it's bad! The Magic Chef Da Dou suddenly appeared and snatched our giant!”

"What?!" The group gasped in shock.

Chapter 748: Tunnel Staff

“Da Dou snatched the big guy?!” Tripleblade paled in shock.

"This can't stand. We must get the big guy back! Even if the opponent is a gold level." Tripleblade hesitated not a moment.

The big guy was too crucial!

Tripleblade could see the big guy possessed at least saint level potential!

What organization would abandon a saint talent? Not even the mightiest force, the Sheng Ming Empire, would do such a thing.

Da Dou's actions were essentially poaching the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' future pillar. This was absolutely intolerable!

Tripleblade immediately ordered Chi Lai, Men Shi, and others to assemble elite forces to intercept Da Dou.

Simultaneously, he mobilized the alchemy puppets aboard the fleet, determined to thwart Da Dou at all costs.

But the next words from the reporting mercenary plunged Tripleblade into an icy abyss.

"What?! Da Dou vanished right where he stood?!” Tripleblade demanded, eyes wide.

The mercenary nodded, his expression filled with terror.

“Damn it!” Tripleblade cursed, spinning on his heel and racing toward the observation chamber.

Upon entering, he sought aid from Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord agreed to intervene, declaring that Da Dou's actions violated the harbor's order and that as its ruler, he could not stand idly by.

Seated nearby, the Sea Bird Bishop also volunteered his assistance. He declared: It is precisely the presence of the big guy that has added luster to this rare culinary showdown. He held a genuine fondness for this simple giant!

Tripleblade understood perfectly: "The big guy's unexpected involvement in this culinary duel exposed much of his potential. Both Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Bishop showed interest, but Da Dou was the most direct, he simply snatched the big guy away!

Though Da Dou vanished instantly, that didn't mean he left no trace.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord immediately led Tripleblade and the others back to the docks, entering the mage tower.

The magician tower maintained constant surveillance, naturally capturing magic footage of Da Dou's intervention and the big guy's abduction.

Tripleblade and the others witnessed the entire sequence.

The mercenaries escorting the big guy moved slowly. Suddenly, a spatial ripple appeared, and Da Dou materialized, scattering a handful of pepper.

The pepper landed on the mercenaries, igniting instantly and burning fiercely.

The mercenaries, struggling to save themselves, descended into chaos.

Da Dou struck again, instantly knocking the big guy unconscious. He captured him and teleported away once more.

Tripleblade and the others were filled with shock and rage.

Da Dou's actions were remarkably efficient. With just two moves, he had snatched the big guy away.

The magician reported with a troubled expression: Everything happened too quickly. Only 48.3% of the spatial fluctuations from Da Dou's teleportation were recorded by the mage tower.

At this level, it was nearly impossible to deduce Dà Dōu's destination through spatial fluctuations alone.

Tripleblade's heart sank to the depths.

The magician asked him: “If you provided more detailed information about the big guy, perhaps we could use it to track his location.”

This made Tripleblade hesitant.

The big guy's origins and bloodline were secrets. Exposing them would endanger all the survivors.

But without providing it, how could he find the big guy?

Tripleblade suspected the magician's words might be a ploy, merely an attempt to pry more information about the big guy from him.

This made Tripleblade even more flustered.

He understood clearly: under the current circumstances, most believed the big guy possessed a gold level bloodline. Da Dou harbored doubts. Yet if the big guy's bloodline secret were exposed, everyone would know he was destined for at least the saint level. Even if Snow Bird Harbor City Lord found the big guy, he'd never return him to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

As Tripleblade hesitated, the mage tower suddenly emitted a shrill alarm.

The mage tower detected a massive, high-energy object approaching Snow Bird Harbor. Moreover, this object was rapidly descending, targeting the harbor's docks.

Just as the mage tower prepared to engage, it received a communication from Shuang Lian.

Realizing the situation, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord finally calmed down and immediately expressed his welcome for Shuang Lian's arrival.

Everyone exited the tower to greet her.

Soon, the Honey Snow Tower revealed itself before their eyes.

Boom…

Honey Snow Tower finally landed on the docks, mere hundreds of meters from the dock's mage tower.

Honey Snow Tower stood five stories tall. Compared to it, the dock's mage tower suddenly seemed much smaller, like a little brother.

Bu Quan was the first to emerge from the Honey Snow Tower.

Tripleblade had long been privy to intelligence regarding Bu Quan and knew full well that Bu Quan's mentor was none other than Shuang Lian.

Seeing Bu Quan return, and hearing that he had persuaded his teacher to aid the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Tripleblade was both astonished and overjoyed.

Unexpectedly powerful reinforcements!

Tripleblade paid a visit to Shuang Lian, earnestly requesting her assistance in helping the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps recover the big guy.

Without any hesitation, Shuang Lian immediately mobilized the search capabilities of the Honey Snow Tower and requested the spatial fluctuation records from the dock's mage tower.

Honey Snow Tower's capabilities were clearly far superior to those of the dock's mage tower.

What the dock's mage tower could not accomplish, under Shuang Lian's direction, yielded a clear response: “The spatial fluctuation records are incomplete. It will take three days to calculate the answer.”

“Da Dou was able to teleport using a Tunnel Staff.”

“This teleportation artifact was mass-produced during the demon war a thousand years ago.”

"It's a consumable item, usable only three times and non-rechargeable. Thanks to it, the allied races' forces could deploy troops faster back then, barely holding off the demons' assaults from all fronts."

“After the war ended, this teleportation device became a banned item, prohibited from production.”

"The Tunnel Staff not only employs spatial teleportation technology but also possesses the ability to monitor and harness earth veins. It is precisely by tapping into these veins that the user's energy expenditure is drastically reduced, allowing for far more precise destination targeting."

"From this, we can infer that even if Da Dou continues using the staff, assuming he has no second one, he can only teleport a maximum of three times. I believe the likelihood of him possessing two Tunnel Staffs is quite low."

“He snatched your people, committing a crime in plain sight. This means he has directly forfeited his participation in the upcoming national ceremony.”

"He'll likely head far from Ice Sculpture Island and probably won't teleport deep inland."

"With the Tunnel Staff, the greater the geomantic enhancement, the farther the teleportation distance. Considering this, Da Dou will likely teleport to larger islands."

"Later, I'll retrieve the specific usage data for the Tunnel Staff from the guild. With that data and nautical charts, we can calculate his departure range and potential escape routes."

Shuang Lian spoke with confident eloquence.

Her words left Tripleblade both thrilled and moved.

Thrilled because, compared to the magician earlier, Shuang Lian was so professional!

She'd applied her knowledge to pinpoint clues in such a short time!!

He was moved because Shuang Lian had offered her help voluntarily, truly a godsend.

Tripleblade's gratitude continued to swell.

Next, Shuang Lian mentioned she'd heard the deputy commander, Shi Qi, was injured and unconscious. She could try to see if she could be of assistance.

Overwhelmed with emotion, Tripleblade dared not suggest Shuang Lian attempt treatment.

Though he was confident in his Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, it was better to avoid trouble. After all, the current “Shi Qi” was merely an alchemy puppet.

Tripleblade protested, "Shi Qi's injuries aren't severe. He possesses unique healing battle skills. This minor wound is nothing to him."

“Really?” Shuang Lian stared at him in disbelief. "Are you certain? You must understand, the one who attacked Shi Qi wasn't merely a silver level assassin. It was a gold level assassin!"

Tripleblade flinched before replying that he had suspected as much. But “Shi Qi” truly was unharmed.

The mention of a gold level assassin sent shockwaves through Snow Bird Harbor City's leadership.

Shuang Lian turned to him, her face icy, her voice laced with fury: “This gold level assassin also attempted to attack my beloved disciple! But now, I have captured him!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was stunned, then immediately declared in a rage that he would pursue this matter to the very end!

This stance earned a slight nod from Shuang Lian.

Her mention of the gold level assassin was precisely to leverage the official authority of Snow Bird Harbor City to deal with the enemy.

If the enemy had only targeted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Shuang Lian wouldn't have been so enraged.

But the gold level assassin Yi Jiu had attacked Bu Quan, this had crossed Shuang Lian's line.

Shuang Lian would retaliate.

But as a magician, her vengeance would be strategic. Her first move was to mobilize every available resource to aid her revenge.

Moreover, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's own position was reason enough for him to act.

Shuang Lian then dispatched her students to repair the destroyed alchemy workshop.

All of this, naturally, was Bu Quan's suggestion.

“Moving forward, I will continue overseeing the alchemy within the city,” Bu Quan stated her intentions.

Tripleblade exclaimed, “Miss Bu Quan, you've truly been a tremendous help this time!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood nearby, inwardly stunned.

What astonished him most was Shuang Lian's attitude.

After all, she was a powerful elder of the Alchemy Guild, possessing formidable strength and the support of the Honey Snow Tower. What price had the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps paid to secure such an existence's assistance?

Even Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sensed that Shuang Lian's assistance had been somewhat overly enthusiastic.

“I shall remain here for the foreseeable future,” Shuang Lian declared to Snow Bird Harbor City Lord before parting.

“I will not rest until I uncover the enemy who targeted my apprentice!”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hastened to respond, “I too shall dedicate myself fully to this matter.”

He then volunteered to divert energy channels from beneath Snow Bird Harbor to support the Honey Snow Tower.

“Thanks,” Shuang Lian replied. “I've already tapped 80% of the energy channels.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord turned to face his mage tower's tower master.

The tower master spread his hands in a wry smile, having just been about to sneak off to report.

Honey Snow Tower was too overbearing. Upon landing, it had immediately struck, severing the underground energy channels. The dock's mage tower simply couldn't compete!

“Cang Xu, your divine spells are truly formidable when used for proselytizing!”

In a makeshift hut along the White Hair Trench, Xiao Wang Zi gave Cang Xu a thumbs-up.

At that moment, a slender soul chain linked Cang Xu and Xiao Wang Zi together.

Chapter 749: Changing Xiao Wang Zi's Faith

Divine spell - Soul Link.

The pale blue soul chain forged an unprecedentedly intimate bond between Cang Xu and Xiao Wang Zi's souls.

Xiao Wang Zi regarded the soul chain with curiosity.

He could sense its fragility—any intense resistance would shatter it.

Cang Xu had already verified this during the Deep Sea Monster Fish incident. Back then, the transcendent fishman he faced wasn't even a magician, let alone silver level, and was in poor condition, yet he could still snap the soul chain cleanly.

Thus, this divine spell proved impractical for combat, suitable only for situations like the present.

Xiao Wang Zi cooperated fully, offering no resistance whatsoever.

He was a skeleton, his eye sockets hollow, yet Cang Xu could sense certain emotions from him through the way he gazed up at him and the connection of the soul chain.

Guilt, closeness, and a faint hint of reverence.

When Cang Xu first teleported back, he did not hold Xiao Wang Zi accountable, which amplified the latter's guilt manifold.

This allowed Cang Xu to breach Xiao Wang Zi's emotional defenses, achieving a breakthrough in their relationship.

Later, Cang Xu schemed against Qi Ling as retribution.

He and Qi Ling engaged in a brilliant game of wits centered around Jian Gui. Qi Ling sought to draw on Jian Gui's power to confront Cang Xu. Cang Xu, however, exploited the situation to thwart Jian Gui's plans against him, successfully sending the entire Da Ji Clan to a teleportation blood sacrifice.

Xiao Wang Zi and Ku Feng had long harbored resentment toward Qi Ling. Cang Xu's scheme against the Da Ji Clan, directly confronting Qi Ling, gave them immense satisfaction upon witnessing it!

Upon his second return, Cang Xu stunned everyone once more.

He had mastered a divine spell, aiding the Da Ji Clan within the Ocean Mother Nest. This led many fishmen to convert successfully, securing their survival.

Cang Xu's move was nothing short of brilliant!

First, he successfully divided the Da Ji Clan.

On the other hand, he held the Da Ji Clan as leverage, forcing Qi Ling to tread carefully.

Finally, Cang Xu's status skyrocketed as a result, earning greater trust from the Charming Blue Church. Zhou Zhang lifted both seals binding him that very day.

Even Xiao Wang Zi, though politically naive, sensed an exceptional ability in Cang Xu's series of actions. His growing admiration felt entirely natural.

“Xiao Wang Zi and I have grown much closer.”

“In time, he will be a valuable ally in my ploy against Qi Ling!”

“Alas, the time has been too brief. My influence over him still pales beside that of Jian Gui.”

As these thoughts crossed his mind, Cang Xu cast an imperceptible glance at Jian Gui standing before him.

This time, when he cast the divine spell to guide Xiao Wang Zi's faith, he did so right in front of Jian Gui.

In truth, it was Jian Gui who had suggested it.

Cang Xu found it hard to refuse, so he agreed on the spot, quite decisively.

Jian Gui was hypocritical, but skilled, able to maintain the charade, deceiving Xiao Wang Zi, Ku Feng, and Qi Ling.

Jian Gui wielded immense influence over the three of them, the result of years of control.

It was not something Cang Xu could rival through mere effort in such a short time.

Divine spell - Spiritual Sound Sermon.

Cang Xu drew upon his memories and poured them into Xiao Wang Zi.

In an instant, the doctrines of the Charming Blue faith, along with Cang Xu's own interpretations of them, flowed through the soul link, seeping deep into the skeleton magician's soul.

Xiao Wang Zi gained profound insights, exclaiming, “So this is another layer of meaning within the Charming Blue faith's doctrines!”

Under the spell's effect, his comprehension of the doctrines reached the same level as Cang Xu's.

Though several days had passed since Cang Xu's second return via teleportation, this marked the first time he had guided Xiao Wang Zi in matters of faith.

In truth, Xiao Wang Zi, lacking confidence in himself, had long ago invited Cang Xu to intervene.

Cang Xu had politely deferred, offering a perfectly reasonable justification.

He had argued: The first guidance of faith was crucial and had to be approached with utmost care. If the first attempt failed, subsequent attempts, no matter how many, would yield little effect.

The optimal moment for the first conversion was during the teleportation blood sacrifice, under the overwhelming pressure of death!

Only then can the effect of the first conversion be maximized.

Cang Xu not only offered his own suggestion but also used himself as an example to substantiate it: “The first time I was in the Mother Nest, it was only under the intense pressure of death brought by the saint beast that I broke through my own limits.”

“I believe the reason Charming Blue Goddess placed Her saint beast within the Mother Nest was likely to harness the overwhelming pressure of death to cultivate faith.”

Cang Xu's words convinced not only Xiao Wang Zi but also Jian Gui.

Thus, the conversion before the teleportation blood sacrifice occurred!

Divine spell - Spiritual Navigation.

As the divine spell unfolded, an image of Charming Blue Goddess spontaneously materialized within Xiao Wang Zi's mind.

The spiritual energy within him flowed naturally into the divine form, as effortlessly as water seeking its lowest point.

Once the spell concluded, Cang Xu inquired what Xiao Wang Zi had witnessed.

The latter truthfully described the vision of Charming Blue Goddess.

He then inquired, “Is there a problem?”

Cang Xu smiled and explained, “The function of this divine spell is actually spatial positioning and teleportation.”

"However, most spatial teleportation spells transport either the caster or the target. This spell, however, acts upon an individual's spiritual energy."

“Over these past days, I've used this spell multiple times and gained deeper understanding.”

“Discussions with Lord Zhou Zhang have further enriched my insights, especially through her guidance.”

"Lord Zhou Zhang holds this divine spell in high regard, for it establishes a direct connection between believers and the deity's essence itself. Unlike conventional prayer, where believers' spiritual energy gathers upon statues, altars, churches, and such facilities before being channeled through them to the deity."

"The Charming Blue Goddess statue you just saw is actually the deity's true form. It merely manifests as a statue based on your level of closeness, understanding, and faith toward Her."

“If you were more devoted to the Charming Blue Goddess, understood the doctrine more deeply, and held steadfast faith, you wouldn't see a statue at all—but rather deeper symbols like altars or churches.”

Xiao Wang Zi realized: “So that's how it is.”

Though simple-minded, he was still a magician and understood Cang Xu's implied meaning.

Immediately, Xiao Wang Zi murmured with worry: "I only saw the statue. That means my faith in the Charming Blue Goddess isn't very deep. This teleportation blood sacrifice is likely to go awry for me!"

Cang Xu hurriedly reassured him: “During my first Spiritual Navigation, I too saw only the Charming Blue Goddess statue.”

“But now, when I navigate, I can already perceive this gold level altar we're using!”

“Keep going, you'll definitely succeed next time.”

Cang Xu's expression turned serious: “The upcoming divine spell is the most critical!”

He instructed Xiao Wang Zi: "Follow my prayer. You may experience intense pain during this process, endure it and do not cease praying."

"Even after the divine spell concludes, keep praying. Sustain it for as long as possible."

Xiao Wang Zi nodded in understanding.

Cang Xu began his invocation.

"Great Charming Blue Goddess, I praise Your mercy. As high as the heavens are above the earth, so great is the kindness You show to Your faithful believers; as far as the east is from the west, so far have You removed our transgressions from us..."

While praying, Cang Xu performed the divine spell - Synchronized Conversion!

Instantly, the pale blue chain binding them both erupted with blinding radiance.

Cang Xu's divine power, spiritual energy, and very soul essence were being violently drained.

Simultaneously, Xiao Wang Zi's soul underwent a brutal transformation. Agonizing pain surged from its deepest recesses, nearly overwhelming his consciousness.

Fortunately, Xiao Wang Zi had prepared himself mentally beforehand, barely clinging to his sanity.

He followed Cang Xu's lead, murmuring his prayers.

The effect was immediate!

The reshaping of his soul amplified the efficacy of his prayers beyond anything he'd ever experienced before.

The degree of enhancement was profound, deeply felt by Xiao Wang Zi.

At that moment, even Jian Gui couldn't conceal the piercing brilliance in his eyes!

"Synchronized Conversion divine spell? It can actually modify another's soul!"

“So that's how it works.”

“Deities cultivate believers by nourishing their bodies and souls with divine power, transforming them into vessels more attuned to that power.”

"Synchronized Conversion divine spell operates on this principle, condensing the divine power transformation process into a short period. The template for soul reshaping is the caster themselves!"

"This divine spell not only consumes divine power but also devours the caster's spirit and soul! It treats the caster's soul as a material for spellcasting. Much like how we utilize soul crystals."

Jian Gui truly lived up to his reputation as an orthodox necromancer. After observing, he uncovered the truth behind this divine spell.

He was profoundly shaken.

The undead orthodoxy had already been deeply researched by other factions. This Synchronized Conversion divine spell had even approached the forefront of orthodoxy from another angle!

While Jian Gui was shaken, he also set aside his previous concerns.

Ever since Cang Xu's second teleportation back, he had been relentlessly gathering intelligence on Cang Xu.

From the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan, he had obtained much hidden knowledge about Cang Xu's new divine spells. Upon accessing this intelligence, Jian Gui had surmised that the divine spells possessed soul-altering effects.

Today's event merely provided him with firsthand confirmation.

“Fortunately, I had already subdued Cang Xu and tampered with his soul beforehand.”

“Had Cang Xu remained uncontrolled, the backdoor embedded in the soul template of Xiao Wang Zi, forged from his essence, would have been erased!”

Jian Gui had installed covert control mechanisms within Ku Feng, Xiao Wang Zi, Qi Ling, and Cang Xu.

This was the foundation of his confidence thus far, and the deepest reason for his trust in the other undead!

Just as Jian Gui had predicted, within a dozen seconds, Cang Xu voluntarily halted the divine spell, Synchronized Conversion.

Cang Xu gasped for breath, his vision darkening.

Without his guidance, Xiao Wang Zi felt the excruciating pain in his soul suddenly lessen, though lingering agony remained.

Chapter 750: Bottleneck

Xiao Wang Zi was obedient and well-behaved, firmly remembering Cang Xu's earlier advice and persevering in his prayers.

But his soul had just undergone a violent transformation, and the act of praying itself demanded the expenditure of spiritual energy, like forcing an exhausted runner to keep going.

“My Lord, Charming Blue Goddess, You are a Goddess full of grace and mercy. Please rescue Your faithful believers. Grant me one chance to believe in You, rather than perish in the jaws of beasts...”

Xiao Wang Zi strained every ounce of strength to sustain his prayer, quickly reaching his limit. His voice grew fainter and fainter until he collapsed into unconsciousness right where he stood.

A brief silence fell over the crude shack.

Seconds later, Jian Gui broke the silence: "Xu Jia, you've worked hard this time. I can see you paid a price in terms of your soul to convert Xiao Wang Zi."

Jian Gui immediately pledged that if the chance arose, he would compensate Cang Xu with hundreds of soul crystals for Xiao Wang Zi.
